## Response:
7/13/2026, 2:12:22 AM

Saya akan susun sebagai peta keterampilan praktis, bukan tutorial MyBatis dasar saja: mulai dari integrasi Jersey/JAX-RS, SQL mapping, transaksi, observability, sampai deployment/runtime concerns. Saya cek rujukan resmi dulu agar versi dan istilah tidak meleset.

## Introduction

**Java MyBatis in Jersey/JAX-RS based application** berarti pola akses database di mana **resource layer JAX-RS/Jersey menangani HTTP**, lalu memanggil **service layer**, lalu **repository/mapper layer MyBatis** menjalankan SQL eksplisit ke database. MyBatis sendiri adalah persistence framework untuk **custom SQL, stored procedure, dan advanced mapping**, dengan konfigurasi via XML atau annotation, serta mapping dari primitive, `Map`, dan POJO ke record database. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/index.html))

Mental model paling penting: **MyBatis bukan full ORM seperti JPA/Hibernate**. MyBatis lebih tepat dilihat sebagai **SQL mapper**: Java tetap memodelkan use case dan domain, sementara SQL tetap eksplisit, bisa di-review DBA, bisa dioptimalkan, dan cocok untuk PostgreSQL-heavy application. Di aplikasi Jersey/JAX-RS yang stateless, `SqlSessionFactory` idealnya application-scope, sedangkan `SqlSession` tidak thread-safe dan harus request/method-scope. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/getting-started.html))

Versi dokumentasi resmi MyBatis yang saya jadikan acuan adalah **MyBatis 3.5.19**, last published **2 Jan 2025**; sedangkan MyBatis Dynamic SQL resmi berada di **2.0.0**, last published **12 Mar 2026**. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/dependency-info.html))

---

## Diagram

```mermaid
flowchart TD
    A[JAX-RS Client Request] --> B[Jersey Resource<br/>@Path, @GET, @POST, @PUT, @DELETE]
    B --> C[Request Validation<br/>DTO, Bean Validation, Auth Context]
    C --> D[Application Service<br/>Use Case / Transaction Boundary]
    D --> E[Repository Interface]
    E --> F[MyBatis Mapper Interface]
    F --> G[Mapper XML / Annotation SQL]
    G --> H[SqlSession]
    H --> I[SqlSessionFactory<br/>Application Scope]
    I --> J[DataSource / Connection Pool]
    J --> K[(PostgreSQL)]

    D --> L[Transaction Policy]
    L --> H

    G --> M[ResultMap / TypeHandler / Dynamic SQL]
    M --> N[Domain Model / DTO Projection]

    H --> O[Logging / Metrics / Tracing]
    G --> P[Query Performance<br/>Index, EXPLAIN, Fetch Size, Batch]
```

**Invariant desain:**  
JAX-RS resource tidak boleh tahu detail SQL. Service layer memegang use case dan transaction boundary. Repository/mapper layer memegang query contract. MyBatis XML/annotation memegang SQL aktual.

---

## Decomposition Map - Java MyBatis in Jersey/JAX-RS Application

| Area / Skill | Fokus Praktis | Yang Perlu Dikuasai | Risiko / Pitfall |
|---|---|---|---|
| **1. MyBatis Mental Model** | Memahami posisi MyBatis sebagai SQL mapper | Custom SQL, mapper interface, XML mapper, annotation mapper, `SqlSession`, `SqlSessionFactory` | Menganggap MyBatis seperti ORM penuh; akhirnya domain model terlalu dipaksa mengikuti relational model |
| **2. Jersey/JAX-RS Integration Boundary** | Menentukan batas antara HTTP layer dan persistence layer | `@Path`, HTTP method resource, DTO input/output, service call | Resource langsung memanggil mapper dan membuat transaction logic tersebar |
| **3. Application Bootstrap** | Membuat `SqlSessionFactory` saat aplikasi start | Load `mybatis-config.xml`, register mapper, pilih environment | Membuat `SqlSessionFactory` berulang-ulang per request; ini bad smell karena seharusnya application-scope ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/getting-started.html)) |
| **4. Dependency Injection Integration** | Inject repository/service/resource | HK2 binder, CDI/Weld, factory/provider pattern untuk MyBatis | Salah scope: mapper/session disimpan sebagai singleton padahal session tidak thread-safe |
| **5. SqlSession Lifecycle** | Membuka dan menutup session secara benar | `try-with-resources`, request/method-scope, `commit`, `rollback`, `close` | Connection leak, stale local cache, race condition karena `SqlSession` dishare antar thread |
| **6. Transaction Boundary** | Menentukan letak commit/rollback | Service-layer transaction, per-use-case unit of work, JDBC vs MANAGED transaction | Commit di mapper/resource; rollback tidak konsisten; partial write pada multi-query use case |
| **7. DataSource & Connection Pooling** | Menghubungkan MyBatis ke PostgreSQL | `UNPOOLED`, `POOLED`, `JNDI`, atau external pool seperti HikariCP | Pool terlalu kecil/besar; connection timeout tidak dimonitor; tidak cocok untuk Kubernetes autoscaling |
| **8. MyBatis Configuration XML** | Konfigurasi runtime MyBatis | `properties`, `settings`, `typeAliases`, `typeHandlers`, `plugins`, `environments`, `mappers` ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/configuration.html)) | Config tumbuh tidak terkontrol; environment dev/test/prod bercampur |
| **9. Mapper Interface Design** | Membuat contract persistence yang type-safe | Interface per aggregate/table/use-case, method name konsisten, parameter object | Mapper terlalu “God interface”; query sulit ditemukan |
| **10. Mapper XML Design** | Menulis SQL eksplisit dan maintainable | `<select>`, `<insert>`, `<update>`, `<delete>`, namespace, statement id | SQL tersebar tanpa konvensi; duplikasi query; namespace bentrok |
| **11. Annotation-based Mapper** | Query sederhana tanpa XML | `@Select`, `@Insert`, `@Update`, `@Delete` | Annotation jadi tidak readable untuk query kompleks; MyBatis sendiri menyebut XML lebih cocok untuk mapping advanced ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/getting-started.html)) |
| **12. Parameter Binding** | Mengikat input Java ke SQL | `#{}` safe binding, `@Param`, parameter object, DTO criteria | Pakai `${}` untuk user input dan membuka SQL injection |
| **13. Result Mapping** | Mapping row/result set ke object | `resultType`, `resultMap`, alias column, constructor mapping | Mengandalkan auto mapping terlalu banyak; bug silent pada column/property mismatch |
| **14. Advanced ResultMap** | Mapping object graph kompleks | `association`, `collection`, `discriminator`, nested result, column prefix | N+1 query, object duplication, performa buruk jika `<id>` result tidak didefinisikan dengan benar ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/sqlmap-xml.html)) |
| **15. Dynamic SQL XML** | Query dengan filter opsional | `<if>`, `<choose>`, `<where>`, `<set>`, `<trim>`, `<foreach>` ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/dynamic-sql.html)) | Query branch sulit dites; kondisi kosong menghasilkan full table scan |
| **16. MyBatis Dynamic SQL Library** | Type-safe SQL builder di Java | DSL untuk `select`, `insert`, `update`, `delete`, flexible `where`, MyBatis3 rendering ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-dynamic-sql/docs/introduction.html)) | Logic SQL pindah terlalu banyak ke Java sehingga review SQL menjadi lebih sulit |
| **17. PostgreSQL-Specific Mapping** | Menggunakan fitur PostgreSQL secara aman | `jsonb`, array, enum, UUID, timestamp, stored function/procedure | Butuh custom `TypeHandler`; mapping waktu/timezone sering salah |
| **18. TypeHandler** | Custom mapping Java ↔ JDBC | Enum, JSON, array, encrypted value object, domain-specific type | Handler terlalu global dan memengaruhi query lain; enum ordinal berisiko saat enum berubah |
| **19. Stored Procedure / Function Call** | Integrasi dengan PL/pgSQL | `statementType=CALLABLE`, IN/OUT parameter, multiple result sets | Logic bisnis tersembunyi di database tanpa versioning/test yang jelas |
| **20. Pagination & Sorting** | Query list endpoint | Limit/offset, keyset pagination, whitelist sort column | Dynamic `ORDER BY` raw input; offset besar lambat |
| **21. Batch Operation** | Insert/update massal | Executor batch, JDBC batch, chunking, retry strategy | Batch terlalu besar; lock contention; gagal sebagian sulit direkonsiliasi |
| **22. Caching** | Mengontrol local cache dan second-level cache | `localCacheScope`, mapper cache, cache invalidation | Cache di aplikasi stateless/distributed bisa stale; local session cache bisa mengejutkan jika object dimutasi ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/java-api.html)) |
| **23. Error Handling** | Translasi error persistence ke API error | SQLException mapping, constraint violation, deadlock, timeout, duplicate key | Bocorkan detail SQL/database ke HTTP response |
| **24. Observability** | Melihat query behavior runtime | SQL logging, slow query log, metrics per mapper, tracing span | Logging parameter sensitif; SQL log terlalu verbose di production |
| **25. Performance Tuning** | Menjaga query scalable | `fetchSize`, timeout, prepared statement reuse, index awareness, EXPLAIN ANALYZE | Tuning hanya di Java, padahal bottleneck ada di SQL/index/schema |
| **26. Security** | Menjaga query dan data aman | Prepared binding, least privilege DB user, masking log, secret management | `${}` injection, hardcoded credential, log berisi PII |
| **27. Testing Strategy** | Menjamin mapper benar | Unit test service, integration test mapper, Testcontainers PostgreSQL, migration seed | Mock mapper berlebihan; query tidak pernah dites terhadap PostgreSQL asli |
| **28. Migration Compatibility** | Sinkron dengan schema evolution | Flyway/Liquibase, backward-compatible SQL, contract test mapper | Deploy aplikasi sebelum migration siap; query mengacu column yang belum ada |
| **29. Multi-Environment Deployment** | Dev/test/prod/on-prem/cloud | Environment-specific datasource, secret injection, Kubernetes config, JNDI/container-managed datasource | Config drift antar environment; pool config tidak cocok antara local dan production |
| **30. Repository Pattern Discipline** | Menjaga architecture bersih | Mapper hanya persistence, repository bisa wrap mapper, service pegang business flow | Mapper dipakai langsung dari banyak layer sehingga sulit refactor |

---

## Struktur Layer yang Direkomendasikan

```text
resource/
  OrderResource.java              -> HTTP contract, status code, request/response DTO

service/
  OrderService.java               -> use case, transaction boundary, orchestration

repository/
  OrderRepository.java            -> persistence abstraction, optional wrapper over mapper

mapper/
  OrderMapper.java                -> MyBatis mapper interface
  OrderMapper.xml                 -> SQL statements and result maps

model/
  Order.java                      -> domain/entity-ish model

dto/
  CreateOrderRequest.java
  OrderResponse.java

config/
  MyBatisFactory.java
  MyBatisBinder.java
  TransactionFilter.java / UnitOfWork.java
```

**Practical rule:**  
Untuk aplikasi Jersey/JAX-RS non-Spring, jangan otomatis meniru MyBatis-Spring. Buat sendiri pola yang jelas: `SqlSessionFactory` singleton/application-scope, `SqlSession` per request/use-case, mapper diperoleh dari session, lalu commit/rollback dikelola di service boundary.

---

## Design Decision Map

| Keputusan | Pilihan Umum | Rekomendasi Praktis |
|---|---|---|
| Mapper style | XML vs annotation | XML untuk query kompleks; annotation hanya untuk query sederhana |
| Transaction boundary | Resource vs service vs repository | Service layer |
| Session scope | Singleton vs request/method | Request/method; jangan singleton |
| SQL ownership | Java code vs XML mapper | XML mapper untuk maintainability dan DBA review |
| Dynamic query | XML dynamic SQL vs MyBatis Dynamic SQL DSL | XML untuk query app biasa; DSL untuk query builder yang sangat composable |
| Result mapping | Auto mapping vs explicit resultMap | Explicit `resultMap` untuk query penting/kompleks |
| Pagination | Offset vs keyset | Offset untuk admin/simple list; keyset untuk high-volume feed/list |
| Pooling | MyBatis POOLED vs external pool | External pool/JNDI lebih cocok untuk production-grade service |
| Error handling | Raw exception vs domain exception | Translasi ke domain/API error di service/resource boundary |
| Testing | Mock vs real DB | Mapper wajib integration test dengan PostgreSQL realistis |

---

## Roadmap Alur Belajar Singkat

| Tahap | Fokus | Target Output |
|---|---|---|
| **1** | MyBatis mental model, `SqlSessionFactory`, `SqlSession`, mapper interface | Bisa membuat query CRUD sederhana dari Jersey service |
| **2** | Mapper XML, parameter binding, resultMap | Bisa mapping query join ke DTO/domain object |
| **3** | Transaction boundary dan session lifecycle | Bisa membuat unit-of-work aman untuk multi-query use case |
| **4** | Dynamic SQL, pagination, sorting, batch | Bisa membuat endpoint list/search/write massal yang aman |
| **5** | PostgreSQL-specific mapping dan TypeHandler | Bisa handle `jsonb`, enum, UUID, timestamp, array |
| **6** | Testing, observability, performance tuning | Bisa membuktikan query benar, cepat, dan bisa di-debug di production |
| **7** | Deployment concerns | Bisa menjalankan MyBatis app stabil di Docker/Kubernetes/on-prem/cloud |

## Response:
7/13/2026, 2:14:26 AM

Kita mulai dari fondasi yang menentukan stabil/tidaknya integrasi: bootstrap MyBatis, lifecycle `SqlSession`, transaction boundary, dan wiring ke Jersey/HK2. Saya akan fokus ke pola praktis production-style, bukan sekadar “hello world”.

# Cheatsheet - Java MyBatis Bootstrap, SqlSession Lifecycle, Transaction Boundary, dan Jersey/HK2 Integration Pattern

## 1. Introduction

Fokus cheatsheet ini adalah **cara memasang MyBatis secara benar di aplikasi Jersey/JAX-RS**, terutama ketika aplikasi **bukan Spring Boot** dan tidak memakai `mybatis-spring`.

Poin paling penting:

- `SqlSessionFactory` adalah **application-scope / singleton-like**.
- `SqlSession` adalah **request-scope atau method-scope**, tidak thread-safe, dan wajib ditutup.
- Mapper MyBatis diperoleh dari `SqlSession`, sehingga mapper juga jangan diperlakukan sebagai singleton global.
- Transaction boundary sebaiknya berada di **service layer**, bukan resource layer dan bukan mapper layer.
- Jersey/HK2 dipakai untuk wiring dependency, tetapi lifecycle database tetap harus dikontrol eksplisit.

MyBatis secara resmi menyatakan bahwa `SqlSessionFactory` sebaiknya hidup selama aplikasi berjalan, sedangkan `SqlSession` tidak boleh dishare antar thread dan scope terbaiknya adalah request atau method scope. Dokumentasi juga menekankan bahwa session harus selalu ditutup, misalnya dengan `try-with-resources`. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/getting-started.html))

---

## 2. Mental Model

```mermaid id="bg9vlp"
flowchart TD
    A[HTTP Request] --> B[Jersey Resource]
    B --> C[Service Layer]
    C --> D[Transaction Boundary]
    D --> E[SqlSession Open]
    E --> F[Mapper Instance]
    F --> G[Mapper XML SQL]
    G --> H[(PostgreSQL)]
    H --> I[Result Mapping]
    I --> C
    C --> J[Commit / Rollback]
    J --> K[SqlSession Close]
    K --> L[HTTP Response]
```

### Core Rule

```text id="n83p3b"
Resource menerima HTTP.
Service menjalankan use case.
Transaction boundary hidup di service.
Repository/mapper menjalankan SQL.
SqlSession hidup hanya selama use case/request.
SqlSessionFactory hidup selama aplikasi berjalan.
```

---

## 3. Scope Map

| Komponen | Scope yang Benar | Boleh Singleton? | Catatan |
|---|---:|---:|---|
| `SqlSessionFactoryBuilder` | Method/local variable | Tidak perlu | Dipakai untuk membangun `SqlSessionFactory`, lalu dibuang |
| `SqlSessionFactory` | Application scope | Ya | Satu factory per database/environment aktif |
| `SqlSession` | Request/method scope | Tidak | Tidak thread-safe; harus ditutup |
| Mapper interface instance | Method/request scope | Tidak ideal | Mapper diperoleh dari `SqlSession`; scope efektifnya mengikuti session |
| Repository | Application scope | Ya, jika stateless | Jangan menyimpan `SqlSession` sebagai field |
| Service | Application scope | Ya, jika stateless | Boleh inject repository/unit-of-work |
| Jersey Resource | Tergantung konfigurasi Jersey | Hati-hati | Jangan simpan mutable request state sebagai field |
| DataSource / Pool | Application scope | Ya | Umumnya external pool/JNDI/HikariCP |

---

## 4. Maven Dependencies

Minimal dependency untuk MyBatis core:

```xml id="bptmhh"
<dependency>
  <groupId>org.mybatis</groupId>
  <artifactId>mybatis</artifactId>
  <version>3.5.19</version>
</dependency>
```

Tambahkan PostgreSQL JDBC driver:

```xml id="8idyuq"
<dependency>
  <groupId>org.postgresql</groupId>
  <artifactId>postgresql</artifactId>
  <version>${postgresql.version}</version>
</dependency>
```

Untuk Jersey/HK2, biasanya aplikasi sudah punya dependency Jersey server/container. Jersey mendukung custom injection dengan `Factory`/`Supplier` dan binder yang diregister ke `ResourceConfig`; dokumentasi Jersey menunjukkan pola register custom binder di `ResourceConfig`. ([Eclipse EE4J](https://eclipse-ee4j.github.io/jersey.github.io/documentation/latest/ioc.html))

---

## 5. File Layout yang Direkomendasikan

```text id="gohgb4"
src/main/java/com/company/app/
  api/
    OrderResource.java

  service/
    OrderService.java

  repository/
    OrderRepository.java
    MyBatisOrderRepository.java

  mapper/
    OrderMapper.java

  persistence/
    MyBatisFactory.java
    MyBatisUnitOfWork.java
    MyBatisUnitOfWorkFactory.java
    MyBatisBinder.java

  model/
    Order.java

  dto/
    CreateOrderRequest.java
    OrderResponse.java

src/main/resources/
  mybatis-config.xml
  mapper/
    OrderMapper.xml
```

**Prinsipnya:** file Java mengatur flow aplikasi; XML mapper mengatur SQL.

---

## 6. `mybatis-config.xml`

Contoh sederhana:

```xml id="jswpvk"
<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8" ?>
<!DOCTYPE configuration
  PUBLIC "-//mybatis.org//DTD Config 3.0//EN"
  "https://mybatis.org/dtd/mybatis-3-config.dtd">

<configuration>
  <properties resource="database.properties"/>

  <settings>
    <setting name="mapUnderscoreToCamelCase" value="true"/>
    <setting name="localCacheScope" value="SESSION"/>
    <setting name="jdbcTypeForNull" value="NULL"/>
  </settings>

  <typeAliases>
    <package name="com.company.app.model"/>
  </typeAliases>

  <environments default="development">
    <environment id="development">
      <transactionManager type="JDBC"/>
      <dataSource type="POOLED">
        <property name="driver" value="${db.driver}"/>
        <property name="url" value="${db.url}"/>
        <property name="username" value="${db.username}"/>
        <property name="password" value="${db.password}"/>
      </dataSource>
    </environment>
  </environments>

  <mappers>
    <mapper resource="mapper/OrderMapper.xml"/>
  </mappers>
</configuration>
```

MyBatis mendukung beberapa `environment`, tetapi hanya **satu environment aktif per `SqlSessionFactory`**. Jika aplikasi terhubung ke dua database berbeda, buat dua `SqlSessionFactory`, satu per database. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/configuration.html))

---

## 7. Bootstrap `SqlSessionFactory`

```java id="6t2bye"
package com.company.app.persistence;

import java.io.IOException;
import java.io.Reader;

import org.apache.ibatis.io.Resources;
import org.apache.ibatis.session.SqlSessionFactory;
import org.apache.ibatis.session.SqlSessionFactoryBuilder;

public final class MyBatisFactory {
    private static final String CONFIG_RESOURCE = "mybatis-config.xml";

    private final SqlSessionFactory sqlSessionFactory;

    public MyBatisFactory(String environment) {
        this.sqlSessionFactory = buildSqlSessionFactory(environment);
    }

    public SqlSessionFactory sqlSessionFactory() {
        return sqlSessionFactory;
    }

    private SqlSessionFactory buildSqlSessionFactory(String environment) {
        try (Reader reader = Resources.getResourceAsReader(CONFIG_RESOURCE)) {
            return new SqlSessionFactoryBuilder().build(reader, environment);
        } catch (IOException e) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("Failed to load MyBatis configuration", e);
        }
    }
}
```

### Kenapa `SqlSessionFactoryBuilder` tidak disimpan?

Karena builder hanya dipakai untuk membuat factory. Setelah `SqlSessionFactory` terbentuk, factory itulah yang dipakai sepanjang runtime aplikasi. Ini selaras dengan rekomendasi MyBatis: builder cukup method scope, factory application scope. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/getting-started.html))

---

## 8. Jersey `ResourceConfig`

Jakarta REST menggunakan `@ApplicationPath` untuk menentukan base URI bagi resource yang memakai `@Path`. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/restful-ws/3.1/apidocs/jakarta.ws.rs/jakarta/ws/rs/applicationpath))

```java id="jvezwb"
package com.company.app;

import jakarta.ws.rs.ApplicationPath;

import org.glassfish.jersey.server.ResourceConfig;

import com.company.app.persistence.MyBatisBinder;

@ApplicationPath("/api")
public class AppResourceConfig extends ResourceConfig {

    public AppResourceConfig() {
        packages("com.company.app.api");

        String environment = resolveEnvironment();

        register(new MyBatisBinder(environment));
    }

    private String resolveEnvironment() {
        return System.getenv().getOrDefault("APP_ENV", "development");
    }
}
```

---

## 9. HK2 Binder untuk MyBatis

Ada dua pendekatan praktis:

1. Inject `SqlSessionFactory`, lalu buka session manual di service/unit-of-work.
2. Inject `UnitOfWorkFactory`, lalu service menjalankan transaksi via callback.

Untuk aplikasi production-style, pendekatan kedua biasanya lebih rapi karena transaction lifecycle tidak tersebar.

```java id="4qbda2"
package com.company.app.persistence;

import jakarta.inject.Singleton;

import org.apache.ibatis.session.SqlSessionFactory;
import org.glassfish.hk2.utilities.binding.AbstractBinder;

public class MyBatisBinder extends AbstractBinder {

    private final String environment;

    public MyBatisBinder(String environment) {
        this.environment = environment;
    }

    @Override
    protected void configure() {
        MyBatisFactory factory = new MyBatisFactory(environment);

        bind(factory.sqlSessionFactory())
            .to(SqlSessionFactory.class)
            .in(Singleton.class);

        bind(MyBatisUnitOfWorkFactory.class)
            .to(MyBatisUnitOfWorkFactory.class)
            .in(Singleton.class);

        bind(MyBatisOrderRepository.class)
            .to(OrderRepository.class)
            .in(Singleton.class);

        bind(OrderService.class)
            .to(OrderService.class)
            .in(Singleton.class);
    }
}
```

Jersey mendokumentasikan bahwa custom binder perlu diregister di `ResourceConfig`, dan binder dapat dipakai untuk mendefinisikan injection binding serta scope. ([Eclipse EE4J](https://eclipse-ee4j.github.io/jersey.github.io/documentation/latest/ioc.html))

---

## 10. Unit of Work Pattern

### Interface

```java id="6n61fc"
package com.company.app.persistence;

@FunctionalInterface
public interface MyBatisWork<T> {
    T execute(MyBatisSessionContext context);
}
```

```java id="s6qvq4"
package com.company.app.persistence;

import org.apache.ibatis.session.SqlSession;

public final class MyBatisSessionContext {
    private final SqlSession session;

    MyBatisSessionContext(SqlSession session) {
        this.session = session;
    }

    public <T> T mapper(Class<T> mapperType) {
        return session.getMapper(mapperType);
    }
}
```

### Factory

```java id="3mrxlq"
package com.company.app.persistence;

import jakarta.inject.Inject;

import org.apache.ibatis.session.SqlSession;
import org.apache.ibatis.session.SqlSessionFactory;

public class MyBatisUnitOfWorkFactory {

    private final SqlSessionFactory sqlSessionFactory;

    @Inject
    public MyBatisUnitOfWorkFactory(SqlSessionFactory sqlSessionFactory) {
        this.sqlSessionFactory = sqlSessionFactory;
    }

    public <T> T readOnly(MyBatisWork<T> work) {
        try (SqlSession session = sqlSessionFactory.openSession()) {
            return work.execute(new MyBatisSessionContext(session));
        }
    }

    public <T> T transactional(MyBatisWork<T> work) {
        try (SqlSession session = sqlSessionFactory.openSession(false)) {
            try {
                T result = work.execute(new MyBatisSessionContext(session));
                session.commit();
                return result;
            } catch (RuntimeException | Error e) {
                session.rollback();
                throw e;
            }
        }
    }
}
```

`SqlSession` memiliki operasi `commit`, `rollback`, `close`, dan `getMapper`; Javadoc MyBatis mendeskripsikan `SqlSession` sebagai interface utama untuk menjalankan command, mengambil mapper, dan mengelola transaksi. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/apidocs/org/apache/ibatis/session/SqlSession.html))

---

## 11. Service Layer sebagai Transaction Boundary

```java id="1jav0u"
package com.company.app.service;

import jakarta.inject.Inject;

import com.company.app.dto.CreateOrderRequest;
import com.company.app.dto.OrderResponse;
import com.company.app.mapper.OrderMapper;
import com.company.app.model.Order;
import com.company.app.persistence.MyBatisUnitOfWorkFactory;

public class OrderService {

    private final MyBatisUnitOfWorkFactory unitOfWorkFactory;

    @Inject
    public OrderService(MyBatisUnitOfWorkFactory unitOfWorkFactory) {
        this.unitOfWorkFactory = unitOfWorkFactory;
    }

    public OrderResponse createOrder(CreateOrderRequest request) {
        return unitOfWorkFactory.transactional(ctx -> {
            OrderMapper mapper = ctx.mapper(OrderMapper.class);

            Order order = Order.create(
                request.customerId(),
                request.items()
            );

            mapper.insertOrder(order);

            return OrderResponse.from(order);
        });
    }

    public OrderResponse getOrder(long orderId) {
        return unitOfWorkFactory.readOnly(ctx -> {
            OrderMapper mapper = ctx.mapper(OrderMapper.class);

            Order order = mapper.findById(orderId);
            if (order == null) {
                throw new OrderNotFoundException(orderId);
            }

            return OrderResponse.from(order);
        });
    }
}
```

### Kenapa service layer?

Karena satu use case bisa berisi beberapa operasi:

```text id="6nztu0"
createOrder:
  insert order
  insert order items
  reserve inventory
  write audit log
  publish outbox event
```

Semua itu harus berada dalam satu transaction boundary bila konsistensi database mengharuskannya. Kalau commit dilakukan di mapper, transaksi akan pecah dan rollback menjadi sulit.

---

## 12. Repository Wrapper Pattern

Ada dua gaya.

### Gaya A - Service langsung pakai Mapper

Cocok untuk aplikasi kecil/menengah.

```java id="a9b7gm"
return unitOfWorkFactory.transactional(ctx -> {
    OrderMapper mapper = ctx.mapper(OrderMapper.class);
    mapper.insertOrder(order);
    mapper.insertItems(order.items());
    return order.id();
});
```

### Gaya B - Service pakai Repository

Cocok untuk aplikasi enterprise/regulatory/complex case management karena repository bisa menyembunyikan detail mapper dan SQL orchestration.

```java id="v74bxt"
public interface OrderRepository {
    Order findById(long id);
    void save(Order order);
}
```

```java id="a7sjtw"
public class MyBatisOrderRepository implements OrderRepository {

    private final MyBatisSessionContext context;

    public MyBatisOrderRepository(MyBatisSessionContext context) {
        this.context = context;
    }

    @Override
    public Order findById(long id) {
        return context.mapper(OrderMapper.class).findById(id);
    }

    @Override
    public void save(Order order) {
        OrderMapper mapper = context.mapper(OrderMapper.class);
        mapper.insertOrder(order);
        mapper.insertItems(order.items());
    }
}
```

Namun hati-hati: contoh di atas membutuhkan repository yang dibuat per session/context. Jika repository dibuat singleton, jangan simpan `MyBatisSessionContext` sebagai field.

Versi lebih aman:

```java id="7y0qrs"
public class OrderService {

    private final MyBatisUnitOfWorkFactory unitOfWorkFactory;

    public OrderResponse createOrder(CreateOrderRequest request) {
        return unitOfWorkFactory.transactional(ctx -> {
            OrderRepository repository = new MyBatisOrderRepository(ctx);

            Order order = Order.create(request.customerId(), request.items());
            repository.save(order);

            return OrderResponse.from(order);
        });
    }
}
```

---

## 13. Mapper Interface

```java id="c4va1t"
package com.company.app.mapper;

import org.apache.ibatis.annotations.Param;

import com.company.app.model.Order;

public interface OrderMapper {

    Order findById(@Param("id") long id);

    int insertOrder(Order order);

    int insertItems(@Param("order") Order order);
}
```

Mapper instance sebaiknya diminta dari `SqlSession` saat dipakai. Dokumentasi MyBatis menyatakan mapper diperoleh dari `SqlSession`; scope terluasnya mengikuti session, tetapi scope terbaiknya adalah method scope. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/getting-started.html))

---

## 14. Mapper XML

```xml id="520emh"
<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8" ?>
<!DOCTYPE mapper
  PUBLIC "-//mybatis.org//DTD Mapper 3.0//EN"
  "https://mybatis.org/dtd/mybatis-3-mapper.dtd">

<mapper namespace="com.company.app.mapper.OrderMapper">

  <resultMap id="OrderResultMap" type="Order">
    <id property="id" column="id"/>
    <result property="customerId" column="customer_id"/>
    <result property="status" column="status"/>
    <result property="createdAt" column="created_at"/>
    <result property="updatedAt" column="updated_at"/>
  </resultMap>

  <select id="findById" resultMap="OrderResultMap">
    SELECT
      id,
      customer_id,
      status,
      created_at,
      updated_at
    FROM orders
    WHERE id = #{id}
  </select>

  <insert id="insertOrder"
          parameterType="Order"
          useGeneratedKeys="true"
          keyProperty="id">
    INSERT INTO orders (
      customer_id,
      status,
      created_at,
      updated_at
    )
    VALUES (
      #{customerId},
      #{status},
      #{createdAt},
      #{updatedAt}
    )
  </insert>

  <insert id="insertItems">
    INSERT INTO order_items (
      order_id,
      product_id,
      quantity,
      unit_price
    )
    VALUES
    <foreach collection="order.items"
             item="item"
             separator=",">
      (
        #{order.id},
        #{item.productId},
        #{item.quantity},
        #{item.unitPrice}
      )
    </foreach>
  </insert>

</mapper>
```

---

## 15. Resource Layer

```java id="yq1vew"
package com.company.app.api;

import jakarta.inject.Inject;
import jakarta.validation.Valid;
import jakarta.ws.rs.GET;
import jakarta.ws.rs.POST;
import jakarta.ws.rs.Path;
import jakarta.ws.rs.PathParam;
import jakarta.ws.rs.core.Response;

import com.company.app.dto.CreateOrderRequest;
import com.company.app.dto.OrderResponse;
import com.company.app.service.OrderService;

@Path("/orders")
public class OrderResource {

    private final OrderService orderService;

    @Inject
    public OrderResource(OrderService orderService) {
        this.orderService = orderService;
    }

    @POST
    public Response create(@Valid CreateOrderRequest request) {
        OrderResponse response = orderService.createOrder(request);
        return Response.status(Response.Status.CREATED)
            .entity(response)
            .build();
    }

    @GET
    @Path("/{id}")
    public Response findById(@PathParam("id") long id) {
        OrderResponse response = orderService.getOrder(id);
        return Response.ok(response).build();
    }
}
```

Resource tidak membuka `SqlSession`, tidak melakukan commit, tidak tahu SQL, dan tidak tahu mapper XML.

---

## 16. Transaction Pattern

| Use Case | Transaction Mode | Pattern |
|---|---|---|
| GET by ID | Read-only session | `unitOfWork.readOnly(...)` |
| Search/list | Read-only session | Batasi pagination dan filter |
| Create aggregate | Transactional | Insert root + child + audit dalam satu transaksi |
| Update status | Transactional | Load current state → validate transition → update |
| Delete/archive | Transactional | Soft delete + audit log |
| Batch import | Transactional per chunk | Chunking, bukan satu transaksi raksasa |
| Outbox event | Same DB transaction | Insert domain data + outbox event bersama |

---

## 17. Anti-Pattern yang Harus Dihindari

| Anti-Pattern | Contoh Buruk | Dampak |
|---|---|---|
| Singleton `SqlSession` | `private static SqlSession session;` | Race condition, stale cache, data corruption |
| Singleton mapper | Mapper disimpan sebagai field singleton | Mapper terikat ke session yang salah |
| Commit di mapper | `mapper.insert(); session.commit();` | Transaction tersebar |
| Resource membuka transaksi | `OrderResource` memanggil `openSession()` | HTTP layer terlalu tahu persistence |
| Semua pakai auto-commit | Tiap query commit sendiri | Use case multi-step tidak atomic |
| XML mapper tanpa resultMap eksplisit | Mengandalkan auto mapping untuk query penting | Bug mapping sulit terlihat |
| `${}` untuk parameter user | `ORDER BY ${sort}` tanpa whitelist | SQL injection |
| Session tidak ditutup | Tidak pakai `try-with-resources` | Connection leak |
| Satu factory untuk banyak DB | Satu `SqlSessionFactory` dipaksa multi-database | Environment/config kacau |

---

## 18. Production-Grade Checklist

| Checklist | Wajib? | Catatan |
|---|---:|---|
| `SqlSessionFactory` dibuat sekali saat aplikasi start | Ya | Application scope |
| `SqlSession` dibuat per use case/request | Ya | Jangan simpan sebagai field |
| Semua session ditutup | Ya | Pakai `try-with-resources` |
| Service layer mengontrol transaction boundary | Ya | Commit/rollback konsisten |
| Mapper XML punya namespace sesuai interface | Ya | Hindari mismatch |
| Query penting pakai explicit `resultMap` | Ya | Lebih aman untuk refactor |
| SQL log bisa diaktifkan saat debugging | Ya | Jangan bocorkan data sensitif |
| Database credential dari env/secret | Ya | Jangan hardcode |
| Migration jalan sebelum app start | Ya | Flyway/Liquibase |
| Integration test mapper dengan PostgreSQL asli/Testcontainers | Sangat disarankan | Mock mapper tidak cukup |
| Timeout query/pool dikontrol | Ya | Hindari thread menggantung |
| Error database diterjemahkan ke API error | Ya | Jangan expose raw SQL exception |

---

## 19. Failure Model

| Failure | Penyebab Umum | Mitigasi |
|---|---|---|
| Connection leak | Session tidak ditutup | `try-with-resources`; test leak; pool metrics |
| Dirty partial write | Commit terlalu awal | Transaction boundary di service |
| Race condition | `SqlSession` dishare | Session per request/method |
| Query lambat | Missing index, full scan, pagination buruk | EXPLAIN ANALYZE, index review, keyset pagination |
| Deadlock | Update row dalam urutan berbeda | Konsistenkan lock ordering |
| Duplicate data | Retry tanpa idempotency | Idempotency key / unique constraint |
| Stale object | Local cache session terlalu panjang | Session pendek; clear cache bila perlu |
| SQL injection | `${}` dengan user input | `#{}` binding; whitelist untuk identifier |
| Config drift | Environment dev/prod beda manual | Config via env/secret/template |
| Mapper mismatch | XML id/namespace salah | Startup validation + integration test |

---

## 20. Minimal End-to-End Flow

```text id="4r91wq"
POST /api/orders
  -> OrderResource.create()
    -> OrderService.createOrder()
      -> unitOfWork.transactional()
        -> open SqlSession
        -> get OrderMapper
        -> insert orders
        -> insert order_items
        -> commit
        -> close SqlSession
    -> return 201 Created
```

**Invariant:** kalau `insert order_items` gagal, `insert orders` harus rollback.

---

## 21. Versi “Bad” vs “Good”

### Bad

```java id="cd92oc"
@Path("/orders")
public class OrderResource {

    private static SqlSession session;

    @POST
    public Response create(CreateOrderRequest request) {
        OrderMapper mapper = session.getMapper(OrderMapper.class);
        mapper.insertOrder(...);
        session.commit();
        return Response.ok().build();
    }
}
```

Masalah:

- `SqlSession` static.
- Tidak thread-safe.
- Resource layer mengelola transaksi.
- Session tidak jelas kapan ditutup.
- Commit tersebar.

### Good

```java id="gp2b7s"
@Path("/orders")
public class OrderResource {

    private final OrderService orderService;

    @Inject
    public OrderResource(OrderService orderService) {
        this.orderService = orderService;
    }

    @POST
    public Response create(CreateOrderRequest request) {
        return Response.status(Response.Status.CREATED)
            .entity(orderService.createOrder(request))
            .build();
    }
}
```

```java id="foy5gi"
public class OrderService {

    private final MyBatisUnitOfWorkFactory uow;

    public OrderResponse createOrder(CreateOrderRequest request) {
        return uow.transactional(ctx -> {
            OrderMapper mapper = ctx.mapper(OrderMapper.class);
            Order order = Order.create(request.customerId(), request.items());

            mapper.insertOrder(order);
            mapper.insertItems(order);

            return OrderResponse.from(order);
        });
    }
}
```

---

## 22. Decision Table

| Kondisi | Pilihan yang Tepat |
|---|---|
| Aplikasi Jersey murni/non-Spring | Manual `SqlSessionFactory` + Unit of Work |
| Aplikasi memakai Spring | Gunakan MyBatis-Spring / `SqlSessionTemplate` |
| Query sederhana | Annotation mapper boleh |
| Query kompleks/join/dynamic SQL | XML mapper lebih maintainable |
| Banyak optional filter | Dynamic SQL XML atau MyBatis Dynamic SQL |
| Butuh multi-step atomic use case | Transaction boundary di service |
| Butuh read-only high volume | Session pendek + pagination + index |
| Butuh audit/outbox | Satu transaksi dengan write utama |
| Butuh multi-database | Satu `SqlSessionFactory` per database |

MyBatis-Spring berbeda dari pola manual ini: `SqlSessionTemplate` thread-safe, mengelola lifecycle session, serta commit/rollback berdasarkan transaksi Spring. Itu relevan kalau aplikasinya Spring; untuk Jersey non-Spring, kita perlu mengelola lifecycle secara eksplisit atau membangun abstraction seperti Unit of Work. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/spring/sqlsession.html))

---

## 23. Roadmap Belajar Singkat

| Urutan | Materi | Target Praktis |
|---:|---|---|
| 1 | `SqlSessionFactory`, `SqlSession`, mapper lifecycle | Paham scope dan lifecycle |
| 2 | Bootstrap MyBatis di Jersey | Bisa start app dengan config bersih |
| 3 | HK2 binder dan DI | Bisa inject service/repository/factory |
| 4 | Unit of Work | Bisa membuat transaksi konsisten |
| 5 | Mapper interface + XML | Bisa CRUD dengan SQL eksplisit |
| 6 | ResultMap dan parameter binding | Bisa mapping data secara aman |
| 7 | Error handling dan rollback | Bisa desain failure-safe use case |
| 8 | Integration test dengan PostgreSQL | Bisa validasi mapper terhadap DB asli |

---

## Rekomendasi Materi Selanjutnya

Lanjutkan ke **“Java MyBatis Mapper XML, Parameter Binding, ResultMap, Dynamic SQL, dan PostgreSQL Query Pattern”** karena setelah lifecycle dan transaction boundary benar, bagian berikutnya adalah membuat SQL mapper yang aman, maintainable, dan cocok untuk PostgreSQL production workload.

## Response:
7/13/2026, 2:16:52 AM

Lanjut ke bagian mapper yang paling sering dipakai sehari-hari: XML mapper, parameter binding, result/resultMap, dynamic SQL, dan pola query PostgreSQL. Saya akan jaga fokus ke praktik di Jersey/JAX-RS, bukan mengulang lifecycle/session dari bagian sebelumnya.

# Cheatsheet - Java MyBatis Mapper XML, Parameter Binding, ResultMap, Dynamic SQL, dan PostgreSQL Query Pattern

## 1. Introduction

Di aplikasi **Jersey/JAX-RS + MyBatis**, bagian mapper adalah tempat kontrak antara **Java service layer** dan **SQL PostgreSQL** dibuat eksplisit.

Fokus materi ini:

```text
JAX-RS Resource
  -> Service / Use Case
    -> MyBatis Mapper Interface
      -> Mapper XML
        -> PostgreSQL SQL
          -> ResultMap / DTO / Domain Object
```

MyBatis dirancang agar developer tetap fokus menulis SQL; dokumentasi resminya menyebut Mapper XML sebagai pusat kekuatan MyBatis melalui mapped statements seperti `select`, `insert`, `update`, `delete`, `resultMap`, reusable `sql`, dan cache namespace. Versi dokumentasi MyBatis yang dirujuk di sini adalah **3.5.19**, last published **02 Jan 2025**. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/sqlmap-xml.html))

---

## 2. Mental Model Mapper

```mermaid id="ux5cgo"
flowchart TD
    A[Service Method] --> B[Mapper Interface Method]
    B --> C[Mapped Statement ID]
    C --> D[Mapper XML SQL]
    D --> E[Parameter Binding]
    E --> F[(PostgreSQL)]
    F --> G[ResultSet]
    G --> H[resultType / resultMap]
    H --> I[DTO / Domain Object]

    D --> J[Dynamic SQL]
    J --> K[if / choose / where / set / foreach]

    D --> L[Reusable SQL Fragment]
    L --> M[sql / include]
```

**Invariant:**  
Nama method di mapper interface harus cocok dengan `id` statement di XML mapper, dan `namespace` XML harus cocok dengan fully qualified name interface mapper.

---

## 3. Struktur Mapper Interface + XML

### Mapper Interface

```java id="0mtz8v"
package com.company.app.mapper;

import java.util.List;
import java.util.Optional;

import org.apache.ibatis.annotations.Param;

import com.company.app.dto.OrderSearchCriteria;
import com.company.app.model.Order;

public interface OrderMapper {

    Order findById(@Param("id") long id);

    List<Order> search(@Param("criteria") OrderSearchCriteria criteria);

    int insert(Order order);

    int updateStatus(@Param("id") long id,
                     @Param("status") String status,
                     @Param("version") long version);

    int deleteById(@Param("id") long id);
}
```

### Mapper XML

```xml id="htv6lm"
<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8" ?>
<!DOCTYPE mapper
  PUBLIC "-//mybatis.org//DTD Mapper 3.0//EN"
  "https://mybatis.org/dtd/mybatis-3-mapper.dtd">

<mapper namespace="com.company.app.mapper.OrderMapper">

  <resultMap id="OrderResultMap" type="com.company.app.model.Order">
    <id property="id" column="id"/>
    <result property="customerId" column="customer_id"/>
    <result property="status" column="status"/>
    <result property="version" column="version"/>
    <result property="createdAt" column="created_at"/>
    <result property="updatedAt" column="updated_at"/>
  </resultMap>

  <select id="findById" resultMap="OrderResultMap">
    SELECT
      id,
      customer_id,
      status,
      version,
      created_at,
      updated_at
    FROM orders
    WHERE id = #{id}
  </select>

</mapper>
```

---

## 4. Mapper XML First-Class Elements

| Element | Fungsi | Kapan Dipakai | Catatan Praktis |
|---|---|---|---|
| `cache` | Second-level cache per namespace | Jarang untuk microservice stateless | Hati-hati stale data |
| `cache-ref` | Reuse cache namespace lain | Jarang | Tambah coupling antar mapper |
| `resultMap` | Mapping ResultSet ke object | Query penting/kompleks | Lebih aman daripada implicit mapping |
| `sql` | Fragment SQL reusable | Kolom umum, join umum, filter umum | Jangan berlebihan sampai SQL sulit dibaca |
| `include` | Memakai fragment `sql` | Reuse select columns | Baik untuk standard projection |
| `select` | Query read | Paling sering | Gunakan `resultMap` untuk mapping serius |
| `insert` | Insert data | Create use case | Bisa pakai generated key |
| `update` | Update data | State transition / patch | Cocok untuk optimistic locking |
| `delete` | Delete data | Hard delete terbatas | Di enterprise sering diganti soft delete |

MyBatis Mapper XML memiliki first-class elements seperti `resultMap`, `sql`, `insert`, `update`, `delete`, dan `select`; `parameterMap` sudah deprecated dan inline parameter mapping lebih disarankan. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/sqlmap-xml.html))

---

## 5. Parameter Binding

### 5.1 Gunakan `#{}` untuk value binding

```xml id="6iovgo"
<select id="findById" resultMap="OrderResultMap">
  SELECT *
  FROM orders
  WHERE id = #{id}
</select>
```

`#{id}` membuat MyBatis menggunakan prepared statement parameter, mirip placeholder `?` di JDBC. Ini adalah default yang aman untuk value dari user/request. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/sqlmap-xml.html))

---

### 5.2 Hindari `${}` untuk input user

```xml id="n5zism"
<!-- Berbahaya jika sortColumn berasal langsung dari user -->
ORDER BY ${sortColumn}
```

`${}` melakukan string substitution langsung. Ini hanya layak untuk identifier SQL yang sudah **di-whitelist**, misalnya nama kolom sort yang dikontrol aplikasi.

### Pattern aman untuk sorting

```java id="36qe2m"
public enum OrderSortField {
    CREATED_AT("created_at"),
    STATUS("status"),
    CUSTOMER_ID("customer_id");

    private final String column;

    OrderSortField(String column) {
        this.column = column;
    }

    public String column() {
        return column;
    }
}
```

```java id="qdvfkx"
public record OrderSearchCriteria(
    String status,
    Long customerId,
    OrderSortField sortField,
    SortDirection sortDirection,
    int limit,
    int offset
) {}
```

```xml id="i0ap2p"
ORDER BY ${criteria.sortField.column} ${criteria.sortDirection}
```

**Syarat:** `sortField` dan `sortDirection` harus enum / whitelist, bukan raw string dari request.

---

### 5.3 Multiple Parameters dengan `@Param`

```java id="8ihn5s"
int updateStatus(@Param("id") long id,
                 @Param("status") String status,
                 @Param("version") long version);
```

```xml id="03xr1y"
<update id="updateStatus">
  UPDATE orders
  SET
    status = #{status},
    version = version + 1,
    updated_at = now()
  WHERE id = #{id}
    AND version = #{version}
</update>
```

Untuk method mapper dengan banyak parameter, MyBatis mendukung `@Param` agar parameter punya nama eksplisit; tanpa itu MyBatis memberi nama default seperti `param1`, `param2`, dan seterusnya. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/java-api.html))

---

## 6. `resultType` vs `resultMap`

| Pilihan | Cocok Untuk | Kelebihan | Risiko |
|---|---|---|---|
| `resultType` | Query sederhana, mapping 1:1 | Cepat ditulis | Rapuh untuk join/alias/field kompleks |
| `resultMap` | Query penting, join, aggregate object | Eksplisit dan aman | Lebih verbose |
| DTO Projection | API response/query read model | Efisien, tidak over-fetch | Bisa menambah banyak class |
| Domain Object | Use case business logic | Cocok untuk command/state transition | Jangan pakai untuk semua query list |

### `resultType` sederhana

```xml id="flyfe3"
<select id="countByStatus" resultType="long">
  SELECT count(*)
  FROM orders
  WHERE status = #{status}
</select>
```

### `resultMap` eksplisit

```xml id="5zreze"
<resultMap id="OrderResultMap" type="com.company.app.model.Order">
  <id property="id" column="id"/>
  <result property="customerId" column="customer_id"/>
  <result property="status" column="status"/>
  <result property="version" column="version"/>
  <result property="createdAt" column="created_at"/>
  <result property="updatedAt" column="updated_at"/>
</resultMap>
```

**Rule of thumb:**  
Untuk query yang dipakai di use case penting, gunakan `resultMap`. Untuk scalar/count/existence check, `resultType` cukup.

---

## 7. ResultMap Pattern

### 7.1 Flat entity mapping

```xml id="za9ehr"
<resultMap id="CustomerResultMap" type="com.company.app.model.Customer">
  <id property="id" column="id"/>
  <result property="name" column="name"/>
  <result property="email" column="email"/>
  <result property="status" column="status"/>
</resultMap>
```

### 7.2 DTO projection mapping

```java id="0r3cr9"
public record OrderSummaryDto(
    long id,
    String customerName,
    String status,
    int itemCount
) {}
```

```xml id="whucbp"
<resultMap id="OrderSummaryResultMap"
           type="com.company.app.dto.OrderSummaryDto">
  <constructor>
    <idArg column="id" javaType="long"/>
    <arg column="customer_name" javaType="string"/>
    <arg column="status" javaType="string"/>
    <arg column="item_count" javaType="int"/>
  </constructor>
</resultMap>
```

### 7.3 Join mapping dengan prefix

```xml id="g9csm9"
<resultMap id="OrderWithCustomerResultMap"
           type="com.company.app.dto.OrderWithCustomerDto">
  <id property="orderId" column="order_id"/>
  <result property="status" column="order_status"/>
  <association property="customer"
               javaType="com.company.app.dto.CustomerDto">
    <id property="id" column="customer_id"/>
    <result property="name" column="customer_name"/>
  </association>
</resultMap>
```

```xml id="kzgtx6"
<select id="findOrderWithCustomer" resultMap="OrderWithCustomerResultMap">
  SELECT
    o.id AS order_id,
    o.status AS order_status,
    c.id AS customer_id,
    c.name AS customer_name
  FROM orders o
  JOIN customers c ON c.id = o.customer_id
  WHERE o.id = #{id}
</select>
```

---

## 8. Reusable SQL Fragment

### 8.1 Standard columns

```xml id="6x11lo"
<sql id="OrderColumns">
  id,
  customer_id,
  status,
  version,
  created_at,
  updated_at
</sql>
```

```xml id="g0xf40"
<select id="findById" resultMap="OrderResultMap">
  SELECT
    <include refid="OrderColumns"/>
  FROM orders
  WHERE id = #{id}
</select>
```

### 8.2 Dengan table alias

```xml id="4vtlho"
<sql id="OrderColumnsWithAlias">
  o.id,
  o.customer_id,
  o.status,
  o.version,
  o.created_at,
  o.updated_at
</sql>
```

**Practical warning:**  
Fragment yang terlalu generic sering membuat SQL sulit dibaca. Gunakan fragment untuk kolom standar, bukan untuk menyembunyikan logic bisnis.

---

## 9. Dynamic SQL XML

MyBatis Dynamic SQL di XML menyediakan elemen seperti `if`, `choose`, `trim`, `where`, `set`, dan `foreach`; dokumentasi resminya menyebut elemen-elemen ini sebagai cara MyBatis mengurangi kebutuhan merangkai string SQL secara manual. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/dynamic-sql.html))

### 9.1 `<if>`

```xml id="mgc1rw"
<select id="search" resultMap="OrderResultMap">
  SELECT
    <include refid="OrderColumns"/>
  FROM orders
  WHERE 1 = 1

  <if test="criteria.status != null">
    AND status = #{criteria.status}
  </if>

  <if test="criteria.customerId != null">
    AND customer_id = #{criteria.customerId}
  </if>
</select>
```

Bisa jalan, tetapi `WHERE 1 = 1` biasanya lebih baik diganti `<where>`.

---

### 9.2 `<where>`

```xml id="pl5yd1"
<select id="search" resultMap="OrderResultMap">
  SELECT
    <include refid="OrderColumns"/>
  FROM orders

  <where>
    <if test="criteria.status != null">
      status = #{criteria.status}
    </if>

    <if test="criteria.customerId != null">
      AND customer_id = #{criteria.customerId}
    </if>
  </where>

  ORDER BY created_at DESC, id DESC
  LIMIT #{criteria.limit}
  OFFSET #{criteria.offset}
</select>
```

`<where>` membantu menghindari SQL invalid seperti `WHERE AND ...`.

---

### 9.3 `<choose>`

Gunakan ketika hanya boleh satu branch filter aktif.

```xml id="ty94y0"
<select id="findByReference" resultMap="OrderResultMap">
  SELECT
    <include refid="OrderColumns"/>
  FROM orders

  <where>
    <choose>
      <when test="id != null">
        id = #{id}
      </when>
      <when test="externalRef != null">
        external_ref = #{externalRef}
      </when>
      <otherwise>
        status = 'ACTIVE'
      </otherwise>
    </choose>
  </where>
</select>
```

Cocok untuk endpoint seperti:

```text
GET /orders?referenceType=ID&value=...
GET /orders?referenceType=EXTERNAL_REF&value=...
```

---

### 9.4 `<set>` untuk dynamic update

```xml id="j8f457"
<update id="patchOrder">
  UPDATE orders

  <set>
    <if test="patch.status != null">
      status = #{patch.status},
    </if>
    <if test="patch.notes != null">
      notes = #{patch.notes},
    </if>
    updated_at = now()
  </set>

  WHERE id = #{id}
    AND version = #{version}
</update>
```

`<set>` menghindari trailing comma invalid pada dynamic update.

---

### 9.5 `<foreach>` untuk `IN (...)`

```xml id="23nyyk"
<select id="findByIds" resultMap="OrderResultMap">
  SELECT
    <include refid="OrderColumns"/>
  FROM orders
  WHERE id IN
  <foreach collection="ids"
           item="id"
           open="("
           separator=","
           close=")">
    #{id}
  </foreach>
</select>
```

MyBatis mendokumentasikan `foreach` sebagai elemen umum untuk membangun kondisi `IN`, dengan dukungan `open`, `separator`, dan `close`, serta tidak menambahkan separator ekstra secara keliru. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/dynamic-sql.html))

**Pitfall:** list kosong bisa menghasilkan SQL invalid. Validasi di service layer:

```java id="4qy7hx"
if (ids == null || ids.isEmpty()) {
    return List.of();
}
```

---

## 10. PostgreSQL Query Pattern untuk MyBatis

### 10.1 Search endpoint pattern

```java id="v8jsnk"
public record OrderSearchCriteria(
    String status,
    Long customerId,
    Instant createdFrom,
    Instant createdTo,
    int limit,
    int offset
) {}
```

```xml id="zgvftf"
<select id="search" resultMap="OrderResultMap">
  SELECT
    <include refid="OrderColumns"/>
  FROM orders

  <where>
    <if test="criteria.status != null">
      status = #{criteria.status}
    </if>

    <if test="criteria.customerId != null">
      AND customer_id = #{criteria.customerId}
    </if>

    <if test="criteria.createdFrom != null">
      AND created_at &gt;= #{criteria.createdFrom}
    </if>

    <if test="criteria.createdTo != null">
      AND created_at &lt; #{criteria.createdTo}
    </if>
  </where>

  ORDER BY created_at DESC, id DESC
  LIMIT #{criteria.limit}
  OFFSET #{criteria.offset}
</select>
```

PostgreSQL menegaskan bahwa `LIMIT` tanpa `ORDER BY` yang menghasilkan urutan unik dapat memberi subset yang tidak predictable, dan `OFFSET` besar bisa tidak efisien karena row yang dilewati tetap perlu dihitung di server. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/queries-limit.html))

---

### 10.2 Keyset pagination pattern

Offset pagination:

```sql id="38s2yd"
ORDER BY created_at DESC, id DESC
LIMIT 50 OFFSET 10000
```

Keyset pagination:

```xml id="o7wwlo"
<select id="searchAfter" resultMap="OrderResultMap">
  SELECT
    <include refid="OrderColumns"/>
  FROM orders

  <where>
    <if test="criteria.status != null">
      status = #{criteria.status}
    </if>

    <if test="cursorCreatedAt != null and cursorId != null">
      AND (
        created_at &lt; #{cursorCreatedAt}
        OR (created_at = #{cursorCreatedAt} AND id &lt; #{cursorId})
      )
    </if>
  </where>

  ORDER BY created_at DESC, id DESC
  LIMIT #{limit}
</select>
```

**Gunakan keyset pagination jika:**

| Kondisi | Rekomendasi |
|---|---|
| List kecil/admin page | Offset masih oke |
| High-volume event/order/case list | Keyset lebih aman |
| User butuh lompat ke page 100 | Offset lebih sederhana |
| User scroll next/previous | Keyset lebih cocok |
| Data sering berubah | Keyset lebih stabil |

---

### 10.3 Existence check

Jangan ambil semua kolom hanya untuk cek ada/tidak.

```xml id="b2wziq"
<select id="existsByExternalRef" resultType="boolean">
  SELECT EXISTS (
    SELECT 1
    FROM orders
    WHERE external_ref = #{externalRef}
  )
</select>
```

---

### 10.4 Count query

```xml id="zd5gux"
<select id="countSearch" resultType="long">
  SELECT count(*)
  FROM orders

  <where>
    <if test="criteria.status != null">
      status = #{criteria.status}
    </if>

    <if test="criteria.customerId != null">
      AND customer_id = #{criteria.customerId}
    </if>
  </where>
</select>
```

**Warning:** `count(*)` pada tabel besar dengan filter kompleks bisa mahal. Jangan otomatis count di setiap list endpoint jika UI tidak benar-benar butuh total count.

---

### 10.5 Optimistic locking update

```xml id="05d2zr"
<update id="transitionStatus">
  UPDATE orders
  SET
    status = #{newStatus},
    version = version + 1,
    updated_at = now()
  WHERE id = #{id}
    AND status = #{expectedStatus}
    AND version = #{expectedVersion}
</update>
```

Service layer:

```java id="2gxz56"
int updated = mapper.transitionStatus(
    orderId,
    expectedStatus,
    newStatus,
    expectedVersion
);

if (updated == 0) {
    throw new ConcurrentModificationException(
        "Order was modified by another transaction"
    );
}
```

Pattern ini cocok untuk lifecycle object seperti:

```text
DRAFT -> SUBMITTED -> APPROVED -> FULFILLED
```

---

### 10.6 Soft delete / archive

```xml id="j4a9uf"
<update id="archiveById">
  UPDATE orders
  SET
    archived = true,
    archived_at = now(),
    updated_at = now(),
    version = version + 1
  WHERE id = #{id}
    AND archived = false
</update>
```

Untuk sistem enterprise/regulatory, soft delete biasanya lebih aman daripada hard delete karena auditability.

---

### 10.7 Insert dengan generated key

```xml id="3m6iea"
<insert id="insert"
        parameterType="com.company.app.model.Order"
        useGeneratedKeys="true"
        keyProperty="id">
  INSERT INTO orders (
    customer_id,
    status,
    version,
    created_at,
    updated_at
  )
  VALUES (
    #{customerId},
    #{status},
    #{version},
    #{createdAt},
    #{updatedAt}
  )
</insert>
```

Jika memakai PostgreSQL `RETURNING`, bisa gunakan pattern select/insert tergantung kebutuhan. Untuk flow kompleks, explicit `RETURNING` sering lebih jelas.

---

### 10.8 PostgreSQL `RETURNING` pattern

```xml id="88qs7z"
<select id="insertReturning" resultMap="OrderResultMap">
  INSERT INTO orders (
    customer_id,
    status,
    version,
    created_at,
    updated_at
  )
  VALUES (
    #{customerId},
    #{status},
    0,
    now(),
    now()
  )
  RETURNING
    id,
    customer_id,
    status,
    version,
    created_at,
    updated_at
</select>
```

Catatan: secara semantik ini SQL `INSERT`, tetapi di MyBatis bisa dipetakan sebagai statement yang mengembalikan result. Gunakan konsisten di project agar tidak membingungkan reviewer.

---

## 11. Batch Insert Pattern

### 11.1 Multi-row insert dengan `<foreach>`

```xml id="2i399d"
<insert id="insertItems">
  INSERT INTO order_items (
    order_id,
    product_id,
    quantity,
    unit_price
  )
  VALUES
  <foreach collection="items"
           item="item"
           separator=",">
    (
      #{item.orderId},
      #{item.productId},
      #{item.quantity},
      #{item.unitPrice}
    )
  </foreach>
</insert>
```

### 11.2 Chunking di service layer

```java id="4r4e8r"
private static final int CHUNK_SIZE = 500;

public void insertItems(List<OrderItem> items) {
    for (int start = 0; start < items.size(); start += CHUNK_SIZE) {
        int end = Math.min(start + CHUNK_SIZE, items.size());
        mapper.insertItems(items.subList(start, end));
    }
}
```

**Kenapa perlu chunking?**

| Risiko | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| SQL terlalu besar | Multi-row insert bisa melewati batas praktis |
| Lock terlalu lama | Transaction besar menahan resource |
| Error recovery sulit | Gagal di tengah batch besar susah direkonsiliasi |
| Memory pressure | Parameter list terlalu besar |

---

## 12. Dynamic ORDER BY yang Aman

### Bad

```xml id="95ujq5"
ORDER BY ${sort}
```

Jika `sort` berasal dari query parameter user, ini injection risk.

### Good

```java id="l7z01c"
public enum SortDirection {
    ASC,
    DESC
}
```

```java id="98ep2b"
public enum OrderSort {
    CREATED_AT("created_at"),
    UPDATED_AT("updated_at"),
    STATUS("status");

    private final String column;

    OrderSort(String column) {
        this.column = column;
    }

    public String column() {
        return column;
    }
}
```

```xml id="q21d3k"
ORDER BY ${criteria.sort.column} ${criteria.direction}
```

**Invariant:** hanya enum dari aplikasi yang boleh masuk ke `${}`. Semua value data tetap pakai `#{}`.

PostgreSQL sendiri mendefinisikan `ORDER BY` sebagai mekanisme eksplisit untuk menjamin urutan hasil; tanpa sort yang dipilih, urutan row tidak boleh diandalkan. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/queries-order.html))

---

## 13. Query untuk State Machine / Lifecycle

Untuk regulatory/case/order management, query sering terkait state transition.

### Find candidate cases

```xml id="jwzfb7"
<select id="findCasesEligibleForEscalation" resultMap="CaseResultMap">
  SELECT
    id,
    status,
    priority,
    assigned_team,
    due_at,
    version
  FROM cases
  WHERE status IN ('OPEN', 'IN_REVIEW')
    AND due_at &lt; now()
    AND escalation_locked = false
  ORDER BY due_at ASC, id ASC
  LIMIT #{limit}
</select>
```

### Lock candidate for processing

```xml id="rgrnlu"
<select id="lockCaseForEscalation" resultMap="CaseResultMap">
  SELECT
    id,
    status,
    priority,
    assigned_team,
    due_at,
    version
  FROM cases
  WHERE id = #{id}
  FOR UPDATE
</select>
```

### Transition with invariant

```xml id="pkcphy"
<update id="markEscalated">
  UPDATE cases
  SET
    status = 'ESCALATED',
    escalated_at = now(),
    version = version + 1
  WHERE id = #{id}
    AND status IN ('OPEN', 'IN_REVIEW')
    AND version = #{version}
</update>
```

**Practical point:** business invariant jangan hanya ada di Java. Untuk state transition penting, letakkan invariant juga di `WHERE` clause update.

---

## 14. Query Contract Pattern

Mapper method sebaiknya merepresentasikan **intent**, bukan sekadar SQL operation.

| Kurang Bagus | Lebih Bagus |
|---|---|
| `update(Order order)` | `transitionStatus(...)` |
| `selectOrders(...)` | `searchOrders(...)` |
| `getCase(...)` | `findCaseForReview(...)` |
| `updateFlag(...)` | `markEscalationLocked(...)` |
| `delete(...)` | `archiveById(...)` |
| `selectByStatus(...)` | `findCasesEligibleForEscalation(...)` |

Mapper tetap persistence layer, tetapi nama method harus membantu reviewer memahami use case.

---

## 15. Error Handling Pattern dari Mapper Result

### Update count sebagai signal

```java id="ed084i"
int updated = mapper.transitionStatus(id, expectedStatus, newStatus, version);

if (updated == 0) {
    throw new InvalidStateTransitionException(id, expectedStatus, newStatus);
}
```

### Insert count

```java id="47kyt2"
int inserted = mapper.insert(order);

if (inserted != 1) {
    throw new PersistenceException("Expected exactly one order row to be inserted");
}
```

### Delete/archive count

```java id="timysl"
int archived = mapper.archiveById(id);

if (archived == 0) {
    throw new NotFoundOrAlreadyArchivedException(id);
}
```

**Rule:** jangan abaikan return value `insert/update/delete`. Itu sering menjadi sinyal concurrency, invalid state, atau missing row.

---

## 16. Local Cache Awareness

MyBatis membuat local cache per `SqlSession`; query yang sama dengan parameter sama dalam session yang sama bisa memakai cache lokal, dan cache dibersihkan pada `update`, `commit`, `rollback`, atau `close`. Default local cache berlaku sepanjang session, meski bisa dikonfigurasi menjadi `STATEMENT`. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/java-api.html))

Implikasi praktis:

| Kondisi | Risiko | Mitigasi |
|---|---|---|
| Session terlalu panjang | Data terlihat stale | Session pendek per use case |
| Object hasil query dimutasi | Cache lokal ikut terpengaruh | Jangan mutate object read model |
| Query read setelah update | Bisa misleading jika flow tidak jelas | Pisahkan command/read atau clear cache bila perlu |
| Batch process panjang | Memory/cache membesar | Chunking + session pendek |

---

## 17. Pattern untuk DTO vs Domain Object

| Query Type | Return Type Disarankan | Alasan |
|---|---|---|
| Detail command object | Domain object | Butuh invariant dan state transition |
| List/search page | DTO projection | Lebih ringan |
| Dropdown/reference data | Simple DTO/record | Tidak perlu full aggregate |
| Count/existence | Scalar | Efisien |
| Audit log | DTO/read model | Biasanya append-only |
| Report query | Dedicated report DTO | Hindari domain object terlalu berat |

### DTO projection example

```java id="3o90c1"
public record CaseQueueItemDto(
    long id,
    String caseNumber,
    String status,
    String priority,
    Instant dueAt
) {}
```

```xml id="7vo8x0"
<select id="findQueueItems" resultType="com.company.app.dto.CaseQueueItemDto">
  SELECT
    id,
    case_number AS caseNumber,
    status,
    priority,
    due_at AS dueAt
  FROM cases
  WHERE assigned_team = #{team}
  ORDER BY due_at ASC, id ASC
  LIMIT #{limit}
</select>
```

Jika constructor/record mapping tidak bekerja sesuai konfigurasi project, gunakan explicit `resultMap` dengan `<constructor>`.

---

## 18. Mapper XML Style Guide

| Rule | Contoh |
|---|---|
| Satu mapper per aggregate/module | `OrderMapper`, `CaseMapper`, `CustomerMapper` |
| Namespace harus FQCN interface | `com.company.app.mapper.OrderMapper` |
| Statement id sama dengan method name | `findById`, `search`, `transitionStatus` |
| Kolom selalu eksplisit | Hindari `SELECT *` |
| Alias kolom untuk join | `o.id AS order_id` |
| Gunakan `resultMap` untuk query penting | Jangan bergantung penuh pada auto mapping |
| Dynamic filter pakai `<where>` | Hindari `WHERE 1=1` jika bisa |
| Dynamic update pakai `<set>` | Hindari trailing comma |
| `IN` list pakai `<foreach>` | Validasi list kosong di service |
| Sorting pakai whitelist enum | Jangan raw `${sort}` |
| Pagination selalu punya deterministic `ORDER BY` | Tambahkan tie-breaker `id` |
| Update state pakai optimistic locking | `WHERE id = ? AND version = ?` |
| Return count update harus dicek | Jangan abaikan `int updated` |

---

## 19. Anti-Pattern

| Anti-Pattern | Contoh | Dampak |
|---|---|---|
| `SELECT *` | Ambil semua kolom | Fragile, boros, mapping tidak eksplisit |
| Raw dynamic SQL | `${userInput}` | SQL injection |
| Mapper terlalu generic | `executeQuery(String sql)` | Hilang type safety dan auditability |
| Tidak cek update count | `mapper.update(...);` lalu lanjut | Concurrency bug tersembunyi |
| Offset besar tanpa batas | `OFFSET 1000000` | Query lambat |
| Pagination tanpa order unik | `LIMIT 50` saja | Hasil tidak stabil |
| Domain object untuk semua query | List page mengambil aggregate penuh | Over-fetching |
| Join result tanpa alias | `id`, `id`, `status`, `status` | Mapping ambiguous |
| Dynamic filter tanpa guard | Search kosong return seluruh tabel | Incident production |
| Batch insert tanpa chunk | 100k rows sekali query | Memory/lock/timeout issue |
| Logic state hanya di Java | `if valid then update id` | Race condition |
| XML fragment terlalu abstrak | SQL tersembunyi di banyak include | Sulit direview |

---

## 20. Practical Template - Search Mapper

### Java Criteria

```java id="34zjdy"
public record SearchOrdersQuery(
    String status,
    Long customerId,
    Instant createdFrom,
    Instant createdTo,
    OrderSort sort,
    SortDirection direction,
    int limit,
    int offset
) {
    public SearchOrdersQuery {
        if (limit <= 0 || limit > 200) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("limit must be between 1 and 200");
        }
        if (offset < 0) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("offset must be >= 0");
        }
        if (sort == null) {
            sort = OrderSort.CREATED_AT;
        }
        if (direction == null) {
            direction = SortDirection.DESC;
        }
    }
}
```

### Mapper

```java id="8937i0"
List<OrderSummaryDto> search(@Param("query") SearchOrdersQuery query);
```

### XML

```xml id="dpkghf"
<select id="search" resultType="com.company.app.dto.OrderSummaryDto">
  SELECT
    o.id AS id,
    o.customer_id AS customerId,
    c.name AS customerName,
    o.status AS status,
    o.created_at AS createdAt
  FROM orders o
  JOIN customers c ON c.id = o.customer_id

  <where>
    <if test="query.status != null">
      o.status = #{query.status}
    </if>

    <if test="query.customerId != null">
      AND o.customer_id = #{query.customerId}
    </if>

    <if test="query.createdFrom != null">
      AND o.created_at &gt;= #{query.createdFrom}
    </if>

    <if test="query.createdTo != null">
      AND o.created_at &lt; #{query.createdTo}
    </if>
  </where>

  ORDER BY ${query.sort.column} ${query.direction}, o.id DESC
  LIMIT #{query.limit}
  OFFSET #{query.offset}
</select>
```

---

## 21. Practical Template - State Transition Mapper

### Mapper

```java id="yrong6"
int transitionStatus(@Param("id") long id,
                     @Param("fromStatus") String fromStatus,
                     @Param("toStatus") String toStatus,
                     @Param("version") long version);
```

### XML

```xml id="p7zdzz"
<update id="transitionStatus">
  UPDATE orders
  SET
    status = #{toStatus},
    version = version + 1,
    updated_at = now()
  WHERE id = #{id}
    AND status = #{fromStatus}
    AND version = #{version}
</update>
```

### Service

```java id="cxsuxn"
int updated = orderMapper.transitionStatus(
    command.orderId(),
    "SUBMITTED",
    "APPROVED",
    command.version()
);

if (updated != 1) {
    throw new InvalidOrderTransitionException(command.orderId());
}
```

---

## 22. Practical Template - Outbox Insert

```xml id="9vdyl3"
<insert id="insertOutboxEvent">
  INSERT INTO outbox_events (
    aggregate_type,
    aggregate_id,
    event_type,
    payload,
    status,
    created_at
  )
  VALUES (
    #{aggregateType},
    #{aggregateId},
    #{eventType},
    CAST(#{payloadJson} AS jsonb),
    'PENDING',
    now()
  )
</insert>
```

**Pattern:** insert business row + insert outbox event dalam transaksi yang sama.

```text
create order
  -> insert orders
  -> insert order_items
  -> insert outbox_events
  -> commit
```

---

## 23. Decomposition Table

| Skill | Yang Dikuasai | Output Praktis |
|---|---|---|
| Mapper namespace | FQCN interface = XML namespace | Mapper auto-resolve benar |
| Statement id | Method name = XML id | Contract jelas |
| Parameter binding | `#{}`, `@Param`, parameter object | Query aman dan readable |
| Safe substitution | `${}` hanya untuk whitelist identifier | Dynamic sorting aman |
| ResultMap | `id`, `result`, `association`, `constructor` | Mapping eksplisit |
| SQL fragment | `sql` + `include` | Reuse kolom standar |
| Dynamic WHERE | `<where>` + `<if>` | Search filter maintainable |
| Dynamic UPDATE | `<set>` | Patch update aman |
| Collection binding | `<foreach>` | `IN` query dan batch insert |
| Pagination | `LIMIT/OFFSET`, keyset | List endpoint stabil |
| PostgreSQL ordering | Deterministic `ORDER BY` | Pagination tidak random |
| Optimistic locking | `version` di `WHERE` | Race condition terdeteksi |
| Update count handling | Cek affected rows | State/concurrency error terlihat |
| DTO projection | Query read model ringan | API list efisien |
| Query invariant | Business condition di SQL | Defensif terhadap race |
| Batch chunking | Pecah operasi besar | Mengurangi timeout/lock |

---

## 24. Roadmap Belajar Singkat

| Urutan | Materi | Target |
|---:|---|---|
| 1 | Mapper interface + XML namespace | Bisa menghubungkan Java method ke SQL |
| 2 | `#{}` parameter binding + `@Param` | Query aman dan eksplisit |
| 3 | `resultType` vs `resultMap` | Bisa memilih mapping yang tepat |
| 4 | Dynamic SQL: `if`, `where`, `set`, `foreach` | Bisa membuat search/update fleksibel |
| 5 | Pagination dan sorting aman | Bisa membuat list endpoint production-ready |
| 6 | Optimistic locking dan affected row check | Bisa mencegah lost update |
| 7 | PostgreSQL-specific patterns | Bisa memakai `RETURNING`, `jsonb`, keyset pagination |
| 8 | Mapper integration testing | Bisa membuktikan SQL benar terhadap PostgreSQL asli |

---

## Rekomendasi Materi Selanjutnya

Lanjutkan ke **“Java MyBatis TypeHandler, PostgreSQL-specific Mapping, JSONB, UUID, Enum, Array, Timestamp/Timezone, dan Custom Value Object Mapping”** karena setelah mapper XML dan dynamic SQL kuat, tantangan berikutnya biasanya muncul di mapping tipe data PostgreSQL ke model Java.

**Sources:**
