## Response:
7/13/2026, 2:19:20 AM

Lanjut ke mapping tipe data PostgreSQL ke Java: ini area yang sering terlihat kecil, tapi efeknya besar ke correctness, timezone, enum evolution, JSONB, dan auditability. Saya cek dokumentasi resmi terkait MyBatis TypeHandler dan tipe PostgreSQL agar istilahnya akurat.

# Cheatsheet - Java MyBatis TypeHandler, PostgreSQL-specific Mapping, JSONB, UUID, Enum, Array, Timestamp/Timezone, dan Custom Value Object

## 1. Introduction

Di MyBatis, **TypeHandler** adalah komponen yang menjembatani value Java dengan value JDBC/PostgreSQL. Setiap kali MyBatis mengisi parameter ke `PreparedStatement` atau membaca value dari `ResultSet`, TypeHandler dipakai untuk memilih cara konversi yang sesuai dengan tipe Java dan JDBC. MyBatis juga sudah mendukung Java Time API / JSR-310 secara default sejak 3.4.5. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/configuration.html))

Untuk PostgreSQL-heavy application, TypeHandler menjadi penting karena PostgreSQL punya tipe yang lebih kaya daripada SQL/JDBC generik, misalnya:

```text id="624snh"
uuid
jsonb
enum
text[]
integer[]
timestamp with time zone
inet / cidr
numeric
domain type
range type
```

Tujuan praktisnya: **jangan biarkan mapping tipe data menjadi implicit accident**. Untuk tipe penting, buat mapping eksplisit agar behavior konsisten di local, test, staging, dan production.

---

## 2. Mental Model

```mermaid id="ux7rnq"
flowchart TD
    A[Java Service / Mapper Method] --> B[Mapper Parameter]
    B --> C[MyBatis TypeHandler]
    C --> D[JDBC PreparedStatement]
    D --> E[(PostgreSQL Column Type)]

    E --> F[JDBC ResultSet]
    F --> G[MyBatis TypeHandler]
    G --> H[Java Model / DTO / Value Object]

    C --> I[setNonNullParameter]
    G --> J[getNullableResult]

    K[mybatis-config.xml] --> C
    L[ResultMap] --> G
    M[Parameter Mapping] --> C
```

**Core invariant:**

```text id="64u9kd"
PostgreSQL type != JDBC type != Java type.

Mapping harus jelas:
Java value object
  <-> MyBatis TypeHandler
    <-> JDBC parameter/result
      <-> PostgreSQL column type
```

---

## 3. Decomposition Map

| Area | Fokus Praktis | Contoh | Risiko Jika Salah |
|---|---|---|---|
| **Default TypeHandler** | Memakai handler bawaan MyBatis | `String`, `Integer`, `Long`, `BigDecimal`, `Instant`, `LocalDate` | Overengineering kalau semua dibuat custom |
| **Custom TypeHandler** | Mapping tipe non-standar | `jsonb`, native enum, value object | Mapping implicit, error runtime |
| **JSONB Mapping** | Simpan struktur fleksibel | audit payload, outbox payload, metadata | JSON string invalid, query lambat, schema tidak jelas |
| **UUID Mapping** | ID distributed-safe | `UUID id` ↔ `uuid` | String UUID manual, format error |
| **Enum Mapping** | State/status domain | `OrderStatus.APPROVED` ↔ `order_status` | Ordinal berubah, enum migration sulit |
| **Array Mapping** | Multi-value column terbatas | `text[]`, `uuid[]` | Sulit query/index; kadang harus normalisasi |
| **Timestamp/Timezone** | Correctness waktu | `Instant`, `OffsetDateTime`, `LocalDateTime` | Timezone drift, audit timestamp salah |
| **Numeric Mapping** | Uang/quantity/precision | `BigDecimal` ↔ `numeric` | Floating point error |
| **Custom Value Object** | Domain defensibility | `CaseNumber`, `Money`, `EmailAddress` | Primitive obsession, validasi tersebar |
| **ResultMap Binding** | Explicit mapping | `typeHandler="..."` | Handler tidak terpanggil |
| **Parameter Binding** | Explicit parameter behavior | `jdbcType=OTHER` | PostgreSQL tidak bisa infer tipe |
| **Testing** | Validasi mapping aktual | Testcontainers PostgreSQL | Bug baru terlihat di production |

---

## 4. TypeHandler Contract

MyBatis menyediakan `BaseTypeHandler<T>` sebagai base class. Sejak MyBatis 3.5.0, `BaseTypeHandler` tidak lagi memanggil `ResultSet.wasNull()` atau `CallableStatement.wasNull()` untuk subclass, sehingga null handling harus dilakukan sendiri di subclass handler. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/apidocs/org/apache/ibatis/type/BaseTypeHandler.html))

Skeleton:

```java id="10lh03"
package com.company.app.persistence.typehandler;

import java.sql.CallableStatement;
import java.sql.PreparedStatement;
import java.sql.ResultSet;
import java.sql.SQLException;

import org.apache.ibatis.type.BaseTypeHandler;
import org.apache.ibatis.type.JdbcType;

public class ExampleTypeHandler extends BaseTypeHandler<ExampleValue> {

    @Override
    public void setNonNullParameter(
        PreparedStatement ps,
        int i,
        ExampleValue parameter,
        JdbcType jdbcType
    ) throws SQLException {
        ps.setString(i, parameter.value());
    }

    @Override
    public ExampleValue getNullableResult(ResultSet rs, String columnName)
        throws SQLException {
        String value = rs.getString(columnName);
        return value == null ? null : new ExampleValue(value);
    }

    @Override
    public ExampleValue getNullableResult(ResultSet rs, int columnIndex)
        throws SQLException {
        String value = rs.getString(columnIndex);
        return value == null ? null : new ExampleValue(value);
    }

    @Override
    public ExampleValue getNullableResult(CallableStatement cs, int columnIndex)
        throws SQLException {
        String value = cs.getString(columnIndex);
        return value == null ? null : new ExampleValue(value);
    }
}
```

---

## 5. Registrasi TypeHandler

### 5.1 Register satu per satu

```xml id="0ilxsa"
<typeHandlers>
  <typeHandler handler="com.company.app.persistence.typehandler.JsonNodeJsonbTypeHandler"/>
  <typeHandler handler="com.company.app.persistence.typehandler.OrderStatusTypeHandler"/>
  <typeHandler handler="com.company.app.persistence.typehandler.CaseNumberTypeHandler"/>
</typeHandlers>
```

### 5.2 Package scanning

```xml id="7g5ju2"
<typeHandlers>
  <package name="com.company.app.persistence.typehandler"/>
</typeHandlers>
```

MyBatis bisa mendaftarkan custom TypeHandler via `<typeHandler handler="..."/>` atau package scanning via `<package name="..."/>`. Namun, saat autodiscovery, JDBC type hanya bisa ditentukan melalui annotation. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/configuration.html))

---

## 6. Mapping Matrix - PostgreSQL ke Java

| PostgreSQL Type | Java Type Umum | TypeHandler | Rekomendasi |
|---|---|---|---|
| `text`, `varchar` | `String` | Default | Oke untuk plain text |
| `integer`, `bigint` | `Integer`, `Long` | Default | Oke |
| `numeric` | `BigDecimal` | Default | Gunakan untuk uang/amount |
| `uuid` | `UUID` | Driver/Object mapping | Prefer `java.util.UUID`, bukan `String` |
| `jsonb` | `JsonNode`, DTO, `String` | Custom | Prefer custom handler |
| `json` | `JsonNode`, `String` | Custom | Gunakan jika perlu preserve whitespace/order |
| PostgreSQL enum | Java enum | Default/custom | Custom jika pakai native enum type |
| `text[]` | `List<String>` / `String[]` | Custom atau array handling | Pertimbangkan normalisasi |
| `uuid[]` | `List<UUID>` / `UUID[]` | Custom | Validasi kebutuhan query |
| `timestamp with time zone` | `Instant` / `OffsetDateTime` | Default + disiplin schema | Cocok untuk event/audit time |
| `timestamp without time zone` | `LocalDateTime` | Default | Cocok untuk local business time, bukan instant global |
| `date` | `LocalDate` | Default | Cocok untuk tanggal bisnis |
| `time` | `LocalTime` | Default | Cocok untuk jam tanpa tanggal |
| `inet`, `cidr` | `String` / custom value object | Custom | Buat VO jika domain penting |

PostgreSQL menyediakan `json` dan `jsonb`; `jsonb` disimpan dalam format decomposed/binary, lebih lambat saat input tetapi lebih cepat diproses karena tidak perlu reparsing, dan mendukung indexing. PostgreSQL sendiri menyarankan kebanyakan aplikasi memilih `jsonb` kecuali ada kebutuhan khusus seperti mempertahankan urutan key atau whitespace. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/datatype-json.html))

---

## 7. JSONB Mapping

### 7.1 Schema

```sql id="vcuwk5"
CREATE TABLE outbox_events (
    id uuid PRIMARY KEY,
    aggregate_type text NOT NULL,
    aggregate_id text NOT NULL,
    event_type text NOT NULL,
    payload jsonb NOT NULL,
    created_at timestamp with time zone NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);
```

### 7.2 Java Model

```java id="nk1sso"
package com.company.app.model;

import java.time.Instant;
import java.util.UUID;

import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.JsonNode;

public record OutboxEvent(
    UUID id,
    String aggregateType,
    String aggregateId,
    String eventType,
    JsonNode payload,
    Instant createdAt
) {}
```

### 7.3 JSONB TypeHandler

```java id="uie2y0"
package com.company.app.persistence.typehandler;

import java.sql.CallableStatement;
import java.sql.PreparedStatement;
import java.sql.ResultSet;
import java.sql.SQLException;

import com.fasterxml.jackson.core.JsonProcessingException;
import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.JsonNode;
import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.ObjectMapper;

import org.apache.ibatis.type.BaseTypeHandler;
import org.apache.ibatis.type.JdbcType;
import org.apache.ibatis.type.MappedJdbcTypes;
import org.apache.ibatis.type.MappedTypes;
import org.postgresql.util.PGobject;

@MappedTypes(JsonNode.class)
@MappedJdbcTypes(value = JdbcType.OTHER, includeNullJdbcType = true)
public class JsonNodeJsonbTypeHandler extends BaseTypeHandler<JsonNode> {

    private static final ObjectMapper OBJECT_MAPPER = new ObjectMapper();

    @Override
    public void setNonNullParameter(
        PreparedStatement ps,
        int i,
        JsonNode parameter,
        JdbcType jdbcType
    ) throws SQLException {
        PGobject jsonb = new PGobject();
        jsonb.setType("jsonb");

        try {
            jsonb.setValue(OBJECT_MAPPER.writeValueAsString(parameter));
        } catch (JsonProcessingException e) {
            throw new SQLException("Failed to serialize JsonNode to jsonb", e);
        }

        ps.setObject(i, jsonb);
    }

    @Override
    public JsonNode getNullableResult(ResultSet rs, String columnName)
        throws SQLException {
        return parse(rs.getString(columnName));
    }

    @Override
    public JsonNode getNullableResult(ResultSet rs, int columnIndex)
        throws SQLException {
        return parse(rs.getString(columnIndex));
    }

    @Override
    public JsonNode getNullableResult(CallableStatement cs, int columnIndex)
        throws SQLException {
        return parse(cs.getString(columnIndex));
    }

    private JsonNode parse(String value) throws SQLException {
        if (value == null) {
            return null;
        }

        try {
            return OBJECT_MAPPER.readTree(value);
        } catch (JsonProcessingException e) {
            throw new SQLException("Failed to parse jsonb value", e);
        }
    }
}
```

### 7.4 Mapper XML

```xml id="u7hr5v"
<resultMap id="OutboxEventResultMap"
           type="com.company.app.model.OutboxEvent">
  <constructor>
    <idArg column="id" javaType="java.util.UUID"/>
    <arg column="aggregate_type" javaType="string"/>
    <arg column="aggregate_id" javaType="string"/>
    <arg column="event_type" javaType="string"/>
    <arg column="payload"
         javaType="com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.JsonNode"
         typeHandler="com.company.app.persistence.typehandler.JsonNodeJsonbTypeHandler"/>
    <arg column="created_at" javaType="java.time.Instant"/>
  </constructor>
</resultMap>
```

```xml id="kvyrku"
<insert id="insertOutboxEvent">
  INSERT INTO outbox_events (
    id,
    aggregate_type,
    aggregate_id,
    event_type,
    payload,
    created_at
  )
  VALUES (
    #{id},
    #{aggregateType},
    #{aggregateId},
    #{eventType},
    #{payload, jdbcType=OTHER,
      typeHandler=com.company.app.persistence.typehandler.JsonNodeJsonbTypeHandler},
    #{createdAt}
  )
</insert>
```

**Practical warning:** jangan pakai JSONB untuk menyembunyikan model relational yang sebenarnya perlu constraint, foreign key, indexing, dan query lifecycle. JSONB bagus untuk payload fleksibel, event payload, metadata, audit snapshot, atau integration envelope.

---

## 8. JSONB Query Pattern

### 8.1 Query field JSONB

```xml id="v6ke0h"
<select id="findByPayloadCorrelationId" resultMap="OutboxEventResultMap">
  SELECT
    id,
    aggregate_type,
    aggregate_id,
    event_type,
    payload,
    created_at
  FROM outbox_events
  WHERE payload ->> 'correlationId' = #{correlationId}
  ORDER BY created_at DESC
  LIMIT #{limit}
</select>
```

### 8.2 Containment query

```xml id="m99agq"
<select id="findByPayloadFragment" resultMap="OutboxEventResultMap">
  SELECT
    id,
    aggregate_type,
    aggregate_id,
    event_type,
    payload,
    created_at
  FROM outbox_events
  WHERE payload @> #{payloadFragment, jdbcType=OTHER,
    typeHandler=com.company.app.persistence.typehandler.JsonNodeJsonbTypeHandler}
  ORDER BY created_at DESC
  LIMIT #{limit}
</select>
```

PostgreSQL menyediakan operator dan function khusus untuk JSON/JSONB; operator JSON field/path yang tidak cocok dengan struktur JSON mengembalikan `NULL`, bukan langsung gagal, sehingga query harus didesain dengan asumsi field bisa hilang. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/functions-json.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 9. UUID Mapping

PostgreSQL `uuid` menyimpan UUID 128-bit dan cocok untuk distributed systems karena tidak bergantung pada sequence di satu database. PostgreSQL menerima beberapa bentuk input UUID, tetapi output selalu dalam standard form. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/datatype-uuid.html))

### 9.1 Schema

```sql id="oqnrex"
CREATE TABLE cases (
    id uuid PRIMARY KEY,
    case_number text NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    status text NOT NULL,
    created_at timestamp with time zone NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);
```

### 9.2 Java

```java id="ykufhn"
public record CaseRecord(
    UUID id,
    String caseNumber,
    String status,
    Instant createdAt
) {}
```

### 9.3 Mapper XML

```xml id="h8pcxc"
<insert id="insertCase">
  INSERT INTO cases (
    id,
    case_number,
    status,
    created_at
  )
  VALUES (
    #{id},
    #{caseNumber},
    #{status},
    #{createdAt}
  )
</insert>
```

### 9.4 Decision

| ID Strategy | Cocok Untuk | Trade-off |
|---|---|---|
| DB-generated UUID | DB punya kontrol ID | App perlu baca `RETURNING id` |
| App-generated UUID | Event/outbox/distributed creation | App bisa tahu ID sebelum insert |
| UUIDv4 | Random, umum | Index locality lebih buruk |
| UUIDv7 | Time-sortable | Perlu pastikan versi PostgreSQL/library mendukung |
| Sequence bigint | Simple dan compact | Tidak portable untuk multi-writer distributed |

---

## 10. Enum Mapping

PostgreSQL enum adalah tipe yang berisi set value statis dan dibuat dengan `CREATE TYPE ... AS ENUM`. PostgreSQL enum punya type safety, tetapi perubahan value harus direncanakan sebagai migration schema. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/datatype-enum.html))

### 10.1 Pilihan mapping enum

| DB Representation | Java Representation | Kelebihan | Risiko |
|---|---|---|---|
| `text` + CHECK constraint | Java enum | Migration mudah | Type safety DB lebih lemah |
| Native PostgreSQL enum | Java enum + custom handler | Type safety kuat | Migration enum lebih sensitif |
| `smallint` code | Java enum + code | Compact | Kurang readable |
| ordinal enum | Java enum ordinal | Tidak direkomendasikan | Rusak jika urutan enum berubah |

MyBatis secara default menggunakan `EnumTypeHandler` untuk mengubah enum ke nama enum; `EnumOrdinalTypeHandler` juga tersedia, tetapi ordinal menyimpan posisi, bukan code, sehingga lebih berisiko untuk domain state yang berevolusi. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/configuration.html))

---

### 10.2 Native PostgreSQL enum

```sql id="fjohah"
CREATE TYPE order_status AS ENUM (
    'DRAFT',
    'SUBMITTED',
    'APPROVED',
    'REJECTED',
    'CANCELLED'
);

CREATE TABLE orders (
    id uuid PRIMARY KEY,
    status order_status NOT NULL,
    created_at timestamp with time zone NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);
```

```java id="nscv8a"
public enum OrderStatus {
    DRAFT,
    SUBMITTED,
    APPROVED,
    REJECTED,
    CANCELLED
}
```

### 10.3 Custom TypeHandler untuk native enum

```java id="wrw8nl"
package com.company.app.persistence.typehandler;

import java.sql.CallableStatement;
import java.sql.PreparedStatement;
import java.sql.ResultSet;
import java.sql.SQLException;

import com.company.app.model.OrderStatus;

import org.apache.ibatis.type.BaseTypeHandler;
import org.apache.ibatis.type.JdbcType;
import org.apache.ibatis.type.MappedJdbcTypes;
import org.apache.ibatis.type.MappedTypes;
import org.postgresql.util.PGobject;

@MappedTypes(OrderStatus.class)
@MappedJdbcTypes(value = JdbcType.OTHER, includeNullJdbcType = true)
public class OrderStatusTypeHandler extends BaseTypeHandler<OrderStatus> {

    @Override
    public void setNonNullParameter(
        PreparedStatement ps,
        int i,
        OrderStatus parameter,
        JdbcType jdbcType
    ) throws SQLException {
        PGobject enumValue = new PGobject();
        enumValue.setType("order_status");
        enumValue.setValue(parameter.name());
        ps.setObject(i, enumValue);
    }

    @Override
    public OrderStatus getNullableResult(ResultSet rs, String columnName)
        throws SQLException {
        return parse(rs.getString(columnName));
    }

    @Override
    public OrderStatus getNullableResult(ResultSet rs, int columnIndex)
        throws SQLException {
        return parse(rs.getString(columnIndex));
    }

    @Override
    public OrderStatus getNullableResult(CallableStatement cs, int columnIndex)
        throws SQLException {
        return parse(cs.getString(columnIndex));
    }

    private OrderStatus parse(String value) {
        return value == null ? null : OrderStatus.valueOf(value);
    }
}
```

### 10.4 Mapper XML

```xml id="zh2tfz"
<result column="status"
        property="status"
        javaType="com.company.app.model.OrderStatus"
        jdbcType="OTHER"
        typeHandler="com.company.app.persistence.typehandler.OrderStatusTypeHandler"/>
```

```xml id="nk9k3o"
UPDATE orders
SET status = #{status, jdbcType=OTHER,
  typeHandler=com.company.app.persistence.typehandler.OrderStatusTypeHandler}
WHERE id = #{id}
```

**Practical rule:** untuk state machine yang sering berubah, `text + CHECK constraint` kadang lebih mudah dimigrasikan daripada native enum. Untuk state yang stabil dan defensibility tinggi, native enum bisa memberi type safety yang lebih kuat.

---

## 11. Array Mapping

PostgreSQL mendukung array variable-length dan multidimensional; array bisa dibuat dari built-in type, enum type, composite type, range type, atau domain type. Namun, PostgreSQL saat ini tidak menegakkan ukuran/dimensi array yang dideklarasikan di `CREATE TABLE`; ukuran/dimensi tersebut lebih berfungsi sebagai dokumentasi. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/arrays.html))

### 11.1 Kapan array layak?

| Use Case | Array Layak? | Catatan |
|---|---:|---|
| Tags sederhana | Bisa | `text[]`, kalau query ringan |
| Role/permission kompleks | Tidak ideal | Lebih baik table relasi |
| Case participants | Tidak | Butuh FK, audit, role, lifecycle |
| Search keywords snapshot | Bisa | Kalau hanya metadata |
| UUID references yang perlu integrity | Tidak ideal | FK tidak bisa langsung per elemen array |
| Small immutable list | Bisa | Misalnya supported channels |

---

### 11.2 Schema

```sql id="b64uq3"
CREATE TABLE notification_rules (
    id uuid PRIMARY KEY,
    name text NOT NULL,
    channels text[] NOT NULL,
    created_at timestamp with time zone NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);
```

### 11.3 TypeHandler `List<String>` ↔ `text[]`

```java id="e47q36"
package com.company.app.persistence.typehandler;

import java.sql.Array;
import java.sql.CallableStatement;
import java.sql.Connection;
import java.sql.PreparedStatement;
import java.sql.ResultSet;
import java.sql.SQLException;
import java.util.Arrays;
import java.util.List;

import org.apache.ibatis.type.BaseTypeHandler;
import org.apache.ibatis.type.JdbcType;
import org.apache.ibatis.type.MappedJdbcTypes;
import org.apache.ibatis.type.MappedTypes;

@MappedTypes(List.class)
@MappedJdbcTypes(value = JdbcType.ARRAY, includeNullJdbcType = true)
public class StringListArrayTypeHandler extends BaseTypeHandler<List<String>> {

    @Override
    public void setNonNullParameter(
        PreparedStatement ps,
        int i,
        List<String> parameter,
        JdbcType jdbcType
    ) throws SQLException {
        Connection connection = ps.getConnection();

        Array array = connection.createArrayOf(
            "text",
            parameter.toArray(new String[0])
        );

        ps.setArray(i, array);
    }

    @Override
    public List<String> getNullableResult(ResultSet rs, String columnName)
        throws SQLException {
        return toList(rs.getArray(columnName));
    }

    @Override
    public List<String> getNullableResult(ResultSet rs, int columnIndex)
        throws SQLException {
        return toList(rs.getArray(columnIndex));
    }

    @Override
    public List<String> getNullableResult(CallableStatement cs, int columnIndex)
        throws SQLException {
        return toList(cs.getArray(columnIndex));
    }

    private List<String> toList(Array array) throws SQLException {
        if (array == null) {
            return null;
        }

        Object value = array.getArray();
        if (value instanceof String[] strings) {
            return List.copyOf(Arrays.asList(strings));
        }

        Object[] objects = (Object[]) value;
        return Arrays.stream(objects)
            .map(String::valueOf)
            .toList();
    }
}
```

### 11.4 Mapper XML

```xml id="ky9874"
<result column="channels"
        property="channels"
        jdbcType="ARRAY"
        typeHandler="com.company.app.persistence.typehandler.StringListArrayTypeHandler"/>
```

```xml id="abp8yr"
INSERT INTO notification_rules (
  id,
  name,
  channels,
  created_at
)
VALUES (
  #{id},
  #{name},
  #{channels, jdbcType=ARRAY,
    typeHandler=com.company.app.persistence.typehandler.StringListArrayTypeHandler},
  #{createdAt}
)
```

## 12. Timestamp / Timezone Mapping

PostgreSQL mendukung `timestamp with time zone`, `timestamp without time zone`, `date`, `time`, dan `interval`. `timestamp with time zone` disimpan/ditampilkan dengan behavior timezone-aware: saat output, nilainya dikonversi dari UTC ke timezone session saat ini. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/datatype-datetime.html))

### 12.1 Decision table

| PostgreSQL Type | Java Type | Cocok Untuk | Hindari Untuk |
|---|---|---|---|
| `timestamp with time zone` | `Instant` | Audit/event time global | Local business schedule |
| `timestamp with time zone` | `OffsetDateTime` | Perlu offset di API | Domain yang hanya butuh instant |
| `timestamp without time zone` | `LocalDateTime` | Jadwal lokal tanpa timezone | Event global/audit |
| `date` | `LocalDate` | Tanggal bisnis, due date | Timestamp event |
| `time` | `LocalTime` | Jam operasional | Absolute instant |
| `interval` | `Duration` / custom | Durasi proses/SLA | Tanggal absolut |

### 12.2 Recommended convention

```text id="bpbca9"
Audit timestamp:
  PostgreSQL: timestamp with time zone
  Java: Instant

Business date:
  PostgreSQL: date
  Java: LocalDate

Local schedule:
  PostgreSQL: timestamp without time zone + explicit timezone column
  Java: LocalDateTime + ZoneId
```

### 12.3 Mapper example

```java id="lqjxin"
public record AuditEntry(
    UUID id,
    String action,
    Instant occurredAt
) {}
```

```xml id="cg7yjr"
<insert id="insertAuditEntry">
  INSERT INTO audit_entries (
    id,
    action,
    occurred_at
  )
  VALUES (
    #{id},
    #{action},
    #{occurredAt}
  )
</insert>
```

MyBatis menyediakan TypeHandler default untuk beberapa tipe Java Time seperti `Instant`, `LocalDateTime`, `LocalDate`, `OffsetDateTime`, `ZonedDateTime`, dan lainnya. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/configuration.html))

---

## 13. Custom Value Object Mapping

Custom value object berguna untuk domain yang tidak boleh diperlakukan sebagai `String` biasa.

### 13.1 Value object

```java id="4ntzpq"
package com.company.app.model;

public record CaseNumber(String value) {

    public CaseNumber {
        if (value == null || value.isBlank()) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("case number must not be blank");
        }

        if (!value.matches("CASE-[0-9]{8}")) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("invalid case number format");
        }
    }
}
```

### 13.2 TypeHandler

```java id="os9vcl"
package com.company.app.persistence.typehandler;

import java.sql.CallableStatement;
import java.sql.PreparedStatement;
import java.sql.ResultSet;
import java.sql.SQLException;

import com.company.app.model.CaseNumber;

import org.apache.ibatis.type.BaseTypeHandler;
import org.apache.ibatis.type.JdbcType;
import org.apache.ibatis.type.MappedJdbcTypes;
import org.apache.ibatis.type.MappedTypes;

@MappedTypes(CaseNumber.class)
@MappedJdbcTypes(value = JdbcType.VARCHAR, includeNullJdbcType = true)
public class CaseNumberTypeHandler extends BaseTypeHandler<CaseNumber> {

    @Override
    public void setNonNullParameter(
        PreparedStatement ps,
        int i,
        CaseNumber parameter,
        JdbcType jdbcType
    ) throws SQLException {
        ps.setString(i, parameter.value());
    }

    @Override
    public CaseNumber getNullableResult(ResultSet rs, String columnName)
        throws SQLException {
        return parse(rs.getString(columnName));
    }

    @Override
    public CaseNumber getNullableResult(ResultSet rs, int columnIndex)
        throws SQLException {
        return parse(rs.getString(columnIndex));
    }

    @Override
    public CaseNumber getNullableResult(CallableStatement cs, int columnIndex)
        throws SQLException {
        return parse(cs.getString(columnIndex));
    }

    private CaseNumber parse(String value) {
        return value == null ? null : new CaseNumber(value);
    }
}
```

### 13.3 Mapper XML

```xml id="4qdmu4"
<result column="case_number"
        property="caseNumber"
        javaType="com.company.app.model.CaseNumber"
        jdbcType="VARCHAR"
        typeHandler="com.company.app.persistence.typehandler.CaseNumberTypeHandler"/>
```

**Practical point:** value object TypeHandler bagus untuk invariant yang sering muncul di domain: `CaseNumber`, `EmailAddress`, `Money`, `UserId`, `TenantId`, `ExternalReference`.

---

## 14. Numeric / Money Mapping

### Recommended

```sql id="bo3z86"
amount numeric(19, 4) NOT NULL
currency char(3) NOT NULL
```

```java id="1q3w9a"
public record Money(
    BigDecimal amount,
    String currency
) {
    public Money {
        if (amount == null) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("amount is required");
        }
        if (currency == null || !currency.matches("[A-Z]{3}")) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("currency must be ISO-like 3-letter code");
        }
    }
}
```

**Rule:** jangan gunakan `double`/`float` untuk uang, fee, tax, balance, atau nilai yang butuh precision. Gunakan `BigDecimal` dan constraint database.

---

## 15. Domain Type di PostgreSQL

PostgreSQL domain adalah user-defined type berbasis underlying type dengan optional constraint; domain bisa dibangun di atas built-in type, enum, array, composite, range, atau domain lain. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/domains.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Contoh:

```sql id="4x5nno"
CREATE DOMAIN case_number AS text
CHECK (VALUE ~ '^CASE-[0-9]{8}$');

CREATE TABLE cases (
    id uuid PRIMARY KEY,
    case_number case_number NOT NULL UNIQUE
);
```

Di Java, domain type seperti ini biasanya tetap dimapping sebagai `String` atau value object dengan TypeHandler. Keuntungannya: invariant dijaga dua lapis, di Java dan di database.

---

## 16. ResultMap Pattern untuk Mixed Types

```xml id="nszqu9"
<resultMap id="CaseResultMap" type="com.company.app.model.CaseRecord">
  <id column="id"
      property="id"
      javaType="java.util.UUID"/>

  <result column="case_number"
          property="caseNumber"
          javaType="com.company.app.model.CaseNumber"
          jdbcType="VARCHAR"
          typeHandler="com.company.app.persistence.typehandler.CaseNumberTypeHandler"/>

  <result column="status"
          property="status"
          javaType="com.company.app.model.CaseStatus"
          jdbcType="OTHER"
          typeHandler="com.company.app.persistence.typehandler.CaseStatusTypeHandler"/>

  <result column="metadata"
          property="metadata"
          javaType="com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.JsonNode"
          jdbcType="OTHER"
          typeHandler="com.company.app.persistence.typehandler.JsonNodeJsonbTypeHandler"/>

  <result column="created_at"
          property="createdAt"
          javaType="java.time.Instant"/>
</resultMap>
```

**Rule:** untuk tipe yang rawan ambiguity, tulis `javaType`, `jdbcType`, dan `typeHandler` eksplisit. Jangan berharap MyBatis selalu bisa infer mapping PostgreSQL-specific type.

---

## 17. Parameter Binding Pattern

```xml id="n7o21o"
<insert id="insertCase">
  INSERT INTO cases (
    id,
    case_number,
    status,
    metadata,
    tags,
    created_at
  )
  VALUES (
    #{id},
    #{caseNumber, jdbcType=VARCHAR,
      typeHandler=com.company.app.persistence.typehandler.CaseNumberTypeHandler},
    #{status, jdbcType=OTHER,
      typeHandler=com.company.app.persistence.typehandler.CaseStatusTypeHandler},
    #{metadata, jdbcType=OTHER,
      typeHandler=com.company.app.persistence.typehandler.JsonNodeJsonbTypeHandler},
    #{tags, jdbcType=ARRAY,
      typeHandler=com.company.app.persistence.typehandler.StringListArrayTypeHandler},
    #{createdAt}
  )
</insert>
```

MyBatis tidak melakukan introspeksi metadata database untuk menentukan tipe kolom sebelum statement dijalankan, sehingga untuk handler tertentu kita perlu memberi `jdbcType`, `javaType`, annotation, atau mapping eksplisit agar handler yang benar dipilih. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/configuration.html))

---

## 18. PostgreSQL JDBC `stringtype` Awareness

pgJDBC memiliki parameter `stringtype` untuk menentukan bagaimana parameter dari `PreparedStatement#setString()` dikirim ke server. Jika default `VARCHAR`, parameter string dikirim sebagai varchar; jika `unspecified`, server mencoba infer tipe. Ini relevan saat aplikasi lama mengirim value non-string sebagai string, tetapi untuk aplikasi baru lebih baik gunakan setter/type mapping yang tepat daripada bergantung pada inference. ([pgJDBC](https://jdbc.postgresql.org/documentation/use/))

**Practical recommendation:**

```text id="qf4nil"
Prefer:
  explicit TypeHandler
  jdbcType=OTHER
  PGobject / setObject for PostgreSQL-specific type

Avoid:
  relying on stringtype=unspecified as generic fix
```

---

## 19. Testing Strategy

### 19.1 Test JSONB round-trip

```java id="6e53oo"
@Test
void shouldRoundTripJsonbPayload() {
    UUID id = UUID.randomUUID();

    JsonNode payload = objectMapper.createObjectNode()
        .put("correlationId", "corr-001")
        .put("action", "ORDER_CREATED");

    mapper.insertOutboxEvent(new OutboxEvent(
        id,
        "ORDER",
        "ORD-001",
        "ORDER_CREATED",
        payload,
        Instant.now()
    ));

    OutboxEvent found = mapper.findById(id);

    assertThat(found.payload().get("correlationId").asText())
        .isEqualTo("corr-001");
}
```

### 19.2 Test enum invalid evolution

```java id="nvsy5n"
@Test
void shouldFailFastForUnknownEnumValue() {
    jdbcTemplate.update("""
        INSERT INTO orders (id, status, created_at)
        VALUES (?, 'UNKNOWN_STATUS'::order_status, now())
        """, UUID.randomUUID());

    assertThatThrownBy(() -> mapper.findLatest())
        .isInstanceOf(PersistenceException.class);
}
```

Catatan: contoh kedua hanya valid kalau enum value tersebut ada di DB tetapi belum ada di Java enum, misalnya saat migration tidak sinkron. Untuk native PostgreSQL enum, value yang tidak ada di type DB akan ditolak oleh database.

### 19.3 Test timezone

```java id="pwvk8w"
@Test
void shouldPersistInstantWithoutLosingMomentInTime() {
    Instant occurredAt = Instant.parse("2026-07-13T01:00:00Z");

    mapper.insertAuditEntry(new AuditEntry(
        UUID.randomUUID(),
        "CASE_APPROVED",
        occurredAt
    ));

    AuditEntry found = mapper.findLatest();

    assertThat(found.occurredAt()).isEqualTo(occurredAt);
}
```

---

## 20. Anti-Pattern

| Anti-Pattern | Contoh | Dampak |
|---|---|---|
| JSONB sebagai dumping ground | Semua field masuk `metadata jsonb` | Constraint, index, dan query lifecycle kabur |
| Enum ordinal | `APPROVED.ordinal()` | Rusak jika urutan enum berubah |
| Timestamp tanpa keputusan | Campur `Instant`, `LocalDateTime`, DB timezone | Audit time salah |
| UUID sebagai string everywhere | `String id` | Validasi tersebar, format error |
| Array untuk relasi penting | `participant_ids uuid[]` | Tidak ada FK per participant |
| Value object tidak dipakai | Semua `String` | Invariant domain bocor |
| TypeHandler global terlalu agresif | Override semua `String` | Side effect tidak terduga |
| Tidak eksplisit `jdbcType` | PostgreSQL-specific type gagal binding | Runtime error |
| Tidak test round-trip | Hanya mock mapper | Mapping bug lolos |
| JSON parse exception ditelan | Return null diam-diam | Data corruption tersembunyi |
| Store money as double | `double amount` | Precision error |
| Timezone pakai server default | `LocalDateTime.now()` untuk audit | Hasil beda antar environment |

---

## 21. Production Checklist

| Checklist | Status Ideal |
|---|---|
| Semua `jsonb` punya alasan jelas | Ya |
| JSONB field yang sering dicari punya index strategy | Ya |
| Enum mapping tidak memakai ordinal | Ya |
| Native enum migration punya backward/forward compatibility plan | Ya |
| Audit timestamp memakai `Instant` + `timestamp with time zone` | Ya |
| Local business date memakai `LocalDate` + `date` | Ya |
| UUID memakai `java.util.UUID` | Ya |
| Money/amount memakai `BigDecimal` | Ya |
| Value object penting punya TypeHandler | Ya |
| TypeHandler null handling eksplisit | Ya |
| Mapper XML menulis `jdbcType` untuk PostgreSQL-specific type | Ya |
| Integration test melakukan round-trip insert/select | Ya |
| Test mencakup null, invalid value, dan edge case timezone | Ya |
| Logging tidak membocorkan payload sensitif dari JSONB | Ya |

---

## 22. Decision Table

| Problem | Pilihan Terbaik |
|---|---|
| Payload event/outbox fleksibel | `jsonb` + `JsonNode` TypeHandler |
| API metadata yang jarang difilter | `jsonb` |
| Field wajib untuk workflow/state | Kolom relational biasa |
| Status lifecycle stabil | Native PostgreSQL enum atau text + CHECK |
| Status lifecycle sering berubah | `text` + CHECK lebih fleksibel |
| ID aggregate distributed | `uuid` + `UUID` |
| Audit time | `timestamp with time zone` + `Instant` |
| Due date bisnis | `date` + `LocalDate` |
| Local schedule | `LocalDateTime` + explicit `ZoneId` field |
| Multi-value kecil non-relational | Array boleh |
| Multi-value dengan ownership/lifecycle | Table relasi |
| Domain primitive penting | Value object + TypeHandler |
| Query JSONB sering | JSONB index + query review |

---

## 23. Roadmap Belajar Singkat

| Urutan | Materi | Target |
|---:|---|---|
| 1 | Default TypeHandler MyBatis | Tahu kapan tidak perlu custom |
| 2 | Custom `BaseTypeHandler` | Bisa membuat mapping sendiri |
| 3 | JSONB TypeHandler | Bisa simpan/baca payload JSONB |
| 4 | UUID dan Java `UUID` | ID mapping bersih |
| 5 | Enum strategy | Bisa pilih native enum vs text/check |
| 6 | Timestamp/timezone | Tidak salah audit time |
| 7 | Array mapping | Tahu kapan array boleh dan kapan harus normalisasi |
| 8 | Value object mapping | Domain invariant lebih kuat |
| 9 | Integration testing | Mapping benar terhadap PostgreSQL asli |

## Response:
7/13/2026, 2:23:22 AM

Lanjut ke concurrency dan transaction correctness: bagian ini penting untuk mencegah lost update, duplicate command, partial write, deadlock, dan retry yang justru menggandakan efek bisnis. Saya akan susun sebagai pola praktis untuk MyBatis + PostgreSQL di service Jersey/JAX-RS.

# Cheatsheet - Java MyBatis Transaction, Concurrency, Locking, Isolation Level, Retry, dan Idempotency Pattern di PostgreSQL

## 1. Introduction

Dalam aplikasi **Jersey/JAX-RS + MyBatis + PostgreSQL**, masalah concurrency biasanya bukan muncul dari syntax mapper, tetapi dari **urutan operasi bisnis**:

```text id="o9db9j"
read current state
validate transition
write new state
write audit
write outbox event
commit
```

Jika beberapa request berjalan bersamaan, use case seperti approval, escalation, order submission, payment capture, queue processing, dan retry dari client bisa menghasilkan:

```text id="sy7j91"
lost update
double submit
duplicate command
partial write
deadlock
serialization failure
stale read
invalid state transition
duplicate outbox event
```

MyBatis memberi kontrol transaction lewat `SqlSession`: session bisa dibuka dengan transaction scope, isolation level, executor type, auto-commit, lalu dikontrol dengan `commit()` dan `rollback()`. Default `openSession()` membuat transaction scope non-auto-commit, memakai connection dari configured `DataSource`, memakai isolation default driver/data source, dan tidak memakai prepared-statement reuse ataupun batch executor. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/java-api.html))

---

## 2. Mental Model

```mermaid id="1ic9of"
flowchart TD
    A[JAX-RS Request] --> B[Service Use Case]
    B --> C[Open SqlSession]
    C --> D[Transaction Begins]
    D --> E[Read Current State]
    E --> F[Validate Business Invariant]
    F --> G[Write New State]
    G --> H[Write Audit / Outbox]
    H --> I{Success?}
    I -->|Yes| J[Commit]
    I -->|No| K[Rollback]
    J --> L[Close SqlSession]
    K --> L
    L --> M[HTTP Response]

    E --> N[Optimistic Lock]
    E --> O[Pessimistic Lock]
    F --> P[Idempotency Check]
    G --> Q[Unique Constraint / ON CONFLICT]
```

**Core invariant:**

```text id="aipd41"
Satu business command = satu transaction boundary.

Jangan:
  read di satu session
  validate di luar transaction
  write di session lain
  commit sebagian
```

---

## 3. Decomposition Map

| Area | Fokus Praktis | Pattern Utama | Risiko Jika Salah |
|---|---|---|---|
| **Transaction Boundary** | Satu use case atomic | Service-level Unit of Work | Partial write |
| **Auto-commit Control** | Hindari commit per statement | `openSession(false)` / default transaction scope | Multi-step use case tidak atomic |
| **Isolation Level** | Pilih konsistensi vs throughput | Read Committed, Repeatable Read, Serializable | Salah asumsi snapshot |
| **Optimistic Locking** | Cegah lost update tanpa lock panjang | `version` column di `WHERE` | Update overwrite diam-diam |
| **Pessimistic Locking** | Lock row sebelum update | `SELECT ... FOR UPDATE` | Blocking/deadlock |
| **Queue Locking** | Parallel worker aman | `FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED` | Worker memproses row sama |
| **Advisory Lock** | Lock berdasarkan business key | `pg_advisory_xact_lock` | Salah scope lock |
| **Deadlock Handling** | Retry transient failure | SQLSTATE `40P01` | User melihat error sporadis |
| **Serialization Retry** | Retry transaction aman | SQLSTATE `40001` | Serializable gagal tanpa retry |
| **Idempotency** | Retry client tidak double-effect | Idempotency key + unique constraint | Double submit/order/payment |
| **UPSERT** | Insert-or-update atomic | `INSERT ... ON CONFLICT` | Race check-then-insert |
| **Outbox Consistency** | Event keluar setelah commit | Insert outbox dalam transaksi sama | Event terkirim tanpa data / data tanpa event |
| **Affected Row Check** | Detect stale state/concurrency | `updated == 1` | Invalid transition lolos |
| **Timeout & Lock Wait** | Cegah request menggantung | statement timeout, lock timeout | Thread pool habis |
| **Test Concurrency** | Buktikan behavior paralel | integration test multi-thread | Bug hanya muncul production |

---

## 4. MyBatis Transaction Control

MyBatis `SqlSessionFactory` menyediakan beberapa overload `openSession`, termasuk auto-commit, custom `Connection`, `TransactionIsolationLevel`, dan `ExecutorType`. MyBatis juga menyediakan wrapper isolation level JDBC seperti `READ_COMMITTED`, `REPEATABLE_READ`, dan `SERIALIZABLE`. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/java-api.html))

### 4.1 Basic Unit of Work

```java id="z2tbmx"
public <T> T transactional(MyBatisWork<T> work) {
    try (SqlSession session = sqlSessionFactory.openSession(false)) {
        try {
            T result = work.execute(new MyBatisSessionContext(session));
            session.commit();
            return result;
        } catch (RuntimeException | Error e) {
            session.rollback();
            throw e;
        }
    }
}
```

MyBatis menyediakan `commit()`, `rollback()`, `commit(boolean force)`, dan `rollback(boolean force)` untuk session yang memakai JDBC transaction manager; session tetap harus ditutup, dan dokumentasi MyBatis merekomendasikan pola `try-with-resources`. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/java-api.html))

---

### 4.2 Unit of Work dengan Isolation Level

```java id="x6d67h"
import org.apache.ibatis.session.SqlSession;
import org.apache.ibatis.session.TransactionIsolationLevel;

public <T> T transactional(
    TransactionIsolationLevel isolationLevel,
    MyBatisWork<T> work
) {
    try (SqlSession session = sqlSessionFactory.openSession(isolationLevel)) {
        try {
            T result = work.execute(new MyBatisSessionContext(session));
            session.commit();
            return result;
        } catch (RuntimeException | Error e) {
            session.rollback();
            throw e;
        }
    }
}
```

### 4.3 Kapan pakai isolation eksplisit?

| Use Case | Isolation Praktis | Catatan |
|---|---|---|
| Simple CRUD | Default / Read Committed | Cukup untuk mayoritas endpoint |
| State transition dengan version check | Read Committed + optimistic locking | Umumnya efisien |
| Report konsisten dalam satu transaksi | Repeatable Read | Snapshot lebih stabil |
| Invariant lintas banyak row | Serializable atau explicit locking | Siap retry |
| Queue worker | Read Committed + `FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED` | Umum untuk job claiming |
| Payment/order idempotency | Read Committed + unique constraint + idempotency table | Jangan hanya lock in-memory |

PostgreSQL default isolation level adalah **Read Committed**; dalam level ini, satu `SELECT` melihat snapshot pada awal query, tetapi dua `SELECT` berturut-turut dalam transaksi yang sama bisa melihat data berbeda jika transaksi lain commit di antaranya. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/transaction-iso.html))

---

## 5. PostgreSQL Isolation Level

PostgreSQL menerima empat nama isolation level standar SQL, tetapi secara internal hanya mengimplementasikan tiga behavior berbeda: `READ UNCOMMITTED` berperilaku seperti `READ COMMITTED`. Dokumentasi PostgreSQL juga menyatakan `Repeatable Read` di PostgreSQL tidak mengizinkan phantom read, sedangkan `Serializable` mencegah dirty read, nonrepeatable read, phantom read, dan serialization anomaly. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/transaction-iso.html))

| Isolation Level | Dirty Read | Nonrepeatable Read | Phantom Read | Serialization Anomaly | Praktis di PostgreSQL |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---|
| Read Uncommitted | Tidak terjadi di PG | Bisa | Bisa | Bisa | Sama seperti Read Committed |
| Read Committed | Tidak | Bisa | Bisa | Bisa | Default, throughput bagus |
| Repeatable Read | Tidak | Tidak | Tidak di PG | Bisa | Snapshot stabil, masih bisa serialization failure |
| Serializable | Tidak | Tidak | Tidak | Tidak | Paling kuat, wajib siap retry |

**Rule of thumb:** jangan menaikkan isolation level sebagai solusi pertama. Untuk banyak workflow enterprise, kombinasi **Read Committed + optimistic locking + unique constraint + affected-row check** lebih sederhana dan scalable.

---

## 6. Optimistic Locking Pattern

Optimistic locking cocok ketika konflik tidak terlalu sering dan kita ingin menghindari lock panjang.

### 6.1 Schema

```sql id="vejp4f"
CREATE TABLE cases (
    id uuid PRIMARY KEY,
    case_number text NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    status text NOT NULL,
    assigned_team text NOT NULL,
    version bigint NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    updated_at timestamp with time zone NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);
```

### 6.2 Mapper

```java id="h1f3mo"
int transitionStatus(
    @Param("id") UUID id,
    @Param("expectedStatus") String expectedStatus,
    @Param("newStatus") String newStatus,
    @Param("expectedVersion") long expectedVersion
);
```

### 6.3 XML

```xml id="c9761g"
<update id="transitionStatus">
  UPDATE cases
  SET
    status = #{newStatus},
    version = version + 1,
    updated_at = now()
  WHERE id = #{id}
    AND status = #{expectedStatus}
    AND version = #{expectedVersion}
</update>
```

### 6.4 Service

```java id="dpr1ib"
public void approveCase(ApproveCaseCommand command) {
    unitOfWork.transactional(ctx -> {
        CaseMapper mapper = ctx.mapper(CaseMapper.class);

        CaseRecord current = mapper.findById(command.caseId());
        if (current == null) {
            throw new CaseNotFoundException(command.caseId());
        }

        if (!current.canApprove()) {
            throw new InvalidCaseTransitionException(
                current.status(),
                "APPROVED"
            );
        }

        int updated = mapper.transitionStatus(
            command.caseId(),
            current.status(),
            "APPROVED",
            current.version()
        );

        if (updated != 1) {
            throw new ConcurrentCaseModificationException(command.caseId());
        }

        mapper.insertAuditLog(...);
        mapper.insertOutboxEvent(...);

        return null;
    });
}
```

**Kenapa status ikut di `WHERE`?**  
Karena version saja mendeteksi concurrent write, tetapi status di `WHERE` membuat invariant bisnis ikut ditegakkan di database.

---

## 7. Lost Update Pattern

### Bad

```text id="n9v9j2"
T1 read status = SUBMITTED
T2 read status = SUBMITTED
T1 update status = APPROVED
T2 update status = REJECTED
```

Jika update hanya:

```sql id="9oz2fa"
UPDATE cases
SET status = ?
WHERE id = ?
```

maka update terakhir bisa menimpa update pertama.

### Good

```sql id="5cy9dv"
UPDATE cases
SET status = ?, version = version + 1
WHERE id = ?
  AND status = ?
  AND version = ?
```

Lalu service wajib cek:

```java id="plvm92"
if (updated != 1) {
    throw new ConcurrentModificationException();
}
```

---

## 8. Pessimistic Locking Pattern

Pessimistic locking cocok ketika konflik sering, state transition mahal, atau kita perlu memastikan row tidak berubah selama proses validasi.

PostgreSQL `SELECT ... FOR UPDATE` mengunci row yang dipilih agar tidak bisa di-lock, dimodifikasi, atau dihapus oleh transaksi lain sampai transaksi saat ini berakhir. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/explicit-locking.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### 8.1 Mapper

```java id="7r9sz9"
CaseRecord findByIdForUpdate(@Param("id") UUID id);
```

### 8.2 XML

```xml id="fbc0ub"
<select id="findByIdForUpdate" resultMap="CaseResultMap">
  SELECT
    id,
    case_number,
    status,
    assigned_team,
    version,
    updated_at
  FROM cases
  WHERE id = #{id}
  FOR UPDATE
</select>
```

### 8.3 Service

```java id="ietoky"
public void assignCase(AssignCaseCommand command) {
    unitOfWork.transactional(ctx -> {
        CaseMapper mapper = ctx.mapper(CaseMapper.class);

        CaseRecord current = mapper.findByIdForUpdate(command.caseId());
        if (current == null) {
            throw new CaseNotFoundException(command.caseId());
        }

        if (!current.canAssignTo(command.team())) {
            throw new InvalidAssignmentException(command.caseId());
        }

        mapper.assignCase(command.caseId(), command.team());

        mapper.insertAuditLog(...);

        return null;
    });
}
```

### 8.4 Kapan pakai optimistic vs pessimistic?

| Kondisi | Pilihan |
|---|---|
| Konflik jarang | Optimistic locking |
| Konflik sering di row yang sama | Pessimistic locking |
| Validasi murah | Optimistic |
| Validasi mahal dan tidak boleh diulang | Pessimistic |
| Endpoint high throughput | Optimistic dulu |
| Workflow approval/escalation kritis | Bisa pessimistic pada row utama |
| Batch worker queue | `FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED` |
| Lock banyak row | Hati-hati deadlock; urutkan lock |

---

## 9. Queue Worker Pattern dengan `FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED`

Pattern ini cocok untuk job table, outbox publisher, escalation worker, retry scheduler, dan background processor.

PostgreSQL `SELECT` mendukung locking clause seperti `FOR UPDATE`, `FOR NO KEY UPDATE`, `FOR SHARE`, dan `FOR KEY SHARE`; locking clause membutuhkan privilege update pada minimal satu kolom table yang dikunci. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-select.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### 9.1 Schema

```sql id="a6nd31"
CREATE TABLE outbox_events (
    id uuid PRIMARY KEY,
    aggregate_type text NOT NULL,
    aggregate_id text NOT NULL,
    event_type text NOT NULL,
    payload jsonb NOT NULL,
    status text NOT NULL,
    available_at timestamp with time zone NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    attempt_count integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    locked_at timestamp with time zone,
    created_at timestamp with time zone NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);
```

### 9.2 Claim jobs

```xml id="rcyyt5"
<select id="claimPendingEvents" resultMap="OutboxEventResultMap">
  SELECT
    id,
    aggregate_type,
    aggregate_id,
    event_type,
    payload,
    status,
    available_at,
    attempt_count,
    locked_at,
    created_at
  FROM outbox_events
  WHERE status = 'PENDING'
    AND available_at &lt;= now()
  ORDER BY created_at ASC, id ASC
  LIMIT #{limit}
  FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED
</select>
```

### 9.3 Mark processing

```xml id="i2cc32"
<update id="markProcessing">
  UPDATE outbox_events
  SET
    status = 'PROCESSING',
    locked_at = now(),
    attempt_count = attempt_count + 1
  WHERE id = #{id}
    AND status = 'PENDING'
</update>
```

### 9.4 Worker flow

```java id="g8z6jh"
public List<OutboxEvent> claimEvents(int limit) {
    return unitOfWork.transactional(ctx -> {
        OutboxMapper mapper = ctx.mapper(OutboxMapper.class);

        List<OutboxEvent> events = mapper.claimPendingEvents(limit);

        for (OutboxEvent event : events) {
            int updated = mapper.markProcessing(event.id());
            if (updated != 1) {
                throw new ConcurrentModificationException(
                    "Failed to mark event as processing: " + event.id()
                );
            }
        }

        return events;
    });
}
```

**Practical note:** ambil dan mark dalam transaksi pendek. Jangan tahan row lock sambil memanggil Kafka/HTTP service eksternal.

---

## 10. Advisory Lock Pattern

Advisory lock berguna ketika lock tidak natural berbasis satu row, misalnya business key seperti `tenantId + caseNumber`, scheduled job singleton per tenant, atau deduplication lintas table.

PostgreSQL menyediakan advisory lock dengan makna yang ditentukan aplikasi. Ada session-level advisory lock dan transaction-level advisory lock; transaction-level advisory lock otomatis dilepas saat transaksi selesai, sehingga biasanya lebih aman untuk short-lived service transaction. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/explicit-locking.html))

### 10.1 Mapper

```java id="ko4sr1"
void acquireCaseNumberLock(@Param("lockKey") long lockKey);
```

### 10.2 XML

```xml id="f6v8vr"
<select id="acquireCaseNumberLock" resultType="void">
  SELECT pg_advisory_xact_lock(#{lockKey})
</select>
```

### 10.3 Java lock key

```java id="ow3hyd"
public final class AdvisoryLockKey {

    private AdvisoryLockKey() {}

    public static long fromBusinessKey(String value) {
        return Hashing.sha256()
            .hashString(value, StandardCharsets.UTF_8)
            .asLong();
    }
}
```

### 10.4 Service

```java id="se62pf"
public CaseResponse createCase(CreateCaseCommand command) {
    return unitOfWork.transactional(ctx -> {
        CaseMapper mapper = ctx.mapper(CaseMapper.class);

        long lockKey = AdvisoryLockKey.fromBusinessKey(
            command.tenantId() + ":" + command.caseNumber()
        );

        mapper.acquireCaseNumberLock(lockKey);

        if (mapper.existsByCaseNumber(command.caseNumber())) {
            throw new DuplicateCaseNumberException(command.caseNumber());
        }

        mapper.insertCase(...);
        mapper.insertAuditLog(...);

        return ...;
    });
}
```

**Warning:** advisory lock adalah kontrak aplikasi. Database tidak otomatis tahu bahwa semua code path harus memakai lock tersebut. Jika ada satu code path yang bypass lock, invariant tetap bisa rusak.

---

## 11. Idempotency Pattern

Idempotency wajib untuk endpoint yang bisa di-retry oleh client, API gateway, scheduler, worker, atau message consumer.

### 11.1 Problem

```text id="m4cq9u"
Client POST /orders timeout.
Client tidak tahu apakah order sudah dibuat.
Client retry.
Tanpa idempotency, order bisa dibuat dua kali.
```

### 11.2 Schema

```sql id="tngmhf"
CREATE TABLE idempotency_keys (
    key text PRIMARY KEY,
    request_hash text NOT NULL,
    status text NOT NULL,
    response_code integer,
    response_body jsonb,
    created_at timestamp with time zone NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    completed_at timestamp with time zone
);
```

### 11.3 Insert idempotency key

PostgreSQL `INSERT ... ON CONFLICT` dapat mengubah unique violation menjadi `DO NOTHING` atau `DO UPDATE`; untuk `ON CONFLICT DO UPDATE`, PostgreSQL menjamin hasil atomic insert-or-update pada concurrency tinggi, selama tidak ada error independen. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-insert.html))

```xml id="ep7kl4"
<insert id="insertIdempotencyKey">
  INSERT INTO idempotency_keys (
    key,
    request_hash,
    status,
    created_at
  )
  VALUES (
    #{key},
    #{requestHash},
    'PROCESSING',
    now()
  )
  ON CONFLICT (key) DO NOTHING
</insert>
```

### 11.4 Lock existing idempotency row

```xml id="cc3wnh"
<select id="findIdempotencyKeyForUpdate" resultMap="IdempotencyKeyResultMap">
  SELECT
    key,
    request_hash,
    status,
    response_code,
    response_body,
    created_at,
    completed_at
  FROM idempotency_keys
  WHERE key = #{key}
  FOR UPDATE
</select>
```

### 11.5 Complete idempotency key

```xml id="x7epn4"
<update id="completeIdempotencyKey">
  UPDATE idempotency_keys
  SET
    status = 'COMPLETED',
    response_code = #{responseCode},
    response_body = #{responseBody, jdbcType=OTHER,
      typeHandler=com.company.app.persistence.typehandler.JsonNodeJsonbTypeHandler},
    completed_at = now()
  WHERE key = #{key}
    AND status = 'PROCESSING'
</update>
```

### 11.6 Service flow

```java id="tzy19t"
public OrderResponse createOrder(CreateOrderCommand command) {
    return unitOfWork.transactional(ctx -> {
        IdempotencyMapper idem = ctx.mapper(IdempotencyMapper.class);
        OrderMapper orderMapper = ctx.mapper(OrderMapper.class);

        int inserted = idem.insertIdempotencyKey(
            command.idempotencyKey(),
            command.requestHash()
        );

        IdempotencyRecord record = idem.findIdempotencyKeyForUpdate(
            command.idempotencyKey()
        );

        if (!record.requestHash().equals(command.requestHash())) {
            throw new IdempotencyKeyConflictException();
        }

        if ("COMPLETED".equals(record.status())) {
            return deserializeOrderResponse(record.responseBody());
        }

        Order order = Order.create(...);
        orderMapper.insertOrder(order);
        orderMapper.insertItems(order.items());

        OrderResponse response = OrderResponse.from(order);

        idem.completeIdempotencyKey(
            command.idempotencyKey(),
            201,
            serialize(response)
        );

        return response;
    });
}
```

**Invariant:** idempotency key tidak hanya mencegah duplikasi. Ia juga harus memastikan **same key + different request body** ditolak.

---

## 12. Retry Pattern

PostgreSQL menyatakan bahwa transaksi pada `Repeatable Read` dan `Serializable` bisa gagal dengan `serialization_failure` SQLSTATE `40001`, dan aplikasi harus siap retry. Deadlock failure juga dapat di-retry dengan SQLSTATE `40P01`; unique violation `23505` dan exclusion violation `23P01` kadang layak di-retry, tetapi harus lebih hati-hati karena bisa juga persistent error. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/mvcc-serialization-failure-handling.html))

### 12.1 SQLSTATE classifier

```java id="e4fch8"
public final class PostgresTransientFailureClassifier {

    private static final Set<String> RETRYABLE_SQL_STATES = Set.of(
        "40001", // serialization_failure
        "40P01"  // deadlock_detected
    );

    private PostgresTransientFailureClassifier() {}

    public static boolean isRetryable(Throwable throwable) {
        SQLException sqlException = findSqlException(throwable);
        return sqlException != null
            && RETRYABLE_SQL_STATES.contains(sqlException.getSQLState());
    }

    private static SQLException findSqlException(Throwable throwable) {
        Throwable current = throwable;

        while (current != null) {
            if (current instanceof SQLException sqlException) {
                return sqlException;
            }
            current = current.getCause();
        }

        return null;
    }
}
```

### 12.2 Retry wrapper

```java id="vqpfr5"
public <T> T retryableTransaction(MyBatisWork<T> work) {
    int maxAttempts = 3;
    long backoffMillis = 50;

    RuntimeException lastFailure = null;

    for (int attempt = 1; attempt <= maxAttempts; attempt++) {
        try {
            return transactional(work);
        } catch (RuntimeException e) {
            if (!PostgresTransientFailureClassifier.isRetryable(e)
                || attempt == maxAttempts) {
                throw e;
            }

            lastFailure = e;
            sleep(backoffMillis * attempt);
        }
    }

    throw lastFailure;
}

private void sleep(long millis) {
    try {
        Thread.sleep(millis);
    } catch (InterruptedException e) {
        Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
        throw new IllegalStateException("Interrupted while retrying transaction", e);
    }
}
```

### 12.3 Retry safety rule

| Operation | Retry Aman? | Syarat |
|---|---:|---|
| Pure DB transaction | Ya | Seluruh efek ada dalam transaction |
| DB + outbox insert | Ya | External publish dilakukan setelah commit |
| DB + HTTP call di dalam transaction | Tidak | Bisa double-call |
| Payment capture langsung dalam transaction | Tidak | Butuh idempotency provider/payment key |
| Kafka publish dalam transaction DB | Tidak otomatis | Gunakan outbox |
| Email send dalam transaction | Tidak | Simpan notification request, kirim async |
| Audit insert dalam same transaction | Ya | Rollback bersama command |

**Rule keras:** retry boleh mengulang transaksi database, tetapi jangan mengulang side effect eksternal non-idempotent.

---

## 13. Deadlock Avoidance

Deadlock biasanya muncul ketika dua transaksi mengunci resource yang sama dalam urutan berbeda.

```text id="4g52p3"
T1 locks case A
T2 locks case B
T1 wants case B
T2 wants case A
deadlock
```

### 13.1 Lock ordering

Selalu lock dalam urutan deterministic:

```xml id="agbibm"
<select id="findCasesForUpdate" resultMap="CaseResultMap">
  SELECT
    id,
    case_number,
    status,
    version
  FROM cases
  WHERE id IN
  <foreach collection="ids"
           item="id"
           open="("
           separator=","
           close=")">
    #{id}
  </foreach>
  ORDER BY id ASC
  FOR UPDATE
</select>
```

### 13.2 Service

```java id="k89gu4"
List<UUID> sortedIds = command.caseIds().stream()
    .sorted()
    .toList();

List<CaseRecord> lockedCases = mapper.findCasesForUpdate(sortedIds);
```

### 13.3 Practical checklist

| Rule | Why |
|---|---|
| Lock parent sebelum child | Urutan konsisten |
| Lock berdasarkan primary key ascending | Mengurangi cycle |
| Jangan tahan lock saat call service eksternal | Lock duration meledak |
| Transaksi harus pendek | Mengurangi contention |
| Update row dalam urutan sama di semua code path | Mengurangi deadlock |
| Retry `40P01` dengan backoff | Deadlock bisa transient |

---

## 14. Timeout Pattern

Timeout penting agar thread Jersey tidak habis menunggu lock/query terlalu lama.

### 14.1 Set transaction-local timeout

```xml id="kbyi17"
<update id="setLocalLockTimeout">
  SET LOCAL lock_timeout = #{timeout}
</update>
```

```xml id="c7an39"
<update id="setLocalStatementTimeout">
  SET LOCAL statement_timeout = #{timeout}
</update>
```

Contoh service:

```java id="8lsrtq"
unitOfWork.transactional(ctx -> {
    RuntimeMapper runtime = ctx.mapper(RuntimeMapper.class);

    runtime.setLocalLockTimeout("2s");
    runtime.setLocalStatementTimeout("10s");

    // business operation here
    return null;
});
```

**Catatan:** `SET LOCAL` berlaku hanya di transaksi saat ini, sehingga cocok untuk per-use-case policy.

---

## 15. Outbox + Transaction Consistency

### 15.1 Problem

```text id="mf37oy"
DB commit berhasil, Kafka publish gagal -> data ada, event hilang.
Kafka publish berhasil, DB rollback -> event ada, data tidak ada.
```

### 15.2 Pattern

```text id="h1atqe"
Dalam transaksi:
  insert/update business data
  insert outbox_events row
  commit

Setelah commit:
  worker membaca outbox_events
  publish ke Kafka/HTTP
  mark published
```

### 15.3 XML

```xml id="od1lg6"
<insert id="insertOutboxEvent">
  INSERT INTO outbox_events (
    id,
    aggregate_type,
    aggregate_id,
    event_type,
    payload,
    status,
    created_at
  )
  VALUES (
    #{id},
    #{aggregateType},
    #{aggregateId},
    #{eventType},
    #{payload, jdbcType=OTHER,
      typeHandler=com.company.app.persistence.typehandler.JsonNodeJsonbTypeHandler},
    'PENDING',
    now()
  )
</insert>
```

### 15.4 Service

```java id="rnnx4z"
public void submitOrder(SubmitOrderCommand command) {
    unitOfWork.transactional(ctx -> {
        OrderMapper orderMapper = ctx.mapper(OrderMapper.class);
        OutboxMapper outboxMapper = ctx.mapper(OutboxMapper.class);

        int updated = orderMapper.transitionStatus(
            command.orderId(),
            "DRAFT",
            "SUBMITTED",
            command.version()
        );

        if (updated != 1) {
            throw new InvalidOrderTransitionException();
        }

        outboxMapper.insertOutboxEvent(
            OutboxEvent.orderSubmitted(command.orderId())
        );

        return null;
    });
}
```

---

## 16. UPSERT Pattern

### 16.1 Insert only if absent

```xml id="rv5ugy"
<insert id="insertIfAbsent">
  INSERT INTO customer_references (
    customer_id,
    external_reference,
    created_at
  )
  VALUES (
    #{customerId},
    #{externalReference},
    now()
  )
  ON CONFLICT (external_reference) DO NOTHING
</insert>
```

Service:

```java id="pj42fq"
int inserted = mapper.insertIfAbsent(reference);

if (inserted == 0) {
    throw new DuplicateExternalReferenceException(reference.externalReference());
}
```

### 16.2 Upsert update

```xml id="azt8qo"
<insert id="upsertProcessingCheckpoint">
  INSERT INTO processing_checkpoints (
    worker_name,
    checkpoint_value,
    updated_at
  )
  VALUES (
    #{workerName},
    #{checkpointValue},
    now()
  )
  ON CONFLICT (worker_name)
  DO UPDATE SET
    checkpoint_value = EXCLUDED.checkpoint_value,
    updated_at = now()
</insert>
```

### 16.3 When not to upsert

| Situation | Avoid Blind UPSERT? | Reason |
|---|---:|---|
| State transition workflow | Ya | Bisa bypass invariant |
| Approval/rejection | Ya | Harus validasi current state |
| Idempotency response replay | Tidak selalu | Upsert/insert-if-absent useful |
| Reference table sync | Boleh | Biasanya desired behavior |
| Counter increment | Hati-hati | Butuh atomic expression |
| Payment/order creation | Hati-hati | Butuh idempotency semantics |

---

## 17. Mapper Patterns for Concurrency

| Pattern | SQL Shape | Signal ke Service |
|---|---|---|
| Optimistic transition | `UPDATE ... WHERE id=? AND version=?` | `updated == 0` berarti stale |
| State transition | `UPDATE ... WHERE status=?` | `updated == 0` berarti invalid state |
| Idempotent insert | `INSERT ... ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING` | `inserted == 0` berarti duplicate |
| Claim queue | `SELECT ... FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED` | Empty list berarti no work |
| Pessimistic edit | `SELECT ... FOR UPDATE` | Blocking / timeout |
| Advisory critical section | `pg_advisory_xact_lock` | Serializes business key |
| Retryable transaction | SQLSTATE classifier | Retry full unit of work |
| Outbox | Insert event in same transaction | Event eventually published |

---

## 18. Jersey/JAX-RS Error Mapping

Concurrency error harus diterjemahkan ke HTTP response yang meaningful.

| Condition | Java Exception | HTTP Status | Response Meaning |
|---|---|---:|---|
| Version mismatch | `ConcurrentModificationException` | `409 Conflict` | Client state stale |
| Invalid state transition | `InvalidStateTransitionException` | `409 Conflict` | Command not valid now |
| Duplicate idempotency key with different body | `IdempotencyKeyConflictException` | `409 Conflict` | Same key reused incorrectly |
| Duplicate unique business key | `DuplicateReferenceException` | `409 Conflict` | Resource already exists |
| Lock timeout | `LockTimeoutException` | `503` or `409` | Try later / resource busy |
| Serialization failure after retries | `TransientPersistenceException` | `503` | Retryable server-side contention |
| Deadlock after retries | `TransientPersistenceException` | `503` | Retryable contention |
| Not found | `NotFoundException` | `404` | Resource missing |

### ExceptionMapper example

```java id="oy4ev3"
@Provider
public class ConcurrentModificationExceptionMapper
    implements ExceptionMapper<ConcurrentCaseModificationException> {

    @Override
    public Response toResponse(ConcurrentCaseModificationException exception) {
        return Response.status(Response.Status.CONFLICT)
            .entity(ApiError.of(
                "CONCURRENT_MODIFICATION",
                "The resource was modified by another transaction. Reload and retry."
            ))
            .build();
    }
}
```

---

## 19. Testing Concurrency

### 19.1 Lost update test

```java id="7mapzr"
@Test
void shouldAllowOnlyOneApprovalForSameVersion() throws Exception {
    UUID caseId = fixture.createSubmittedCase();

    ExecutorService executor = Executors.newFixedThreadPool(2);
    CountDownLatch start = new CountDownLatch(1);

    Callable<Boolean> approve = () -> {
        start.await();
        try {
            service.approveCase(new ApproveCaseCommand(caseId, 0L));
            return true;
        } catch (ConcurrentCaseModificationException e) {
            return false;
        }
    };

    Future<Boolean> first = executor.submit(approve);
    Future<Boolean> second = executor.submit(approve);

    start.countDown();

    List<Boolean> results = List.of(first.get(), second.get());

    assertThat(results).containsExactlyInAnyOrder(true, false);
}
```

### 19.2 Idempotency replay test

```java id="k50c89"
@Test
void shouldReturnSameResponseForSameIdempotencyKeyAndSameRequest() {
    String key = UUID.randomUUID().toString();

    OrderResponse first = service.createOrder(commandWithKey(key));
    OrderResponse second = service.createOrder(commandWithKey(key));

    assertThat(second.orderId()).isEqualTo(first.orderId());
}
```

### 19.3 Idempotency conflict test

```java id="wq8wz9"
@Test
void shouldRejectSameIdempotencyKeyWithDifferentPayload() {
    String key = UUID.randomUUID().toString();

    service.createOrder(commandWithKeyAndProduct(key, "A"));

    assertThatThrownBy(() ->
        service.createOrder(commandWithKeyAndProduct(key, "B"))
    ).isInstanceOf(IdempotencyKeyConflictException.class);
}
```

---

## 20. Anti-Pattern

| Anti-Pattern | Contoh | Dampak |
|---|---|---|
| Read-then-update tanpa version | `SELECT`, validate, `UPDATE WHERE id` | Lost update |
| Commit di tengah use case | Insert order commit, insert audit gagal | Partial write |
| Retry hanya statement terakhir | Retry `UPDATE`, bukan full transaction | State tidak konsisten |
| External call dalam DB transaction | Call payment/Kafka sebelum commit | Double side effect / lock panjang |
| Lock row lalu proses lama | `FOR UPDATE`, lalu HTTP call | Blocking parah |
| Blind upsert untuk state transition | `ON CONFLICT DO UPDATE status` | Bypass lifecycle invariant |
| Idempotency key tanpa request hash | Same key untuk payload beda dianggap sama | Data salah |
| Tidak cek affected rows | `mapper.update(...)` ignored | Concurrency bug tersembunyi |
| Advisory lock session-level sembarang | Lupa release | Lock bocor sampai session mati |
| Lock banyak row tanpa urutan | Random order | Deadlock |
| Serializable tanpa retry | Naik isolation lalu error 40001 | Failure sporadis |
| In-memory lock di multi-pod | `synchronized` di Kubernetes | Tidak efektif antar instance |
| Queue worker tanpa `SKIP LOCKED` | Banyak worker ambil row sama | Duplicate processing |

---

## 21. Production Checklist

| Checklist | Status Ideal |
|---|---|
| Semua command multi-step ada transaction boundary eksplisit | Ya |
| `SqlSession` tidak auto-commit untuk use case write | Ya |
| Semua update state mengecek affected row | Ya |
| State transition memakai `status` dan/atau `version` di `WHERE` | Ya |
| Endpoint retryable punya idempotency key | Ya |
| Same idempotency key + different request hash ditolak | Ya |
| External side effect tidak dilakukan di dalam DB transaction | Ya |
| Outbox event ditulis dalam transaksi yang sama dengan business data | Ya |
| Worker memakai claim pattern yang aman | Ya |
| Lock row tidak ditahan saat call eksternal | Ya |
| Lock ordering deterministic | Ya |
| Retry hanya untuk SQLSTATE transient yang jelas | Ya |
| Retry mengulang seluruh unit of work | Ya |
| Lock timeout dan statement timeout dikonfigurasi | Ya |
| Concurrency integration test tersedia | Ya |
| Metrics untuk deadlock, retry, lock timeout, conflict tersedia | Ya |

---

## 22. Decision Table

| Problem | Pattern yang Disarankan |
|---|---|
| Dua user approve case yang sama | Optimistic locking |
| Satu case harus diedit eksklusif | `SELECT ... FOR UPDATE` |
| Banyak worker proses outbox | `FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED` |
| Client retry POST create order | Idempotency key table |
| Insert kalau belum ada | `ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING` |
| Insert/update reference sync | `ON CONFLICT DO UPDATE` |
| Invariant lintas banyak row | Serializable + retry, atau explicit lock |
| Business key butuh critical section | Transaction-level advisory lock |
| Deadlock sporadis | Lock ordering + retry `40P01` |
| Serialization failure | Retry full transaction `40001` |
| Publish event setelah DB commit | Transactional outbox |
| Duplicate message consumer | Idempotent consumer table + unique constraint |

---

## 23. Roadmap Belajar Singkat

| Urutan | Materi | Target |
|---:|---|---|
| 1 | MyBatis transaction control | Bisa commit/rollback dengan benar |
| 2 | PostgreSQL Read Committed behavior | Paham default isolation |
| 3 | Optimistic locking | Bisa cegah lost update |
| 4 | Pessimistic locking | Bisa lock row saat perlu |
| 5 | `FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED` | Bisa buat worker queue aman |
| 6 | Idempotency key | Bisa handle client retry |
| 7 | SQLSTATE retry | Bisa retry transient failure dengan aman |
| 8 | Deadlock prevention | Bisa desain lock ordering |
| 9 | Transactional outbox | Bisa pisahkan DB commit dan external publish |
| 10 | Concurrency integration test | Bisa membuktikan correctness |
