## Response:
7/13/2026, 2:25:35 AM

Lanjut ke performance dan query tuning: fokusnya bukan hanya “query cepat”, tapi bagaimana MyBatis, JDBC driver, connection pool, dan PostgreSQL planner saling memengaruhi di runtime service.

# Cheatsheet - Java MyBatis Performance, Query Tuning, ExecutorType, Batch Processing, Fetch Size, Cursor Streaming, Connection Pooling, dan PostgreSQL EXPLAIN/Index Awareness

## 1. Introduction

Performance di aplikasi **Jersey/JAX-RS + MyBatis + PostgreSQL** tidak bisa dilihat hanya dari “SQL cepat atau lambat”. Ada beberapa lapisan yang saling memengaruhi:

```text id="g23fe5"
JAX-RS request throughput
  -> service transaction duration
    -> MyBatis SqlSession lifecycle
      -> ExecutorType behavior
        -> JDBC PreparedStatement / ResultSet behavior
          -> connection pool availability
            -> PostgreSQL query plan
              -> index, statistics, lock, IO, memory
```

MyBatis memberi beberapa tuas performa penting: `ExecutorType.SIMPLE`, `ExecutorType.REUSE`, `ExecutorType.BATCH`, `Cursor`, `ResultHandler`, `fetchSize`, `timeout`, local cache, dan statement-level options. `SqlSessionFactory.openSession(...)` juga bisa memilih executor type, isolation level, auto-commit, atau connection tertentu. MyBatis mendokumentasikan bahwa default `openSession()` membuat transaction scope non-auto-commit, mengambil connection dari configured `DataSource`, memakai isolation default driver/data source, tidak reuse prepared statement, dan tidak batch update. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/java-api.html))

---

## 2. Mental Model Performance

```mermaid id="fk3b8k"
flowchart TD
    A[JAX-RS Request] --> B[Service Method]
    B --> C[SqlSession]
    C --> D{ExecutorType}
    D -->|SIMPLE| E[New PreparedStatement per execution]
    D -->|REUSE| F[Reuse PreparedStatement]
    D -->|BATCH| G[Batch update statements]

    C --> H[Mapper XML SQL]
    H --> I[JDBC PreparedStatement]
    I --> J[pgJDBC Driver]
    J --> K[Connection Pool]
    K --> L[(PostgreSQL)]

    L --> M[Planner]
    M --> N[Seq Scan / Index Scan / Bitmap Scan / Join / Sort]
    N --> O[ResultSet]

    O --> P{Result Handling}
    P -->|List| Q[Materialize all rows]
    P -->|Cursor| R[Lazy iteration]
    P -->|ResultHandler| S[Custom row handling]

    R --> T[fetchSize / cursor mode]
    S --> T
```

**Core invariant:**

```text id="lwz0cr"
Performance problem bisa berada di:
- query shape
- missing/wrong index
- bad cardinality estimate
- result set terlalu besar
- session terlalu lama
- pool terlalu kecil/besar
- batch salah digunakan
- transaction menahan lock terlalu lama
- endpoint terlalu banyak melakukan round trip
```

---

## 3. Decomposition Map

| Area | Fokus Praktis | Skill yang Dikuasai | Risiko Jika Salah |
|---|---|---|---|
| **Query Shape** | Bentuk SQL yang planner-friendly | Predicate, join order awareness, projection, pagination | Full scan, sort besar, nested loop mahal |
| **EXPLAIN / ANALYZE** | Membaca execution plan | Seq scan, index scan, bitmap scan, sort, hash join, actual vs estimated rows | Optimasi berdasarkan tebakan |
| **Index Awareness** | Index sesuai access pattern | Composite, partial, covering, expression, GIN/BRIN | Index tidak dipakai atau terlalu banyak index |
| **ExecutorType.SIMPLE** | Default execution | Statement baru per execution | Overhead jika statement sama dieksekusi banyak kali |
| **ExecutorType.REUSE** | Reuse prepared statement | Mengurangi statement creation | Tidak menyelesaikan query lambat |
| **ExecutorType.BATCH** | Batch insert/update/delete | Flush batch, chunking, transaction control | Memory besar, error partial sulit ditangani |
| **Fetch Size** | Mengontrol pengambilan row dari driver | Cursor mode pgJDBC, streaming besar | Semua row dimaterialisasi di memory |
| **Cursor Streaming** | Proses row besar secara lazy | MyBatis `Cursor`, session tetap terbuka | Connection tertahan lama |
| **ResultHandler** | Custom row processing | Aggregation/export/streaming custom | Tidak cocok untuk advanced nested resultMap |
| **Pagination** | List endpoint scalable | Keyset vs offset, deterministic ordering | Offset besar lambat |
| **Connection Pooling** | Throughput dan backpressure | `maximumPoolSize`, timeout, lifetime, metrics | Pool exhaustion, DB overload |
| **Timeouts** | Fail fast saat query/lock lambat | statement timeout, lock timeout, connection timeout | Request menggantung |
| **N+1 Query Avoidance** | Mengurangi round trip | Join projection, nested result, batch fetch | Latency meledak |
| **Local Cache** | Cache per `SqlSession` | Session pendek, cache awareness | Stale/memory pressure |
| **Batch Write Design** | Insert/update massal aman | Chunk, flush, commit boundary | Lock lama dan rollback mahal |
| **Observability** | Melihat bottleneck nyata | SQL timing, pool metrics, slow query log | Tuning buta |

---

## 4. MyBatis ExecutorType

MyBatis mendefinisikan tiga executor type: `SIMPLE` membuat `PreparedStatement` baru setiap execution, `REUSE` mencoba reuse prepared statement, dan `BATCH` melakukan batch update statements serta melakukan demarcation bila `SELECT` dijalankan di antara batch agar behavior tetap mudah dipahami. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/java-api.html))

| ExecutorType | Cocok Untuk | Hindari Untuk | Catatan |
|---|---|---|---|
| `SIMPLE` | Default CRUD, query biasa | Loop ribuan statement sama | Aman dan predictable |
| `REUSE` | Banyak statement sama dalam satu session | Query sangat bervariasi | Mengurangi overhead prepared statement |
| `BATCH` | Insert/update/delete massal | Flow yang butuh generated result per row langsung | Perlu `flushStatements()` dan chunking |

---

## 5. Unit of Work dengan ExecutorType

### 5.1 Default transactional

```java id="mkeo8k"
public <T> T transactional(MyBatisWork<T> work) {
    try (SqlSession session = sqlSessionFactory.openSession(false)) {
        try {
            T result = work.execute(new MyBatisSessionContext(session));
            session.commit();
            return result;
        } catch (RuntimeException | Error e) {
            session.rollback();
            throw e;
        }
    }
}
```

### 5.2 Batch transactional

```java id="fsy3a2"
import org.apache.ibatis.session.ExecutorType;
import org.apache.ibatis.session.SqlSession;

public <T> T batchTransactional(MyBatisWork<T> work) {
    try (SqlSession session = sqlSessionFactory.openSession(ExecutorType.BATCH, false)) {
        try {
            T result = work.execute(new MyBatisSessionContext(session));

            session.flushStatements();
            session.commit();

            return result;
        } catch (RuntimeException | Error e) {
            session.rollback();
            throw e;
        }
    }
}
```

MyBatis menyediakan `flushStatements()` untuk mengeksekusi batch update statements saat menggunakan `ExecutorType.BATCH`. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/java-api.html))

---

## 6. Batch Processing Pattern

### 6.1 Mapper XML

```xml id="r4tlzy"
<insert id="insertAuditLog">
  INSERT INTO audit_logs (
    id,
    aggregate_type,
    aggregate_id,
    action,
    actor_id,
    payload,
    created_at
  )
  VALUES (
    #{id},
    #{aggregateType},
    #{aggregateId},
    #{action},
    #{actorId},
    #{payload, jdbcType=OTHER,
      typeHandler=com.company.app.persistence.typehandler.JsonNodeJsonbTypeHandler},
    #{createdAt}
  )
</insert>
```

### 6.2 Service chunking

```java id="xadm1j"
private static final int CHUNK_SIZE = 500;

public void insertAuditLogs(List<AuditLog> logs) {
    for (int start = 0; start < logs.size(); start += CHUNK_SIZE) {
        int end = Math.min(start + CHUNK_SIZE, logs.size());
        List<AuditLog> chunk = logs.subList(start, end);

        unitOfWork.batchTransactional(ctx -> {
            AuditLogMapper mapper = ctx.mapper(AuditLogMapper.class);

            for (AuditLog log : chunk) {
                mapper.insertAuditLog(log);
            }

            return null;
        });
    }
}
```

### 6.3 Batch decision table

| Kondisi | Gunakan Batch? | Alasan |
|---|---:|---|
| Insert ribuan audit row | Ya | Mengurangi round trip |
| Update banyak row dengan statement sama | Ya | Cocok untuk `ExecutorType.BATCH` |
| Butuh generated key per row langsung | Hati-hati | Batch bisa menyulitkan flow |
| Setiap row punya validation DB berbeda | Hati-hati | Error batch lebih sulit direkonsiliasi |
| Data kecil < 20 row | Tidak wajib | Complexity mungkin tidak sepadan |
| Perlu atomic semua-or-nothing 100k row | Hati-hati | Lock dan rollback mahal |
| Import file besar | Ya, tapi chunked | Jangan satu transaksi raksasa |

---

## 7. Multi-row Insert vs ExecutorType.BATCH

Ada dua pola batch yang berbeda.

### 7.1 Multi-row insert via SQL

```xml id="uvrxy4"
<insert id="insertItems">
  INSERT INTO order_items (
    order_id,
    product_id,
    quantity,
    unit_price
  )
  VALUES
  <foreach collection="items"
           item="item"
           separator=",">
    (
      #{item.orderId},
      #{item.productId},
      #{item.quantity},
      #{item.unitPrice}
    )
  </foreach>
</insert>
```

### 7.2 Batch executor via repeated mapper call

```java id="hmsm9d"
unitOfWork.batchTransactional(ctx -> {
    OrderItemMapper mapper = ctx.mapper(OrderItemMapper.class);

    for (OrderItem item : items) {
        mapper.insertItem(item);
    }

    return null;
});
```

| Pattern | Kelebihan | Kekurangan |
|---|---|---|
| Multi-row insert | Satu SQL statement, cepat untuk chunk sedang | SQL bisa sangat besar |
| `ExecutorType.BATCH` | Statement tetap kecil, driver batching | Butuh flush dan error handling lebih hati-hati |
| PostgreSQL `COPY` | Sangat cepat untuk bulk load | Di luar MyBatis normal mapper pattern |
| Single insert loop tanpa batch | Simple | Round trip besar |

**Practical rule:** untuk aplikasi service biasa, mulai dari multi-row insert chunked atau `ExecutorType.BATCH`. Untuk bulk load serius, pertimbangkan pipeline khusus, bukan endpoint JAX-RS biasa.

---

## 8. Fetch Size dan Cursor Mode pgJDBC

pgJDBC menjelaskan bahwa cursor mode dapat diaktifkan dengan `Statement#setFetchSize(...)`; `fetchSize=0` menyebabkan seluruh row dicache, yang merupakan default behavior. Agar cursor mode bekerja, connection tidak boleh auto-commit, statement harus forward-only, dan query harus single statement. ([pgJDBC](https://jdbc.postgresql.org/documentation/query/))

### 8.1 Mapper XML dengan `fetchSize`

```xml id="bzr2fm"
<select id="streamLargeAuditLogs"
        resultMap="AuditLogResultMap"
        fetchSize="500">
  SELECT
    id,
    aggregate_type,
    aggregate_id,
    action,
    actor_id,
    payload,
    created_at
  FROM audit_logs
  WHERE created_at &gt;= #{from}
    AND created_at &lt; #{to}
  ORDER BY created_at ASC, id ASC
</select>
```

### 8.2 Mapper dengan Cursor

```java id="aumbyq"
import org.apache.ibatis.cursor.Cursor;
import org.apache.ibatis.annotations.Param;

public interface AuditLogMapper {
    Cursor<AuditLog> streamLargeAuditLogs(
        @Param("from") Instant from,
        @Param("to") Instant to
    );
}
```

### 8.3 Service streaming

```java id="ty3aa0"
public void exportAuditLogs(Instant from, Instant to, AuditLogSink sink) {
    unitOfWork.readOnly(ctx -> {
        AuditLogMapper mapper = ctx.mapper(AuditLogMapper.class);

        try (Cursor<AuditLog> cursor = mapper.streamLargeAuditLogs(from, to)) {
            for (AuditLog log : cursor) {
                sink.write(log);
            }
        } catch (IOException e) {
            throw new ExportFailedException(e);
        }

        return null;
    });
}
```

MyBatis `Cursor` memberikan hasil seperti `List`, tetapi data difetch secara lazy menggunakan `Iterator`. Dokumentasi MyBatis juga menunjukkan penggunaan `try-with-resources` untuk menutup cursor. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/java-api.html))

---

## 9. Cursor Streaming: Kapan Tepat?

| Use Case | Cursor Cocok? | Catatan |
|---|---:|---|
| Export audit log besar | Ya | Jangan materialize semua row |
| Background reconciliation | Ya | Proses incremental |
| HTTP response streaming | Bisa | Hati-hati timeout client dan connection tertahan |
| Query list page biasa | Tidak perlu | Pagination cukup |
| Query dengan nested complex resultMap | Hati-hati | Mapping bisa butuh beberapa row |
| Proses jutaan row lama | Bisa, tapi hati-hati | Connection pool slot tertahan lama |
| Real-time API high QPS | Biasanya tidak | Lebih baik page/keyset |

**Warning penting:** cursor mengurangi memory pressure, tetapi **tidak gratis**. Selama cursor dibaca, transaction/session/connection tetap hidup. Kalau export berjalan 10 menit, satu connection pool slot tertahan 10 menit.

---

## 10. ResultHandler Pattern

MyBatis `ResultHandler` memungkinkan aplikasi menangani row satu per satu, misalnya untuk aggregation, export, atau custom sink. Namun MyBatis mencatat dua batasan: data yang diproses dengan `ResultHandler` tidak dicache, dan advanced `resultMap` dapat menghasilkan object yang association/collection-nya belum lengkap saat handler dipanggil. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/java-api.html))

### 10.1 Use case

```java id="wj75me"
public void computeAuditSummary(Instant from, Instant to) {
    unitOfWork.readOnly(ctx -> {
        SqlSession session = ctx.session();

        AuditSummaryAccumulator accumulator = new AuditSummaryAccumulator();

        session.select(
            "com.company.app.mapper.AuditLogMapper.findForSummary",
            Map.of("from", from, "to", to),
            resultContext -> {
                AuditLog log = (AuditLog) resultContext.getResultObject();
                accumulator.accept(log);
            }
        );

        return accumulator.snapshot();
    });
}
```

### 10.2 Kapan pakai ResultHandler?

| Kondisi | Gunakan? |
|---|---:|
| Per-row processing sederhana | Ya |
| Aggregation custom di Java | Bisa |
| Export streaming | Bisa |
| Nested `association/collection` kompleks | Hindari |
| Butuh cache MyBatis | Hindari |
| Bisa diselesaikan di SQL aggregate | Lebih baik SQL |

---

## 11. RowBounds Warning

MyBatis `RowBounds` memungkinkan offset/limit di level API, tetapi efisiensinya tergantung driver. Untuk PostgreSQL production query, lebih aman menulis `LIMIT/OFFSET` atau keyset pagination langsung di SQL mapper. MyBatis sendiri menyebut `RowBounds` membuat MyBatis skip sejumlah record dan membatasi jumlah hasil, tetapi efisiensinya bergantung pada driver. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/java-api.html))

### Prefer SQL pagination

```xml id="mo77gy"
<select id="searchCases" resultMap="CaseResultMap">
  SELECT
    id,
    case_number,
    status,
    priority,
    created_at
  FROM cases
  WHERE assigned_team = #{team}
  ORDER BY created_at DESC, id DESC
  LIMIT #{limit}
  OFFSET #{offset}
</select>
```

### Untuk high-volume list, prefer keyset

```xml id="bbk5gu"
<select id="searchCasesAfter" resultMap="CaseResultMap">
  SELECT
    id,
    case_number,
    status,
    priority,
    created_at
  FROM cases
  WHERE assigned_team = #{team}

  <if test="cursorCreatedAt != null and cursorId != null">
    AND (
      created_at &lt; #{cursorCreatedAt}
      OR (created_at = #{cursorCreatedAt} AND id &lt; #{cursorId})
    )
  </if>

  ORDER BY created_at DESC, id DESC
  LIMIT #{limit}
</select>
```

---

## 12. PostgreSQL EXPLAIN / ANALYZE

PostgreSQL memilih query plan untuk setiap query; dokumentasinya menekankan bahwa memilih plan yang cocok dengan struktur query dan properti data sangat penting untuk performa. `EXPLAIN` digunakan untuk melihat plan yang dibuat planner, dan plan berbentuk tree dengan node seperti sequential scan, index scan, bitmap index scan, join, sort, aggregation, dan lain-lain. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/using-explain.html))

### 12.1 Basic EXPLAIN

```sql id="mbiew1"
EXPLAIN
SELECT
    id,
    case_number,
    status,
    priority,
    created_at
FROM cases
WHERE assigned_team = 'ENFORCEMENT'
  AND status = 'OPEN'
ORDER BY created_at DESC, id DESC
LIMIT 50;
```

### 12.2 EXPLAIN ANALYZE

```sql id="mxykb5"
EXPLAIN (ANALYZE, BUFFERS)
SELECT
    id,
    case_number,
    status,
    priority,
    created_at
FROM cases
WHERE assigned_team = 'ENFORCEMENT'
  AND status = 'OPEN'
ORDER BY created_at DESC, id DESC
LIMIT 50;
```

**Caution:** `EXPLAIN ANALYZE` menjalankan query. Untuk `UPDATE`, `DELETE`, atau `INSERT`, jalankan di transaction lalu rollback jika hanya ingin analisis.

```sql id="1ie3bh"
BEGIN;

EXPLAIN (ANALYZE, BUFFERS)
UPDATE cases
SET status = 'ESCALATED'
WHERE status = 'OPEN'
  AND due_at < now();

ROLLBACK;
```

---

## 13. EXPLAIN Reading Checklist

| Signal | Artinya | Tindakan |
|---|---|---|
| `Seq Scan` pada tabel besar | Full table scan | Cek predicate/index/selectivity |
| `Index Scan` | Index dipakai untuk access | Cek apakah row count masuk akal |
| `Bitmap Heap Scan` | Index dipakai lalu heap fetch | Umum untuk banyak matching row |
| `Sort` besar | Sorting memory/temporary file | Cek index order atau `work_mem` |
| `Nested Loop` besar | Join berulang banyak kali | Cek cardinality dan index join key |
| `Hash Join` | Build hash table | Cek memory dan row count |
| Estimated rows jauh dari actual | Statistics/selectivity buruk | `ANALYZE`, extended stats, query rewrite |
| Banyak shared read blocks | Disk IO tinggi | Index/cache/query shape |
| `Rows Removed by Filter` besar | Banyak row dibaca lalu dibuang | Index predicate kurang tepat |
| High planning time | Query kompleks/dynamic | Simplify query atau prepared plan awareness |
| High execution time | Runtime bottleneck | Index, join, IO, lock, result volume |

---

## 14. Index Awareness

PostgreSQL menjelaskan index sebagai cara umum meningkatkan performa karena database bisa menemukan row lebih cepat dibanding tanpa index, tetapi index juga menambah overhead ke sistem sehingga harus digunakan secara bijak. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/indexes.html))

### 14.1 Query-driven index design

Mulai dari query:

```sql id="6zz4rz"
SELECT
    id,
    case_number,
    status,
    priority,
    created_at
FROM cases
WHERE assigned_team = ?
  AND status = ?
ORDER BY created_at DESC, id DESC
LIMIT 50;
```

Index yang masuk akal:

```sql id="b7l7qx"
CREATE INDEX cases_team_status_created_id_idx
ON cases (
    assigned_team,
    status,
    created_at DESC,
    id DESC
);
```

**Kenapa urutannya begitu?**

```text id="tn9gy7"
Equality predicates dulu:
  assigned_team, status

Lalu ordering/range:
  created_at DESC, id DESC

Tie-breaker:
  id DESC
```

---

## 15. Partial Index

Partial index adalah index atas subset row yang memenuhi predicate tertentu. PostgreSQL menyebut partial index berguna, misalnya untuk menghindari indexing value umum atau hanya mengindex bagian data yang sering dicari; tetapi predicate query harus cocok/terimplikasi oleh predicate index agar index bisa dipakai. PostgreSQL juga menekankan bahwa prepared query dengan parameter seperti `x < ?` tidak bisa secara umum mengimplikasikan predicate partial index seperti `x < 2`. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/indexes-partial.html))

### 15.1 Cocok untuk queue/outbox

```sql id="qbifxu"
CREATE INDEX outbox_pending_available_idx
ON outbox_events (available_at ASC, id ASC)
WHERE status = 'PENDING';
```

Query:

```sql id="s1g3kp"
SELECT
    id,
    payload
FROM outbox_events
WHERE status = 'PENDING'
  AND available_at <= now()
ORDER BY available_at ASC, id ASC
LIMIT 100
FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED;
```

### 15.2 Cocok untuk active case

```sql id="e4pcep"
CREATE INDEX cases_open_due_idx
ON cases (due_at ASC, priority DESC, id ASC)
WHERE status IN ('OPEN', 'IN_REVIEW');
```

**Warning:** jangan membuat banyak partial index seperti partition palsu per status/category. PostgreSQL sendiri memperingatkan bahwa banyak partial index non-overlap sering lebih buruk daripada satu composite index yang tepat. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/indexes-partial.html))

---

## 16. Index Decision Table

| Query Pattern | Index Candidate |
|---|---|
| `WHERE id = ?` | Primary key |
| `WHERE tenant_id = ? AND external_ref = ?` | Unique/composite `(tenant_id, external_ref)` |
| `WHERE status = ? ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT ?` | `(status, created_at DESC, id DESC)` |
| `WHERE assigned_team = ? AND status = ? ORDER BY due_at` | `(assigned_team, status, due_at, id)` |
| Pending outbox only | Partial index `WHERE status = 'PENDING'` |
| JSONB containment `payload @> ...` | GIN index on `payload` |
| Case-insensitive email lookup | Expression index on `lower(email)` |
| Time-series append-heavy | BRIN may be useful |
| Large text search | Full-text index, not plain B-tree |
| High-write table | Fewer carefully chosen indexes |

---

## 17. Avoid N+1 Query

### 17.1 Bad pattern

```java id="mr3img"
List<Order> orders = orderMapper.searchOrders(query);

for (Order order : orders) {
    List<OrderItem> items = orderItemMapper.findByOrderId(order.id());
    order.setItems(items);
}
```

Jika `orders` berisi 100 row, ini menjadi 101 query.

### 17.2 Better: DTO projection join

```xml id="cfeyu4"
<select id="searchOrderSummaries"
        resultType="com.company.app.dto.OrderSummaryDto">
  SELECT
    o.id AS id,
    o.order_number AS orderNumber,
    o.status AS status,
    c.name AS customerName,
    count(oi.id) AS itemCount
  FROM orders o
  JOIN customers c ON c.id = o.customer_id
  LEFT JOIN order_items oi ON oi.order_id = o.id
  WHERE o.status = #{status}
  GROUP BY o.id, o.order_number, o.status, c.name
  ORDER BY o.created_at DESC, o.id DESC
  LIMIT #{limit}
</select>
```

### 17.3 Better: two-query batch fetch

```java id="okh6j0"
List<Order> orders = orderMapper.searchOrders(query);
List<UUID> orderIds = orders.stream().map(Order::id).toList();

Map<UUID, List<OrderItem>> itemsByOrderId =
    orderItemMapper.findByOrderIds(orderIds).stream()
        .collect(Collectors.groupingBy(OrderItem::orderId));
```

```xml id="kylwks"
<select id="findByOrderIds" resultMap="OrderItemResultMap">
  SELECT
    id,
    order_id,
    product_id,
    quantity,
    unit_price
  FROM order_items
  WHERE order_id IN
  <foreach collection="orderIds"
           item="orderId"
           open="("
           separator=","
           close=")">
    #{orderId}
  </foreach>
  ORDER BY order_id ASC, id ASC
</select>
```

| Pattern | Query Count | Cocok Untuk |
|---|---:|---|
| N+1 lazy per parent | 1 + N | Hindari untuk list endpoint |
| Join projection | 1 | Summary/list/read model |
| Two-query batch fetch | 2 | Parent-child detail page |
| Nested resultMap join | 1 | Object graph terbatas |
| Separate endpoint | Banyak request | Kalau client memang lazy-load detail |

---

## 18. Projection Discipline

### Bad

```sql id="efc7rv"
SELECT *
FROM cases
WHERE status = 'OPEN';
```

### Good

```sql id="423cmd"
SELECT
    id,
    case_number,
    status,
    priority,
    due_at
FROM cases
WHERE status = 'OPEN'
ORDER BY due_at ASC, id ASC
LIMIT 50;
```

| Endpoint | Return Model | Query Strategy |
|---|---|---|
| List page | DTO projection | Ambil kolom yang ditampilkan |
| Detail page | Domain/detail DTO | Ambil object lengkap secukupnya |
| Export | Streaming DTO | Cursor/fetchSize |
| Count badge | Scalar | `count(*)` dengan filter/index |
| Existence check | Boolean | `SELECT EXISTS (...)` |
| State transition | Minimal domain state | Ambil status/version/owner saja |

---

## 19. Count Query Awareness

`count(*)` tidak selalu murah pada tabel besar dengan filter kompleks.

### Pattern: count hanya jika dibutuhkan

```text id="fte8kt"
Search API:
  return items + nextCursor

Admin/report API:
  optionally return totalCount

UI badge:
  cached/aggregated count jika high traffic
```

### Query

```xml id="fs8v7a"
<select id="countOpenCasesByTeam" resultType="long">
  SELECT count(*)
  FROM cases
  WHERE assigned_team = #{team}
    AND status = 'OPEN'
</select>
```

**Rule:** jangan otomatis menjalankan `count(*)` untuk setiap search endpoint jika UI hanya butuh “ada halaman berikutnya”. Untuk keyset pagination, ambil `limit + 1` row.

---

## 20. Keyset Pagination Performance

### Mapper XML

```xml id="cmus64"
<select id="findOpenCasesAfter" resultMap="CaseQueueItemResultMap">
  SELECT
    id,
    case_number,
    priority,
    due_at
  FROM cases
  WHERE status = 'OPEN'
    AND assigned_team = #{team}

  <if test="cursorDueAt != null and cursorId != null">
    AND (
      due_at &gt; #{cursorDueAt}
      OR (due_at = #{cursorDueAt} AND id &gt; #{cursorId})
    )
  </if>

  ORDER BY due_at ASC, id ASC
  LIMIT #{limit}
</select>
```

### Supporting index

```sql id="dxb89w"
CREATE INDEX cases_open_team_due_idx
ON cases (assigned_team, due_at ASC, id ASC)
WHERE status = 'OPEN';
```

| Offset Pagination | Keyset Pagination |
|---|---|
| Mudah untuk page number | Bagus untuk next/previous cursor |
| Lambat untuk offset besar | Stabil untuk data besar |
| Rentan duplicate/missing saat data berubah | Lebih stabil |
| Butuh `count(*)` untuk total page | Tidak perlu total count |
| Cocok admin kecil | Cocok high-volume feed/queue |

---

## 21. Connection Pooling

Untuk Jersey/JAX-RS non-Spring, MyBatis bisa memakai `DataSource` eksternal seperti HikariCP. HikariCP mendokumentasikan beberapa konfigurasi penting: `connectionTimeout` adalah waktu maksimum menunggu connection dari pool sebelum `SQLException`, default 30 detik; `maximumPoolSize` menentukan jumlah maksimum connection termasuk idle dan in-use; ketika pool penuh dan tidak ada idle connection, pemanggil akan block sampai `connectionTimeout`; `maxLifetime` sebaiknya diset beberapa detik lebih pendek dari batas connection yang diberlakukan database/infrastruktur. ([GitHub](https://github.com/brettwooldridge/hikaricp))

### 21.1 HikariCP DataSource factory

```java id="mkq7fu"
public final class DataSourceFactory {

    public static DataSource create() {
        HikariConfig config = new HikariConfig();

        config.setJdbcUrl(requiredEnv("DB_URL"));
        config.setUsername(requiredEnv("DB_USERNAME"));
        config.setPassword(requiredEnv("DB_PASSWORD"));

        config.setMaximumPoolSize(intEnv("DB_POOL_MAX_SIZE", 20));
        config.setMinimumIdle(intEnv("DB_POOL_MIN_IDLE", 20));
        config.setConnectionTimeout(longEnv("DB_POOL_CONNECTION_TIMEOUT_MS", 3000));
        config.setMaxLifetime(longEnv("DB_POOL_MAX_LIFETIME_MS", 1_740_000));
        config.setIdleTimeout(longEnv("DB_POOL_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS", 600_000));
        config.setPoolName("app-postgres-pool");

        return new HikariDataSource(config);
    }

    private static String requiredEnv(String name) {
        String value = System.getenv(name);
        if (value == null || value.isBlank()) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("Missing env: " + name);
        }
        return value;
    }

    private static int intEnv(String name, int defaultValue) {
        String value = System.getenv(name);
        return value == null ? defaultValue : Integer.parseInt(value);
    }

    private static long longEnv(String name, long defaultValue) {
        String value = System.getenv(name);
        return value == null ? defaultValue : Long.parseLong(value);
    }
}
```

### 21.2 MyBatis environment dengan DataSource programmatic

```java id="to3u19"
DataSource dataSource = DataSourceFactory.create();

TransactionFactory transactionFactory = new JdbcTransactionFactory();

Environment environment = new Environment(
    "production",
    transactionFactory,
    dataSource
);

Configuration configuration = new Configuration(environment);
configuration.addMapper(CaseMapper.class);
configuration.addMapper(AuditLogMapper.class);

SqlSessionFactory sqlSessionFactory =
    new SqlSessionFactoryBuilder().build(configuration);
```

---

## 22. Pool Sizing Mental Model

```text id="1nf9co"
JAX-RS worker threads != DB connections.

Kalau request CPU-bound:
  DB pool kecil bisa cukup.

Kalau request DB-bound:
  pool terlalu kecil -> queue di app.
  pool terlalu besar -> DB overload, lock contention, context switching.
```

| Signal | Kemungkinan Masalah | Tindakan |
|---|---|---|
| Banyak request menunggu connection | Pool terlalu kecil atau query terlalu lama | Cek pool wait time dan slow query |
| DB CPU 100% | Pool terlalu besar atau query berat | Kurangi concurrency / tune query |
| Connection timeout naik | Pool exhausted | Cari query lama, leak, transaction panjang |
| Idle connection tinggi | Pool terlalu besar | Turunkan max/min idle |
| P95 latency naik saat traffic naik | Saturasi DB/pool | Load test + capacity model |
| Export besar membuat API lambat | Cursor menahan connection | Pisahkan worker/export pool |

**Practical rule:** pool bukan solusi untuk query lambat. Membesarkan pool bisa memperparah database overload.

---

## 23. Timeout Layering

| Timeout | Layer | Tujuan |
|---|---|---|
| `connectionTimeout` | HikariCP | Maksimum menunggu connection dari pool |
| `statement_timeout` | PostgreSQL | Maksimum durasi statement |
| `lock_timeout` | PostgreSQL | Maksimum menunggu lock |
| HTTP server timeout | Jersey/container/proxy | Maksimum request lifecycle |
| Client timeout | Caller | Batas tunggu client |
| Worker lease timeout | Job/outbox | Mencegah job stuck |

### 23.1 Transaction-local timeout

```xml id="e36dfi"
<update id="setLocalStatementTimeout">
  SET LOCAL statement_timeout = #{timeout}
</update>
```

```xml id="p3i4km"
<update id="setLocalLockTimeout">
  SET LOCAL lock_timeout = #{timeout}
</update>
```

```java id="a6q8bq"
unitOfWork.transactional(ctx -> {
    RuntimeMapper runtime = ctx.mapper(RuntimeMapper.class);

    runtime.setLocalStatementTimeout("10s");
    runtime.setLocalLockTimeout("2s");

    // execute business operation

    return null;
});
```

---

## 24. MyBatis Local Cache Performance Awareness

MyBatis membuat local cache per session. Query yang sama dengan parameter sama dalam session yang sama bisa memakai local cache; cache dibersihkan saat `update`, `commit`, `rollback`, dan `close`. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/java-api.html))

| Session Pattern | Efek |
|---|---|
| Session pendek per use case | Cache kecil, predictable |
| Session panjang batch process | Cache bisa membesar |
| Query sama berulang dalam session | Bisa mengurangi DB hit |
| Object hasil query dimutasi | Bisa mencemari cache lokal |
| Banyak row besar dalam satu session | Memory pressure |

### Optional config

```xml id="dkpj7y"
<settings>
  <setting name="localCacheScope" value="STATEMENT"/>
</settings>
```

| `localCacheScope` | Dampak |
|---|---|
| `SESSION` | Default; cache sepanjang session |
| `STATEMENT` | Cache hanya per statement; lebih rendah risiko stale/memory |

---

## 25. Mapper XML Performance Options

### 25.1 `fetchSize`

```xml id="zvm0ge"
<select id="streamEvents"
        resultMap="OutboxEventResultMap"
        fetchSize="500">
  SELECT
    id,
    aggregate_type,
    aggregate_id,
    event_type,
    payload,
    created_at
  FROM outbox_events
  WHERE created_at &gt;= #{from}
  ORDER BY created_at ASC, id ASC
</select>
```

### 25.2 `timeout`

```xml id="f7hpyy"
<select id="findDashboardSummary"
        resultMap="DashboardSummaryResultMap"
        timeout="5">
  SELECT
    assigned_team,
    status,
    count(*) AS total
  FROM cases
  GROUP BY assigned_team, status
</select>
```

### 25.3 `useCache`

```xml id="6g1nw9"
<select id="findReferenceData"
        resultMap="ReferenceDataResultMap"
        useCache="true">
  SELECT
    code,
    label,
    sort_order
  FROM reference_data
  WHERE active = true
  ORDER BY sort_order ASC
</select>
```

**Warning:** second-level cache di microservice stateless/distributed harus dipakai sangat selektif. Reference data yang jarang berubah mungkin cocok; mutable workflow data biasanya tidak.

## 26. PostgreSQL Query Pattern: Queue Claim

### Query

```xml id="m9v0no"
<select id="claimPendingJobs" resultMap="JobResultMap">
  SELECT
    id,
    job_type,
    payload,
    attempt_count,
    available_at
  FROM jobs
  WHERE status = 'PENDING'
    AND available_at &lt;= now()
  ORDER BY available_at ASC, id ASC
  LIMIT #{limit}
  FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED
</select>
```

### Index

```sql id="wp067u"
CREATE INDEX jobs_pending_available_idx
ON jobs (available_at ASC, id ASC)
WHERE status = 'PENDING';
```

### Why it performs

```text id="f0p6re"
WHERE status = 'PENDING'
  -> matches partial index predicate

ORDER BY available_at, id
  -> matches index order

LIMIT
  -> can stop early
```

---

## 27. PostgreSQL Query Pattern: Dashboard Summary

### Bad for high-QPS dashboard

```sql id="c8rrwy"
SELECT
    status,
    count(*)
FROM cases
GROUP BY status;
```

Jika tabel sangat besar dan dashboard dipanggil sering, ini bisa mahal.

### Better options

| Strategy | Kapan |
|---|---|
| Query langsung + index | Data kecil/medium |
| Materialized view | Dashboard periodik |
| Summary table | High-QPS operational dashboard |
| Event-driven counter | Butuh low latency |
| Cache with TTL | Toleransi stale data |
| Partition-aware query | Data time-series besar |

### Summary table

```sql id="b97cxm"
CREATE TABLE case_status_summary (
    assigned_team text NOT NULL,
    status text NOT NULL,
    total bigint NOT NULL,
    updated_at timestamp with time zone NOT NULL,
    PRIMARY KEY (assigned_team, status)
);
```

---

## 28. Large Export Pattern

### Anti-pattern

```java id="r4aly3"
List<AuditLog> logs = mapper.findAllLogs(from, to);
return Response.ok(logs).build();
```

Masalah:

```text id="h40gdf"
- semua row masuk memory
- serialization besar
- response lama
- connection tertahan
- client disconnect bisa buang kerja besar
```

### Better: async export job

```text id="1a8pw5"
POST /exports/audit-logs
  -> create export job
  -> return 202 Accepted + job id

Worker:
  -> cursor stream from DB
  -> write file/object storage
  -> mark job completed

GET /exports/{id}
  -> status + download link
```

### Jika harus streaming HTTP

```java id="o617aw"
@GET
@Path("/audit-logs/export")
@Produces("text/csv")
public Response export(@QueryParam("from") Instant from,
                       @QueryParam("to") Instant to) {
    StreamingOutput output = stream -> {
        auditExportService.writeCsv(from, to, stream);
    };

    return Response.ok(output)
        .header("Content-Disposition", "attachment; filename=\"audit-logs.csv\"")
        .build();
}
```

**Caution:** HTTP streaming tetap menahan request thread/resource dan DB connection selama export. Untuk export besar/regulatory audit, async export job biasanya lebih defensible.

---

## 29. Observability untuk Performance

| Metric / Log | Sumber | Kenapa Penting |
|---|---|---|
| SQL duration per mapper method | App/MyBatis interceptor | Tahu query lambat |
| Rows returned | App instrumentation | Deteksi over-fetching |
| Connection acquire time | HikariCP metrics | Pool saturation |
| Active/idle/pending connections | HikariCP metrics | Sizing pool |
| Transaction duration | Service/unit-of-work | Lock duration |
| Deadlock/lock timeout count | DB/app exception | Contention |
| Statement timeout count | DB/app exception | Query runaway |
| Slow query log | PostgreSQL | Query nyata di production |
| EXPLAIN sampled query | Manual/diagnostic | Planner behavior |
| P95/P99 endpoint latency | API metrics | User impact |

### Mapper method timing wrapper

```java id="oexnx2"
public <T> T timed(String operationName, Supplier<T> supplier) {
    long startNanos = System.nanoTime();

    try {
        return supplier.get();
    } finally {
        long elapsedMillis =
            TimeUnit.NANOSECONDS.toMillis(System.nanoTime() - startNanos);

        metrics.timer("db.operation")
            .tag("operation", operationName)
            .record(elapsedMillis, TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS);
    }
}
```

---

## 30. Performance Investigation Playbook

```text id="9tm3ef"
1. Identify slow endpoint
2. Identify slow mapper/query
3. Capture SQL + parameters shape
4. Run EXPLAIN (ANALYZE, BUFFERS) in similar environment
5. Check row estimates vs actual rows
6. Check scan type and sort/join nodes
7. Check indexes and predicates
8. Check returned row count and projection size
9. Check transaction duration and locks
10. Check connection pool wait time
11. Apply one change
12. Re-measure
```

### Jangan langsung tambah index

Index membantu read, tetapi menambah overhead write dan storage. PostgreSQL sendiri menekankan bahwa index harus digunakan secara bijak karena ada overhead ke database system. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/indexes.html))

---

## 31. Common Performance Smells

| Smell | Gejala | Perbaikan |
|---|---|---|
| `SELECT *` | Network/memory besar | Projection eksplisit |
| N+1 query | Banyak query kecil | Join projection / batch fetch |
| Offset besar | Page jauh lambat | Keyset pagination |
| Count selalu jalan | Search endpoint lambat | Optional count / limit+1 |
| Missing deterministic order | Pagination tidak stabil | `ORDER BY created_at, id` |
| Query dynamic terlalu fleksibel | Index sulit cocok | Batasi filter/sort |
| Batch tanpa chunk | Memory/lock besar | Chunk 500/1000, ukur |
| Cursor untuk API high-QPS | Pool tertahan | Async export/job |
| Pool terlalu besar | DB overload | Sizing berdasarkan load test |
| Transaction panjang | Lock contention | Pendekkan transaction |
| External call dalam transaction | Lock lama | Outbox / after commit |
| Partial index tidak dipakai | Predicate tidak match | Cocokkan query predicate |
| JSONB filter tanpa index | Seq scan | GIN/expression index |
| ResultMap terlalu berat | Mapping CPU tinggi | DTO projection |
| Local cache membesar | Memory naik | Session pendek / `STATEMENT` |

---

## 32. Code Template - Performance-Oriented Search

### Criteria

```java id="o0f8rp"
public record CaseSearchQuery(
    String assignedTeam,
    String status,
    Instant createdFrom,
    Instant createdTo,
    UUID cursorId,
    Instant cursorCreatedAt,
    int limit
) {
    public CaseSearchQuery {
        if (limit <= 0 || limit > 200) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("limit must be between 1 and 200");
        }
    }

    public int limitPlusOne() {
        return limit + 1;
    }
}
```

### Mapper

```java id="x337l9"
List<CaseQueueItemDto> searchCases(@Param("query") CaseSearchQuery query);
```

### XML

```xml id="fqx9yp"
<select id="searchCases"
        resultType="com.company.app.dto.CaseQueueItemDto"
        timeout="5">
  SELECT
    id,
    case_number AS caseNumber,
    status,
    priority,
    created_at AS createdAt
  FROM cases

  <where>
    <if test="query.assignedTeam != null">
      assigned_team = #{query.assignedTeam}
    </if>

    <if test="query.status != null">
      AND status = #{query.status}
    </if>

    <if test="query.createdFrom != null">
      AND created_at &gt;= #{query.createdFrom}
    </if>

    <if test="query.createdTo != null">
      AND created_at &lt; #{query.createdTo}
    </if>

    <if test="query.cursorCreatedAt != null and query.cursorId != null">
      AND (
        created_at &lt; #{query.cursorCreatedAt}
        OR (created_at = #{query.cursorCreatedAt} AND id &lt; #{query.cursorId})
      )
    </if>
  </where>

  ORDER BY created_at DESC, id DESC
  LIMIT #{query.limitPlusOne}
</select>
```

### Supporting index candidate

```sql id="sl5jd6"
CREATE INDEX cases_team_status_created_idx
ON cases (
    assigned_team,
    status,
    created_at DESC,
    id DESC
);
```

---

## 33. Code Template - Batch Import

```java id="oujr8m"
public ImportResult importRows(List<ImportRow> rows) {
    ImportResult result = new ImportResult();

    for (List<ImportRow> chunk : chunks(rows, 500)) {
        unitOfWork.batchTransactional(ctx -> {
            ImportMapper mapper = ctx.mapper(ImportMapper.class);

            for (ImportRow row : chunk) {
                mapper.insertImportRow(row);
                result.incrementInserted();
            }

            return null;
        });
    }

    return result;
}

private static <T> List<List<T>> chunks(List<T> source, int size) {
    List<List<T>> chunks = new ArrayList<>();

    for (int start = 0; start < source.size(); start += size) {
        int end = Math.min(start + size, source.size());
        chunks.add(source.subList(start, end));
    }

    return chunks;
}
```

**Failure design:** kalau chunk ke-7 gagal, chunk 1-6 sudah commit. Ini cocok untuk import resumable. Kalau seluruh import harus atomic, jangan commit per chunk, tetapi risiko lock/rollback/memory akan jauh lebih besar.

---

## 34. Code Template - Streaming Export with Cursor

```java id="bwiice"
public void writeCsv(Instant from, Instant to, OutputStream outputStream) {
    unitOfWork.readOnly(ctx -> {
        AuditLogMapper mapper = ctx.mapper(AuditLogMapper.class);

        try (
            Writer writer = new BufferedWriter(
                new OutputStreamWriter(outputStream, StandardCharsets.UTF_8)
            );
            Cursor<AuditLog> cursor = mapper.streamLargeAuditLogs(from, to)
        ) {
            writer.write("id,aggregateType,aggregateId,action,createdAt\n");

            for (AuditLog log : cursor) {
                writer.write(toCsvLine(log));
            }

            writer.flush();
        } catch (IOException e) {
            throw new ExportFailedException(e);
        }

        return null;
    });
}
```

**Checklist cursor export:**

| Item | Harus |
|---|---:|
| `fetchSize` diset | Ya |
| Auto-commit off | Ya |
| Query single statement | Ya |
| Ordering deterministic | Ya |
| Session/cursor ditutup | Ya |
| Export punya timeout/limit | Ya |
| Connection pool impact dipantau | Ya |
| Untuk export besar, prefer async job | Ya |

---

## 35. Anti-Pattern

| Anti-Pattern | Contoh | Dampak |
|---|---|---|
| Load semua row ke `List` | Export jutaan audit log | OOM / GC pressure |
| Batch tanpa flush/chunk | 100k update satu session | Memory/lock besar |
| Pool size dinaikkan terus | `maximumPoolSize=200` | DB overload |
| Query tuning tanpa EXPLAIN | “Tambah index saja” | Bisa salah sasaran |
| Banyak index untuk semua filter | Index explosion | Write lambat |
| Partial index asal-asalan | Predicate tidak match | Index tidak dipakai |
| `SELECT *` di endpoint list | Ambil payload besar | Network dan mapping lambat |
| DTO list pakai domain aggregate penuh | Mapping berat | Latency tinggi |
| N+1 query | Loop panggil mapper | Round trip tinggi |
| Offset besar | `OFFSET 500000` | Lambat |
| Cursor di endpoint high traffic | Connection tertahan | Pool starvation |
| Long transaction untuk export | Cursor lama | Vacuum/lock/pool impact |
| External API call dalam transaction | Lock duration panjang | Contention |
| Tidak cek pool metrics | Blind tuning | Root cause kabur |

---

## 36. Production Checklist

| Checklist | Status Ideal |
|---|---|
| Semua query penting punya EXPLAIN baseline | Ya |
| Endpoint list memakai projection eksplisit | Ya |
| Pagination punya deterministic `ORDER BY` | Ya |
| High-volume list memakai keyset pagination | Ya |
| Search filter umum punya index yang cocok | Ya |
| Partial index hanya untuk workload stabil dan predicate cocok | Ya |
| Batch write memakai chunking | Ya |
| `ExecutorType.BATCH` hanya untuk operasi write massal | Ya |
| Cursor/fetchSize dipakai untuk export besar | Ya |
| Cursor tidak dipakai sembarang di high-QPS endpoint | Ya |
| Connection pool metrics aktif | Ya |
| Pool timeout lebih pendek dari HTTP timeout | Ya |
| Query/lock timeout dikonfigurasi | Ya |
| Transaction duration dipantau | Ya |
| Tidak ada external call di dalam DB transaction | Ya |
| Slow query log / SQL timing tersedia | Ya |
| Performance test memakai data volume realistis | Ya |

---

## 37. Decision Table

| Problem | Solusi Utama |
|---|---|
| Insert ribuan row | `ExecutorType.BATCH` atau multi-row insert chunked |
| Export jutaan row | Cursor + fetchSize atau async export job |
| List page lambat di page jauh | Keyset pagination |
| Query lambat tidak jelas | `EXPLAIN (ANALYZE, BUFFERS)` |
| Queue claim lambat | Partial index sesuai status pending |
| Dashboard sering lambat | Summary table/materialized view/cache |
| Banyak query per request | Join projection / batch fetch |
| Pool sering habis | Cari query lama + transaction panjang sebelum menaikkan pool |
| Memory naik saat query besar | Projection + cursor + chunking |
| Write lambat setelah tambah index | Review index overhead |
| JSONB filter lambat | GIN/expression index atau relationalize field |
| Batch error sulit dilacak | Per-chunk commit + import error table |

---

## 38. Roadmap Belajar Singkat

| Urutan | Materi | Target |
|---:|---|---|
| 1 | MyBatis `ExecutorType` | Tahu kapan pakai SIMPLE/REUSE/BATCH |
| 2 | Batch write + chunking | Bisa insert/update massal dengan aman |
| 3 | `fetchSize` dan Cursor | Bisa proses result besar tanpa OOM |
| 4 | Projection dan DTO query | Bisa mengurangi over-fetching |
| 5 | N+1 avoidance | Bisa menurunkan round trip |
| 6 | Keyset pagination | Bisa membuat list high-volume |
| 7 | PostgreSQL EXPLAIN | Bisa membaca plan dasar |
| 8 | Index design | Bisa membuat index berdasarkan query |
| 9 | Connection pool metrics | Bisa bedakan DB bottleneck vs pool bottleneck |
| 10 | Production observability | Bisa tune berdasarkan data nyata |

## Response:
7/13/2026, 2:28:51 AM

Lanjut ke observability dan production diagnostics: targetnya membuat setiap query MyBatis bisa ditelusuri dari HTTP request sampai PostgreSQL, dengan korelasi log, metric, trace, dan klasifikasi error yang jelas.

# Cheatsheet - Java MyBatis Observability, SQL Logging, Metrics, Tracing, Slow Query Investigation, Error Classification, dan Production Diagnostics di Jersey/JAX-RS

## 1. Introduction

Observability untuk **Jersey/JAX-RS + MyBatis + PostgreSQL** berarti kita bisa menjawab pertanyaan operasional seperti:

```text id="proa3c"
Request mana yang lambat?
Mapper method mana yang lambat?
SQL mana yang paling mahal?
Apakah lambat karena query plan, lock, pool, network, atau downstream?
Apakah error DB retryable atau business conflict?
Apakah satu user action bisa ditelusuri dari HTTP request sampai PostgreSQL?
```

MyBatis sendiri mendukung logging melalui log factory dan bisa diarahkan ke implementasi seperti **SLF4J, Log4j2, JDK logging, stdout logging, no logging**, atau custom implementation. Untuk production, biasanya gunakan SLF4J/Logback/Log4j2, bukan `STDOUT_LOGGING`, agar log level, format JSON, MDC, masking, dan shipping bisa dikontrol. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/logging.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 2. Mental Model Observability

```mermaid id="6ad7lg"
flowchart TD
    A[HTTP Request] --> B[Jersey Filter<br/>correlationId / traceId / user / tenant]
    B --> C[JAX-RS Resource]
    C --> D[Service Use Case]
    D --> E[Unit of Work]
    E --> F[MyBatis Mapper]
    F --> G[SQL Statement]
    G --> H[JDBC Driver]
    H --> I[HikariCP / DataSource]
    I --> J[(PostgreSQL)]

    B --> K[Structured Logs]
    D --> L[Business Metrics]
    E --> M[Transaction Metrics]
    F --> N[Mapper Timing Metrics]
    G --> O[SQL Logs / Slow Query Logs]
    H --> P[Trace Span: db call]
    I --> Q[Pool Metrics]
    J --> R[pg_stat_activity / pg_stat_statements]

    K --> S[Log Platform]
    L --> T[Metrics Platform]
    P --> U[Tracing Backend]
    R --> V[DB Diagnostics]
```

**Core invariant:**

```text id="ka5hjx"
Setiap request harus punya correlation id / trace id.

Setiap query penting harus bisa dikaitkan ke:
- endpoint
- service use case
- mapper method
- SQL statement id
- tenant/user/request context
- duration
- row count / affected row count
- SQLSTATE jika gagal
```

---

## 3. Decomposition Map

| Area | Fokus Praktis | Skill yang Dikuasai | Risiko Jika Tidak Ada |
|---|---|---|---|
| **Correlation ID** | Menghubungkan semua log dalam satu request | Jersey filter, MDC, response header | Debugging antar layer sulit |
| **Structured Logging** | Log machine-readable | JSON log, field standar, log level | Log tidak bisa dicari dengan baik |
| **MyBatis SQL Logging** | Melihat statement dan parameter saat perlu | `logImpl`, logger per mapper/package | SQL log terlalu noisy / bocor data |
| **Mapper Timing** | Mengukur durasi per mapper operation | Wrapper/interceptor/timer | Tidak tahu query mana lambat |
| **Transaction Metrics** | Melihat durasi unit-of-work | Commit/rollback duration | Lock lama tidak terlihat |
| **Connection Pool Metrics** | Melihat saturasi pool | active/idle/pending/acquire time | Salah menebak DB lambat padahal pool exhausted |
| **Tracing** | Melihat request path end-to-end | OpenTelemetry Java agent/manual spans | Latency breakdown tidak jelas |
| **Slow Query Investigation** | Mencari SQL mahal | app timing + DB stats + EXPLAIN | Optimasi berdasarkan feeling |
| **PostgreSQL Runtime Views** | Melihat DB sedang apa | `pg_stat_activity`, locks, wait event | Tidak tahu blocking/lock |
| **pg_stat_statements** | Menemukan query paling mahal historis | mean/max time, calls, rows, shared blocks | Hanya tahu satu incident, bukan pattern |
| **Error Classification** | Bedakan retryable vs business error | SQLSTATE, exception mapper | Retry salah / HTTP status salah |
| **Sensitive Data Masking** | Cegah PII/secrets bocor | Mask parameter, payload, headers | Compliance/security incident |
| **Alerting** | Deteksi sebelum user komplain | SLO, latency, error, pool saturation | Incident terlambat diketahui |
| **Runbook Diagnostics** | Langkah investigasi standar | playbook endpoint/query/DB | Setiap incident mulai dari nol |

---

## 4. Correlation ID di Jersey

### 4.1 Request filter

```java id="u9hb6t"
package com.company.app.observability;

import java.io.IOException;
import java.util.UUID;

import jakarta.annotation.Priority;
import jakarta.ws.rs.Priorities;
import jakarta.ws.rs.container.ContainerRequestContext;
import jakarta.ws.rs.container.ContainerRequestFilter;
import jakarta.ws.rs.container.ContainerResponseContext;
import jakarta.ws.rs.container.ContainerResponseFilter;
import jakarta.ws.rs.ext.Provider;

import org.slf4j.MDC;

@Provider
@Priority(Priorities.AUTHENTICATION)
public class CorrelationIdFilter
    implements ContainerRequestFilter, ContainerResponseFilter {

    public static final String HEADER = "X-Correlation-Id";
    public static final String MDC_KEY = "correlationId";

    @Override
    public void filter(ContainerRequestContext requestContext)
        throws IOException {

        String correlationId = requestContext.getHeaderString(HEADER);

        if (correlationId == null || correlationId.isBlank()) {
            correlationId = UUID.randomUUID().toString();
        }

        requestContext.setProperty(MDC_KEY, correlationId);
        MDC.put(MDC_KEY, correlationId);
    }

    @Override
    public void filter(ContainerRequestContext requestContext,
                       ContainerResponseContext responseContext)
        throws IOException {

        Object correlationId = requestContext.getProperty(MDC_KEY);

        if (correlationId != null) {
            responseContext.getHeaders().putSingle(HEADER, correlationId.toString());
        }

        MDC.remove(MDC_KEY);
    }
}
```

### 4.2 Minimal log pattern

```text id="t3uypw"
timestamp
level
service
environment
correlationId
traceId
spanId
tenantId
userId
http.method
http.path
http.status
operation
durationMs
error.code
```

**Rule:** jangan hanya log message string. Log harus punya field yang bisa difilter.

## 5. MyBatis SQL Logging

### 5.1 Konfigurasi `logImpl`

```xml id="gslzod"
<configuration>
  <settings>
    <setting name="logImpl" value="SLF4J"/>
  </settings>
</configuration>
```

Valid value resmi untuk `logImpl` mencakup `SLF4J`, `LOG4J`, `LOG4J2`, `JDK_LOGGING`, `COMMONS_LOGGING`, `STDOUT_LOGGING`, `NO_LOGGING`, atau fully-qualified custom implementation. Jika tidak dikonfigurasi, MyBatis akan autodiscover logging implementation. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/logging.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### 5.2 Logger level per mapper

Contoh Logback:

```xml id="b47x40"
<configuration>
  <appender name="CONSOLE" class="ch.qos.logback.core.ConsoleAppender">
    <encoder>
      <pattern>
        %d{yyyy-MM-dd'T'HH:mm:ss.SSSXXX} %-5level [%thread] [%X{correlationId}] %logger - %msg%n
      </pattern>
    </encoder>
  </appender>

  <logger name="com.company.app.mapper" level="DEBUG"/>

  <root level="INFO">
    <appender-ref ref="CONSOLE"/>
  </root>
</configuration>
```

### 5.3 Production guidance

| Environment | SQL Logging |
|---|---|
| Local dev | DEBUG mapper SQL boleh |
| Integration test | DEBUG selektif |
| Staging | DEBUG sementara untuk investigasi |
| Production normal | INFO/WARN; jangan log semua SQL |
| Production incident | Enable selektif, short window |
| Regulated/PII-heavy system | Masking wajib |

**Warning:** MyBatis SQL logging bisa menampilkan parameter. Untuk payload sensitif seperti token, PII, KYC data, customer profile, legal notes, atau enforcement detail, gunakan masking atau jangan aktifkan full SQL parameter log di production.

---

## 6. Structured Application Logging

### 6.1 Service-level log

```java id="7olewq"
public OrderResponse approveOrder(ApproveOrderCommand command) {
    log.info("Approve order started orderId={} actorId={}",
        command.orderId(),
        command.actorId()
    );

    try {
        OrderResponse response = unitOfWork.transactional(ctx -> {
            OrderMapper mapper = ctx.mapper(OrderMapper.class);

            int updated = mapper.transitionStatus(
                command.orderId(),
                "SUBMITTED",
                "APPROVED",
                command.version()
            );

            if (updated != 1) {
                throw new InvalidOrderTransitionException(command.orderId());
            }

            mapper.insertAuditLog(...);
            mapper.insertOutboxEvent(...);

            return OrderResponse.approved(command.orderId());
        });

        log.info("Approve order completed orderId={}", command.orderId());
        return response;

    } catch (RuntimeException e) {
        log.warn("Approve order failed orderId={} errorType={}",
            command.orderId(),
            e.getClass().getSimpleName(),
            e
        );
        throw e;
    }
}
```

### 6.2 Apa yang dilog?

| Event | Level | Field Minimum |
|---|---|---|
| Request selesai | INFO | method, path, status, duration |
| Use case dimulai | DEBUG/INFO | operation, aggregate id |
| Use case berhasil | INFO | operation, aggregate id, duration |
| Business conflict | WARN | operation, code, aggregate id |
| Retry transient DB | WARN | sqlState, attempt, operation |
| Unexpected DB failure | ERROR | sqlState, mapper, operation |
| Slow query | WARN | mapperId, duration, threshold |
| Security/auth failure | WARN | user/tenant context terbatas |
| Validation failure | INFO/WARN | error code, field summary |

**Rule:** log yang baik membantu investigasi tanpa perlu membuka payload mentah.

---

## 7. Mapper Timing Metrics

MyBatis logging memberi visibility, tetapi untuk dashboard production perlu metric.

### 7.1 Manual timing di repository/service

```java id="j23fk0"
public final class TimedMapperExecutor {

    private final MeterRegistry meterRegistry;

    public TimedMapperExecutor(MeterRegistry meterRegistry) {
        this.meterRegistry = meterRegistry;
    }

    public <T> T record(String mapperId, Supplier<T> supplier) {
        Timer.Sample sample = Timer.start(meterRegistry);

        try {
            return supplier.get();
        } finally {
            sample.stop(
                Timer.builder("db.mybatis.mapper.duration")
                    .tag("mapper", mapperId)
                    .register(meterRegistry)
            );
        }
    }
}
```

Usage:

```java id="e5j2fl"
Order order = timed.record(
    "OrderMapper.findById",
    () -> mapper.findById(orderId)
);
```

Micrometer mendeskripsikan metric sebagai data untuk mengamati, memberi alert, dan bereaksi terhadap kondisi operasional saat ini atau baru-baru ini. Ia biasa dipakai sebagai facade metric menuju backend seperti Prometheus, OTLP, Datadog, dan lain-lain. ([Micrometer Application Observability](https://micrometer.io/docs/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 8. MyBatis Interceptor untuk Timing

Jika ingin otomatis per statement, gunakan plugin interceptor.

### 8.1 Interceptor skeleton

```java id="ojjzo8"
package com.company.app.observability;

import java.util.Properties;
import java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit;

import org.apache.ibatis.executor.Executor;
import org.apache.ibatis.mapping.MappedStatement;
import org.apache.ibatis.plugin.Interceptor;
import org.apache.ibatis.plugin.Invocation;
import org.apache.ibatis.plugin.Intercepts;
import org.apache.ibatis.plugin.Plugin;
import org.apache.ibatis.plugin.Signature;

import io.micrometer.core.instrument.MeterRegistry;
import io.micrometer.core.instrument.Timer;

@Intercepts({
    @Signature(
        type = Executor.class,
        method = "update",
        args = {MappedStatement.class, Object.class}
    ),
    @Signature(
        type = Executor.class,
        method = "query",
        args = {
            MappedStatement.class,
            Object.class,
            org.apache.ibatis.session.RowBounds.class,
            org.apache.ibatis.session.ResultHandler.class
        }
    )
})
public class MyBatisMetricsInterceptor implements Interceptor {

    private final MeterRegistry meterRegistry;

    public MyBatisMetricsInterceptor(MeterRegistry meterRegistry) {
        this.meterRegistry = meterRegistry;
    }

    @Override
    public Object intercept(Invocation invocation) throws Throwable {
        MappedStatement mappedStatement =
            (MappedStatement) invocation.getArgs()[0];

        String statementId = mappedStatement.getId();
        String commandType = mappedStatement.getSqlCommandType().name();

        long startNanos = System.nanoTime();

        try {
            return invocation.proceed();
        } finally {
            long durationNanos = System.nanoTime() - startNanos;

            Timer.builder("db.mybatis.statement.duration")
                .tag("statement", statementId)
                .tag("command", commandType)
                .register(meterRegistry)
                .record(durationNanos, TimeUnit.NANOSECONDS);
        }
    }

    @Override
    public Object plugin(Object target) {
        return Plugin.wrap(target, this);
    }

    @Override
    public void setProperties(Properties properties) {
        // no-op
    }
}
```

### 8.2 Registration programmatic

```java id="qlicg6"
Configuration configuration = new Configuration(environment);
configuration.addInterceptor(new MyBatisMetricsInterceptor(meterRegistry));
```

### 8.3 Metric cardinality warning

| Tag | Aman? | Catatan |
|---|---:|---|
| `statement=OrderMapper.findById` | Ya | Bounded cardinality |
| `command=SELECT` | Ya | Bounded |
| `tenantId` | Hati-hati | Bisa banyak |
| `userId` | Tidak untuk metric tag | High cardinality |
| raw SQL | Tidak | High cardinality dan sensitif |
| parameter value | Tidak | PII + high cardinality |
| endpoint path template | Ya | `/orders/{id}`, bukan `/orders/123` |

---

## 9. Tracing dengan OpenTelemetry

OpenTelemetry menyediakan API, SDK, dan agent untuk telemetry seperti traces, metrics, dan logs. Java zero-code instrumentation menggunakan Java agent yang dapat menangkap telemetry dari banyak library/framework tanpa perubahan kode, termasuk edge seperti inbound request, outbound HTTP, dan database call. ([OpenTelemetry](https://opentelemetry.io/docs/languages/java/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### 9.1 JVM args

```bash id="xvtr9x"
java \
  -javaagent:/opt/opentelemetry-javaagent.jar \
  -Dotel.service.name=order-service \
  -Dotel.resource.attributes=deployment.environment=prod,service.version=1.42.0 \
  -Dotel.exporter.otlp.endpoint=http://otel-collector:4317 \
  -jar app.jar
```

### 9.2 Environment variables

```bash id="22sndj"
OTEL_SERVICE_NAME=order-service
OTEL_RESOURCE_ATTRIBUTES=deployment.environment=prod,service.version=1.42.0
OTEL_EXPORTER_OTLP_ENDPOINT=http://otel-collector:4317
```

### 9.3 Manual span untuk use case

```java id="xjuson"
public OrderResponse approveOrder(ApproveOrderCommand command) {
    Span span = tracer.spanBuilder("OrderService.approveOrder")
        .setAttribute("order.id", command.orderId().toString())
        .setAttribute("operation", "approveOrder")
        .startSpan();

    try (Scope scope = span.makeCurrent()) {
        return doApproveOrder(command);
    } catch (RuntimeException e) {
        span.recordException(e);
        span.setStatus(StatusCode.ERROR);
        throw e;
    } finally {
        span.end();
    }
}
```

### 9.4 Trace hierarchy ideal

```text id="hgyx67"
HTTP POST /orders/{id}/approve
  -> OrderResource.approve
    -> OrderService.approveOrder
      -> MyBatis transaction
        -> OrderMapper.findById
        -> OrderMapper.transitionStatus
        -> AuditLogMapper.insert
        -> OutboxMapper.insert
```

**Practical rule:** tracing bagus untuk melihat latency breakdown, tetapi jangan menaruh data sensitif di span attributes.

---

## 10. Connection Pool Metrics

Untuk service yang memakai HikariCP/DataSource, metric pool sangat penting karena query lambat dan pool starvation sering terlihat mirip dari sisi endpoint. Minimal pantau:

| Metric | Artinya | Indikasi |
|---|---|---|
| Active connections | Connection sedang dipakai | Tinggi terus = saturation |
| Idle connections | Connection siap pakai | Nol terus = pressure |
| Pending threads | Thread menunggu connection | Pool bottleneck |
| Connection acquire time | Waktu ambil connection | Naik = pool exhausted/query lama |
| Connection usage time | Lama connection dipakai | Transaction/query lama |
| Timeout acquiring connection | Gagal dapat connection | User-facing failure |
| Max pool size | Kapasitas pool | Capacity limit |

### 10.1 Hikari config metric registry

```java id="ll8rrb"
HikariConfig config = new HikariConfig();

config.setJdbcUrl(System.getenv("DB_URL"));
config.setUsername(System.getenv("DB_USERNAME"));
config.setPassword(System.getenv("DB_PASSWORD"));
config.setMaximumPoolSize(20);
config.setPoolName("order-service-postgres");

// depending on your Micrometer/Hikari integration version
config.setMetricRegistry(meterRegistry);

DataSource dataSource = new HikariDataSource(config);
```

**Catatan:** integrasi detail HikariCP-Micrometer bisa berbeda tergantung versi library dan framework. Di aplikasi non-Spring, validasi API yang tersedia pada versi HikariCP/Micrometer yang dipakai project; di Spring Boot, banyak metric Hikari biasanya sudah di-auto-configure oleh Actuator/Micrometer. ([Home](https://docs.spring.io/spring-boot/reference/actuator/metrics.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 11. PostgreSQL Runtime Diagnostics

PostgreSQL menyediakan chapter monitoring untuk melihat aktivitas database, termasuk view statistik. `pg_stat_activity` berisi satu row per server process dan menunjukkan informasi aktivitas connection saat ini. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/monitoring.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### 11.1 Active query saat ini

```sql id="nahmzi"
SELECT
    pid,
    usename,
    application_name,
    client_addr,
    state,
    wait_event_type,
    wait_event,
    now() - query_start AS query_age,
    now() - xact_start AS transaction_age,
    query
FROM pg_stat_activity
WHERE datname = current_database()
ORDER BY query_age DESC;
```

### 11.2 Query yang sedang menunggu lock

```sql id="0qgxpe"
SELECT
    pid,
    usename,
    application_name,
    wait_event_type,
    wait_event,
    now() - query_start AS waiting_for,
    query
FROM pg_stat_activity
WHERE wait_event_type = 'Lock'
ORDER BY waiting_for DESC;
```

### 11.3 Long transaction

```sql id="o1zo7g"
SELECT
    pid,
    usename,
    application_name,
    state,
    now() - xact_start AS transaction_age,
    query
FROM pg_stat_activity
WHERE xact_start IS NOT NULL
ORDER BY transaction_age DESC;
```

**Interpretasi praktis:**

| Signal | Kemungkinan |
|---|---|
| `state = active` lama | Query lambat / lock wait |
| `state = idle in transaction` | App membuka transaksi lalu tidak commit/rollback |
| `wait_event_type = Lock` | Blocking transaction |
| `transaction_age` besar | Transaksi panjang, bisa ganggu vacuum/lock |
| Banyak connection app idle | Pool terlalu besar atau traffic turun |
| Banyak active query sama | Endpoint hot / N+1 / retry storm |

---

## 12. pg_stat_statements

`pg_stat_statements` adalah extension PostgreSQL yang melacak statistik planning dan execution untuk semua SQL statement yang dijalankan server. Ini sangat berguna untuk melihat query paling mahal secara agregat, bukan hanya query yang kebetulan sedang berjalan saat dicek. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/pgstatstatements.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### 12.1 Query paling mahal total time

```sql id="m10zdv"
SELECT
    queryid,
    calls,
    total_exec_time,
    mean_exec_time,
    max_exec_time,
    rows,
    shared_blks_hit,
    shared_blks_read,
    query
FROM pg_stat_statements
ORDER BY total_exec_time DESC
LIMIT 20;
```

### 12.2 Query paling lambat rata-rata

```sql id="7ef5kp"
SELECT
    queryid,
    calls,
    mean_exec_time,
    max_exec_time,
    rows,
    query
FROM pg_stat_statements
WHERE calls >= 10
ORDER BY mean_exec_time DESC
LIMIT 20;
```

### 12.3 Query dengan IO tinggi

```sql id="2a3rx6"
SELECT
    queryid,
    calls,
    shared_blks_read,
    shared_blks_hit,
    mean_exec_time,
    query
FROM pg_stat_statements
ORDER BY shared_blks_read DESC
LIMIT 20;
```

**Warning:** `pg_stat_statements` menormalisasi query, jadi parameter value tidak terlihat. Itu bagus untuk security dan agregasi, tetapi untuk reproduce issue tetap butuh contoh parameter dari log/app context yang aman.

---

## 13. SQL Comment untuk Traceability

Untuk menghubungkan query di PostgreSQL dengan mapper/use case, salah satu pattern adalah SQL comment.

```xml id="fxmxtf"
<select id="findById" resultMap="OrderResultMap">
  /* app=order-service mapper=OrderMapper.findById */
  SELECT
    id,
    customer_id,
    status,
    version,
    created_at
  FROM orders
  WHERE id = #{id}
</select>
```

### Decision

| Benefit | Risk |
|---|---|
| Mudah terlihat di `pg_stat_activity` | Bisa memengaruhi query normalization/aggregation jika comment terlalu dinamis |
| Membantu DBA tahu source query | Jangan isi userId/orderId raw |
| Bisa korelasi mapper ↔ DB | Gunakan comment statis |

**Rule:** comment boleh berisi mapper/use case statis, bukan parameter dinamis.

---

## 14. Slow Query Logging di Aplikasi

### 14.1 Interceptor slow query

```java id="3w4qxf"
public class SlowQueryLoggingInterceptor implements Interceptor {

    private static final long SLOW_QUERY_THRESHOLD_MS = 500;

    @Override
    public Object intercept(Invocation invocation) throws Throwable {
        MappedStatement mappedStatement =
            (MappedStatement) invocation.getArgs()[0];

        long start = System.nanoTime();

        try {
            return invocation.proceed();
        } finally {
            long durationMs = TimeUnit.NANOSECONDS.toMillis(
                System.nanoTime() - start
            );

            if (durationMs >= SLOW_QUERY_THRESHOLD_MS) {
                log.warn(
                    "Slow MyBatis statement statementId={} command={} durationMs={}",
                    mappedStatement.getId(),
                    mappedStatement.getSqlCommandType(),
                    durationMs
                );
            }
        }
    }
}
```

### 14.2 Threshold guideline

| Query Type | Initial Warning Threshold |
|---|---:|
| By primary key | 50-100 ms |
| Search/list page | 300-500 ms |
| Dashboard aggregate | 500-1000 ms |
| Batch chunk | 1-5 s |
| Export streaming | Track separately |
| Queue claim | 100-300 ms |

Threshold harus berbasis SLO dan baseline service, bukan angka universal.

---

## 15. Error Classification dengan SQLSTATE

### 15.1 SQLSTATE classifier

```java id="n1hw6f"
public enum PersistenceErrorKind {
    UNIQUE_VIOLATION,
    FOREIGN_KEY_VIOLATION,
    CHECK_VIOLATION,
    NOT_NULL_VIOLATION,
    DEADLOCK,
    SERIALIZATION_FAILURE,
    LOCK_NOT_AVAILABLE,
    QUERY_CANCELED,
    CONNECTION_FAILURE,
    UNKNOWN
}
```

```java id="4r17n7"
public final class PostgresErrorClassifier {

    public static PersistenceErrorKind classify(Throwable throwable) {
        SQLException sqlException = findSqlException(throwable);

        if (sqlException == null) {
            return PersistenceErrorKind.UNKNOWN;
        }

        return switch (sqlException.getSQLState()) {
            case "23505" -> PersistenceErrorKind.UNIQUE_VIOLATION;
            case "23503" -> PersistenceErrorKind.FOREIGN_KEY_VIOLATION;
            case "23514" -> PersistenceErrorKind.CHECK_VIOLATION;
            case "23502" -> PersistenceErrorKind.NOT_NULL_VIOLATION;
            case "40P01" -> PersistenceErrorKind.DEADLOCK;
            case "40001" -> PersistenceErrorKind.SERIALIZATION_FAILURE;
            case "55P03" -> PersistenceErrorKind.LOCK_NOT_AVAILABLE;
            case "57014" -> PersistenceErrorKind.QUERY_CANCELED;
            case "08000", "08003", "08006", "08001" ->
                PersistenceErrorKind.CONNECTION_FAILURE;
            default -> PersistenceErrorKind.UNKNOWN;
        };
    }

    private static SQLException findSqlException(Throwable throwable) {
        Throwable current = throwable;

        while (current != null) {
            if (current instanceof SQLException sqlException) {
                return sqlException;
            }
            current = current.getCause();
        }

        return null;
    }
}
```

PostgreSQL mendokumentasikan bahwa transaksi pada isolation level tertentu dapat gagal dengan `serialization_failure` SQLSTATE `40001`, deadlock dengan `40P01`, dan bahwa aplikasi perlu siap mengulang seluruh transaksi untuk kondisi transient tertentu. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/monitoring-stats.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 16. HTTP Error Mapping

| Persistence Condition | SQLSTATE | HTTP | Retry? | Meaning |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---|
| Unique violation | `23505` | `409 Conflict` | Tidak otomatis | Duplicate business key |
| FK violation | `23503` | `409` / `400` | Tidak | Referensi invalid |
| Check violation | `23514` | `400` / `409` | Tidak | Domain constraint gagal |
| Not null violation | `23502` | `500` atau `400` | Tidak | Bug mapping atau input invalid |
| Serialization failure | `40001` | `503` setelah retry gagal | Ya | Transient concurrency |
| Deadlock | `40P01` | `503` setelah retry gagal | Ya | Transient lock cycle |
| Lock not available | `55P03` | `409` / `503` | Bisa | Resource busy |
| Query canceled / timeout | `57014` | `503` / `504` | Bisa | Timeout/cancel |
| Connection failure | `08xxx` | `503` | Bisa | DB connectivity |

### ExceptionMapper

```java id="fzov24"
@Provider
public class PersistenceExceptionMapper
    implements ExceptionMapper<PersistenceException> {

    @Override
    public Response toResponse(PersistenceException exception) {
        PersistenceErrorKind kind =
            PostgresErrorClassifier.classify(exception);

        return switch (kind) {
            case UNIQUE_VIOLATION -> conflict("DUPLICATE_RESOURCE");
            case FOREIGN_KEY_VIOLATION -> conflict("INVALID_REFERENCE");
            case CHECK_VIOLATION -> conflict("CONSTRAINT_VIOLATION");
            case DEADLOCK, SERIALIZATION_FAILURE, CONNECTION_FAILURE ->
                unavailable("PERSISTENCE_TRANSIENT_FAILURE");
            case LOCK_NOT_AVAILABLE, QUERY_CANCELED ->
                unavailable("PERSISTENCE_TIMEOUT_OR_BUSY");
            default -> serverError("PERSISTENCE_FAILURE");
        };
    }

    private Response conflict(String code) {
        return Response.status(Response.Status.CONFLICT)
            .entity(ApiError.of(code, "Request conflicts with current state."))
            .build();
    }

    private Response unavailable(String code) {
        return Response.status(Response.Status.SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE)
            .entity(ApiError.of(code, "Temporary persistence failure. Retry later."))
            .build();
    }

    private Response serverError(String code) {
        return Response.status(Response.Status.INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR)
            .entity(ApiError.of(code, "Unexpected persistence failure."))
            .build();
    }
}
```

---

## 17. Retry Observability

Retry harus terlihat, bukan diam-diam.

```java id="0e7rhl"
public <T> T retryableTransaction(String operation, MyBatisWork<T> work) {
    int maxAttempts = 3;

    for (int attempt = 1; attempt <= maxAttempts; attempt++) {
        try {
            return unitOfWork.transactional(work);
        } catch (RuntimeException e) {
            PersistenceErrorKind kind = PostgresErrorClassifier.classify(e);

            if (!isRetryable(kind) || attempt == maxAttempts) {
                throw e;
            }

            log.warn(
                "Retrying persistence transaction operation={} attempt={} errorKind={}",
                operation,
                attempt,
                kind,
                e
            );

            meterRegistry.counter(
                "db.transaction.retry",
                "operation", operation,
                "errorKind", kind.name()
            ).increment();

            sleep(backoffMillis(attempt));
        }
    }

    throw new IllegalStateException("unreachable");
}
```

| Retry Signal | Kenapa Penting |
|---|---|
| retry count by operation | Deteksi contention |
| retry final failure | SLO impact |
| SQLSTATE / error kind | Root cause |
| attempt number | Apakah retry efektif |
| latency including retry | User impact |
| idempotency context | Cegah duplicate side effect |

---

## 18. Sensitive Data Masking

### Jangan log mentah

```text id="spk8pv"
password
token
authorization header
session id
KYC data
PII
customer address
legal notes
enforcement evidence
full JSONB payload
payment data
```

### Masking helper

```java id="p2qlp4"
public final class LogMasking {

    private LogMasking() {}

    public static String maskEmail(String email) {
        if (email == null || !email.contains("@")) {
            return "***";
        }

        String[] parts = email.split("@", 2);
        String local = parts[0];

        if (local.length() <= 2) {
            return "***@" + parts[1];
        }

        return local.charAt(0) + "***" + local.charAt(local.length() - 1)
            + "@" + parts[1];
    }

    public static String maskToken(String token) {
        if (token == null || token.length() < 8) {
            return "***";
        }

        return token.substring(0, 4) + "..." + token.substring(token.length() - 4);
    }
}
```

### Observability rule

```text id="8nvjzg"
Log identifiers, not payloads.

Prefer:
  orderId, caseId, tenantId, operation, status, errorCode

Avoid:
  full request body, full DB row, full JSONB payload, raw token
```

---

## 19. Diagnostic Playbook - Endpoint Lambat

```text id="x691gu"
1. Cek API latency: p50/p95/p99 endpoint.
2. Cek correlationId / trace untuk request lambat.
3. Lihat span breakdown: resource, service, DB, external call.
4. Cek mapper statement duration.
5. Cek connection acquire time.
6. Cek PostgreSQL pg_stat_activity saat lambat.
7. Cek pg_stat_statements untuk query agregat.
8. Ambil SQL shape dan parameter representatif.
9. Jalankan EXPLAIN (ANALYZE, BUFFERS) di environment aman.
10. Cek row estimate, scan type, sort, join, blocks read.
11. Cek lock/wait event.
12. Cek apakah regression berasal dari release/migration/index.
```

### Decision table

| Temuan | Kemungkinan Root Cause |
|---|---|
| Connection acquire time tinggi | Pool exhausted / connection leak / query lama |
| DB span tinggi tapi acquire normal | Query/lock lambat |
| `pg_stat_activity` wait lock | Blocking transaction |
| `idle in transaction` | App tidak commit/rollback tepat waktu |
| `pg_stat_statements` calls sangat tinggi | Hot endpoint / N+1 / polling |
| mean time kecil, max time besar | Spike lock/IO/plan variance |
| rows returned besar | Over-fetching |
| shared blocks read tinggi | IO / cache miss / missing index |
| endpoint p99 tinggi tapi DB normal | App CPU/serialization/downstream |

---

## 20. Diagnostic Playbook - Deadlock / Serialization Failure

```text id="hy0nxp"
1. Ambil SQLSTATE dari exception.
2. Cek operation dan mapper statement.
3. Cek apakah retry terjadi dan berhasil.
4. Cek lock ordering di code path terkait.
5. Cek apakah transaksi terlalu panjang.
6. Cek apakah external call dilakukan dalam transaction.
7. Cek pg_stat_activity / logs untuk blocking pattern.
8. Tambahkan deterministic lock ordering jika multi-row update.
9. Pastikan retry mengulang full unit-of-work.
10. Tambahkan metric retry/deadlock per operation.
```

| SQLSTATE | Meaning | Action |
|---|---|---|
| `40P01` | Deadlock detected | Retry + fix lock ordering |
| `40001` | Serialization failure | Retry full transaction |
| `55P03` | Lock not available | Retry/backoff atau return busy |
| `57014` | Query canceled | Cek statement timeout/query plan |
| `23505` | Unique violation | Treat as duplicate/idempotency conflict |

---

## 21. Diagnostic Playbook - Pool Exhaustion

```text id="s2o2wu"
1. Cek pending connection threads.
2. Cek active connections vs max pool.
3. Cek connection usage time.
4. Cek long transaction.
5. Cek slow query.
6. Cek cursor/export yang menahan connection.
7. Cek connection leak.
8. Cek traffic spike/retry storm.
9. Jangan langsung menaikkan pool sebelum tahu root cause.
```

| Signal | Interpretasi |
|---|---|
| Active=max, pending naik | Pool saturated |
| Idle=0 terus | DB concurrency pressure |
| Usage time tinggi | Transaction/query lama |
| Acquire timeout terjadi | User-facing failure |
| DB CPU rendah tapi pool penuh | Connection leak / waiting lock / long transaction |
| DB CPU tinggi dan pool penuh | DB overloaded |

---

## 22. Alerting Matrix

| Alert | Threshold Awal | Severity |
|---|---:|---|
| API p95 latency > SLO | 5-10 menit | Warning/Critical |
| API 5xx rate naik | 5 menit | Critical |
| DB transaction retry rate naik | baseline + anomaly | Warning |
| Deadlock count > 0 berulang | 5-15 menit | Warning |
| Connection acquire timeout > 0 | immediate | Critical |
| Hikari pending threads > 0 terus | 5 menit | Warning |
| Active connections near max | 10 menit | Warning |
| `idle in transaction` lama | > 1-5 menit | Warning |
| Slow query count naik | baseline + anomaly | Warning |
| Query timeout count > 0 | 5 menit | Warning/Critical |
| Outbox pending backlog naik | SLO dependent | Warning |
| Queue claim empty padahal backlog ada | immediate | Critical |

---

## 23. Dashboard Layout

```text id="7xvi5s"
Service Overview
  - request rate
  - error rate
  - latency p50/p95/p99
  - top endpoints by latency

Persistence Overview
  - mapper duration p95/p99
  - top slow mapper statements
  - DB transaction duration
  - retry count by SQLSTATE
  - affected row conflict count

Connection Pool
  - active / idle / pending
  - acquire duration
  - usage duration
  - timeout count

PostgreSQL
  - active sessions
  - lock waits
  - long transactions
  - pg_stat_statements top total time
  - pg_stat_statements top mean time
  - slow query log count

Business Workflow
  - command success/failure by operation
  - invalid transition count
  - idempotency replay/conflict count
  - outbox pending/processing/failed
```
