## 24. Mapper Statement Naming Convention

| Mapper Method | Statement ID | Good Metric Tag |
|---|---|---|
| `OrderMapper.findById` | `com.company.app.mapper.OrderMapper.findById` | `OrderMapper.findById` |
| `OrderMapper.transitionStatus` | full id | `OrderMapper.transitionStatus` |
| `OutboxMapper.claimPendingEvents` | full id | `OutboxMapper.claimPendingEvents` |
| `CaseMapper.findForEscalation` | full id | `CaseMapper.findForEscalation` |

### Helper

```java id="6pqafx"
public final class MapperStatementNames {

    private MapperStatementNames() {}

    public static String shortName(String statementId) {
        int mapperStart = statementId.lastIndexOf('.', statementId.lastIndexOf('.') - 1);
        if (mapperStart < 0) {
            return statementId;
        }
        return statementId.substring(mapperStart + 1);
    }
}
```

**Rule:** metric tag harus bounded. `OrderMapper.findById` aman; raw SQL atau ID user tidak aman.

---

## 25. MyBatis + PostgreSQL Observability Anti-Pattern

| Anti-Pattern | Dampak |
|---|---|
| Tidak ada correlation ID | Log antar layer tidak tersambung |
| SQL log production selalu DEBUG | Noise, storage mahal, data sensitif bocor |
| Metric tag pakai userId/orderId | Cardinality explosion |
| Hanya pantau API latency | Tidak tahu bottleneck DB/pool |
| Hanya pantau DB CPU | Tidak tahu pool/app problem |
| Tidak cek affected row conflict | Lost update/invalid transition tidak terlihat |
| Retry tanpa metric | Contention tersembunyi |
| Error DB jadi HTTP 500 semua | Client tidak tahu conflict vs transient |
| Log full JSONB payload | PII/compliance risk |
| Trace span berisi payload sensitif | Data leakage di tracing backend |
| `pg_stat_activity` tidak pernah dicek | Lock/idle transaction telat ketahuan |
| Tidak punya slow query baseline | Sulit tahu regression |
| Semua query diberi dynamic SQL comment | Query aggregation kacau |
| Dashboard terlalu banyak panel | Tidak actionable |

---

## 26. Production Checklist

| Checklist | Status Ideal |
|---|---|
| Correlation ID dibuat/diteruskan untuk semua request | Ya |
| Correlation ID dikembalikan di response header | Ya |
| MDC dibersihkan setelah request | Ya |
| Log format structured/JSON-ready | Ya |
| MyBatis `logImpl` diarahkan ke SLF4J/Log4j2 | Ya |
| SQL DEBUG production default off | Ya |
| Slow mapper statement log tersedia | Ya |
| Mapper timing metric tersedia | Ya |
| Transaction duration metric tersedia | Ya |
| Hikari/DataSource pool metric tersedia | Ya |
| OpenTelemetry trace tersedia untuk HTTP dan DB | Ya |
| Metric tags bounded cardinality | Ya |
| SQLSTATE diklasifikasi | Ya |
| Retry punya log + metric | Ya |
| ExceptionMapper membedakan conflict/transient/fatal | Ya |
| PII/secrets dimasking | Ya |
| `pg_stat_activity` query runbook tersedia | Ya |
| `pg_stat_statements` dipakai untuk query aggregate diagnostics | Ya |
| Alert untuk pool exhaustion, slow query, deadlock, retries tersedia | Ya |
| Dashboard punya service, DB, pool, business workflow view | Ya |

---

## 27. Decision Table

| Problem | Observability Pattern |
|---|---|
| Request lambat sporadis | Trace + correlation ID + mapper timing |
| Semua endpoint lambat | Pool metrics + DB CPU + pg_stat_activity |
| Query tertentu lambat | MyBatis statement timer + pg_stat_statements |
| Lock contention | pg_stat_activity wait_event + retry/deadlock metrics |
| Lost update sering | affected-row conflict metric |
| Duplicate request | idempotency replay/conflict metric |
| Outbox tidak terkirim | backlog metric + worker claim timing |
| Pool timeout | Hikari pending/acquire/timeout alerts |
| DB transient error | SQLSTATE classifier + retry metric |
| Compliance concern | structured log masking + SQL log policy |
| Susah reproduce query | static SQL comment + mapper statement id |
| N+1 regression | calls per request / mapper count metric |

---

## 28. Roadmap Belajar Singkat

| Urutan | Materi | Target |
|---:|---|---|
| 1 | Correlation ID + MDC | Semua log request tersambung |
| 2 | MyBatis SQL logging | Bisa debug SQL secara selektif |
| 3 | Structured logging | Log bisa dicari dan dianalisis |
| 4 | Mapper timing metric | Query lambat terlihat |
| 5 | Connection pool metrics | Pool saturation terdeteksi |
| 6 | OpenTelemetry tracing | Latency breakdown end-to-end |
| 7 | SQLSTATE classification | Error mapping dan retry benar |
| 8 | PostgreSQL runtime views | Bisa lihat lock/long transaction |
| 9 | pg_stat_statements | Bisa cari query mahal historis |
| 10 | Runbook + alerting | Production diagnostics konsisten |

## Response:
7/13/2026, 2:31:30 AM

Lanjut ke testing strategy: fokusnya membuktikan mapper, migration, transaksi, concurrency, dan SQL behavior benar terhadap PostgreSQL nyata, bukan hanya mock interface.

# Cheatsheet - Java MyBatis Testing Strategy: Unit Test, Mapper Integration Test, Testcontainers PostgreSQL, Migration Test, Transaction Test, Concurrency Test, dan SQL Regression Test

## 1. Introduction

Testing untuk **Jersey/JAX-RS + MyBatis + PostgreSQL** harus membedakan dua hal:

```text
Business logic bisa dites dengan unit test.
SQL mapper harus dites terhadap PostgreSQL nyata.
```

MyBatis mapper bukan sekadar interface Java. Mapper adalah kontrak antara:

```text
Mapper interface
  -> Mapper XML / annotation SQL
    -> PostgreSQL schema
      -> PostgreSQL planner, constraint, type system, transaction behavior
```

Karena itu, mock mapper hanya membuktikan service memanggil method tertentu. Mock tidak membuktikan SQL valid, resultMap benar, TypeHandler bekerja, constraint sesuai, migration kompatibel, atau transaksi aman. MyBatis sendiri menempatkan `SqlSession` sebagai interface utama untuk menjalankan command, mengambil mapper, dan mengelola transaksi; ini berarti testing mapper sebaiknya memvalidasi behavior nyata dari session, mapper, SQL, dan database. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/java-api.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 2. Mental Model Testing

```mermaid id="nauo5f"
flowchart TD
    A[Unit Test] --> B[Domain / Service Logic]
    B --> C[Mock Repository / Fake Gateway]

    D[Mapper Integration Test] --> E[MyBatis SqlSessionFactory]
    E --> F[Mapper Interface]
    F --> G[Mapper XML]
    G --> H[(PostgreSQL Testcontainer)]

    I[Migration Test] --> J[Flyway/Liquibase]
    J --> H

    K[Transaction Test] --> L[Commit / Rollback / Constraint]
    L --> H

    M[Concurrency Test] --> N[Multiple Threads / Sessions]
    N --> O[Locking / Version / Idempotency]
    O --> H

    P[SQL Regression Test] --> Q[Known Query Inputs]
    Q --> R[Expected Rows / Plan / Index Awareness]
    R --> H
```

**Core invariant:**

```text
Unit test:
  fast, isolated, no database

Mapper integration test:
  real MyBatis + real PostgreSQL

Transaction/concurrency test:
  multiple real SqlSession / connections

Migration test:
  empty database -> migrate -> mapper works
```

---

## 3. Decomposition Map

| Area | Fokus Praktis | Dibuktikan Dengan | Risiko Jika Tidak Dites |
|---|---|---|---|
| **Domain Unit Test** | Invariant dan state transition | Plain JUnit test | Logic benar hanya saat happy path |
| **Service Unit Test** | Orchestration use case | Mock repository/unit-of-work | Flow salah tidak terdeteksi cepat |
| **Mapper Integration Test** | SQL + XML + resultMap | Testcontainers PostgreSQL | SQL invalid baru ketahuan runtime |
| **TypeHandler Test** | JSONB, enum, UUID, array, timestamp | Round-trip insert/select | Mapping rusak diam-diam |
| **Migration Test** | Schema bisa dibangun dari nol | Flyway/Liquibase migrate + validate | Migration gagal di deployment |
| **Repository Test** | Persistence abstraction | Real mapper + DB | Repository tidak sesuai kontrak |
| **Transaction Test** | Commit/rollback behavior | Real transaction | Partial write lolos |
| **Constraint Test** | Unique, FK, check, not-null | Real PostgreSQL constraint | Invariant hanya ada di Java |
| **Concurrency Test** | Lost update/deadlock/idempotency | Multi-thread + real DB | Race condition muncul production |
| **Pagination Test** | Stable ordering/keyset | Seed data + assertions | Duplicate/missing row |
| **Batch Test** | Chunking dan batch behavior | Batch insert/update | Batch gagal sebagian tidak jelas |
| **SQL Regression Test** | Query penting tetap benar | Golden dataset | Perubahan SQL mematahkan report/workflow |
| **Performance Smoke Test** | Query tidak regress parah | EXPLAIN/assert duration ringan | Query lambat lolos merge |
| **Error Classification Test** | SQLSTATE mapping | Trigger constraint/deadlock | HTTP status/retry salah |

---

## 4. Testing Pyramid untuk MyBatis App

```text
Banyak:
  Domain unit test
  Service unit test
  Mapper integration test kecil

Sedang:
  Repository integration test
  Migration test
  Transaction test

Sedikit tapi penting:
  Concurrency test
  End-to-end API test
  Performance regression smoke test
```

| Test Type | Database? | Cepat? | Tujuan |
|---|---:|---:|---|
| Domain unit test | Tidak | Sangat cepat | Invariant murni |
| Service unit test | Tidak | Cepat | Orchestration dan decision |
| Mapper integration test | Ya | Sedang | SQL/resultMap/type handler |
| Migration test | Ya | Sedang | Schema lifecycle |
| Transaction test | Ya | Sedang | Commit/rollback |
| Concurrency test | Ya | Lebih lambat | Race/lock/idempotency |
| API integration test | Ya | Lebih lambat | HTTP contract + persistence |
| Performance smoke | Ya | Lebih lambat | Query regression signal |

---

## 5. Dependency Test

Gunakan JUnit 5 + Testcontainers PostgreSQL. Testcontainers menyediakan PostgreSQL module untuk menjalankan container PostgreSQL dari test Java, sehingga test memakai dependency nyata, bukan in-memory database yang behavior-nya berbeda dari PostgreSQL. ([Testcontainers for Java](https://java.testcontainers.org/modules/databases/postgres/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

```xml id="zgmaww"
<dependencies>
  <dependency>
    <groupId>org.mybatis</groupId>
    <artifactId>mybatis</artifactId>
    <version>${mybatis.version}</version>
  </dependency>

  <dependency>
    <groupId>org.postgresql</groupId>
    <artifactId>postgresql</artifactId>
    <version>${postgresql.version}</version>
    <scope>test</scope>
  </dependency>

  <dependency>
    <groupId>org.testcontainers</groupId>
    <artifactId>postgresql</artifactId>
    <version>${testcontainers.version}</version>
    <scope>test</scope>
  </dependency>

  <dependency>
    <groupId>org.testcontainers</groupId>
    <artifactId>junit-jupiter</artifactId>
    <version>${testcontainers.version}</version>
    <scope>test</scope>
  </dependency>

  <dependency>
    <groupId>org.flywaydb</groupId>
    <artifactId>flyway-core</artifactId>
    <version>${flyway.version}</version>
    <scope>test</scope>
  </dependency>
</dependencies>
```

**Catatan praktis:** gunakan version management/BOM sesuai standar project agar versi Testcontainers, PostgreSQL driver, JUnit, dan Flyway konsisten.

---

## 6. Testcontainers PostgreSQL Base Test

```java id="a4s2t5"
@Testcontainers
public abstract class PostgreSqlIntegrationTest {

    @Container
    protected static final PostgreSQLContainer<?> POSTGRES =
        new PostgreSQLContainer<>("postgres:16")
            .withDatabaseName("app_test")
            .withUsername("app")
            .withPassword("app");

    protected static SqlSessionFactory sqlSessionFactory;

    @BeforeAll
    static void beforeAll() {
        runMigrations();
        sqlSessionFactory = buildSqlSessionFactory();
    }

    private static void runMigrations() {
        Flyway.configure()
            .dataSource(
                POSTGRES.getJdbcUrl(),
                POSTGRES.getUsername(),
                POSTGRES.getPassword()
            )
            .locations("classpath:db/migration")
            .load()
            .migrate();
    }

    private static SqlSessionFactory buildSqlSessionFactory() {
        HikariConfig hikariConfig = new HikariConfig();
        hikariConfig.setJdbcUrl(POSTGRES.getJdbcUrl());
        hikariConfig.setUsername(POSTGRES.getUsername());
        hikariConfig.setPassword(POSTGRES.getPassword());
        hikariConfig.setMaximumPoolSize(5);

        DataSource dataSource = new HikariDataSource(hikariConfig);

        TransactionFactory transactionFactory = new JdbcTransactionFactory();

        Environment environment = new Environment(
            "test",
            transactionFactory,
            dataSource
        );

        Configuration configuration = new Configuration(environment);
        configuration.setMapUnderscoreToCamelCase(true);

        configuration.addMapper(OrderMapper.class);
        configuration.addMapper(CaseMapper.class);
        configuration.addMapper(OutboxMapper.class);

        return new SqlSessionFactoryBuilder().build(configuration);
    }

    protected SqlSession openSession() {
        return sqlSessionFactory.openSession(false);
    }
}
```

Flyway `migrate` menjalankan versioned dan repeatable migrations yang belum diterapkan ke database; Flyway juga memiliki `validateOnMigrate`, yang default-nya `true` menurut dokumentasi Redgate/Flyway terbaru, sehingga migration yang berubah atau tidak konsisten bisa terdeteksi saat migrate. ([Redgate Documentation](https://documentation.red-gate.com/fd/migrations-271585107.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 7. Mapper Integration Test

### 7.1 Mapper

```java id="u4wj1u"
public interface OrderMapper {

    int insert(Order order);

    Order findById(@Param("id") UUID id);

    int transitionStatus(
        @Param("id") UUID id,
        @Param("expectedStatus") String expectedStatus,
        @Param("newStatus") String newStatus,
        @Param("expectedVersion") long expectedVersion
    );
}
```

### 7.2 Test

```java id="r6x0is"
class OrderMapperTest extends PostgreSqlIntegrationTest {

    @Test
    void shouldInsertAndFindOrderById() {
        UUID orderId = UUID.randomUUID();

        try (SqlSession session = openSession()) {
            OrderMapper mapper = session.getMapper(OrderMapper.class);

            Order order = new Order(
                orderId,
                UUID.randomUUID(),
                "DRAFT",
                0L,
                Instant.parse("2026-07-13T01:00:00Z")
            );

            int inserted = mapper.insert(order);
            session.commit();

            assertThat(inserted).isEqualTo(1);
        }

        try (SqlSession session = openSession()) {
            OrderMapper mapper = session.getMapper(OrderMapper.class);

            Order found = mapper.findById(orderId);

            assertThat(found).isNotNull();
            assertThat(found.id()).isEqualTo(orderId);
            assertThat(found.status()).isEqualTo("DRAFT");
            assertThat(found.version()).isEqualTo(0L);
        }
    }
}
```

**Yang dibuktikan:**

```text
- Mapper XML namespace benar
- Statement id cocok dengan method
- INSERT valid terhadap schema
- resultMap benar
- UUID/timestamp mapping benar
- commit membuat data terlihat di session lain
```

---

## 8. Rollback Test

```java id="t41yha"
@Test
void shouldRollbackInsertedOrder() {
    UUID orderId = UUID.randomUUID();

    try (SqlSession session = openSession()) {
        OrderMapper mapper = session.getMapper(OrderMapper.class);

        mapper.insert(Order.draft(orderId));
        session.rollback();
    }

    try (SqlSession session = openSession()) {
        OrderMapper mapper = session.getMapper(OrderMapper.class);

        Order found = mapper.findById(orderId);

        assertThat(found).isNull();
    }
}
```

**Tujuan:** membuktikan test setup benar-benar memakai transaction dan tidak diam-diam auto-commit.

---

## 9. ResultMap Regression Test

ResultMap sering rusak saat rename column, alias berubah, constructor record berubah, atau field baru ditambahkan.

```java id="zew26o"
@Test
void shouldMapOrderSummaryProjection() {
    UUID orderId = fixture.insertOrderWithCustomerAndItems();

    try (SqlSession session = openSession()) {
        OrderQueryMapper mapper = session.getMapper(OrderQueryMapper.class);

        OrderSummaryDto summary = mapper.findSummaryById(orderId);

        assertThat(summary.id()).isEqualTo(orderId);
        assertThat(summary.customerName()).isEqualTo("Acme Corp");
        assertThat(summary.itemCount()).isEqualTo(3);
        assertThat(summary.status()).isEqualTo("SUBMITTED");
    }
}
```

**Rule:** setiap query projection penting harus punya test minimal satu happy path dan satu missing/empty path.

---

## 10. TypeHandler Round-Trip Test

### 10.1 JSONB

```java id="yookw3"
@Test
void shouldRoundTripJsonbPayload() {
    UUID eventId = UUID.randomUUID();

    ObjectNode payload = objectMapper.createObjectNode()
        .put("correlationId", "corr-001")
        .put("eventType", "ORDER_CREATED");

    try (SqlSession session = openSession()) {
        OutboxMapper mapper = session.getMapper(OutboxMapper.class);

        mapper.insert(new OutboxEvent(
            eventId,
            "ORDER",
            "ORD-001",
            "ORDER_CREATED",
            payload,
            "PENDING",
            Instant.parse("2026-07-13T01:00:00Z")
        ));

        session.commit();
    }

    try (SqlSession session = openSession()) {
        OutboxMapper mapper = session.getMapper(OutboxMapper.class);

        OutboxEvent found = mapper.findById(eventId);

        assertThat(found.payload().get("correlationId").asText())
            .isEqualTo("corr-001");
    }
}
```

### 10.2 Enum

```java id="1dobhk"
@Test
void shouldRoundTripNativePostgresEnum() {
    UUID orderId = UUID.randomUUID();

    try (SqlSession session = openSession()) {
        OrderMapper mapper = session.getMapper(OrderMapper.class);

        mapper.insert(Order.withStatus(orderId, OrderStatus.SUBMITTED));
        session.commit();
    }

    try (SqlSession session = openSession()) {
        OrderMapper mapper = session.getMapper(OrderMapper.class);

        Order found = mapper.findById(orderId);

        assertThat(found.status()).isEqualTo(OrderStatus.SUBMITTED);
    }
}
```

### 10.3 Timestamp

```java id="3jrm8u"
@Test
void shouldPreserveInstantForAuditTimestamp() {
    UUID auditId = UUID.randomUUID();
    Instant occurredAt = Instant.parse("2026-07-13T01:00:00Z");

    try (SqlSession session = openSession()) {
        AuditMapper mapper = session.getMapper(AuditMapper.class);

        mapper.insert(new AuditEntry(auditId, "CASE_APPROVED", occurredAt));
        session.commit();
    }

    try (SqlSession session = openSession()) {
        AuditMapper mapper = session.getMapper(AuditMapper.class);

        AuditEntry found = mapper.findById(auditId);

        assertThat(found.occurredAt()).isEqualTo(occurredAt);
    }
}
```

---

## 11. Constraint Test

Constraint test memastikan invariant tidak hanya ada di Java.

### 11.1 Unique constraint

```java id="mykn5i"
@Test
void shouldRejectDuplicateCaseNumber() {
    String caseNumber = "CASE-20260713";

    try (SqlSession session = openSession()) {
        CaseMapper mapper = session.getMapper(CaseMapper.class);

        mapper.insert(CaseRecord.open(UUID.randomUUID(), caseNumber));
        session.commit();
    }

    assertThatThrownBy(() -> {
        try (SqlSession session = openSession()) {
            CaseMapper mapper = session.getMapper(CaseMapper.class);

            mapper.insert(CaseRecord.open(UUID.randomUUID(), caseNumber));
            session.commit();
        }
    }).hasRootCauseInstanceOf(SQLException.class);
}
```

### 11.2 Better: classify SQLSTATE

```java id="gslbsc"
@Test
void shouldClassifyDuplicateCaseNumberAsUniqueViolation() {
    String caseNumber = "CASE-20260713";

    fixture.insertCase(caseNumber);

    Throwable thrown = catchThrowable(() -> fixture.insertCase(caseNumber));

    assertThat(PostgresErrorClassifier.classify(thrown))
        .isEqualTo(PersistenceErrorKind.UNIQUE_VIOLATION);
}
```

PostgreSQL uses SQLSTATE codes for database errors, and earlier sections used SQLSTATE such as `23505` for unique violation, `40001` for serialization failure, and `40P01` for deadlock/transaction retry classification. PostgreSQL documents that applications using stricter isolation levels must be prepared to retry transactions on serialization failures. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/transaction-iso.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 12. Migration Test

Migration test menjawab:

```text
Apakah database kosong bisa dibangun sampai versi terbaru?
Apakah migration repeatable valid?
Apakah mapper compatible dengan schema hasil migration?
Apakah test seed compatible dengan constraint terbaru?
```

### 12.1 Empty database migration test

```java id="j4nlgm"
@Testcontainers
class MigrationTest {

    @Container
    static PostgreSQLContainer<?> postgres =
        new PostgreSQLContainer<>("postgres:16")
            .withDatabaseName("migration_test")
            .withUsername("app")
            .withPassword("app");

    @Test
    void shouldMigrateEmptyDatabaseToLatestSchema() {
        Flyway flyway = Flyway.configure()
            .dataSource(
                postgres.getJdbcUrl(),
                postgres.getUsername(),
                postgres.getPassword()
            )
            .locations("classpath:db/migration")
            .load();

        MigrateResult result = flyway.migrate();

        assertThat(result.success).isTrue();
    }
}
```

### 12.2 Migration + mapper smoke test

```java id="6h6osy"
@Test
void latestMigrationShouldSupportOrderMapper() {
    runMigrations();

    SqlSessionFactory factory = buildSqlSessionFactory();

    try (SqlSession session = factory.openSession(false)) {
        OrderMapper mapper = session.getMapper(OrderMapper.class);

        UUID id = UUID.randomUUID();
        mapper.insert(Order.draft(id));
        session.commit();

        assertThat(mapper.findById(id)).isNotNull();
    }
}
```

**Practical rule:** migration test harus berjalan dari database kosong. Untuk sistem besar, tambahkan juga test upgrade dari snapshot versi sebelumnya jika memungkinkan.

## 13. Transaction Boundary Test

### 13.1 Use case harus rollback semua

```java id="qxefss"
@Test
void shouldRollbackOrderWhenOutboxInsertFails() {
    UUID orderId = UUID.randomUUID();

    assertThatThrownBy(() ->
        orderService.createOrderWithBrokenOutbox(orderId)
    ).isInstanceOf(PersistenceException.class);

    try (SqlSession session = openSession()) {
        OrderMapper orderMapper = session.getMapper(OrderMapper.class);
        OutboxMapper outboxMapper = session.getMapper(OutboxMapper.class);

        assertThat(orderMapper.findById(orderId)).isNull();
        assertThat(outboxMapper.findByAggregateId(orderId.toString())).isEmpty();
    }
}
```

### 13.2 Anti-pattern yang ditangkap test ini

```text
insert order
commit
insert outbox
fail
```

Jika test menemukan order tetap ada saat outbox gagal, berarti transaction boundary salah.

---

## 14. Optimistic Locking Test

```java id="g1nxum"
@Test
void shouldRejectStaleVersionUpdate() {
    UUID caseId = fixture.insertSubmittedCase();

    try (SqlSession session = openSession()) {
        CaseMapper mapper = session.getMapper(CaseMapper.class);

        int first = mapper.transitionStatus(
            caseId,
            "SUBMITTED",
            "APPROVED",
            0L
        );

        session.commit();

        assertThat(first).isEqualTo(1);
    }

    try (SqlSession session = openSession()) {
        CaseMapper mapper = session.getMapper(CaseMapper.class);

        int second = mapper.transitionStatus(
            caseId,
            "SUBMITTED",
            "REJECTED",
            0L
        );

        session.commit();

        assertThat(second).isEqualTo(0);
    }
}
```

**Yang dibuktikan:** stale command tidak overwrite state terbaru.

---

## 15. Pessimistic Locking Test

Concurrency test harus memakai **dua session/connection berbeda**.

```java id="xjp1cu"
@Test
void secondTransactionShouldWaitOrTimeoutWhenRowLocked() throws Exception {
    UUID caseId = fixture.insertOpenCase();

    ExecutorService executor = Executors.newFixedThreadPool(2);
    CountDownLatch firstLocked = new CountDownLatch(1);
    CountDownLatch releaseFirst = new CountDownLatch(1);

    Future<Void> first = executor.submit(() -> {
        try (SqlSession session = openSession()) {
            CaseMapper mapper = session.getMapper(CaseMapper.class);

            mapper.findByIdForUpdate(caseId);
            firstLocked.countDown();

            releaseFirst.await(5, TimeUnit.SECONDS);

            session.commit();
        }
        return null;
    });

    Future<Boolean> second = executor.submit(() -> {
        firstLocked.await(5, TimeUnit.SECONDS);

        try (SqlSession session = openSession()) {
            RuntimeMapper runtime = session.getMapper(RuntimeMapper.class);
            CaseMapper mapper = session.getMapper(CaseMapper.class);

            runtime.setLocalLockTimeout("500ms");

            mapper.findByIdForUpdate(caseId);
            session.commit();

            return true;
        } catch (PersistenceException e) {
            return false;
        }
    });

    assertThat(second.get()).isFalse();

    releaseFirst.countDown();
    first.get();
}
```

**Catatan:** test lock harus punya timeout pendek agar tidak menggantung pipeline CI.

---

## 16. Concurrency Test - Lost Update

```java id="dfrqv9"
@Test
void onlyOneConcurrentApprovalShouldSucceed() throws Exception {
    UUID caseId = fixture.insertSubmittedCaseWithVersion(0L);

    ExecutorService executor = Executors.newFixedThreadPool(2);
    CountDownLatch start = new CountDownLatch(1);

    Callable<Boolean> approve = () -> {
        start.await();

        try {
            caseService.approve(new ApproveCaseCommand(caseId, 0L));
            return true;
        } catch (ConcurrentCaseModificationException e) {
            return false;
        }
    };

    Future<Boolean> first = executor.submit(approve);
    Future<Boolean> second = executor.submit(approve);

    start.countDown();

    assertThat(List.of(first.get(), second.get()))
        .containsExactlyInAnyOrder(true, false);

    CaseRecord found = fixture.findCase(caseId);

    assertThat(found.status()).isEqualTo("APPROVED");
    assertThat(found.version()).isEqualTo(1L);
}
```

**Yang dibuktikan:**

```text
- Dua request paralel tidak sama-sama sukses
- Version naik sekali
- State akhir deterministik
- Service benar menerjemahkan affected-row=0 menjadi conflict
```

---

## 17. Idempotency Test

### 17.1 Same key + same payload

```java id="pq5pok"
@Test
void sameIdempotencyKeyAndSamePayloadShouldReturnSameResponse() {
    String key = UUID.randomUUID().toString();

    CreateOrderCommand command = new CreateOrderCommand(
        key,
        "hash-abc",
        UUID.randomUUID(),
        List.of(new OrderItemRequest("SKU-1", 1))
    );

    OrderResponse first = orderService.createOrder(command);
    OrderResponse second = orderService.createOrder(command);

    assertThat(second.orderId()).isEqualTo(first.orderId());
}
```

### 17.2 Same key + different payload

```java id="s4p79d"
@Test
void sameIdempotencyKeyWithDifferentPayloadShouldFail() {
    String key = UUID.randomUUID().toString();

    orderService.createOrder(command(key, "hash-a", "SKU-1"));

    assertThatThrownBy(() ->
        orderService.createOrder(command(key, "hash-b", "SKU-2"))
    ).isInstanceOf(IdempotencyKeyConflictException.class);
}
```

### 17.3 Concurrent same idempotency key

```java id="59gc4i"
@Test
void concurrentSameIdempotencyKeyShouldCreateOnlyOneOrder() throws Exception {
    String key = UUID.randomUUID().toString();
    CreateOrderCommand command = command(key, "hash-a", "SKU-1");

    ExecutorService executor = Executors.newFixedThreadPool(2);
    CountDownLatch start = new CountDownLatch(1);

    Callable<OrderResponse> call = () -> {
        start.await();
        return orderService.createOrder(command);
    };

    Future<OrderResponse> first = executor.submit(call);
    Future<OrderResponse> second = executor.submit(call);

    start.countDown();

    OrderResponse r1 = first.get();
    OrderResponse r2 = second.get();

    assertThat(r2.orderId()).isEqualTo(r1.orderId());
    assertThat(fixture.countOrdersForIdempotencyKey(key)).isEqualTo(1);
}
```

## 18. Retry Test untuk Serialization Failure

PostgreSQL menyatakan bahwa transaksi pada isolation tertentu dapat gagal dengan serialization failure, dan aplikasi perlu siap retry seluruh transaksi dari awal. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/transaction-iso.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Unit test classifier:

```java id="k99ehb"
@Test
void shouldClassifySerializationFailureAsRetryable() {
    SQLException sqlException = new SQLException(
        "could not serialize access",
        "40001"
    );

    PersistenceException exception = new PersistenceException(sqlException);

    assertThat(PostgresErrorClassifier.classify(exception))
        .isEqualTo(PersistenceErrorKind.SERIALIZATION_FAILURE);

    assertThat(PostgresRetryPolicy.isRetryable(exception)).isTrue();
}
```

Integration-style retry test bisa dibuat, tetapi jangan terlalu rapuh. Untuk CI, biasanya cukup:

```text
- classifier unit test
- retry wrapper unit test
- satu integration test untuk optimistic/concurrency conflict
```

---

## 19. Pagination Test

### 19.1 Offset pagination deterministic

```java id="gruc4e"
@Test
void searchShouldReturnStableOrderWithTieBreakerId() {
    Instant sameCreatedAt = Instant.parse("2026-07-13T01:00:00Z");

    UUID a = fixture.insertCase("CASE-A", sameCreatedAt);
    UUID b = fixture.insertCase("CASE-B", sameCreatedAt);
    UUID c = fixture.insertCase("CASE-C", sameCreatedAt);

    List<CaseQueueItemDto> page = caseQueryService.search(
        new CaseSearchQuery("OPEN", 10, 0)
    );

    assertThat(page)
        .extracting(CaseQueueItemDto::id)
        .containsExactly(c, b, a);
}
```

### 19.2 Keyset pagination no duplicate

```java id="xt97gg"
@Test
void keysetPaginationShouldNotDuplicateRowsAcrossPages() {
    fixture.insertManyOpenCases(25);

    CasePage first = caseQueryService.searchAfter(null, 10);
    CasePage second = caseQueryService.searchAfter(first.nextCursor(), 10);

    Set<UUID> firstIds = first.items().stream()
        .map(CaseQueueItemDto::id)
        .collect(Collectors.toSet());

    Set<UUID> secondIds = second.items().stream()
        .map(CaseQueueItemDto::id)
        .collect(Collectors.toSet());

    assertThat(Collections.disjoint(firstIds, secondIds)).isTrue();
}
```

## 20. Batch Test

```java id="pw0q3b"
@Test
void shouldInsertAuditLogsInBatchChunks() {
    List<AuditLog> logs = IntStream.range(0, 1200)
        .mapToObj(i -> AuditLog.fixture("ACTION_" + i))
        .toList();

    auditImportService.insertAuditLogs(logs);

    try (SqlSession session = openSession()) {
        AuditMapper mapper = session.getMapper(AuditMapper.class);

        assertThat(mapper.countAll()).isEqualTo(1200);
    }
}
```

### Batch failure test

```java id="xmq3gf"
@Test
void shouldRollbackCurrentChunkWhenBatchFails() {
    List<AuditLog> logs = fixture.logsWithDuplicateIdInSecondChunk();

    assertThatThrownBy(() -> auditImportService.insertAuditLogs(logs))
        .isInstanceOf(PersistenceException.class);

    assertThat(fixture.countCommittedAuditLogs())
        .isEqualTo(500); // jika chunk pertama commit, chunk kedua rollback
}
```

**Rule:** test harus eksplisit apakah batch bersifat all-or-nothing atau per-chunk resumable.

## 21. SQL Regression Test dengan Golden Dataset

Golden dataset cocok untuk query report, queue, escalation, SLA, dan dashboard.

### 21.1 Seed

```java id="i0njf0"
@BeforeEach
void seed() {
    fixture.insertCase("CASE-001", "OPEN", "HIGH", "TEAM-A", dueDaysAgo(2));
    fixture.insertCase("CASE-002", "IN_REVIEW", "MEDIUM", "TEAM-A", dueDaysAgo(1));
    fixture.insertCase("CASE-003", "CLOSED", "HIGH", "TEAM-A", dueDaysAgo(10));
    fixture.insertCase("CASE-004", "OPEN", "LOW", "TEAM-B", dueDaysAgo(3));
}
```

### 21.2 Query test

```java id="vo1v2r"
@Test
void shouldFindCasesEligibleForEscalation() {
    try (SqlSession session = openSession()) {
        CaseEscalationMapper mapper = session.getMapper(CaseEscalationMapper.class);

        List<CaseRecord> result = mapper.findEligibleForEscalation("TEAM-A", 10);

        assertThat(result)
            .extracting(CaseRecord::caseNumber)
            .containsExactly("CASE-001", "CASE-002");
    }
}
```

**Yang penting:** urutan juga dites, bukan hanya isi.

---

## 22. SQL Plan Smoke Test

Jangan jadikan EXPLAIN test terlalu brittle. Tapi untuk query kritis, boleh ada smoke test yang memastikan tidak jatuh ke full table scan secara tidak sengaja pada dataset representatif.

```java id="f71smo"
@Test
void searchOpenCasesShouldUseExpectedIndex() {
    fixture.insertManyCases(20_000);

    String plan = jdbc.queryForObject("""
        EXPLAIN
        SELECT id, case_number, status, due_at
        FROM cases
        WHERE assigned_team = 'TEAM-A'
          AND status = 'OPEN'
        ORDER BY due_at ASC, id ASC
        LIMIT 50
        """, String.class);

    assertThat(plan).contains("Index");
}
```

**Warning:** plan test bisa berubah karena versi PostgreSQL, statistik, ukuran data, atau konfigurasi. Gunakan hanya untuk query paling kritis dan jangan assert string terlalu spesifik kecuali environment test sangat terkontrol.

---

## 23. Test Data Strategy

| Strategy | Cocok Untuk | Risiko |
|---|---|---|
| Inline fixture builder | Mapper test kecil | Duplikasi jika tidak dirapikan |
| SQL seed file | Dataset regression | Sulit maintain jika schema berubah |
| Factory/helper Java | Domain-heavy test | Bisa terlalu abstrak |
| Migration seed | Reference data | Jangan campur dengan test data dinamis |
| Golden dataset | Report/query critical | Harus stabil dan terdokumentasi |
| Random data | Property-like test | Debug lebih sulit |
| Deterministic UUID/time | Hampir semua test | Lebih mudah assert |

### Fixture helper

```java id="mb6is7"
public final class CaseFixture {

    private final SqlSessionFactory sqlSessionFactory;

    public UUID insertCase(
        String caseNumber,
        String status,
        String priority,
        String assignedTeam,
        Instant dueAt
    ) {
        UUID id = UUID.randomUUID();

        try (SqlSession session = sqlSessionFactory.openSession(false)) {
            CaseMapper mapper = session.getMapper(CaseMapper.class);

            mapper.insert(new CaseRecord(
                id,
                caseNumber,
                status,
                priority,
                assignedTeam,
                dueAt,
                0L,
                Instant.now()
            ));

            session.commit();
        }

        return id;
    }
}
```

**Rule:** fixture boleh membantu setup, tapi jangan sampai menyembunyikan behavior yang sedang dites.

---

## 24. Test Isolation Strategy

| Approach | Kelebihan | Kekurangan |
|---|---|---|
| Container per test class | Cepat, cukup isolated | Data harus dibersihkan antar test |
| Container per test method | Sangat isolated | Lambat |
| Transaction rollback per test | Cepat | Tidak cocok untuk concurrency/multiple session |
| Truncate tables after each test | Jelas | Perlu urutan FK |
| Schema per test | Isolated | Setup lebih kompleks |
| Database per test class | Isolated cukup baik | Container/resource lebih banyak |

### Truncate helper

```java id="nra5qi"
public void truncateAllTables() {
    jdbcTemplate.execute("""
        TRUNCATE TABLE
            outbox_events,
            audit_logs,
            order_items,
            orders,
            cases,
            idempotency_keys
        RESTART IDENTITY CASCADE
        """);
}
```

### JUnit hook

```java id="szlob8"
@BeforeEach
void cleanDatabase() {
    databaseCleaner.truncateAllTables();
}
```

**Warning:** transaction rollback per test sering tidak cocok untuk concurrency test karena test butuh data terlihat dari session/connection lain.

## 25. Unit Test vs Integration Test Boundary

### 25.1 Service unit test dengan fake repository

```java id="pzelko"
@Test
void shouldRejectInvalidTransitionBeforeCallingRepository() {
    FakeCaseRepository repository = new FakeCaseRepository();
    repository.save(CaseRecord.closed(caseId));

    CaseService service = new CaseService(repository);

    assertThatThrownBy(() -> service.approve(caseId))
        .isInstanceOf(InvalidCaseTransitionException.class);
}
```

### 25.2 Mapper integration test untuk SQL

```java id="tg01n7"
@Test
void transitionStatusShouldReturnZeroForInvalidCurrentStatus() {
    UUID caseId = fixture.insertClosedCase();

    try (SqlSession session = openSession()) {
        CaseMapper mapper = session.getMapper(CaseMapper.class);

        int updated = mapper.transitionStatus(
            caseId,
            "SUBMITTED",
            "APPROVED",
            0L
        );

        session.commit();

        assertThat(updated).isEqualTo(0);
    }
}
```

**Practical split:**

```text
Unit test:
  Apakah service memutuskan action yang benar?

Integration test:
  Apakah SQL benar-benar menegakkan invariant?
```

---

## 26. Jersey API Integration Test

Untuk endpoint kritis, test HTTP contract + persistence.

```java id="zhz9px"
@Test
void postOrderShouldCreateOrderAndReturn201() {
    CreateOrderRequest request = new CreateOrderRequest(
        customerId,
        List.of(new CreateOrderItemRequest("SKU-1", 2))
    );

    Response response = target("/orders")
        .request()
        .header("Idempotency-Key", "idem-001")
        .post(Entity.json(request));

    assertThat(response.getStatus()).isEqualTo(201);

    OrderResponse body = response.readEntity(OrderResponse.class);

    assertThat(body.orderId()).isNotNull();

    assertThat(fixture.findOrder(body.orderId())).isNotNull();
}
```

**Jangan semua mapper dites lewat HTTP.** HTTP integration test mahal dan lebih rapuh. Gunakan untuk critical path, auth, validation, error mapping, dan serialization.

---

## 27. Error Mapping Test

### 27.1 Duplicate business key → 409

```java id="xdsmzb"
@Test
void duplicateCaseNumberShouldReturn409() {
    fixture.insertCaseNumber("CASE-20260713");

    CreateCaseRequest request = new CreateCaseRequest("CASE-20260713");

    Response response = target("/cases")
        .request()
        .post(Entity.json(request));

    assertThat(response.getStatus()).isEqualTo(409);

    ApiError error = response.readEntity(ApiError.class);

    assertThat(error.code()).isEqualTo("DUPLICATE_RESOURCE");
}
```

### 27.2 Stale version → 409

```java id="tjlypg"
@Test
void staleVersionShouldReturn409Conflict() {
    UUID caseId = fixture.insertSubmittedCaseWithVersion(1L);

    ApproveCaseRequest request = new ApproveCaseRequest(0L);

    Response response = target("/cases/" + caseId + "/approve")
        .request()
        .post(Entity.json(request));

    assertThat(response.getStatus()).isEqualTo(409);
}
```

## 28. Test Coverage Matrix

| Behavior | Unit Test | Mapper IT | API IT | Concurrency IT |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| Domain state transition | Ya | Opsional | Opsional | Ya untuk critical |
| SQL syntax | Tidak | Ya | Tidak cukup | Tidak cukup |
| ResultMap | Tidak | Ya | Tidak cukup | Tidak |
| TypeHandler | Tidak | Ya | Opsional | Tidak |
| Constraint violation | Tidak | Ya | Ya untuk error mapping | Tidak |
| Commit/rollback | Tidak | Ya/service IT | Opsional | Ya |
| Idempotency | Service unit + IT | Ya | Ya | Ya |
| Pagination ordering | Tidak | Ya | Opsional | Tidak |
| HTTP status mapping | Tidak | Tidak | Ya | Opsional |
| Retry classifier | Ya | Opsional | Tidak | Opsional |
| Lock behavior | Tidak | Ya | Tidak | Ya |
| Migration compatibility | Tidak | Ya | Tidak | Tidak |

---

## 29. CI Pipeline Strategy

```text
Stage 1: Unit tests
  - domain
  - service
  - classifier
  - request validation

Stage 2: Mapper integration tests
  - Testcontainers PostgreSQL
  - migrations
  - mapper XML
  - TypeHandler
  - constraints

Stage 3: API integration tests
  - selected critical endpoints
  - error mapping
  - idempotency

Stage 4: Concurrency/regression tests
  - optimistic locking
  - idempotency race
  - queue claim
  - can run nightly if too slow

Stage 5: Optional performance smoke
  - selected EXPLAIN
  - batch smoke
  - large result streaming smoke
```

**Practical rule:** mapper integration test harus masuk PR pipeline. Concurrency/performance-heavy test bisa dipisah antara PR-critical dan nightly, tergantung durasi.

---

## 30. Anti-Pattern

| Anti-Pattern | Dampak |
|---|---|
| Mapper hanya dimock | SQL/resultMap/type handler tidak pernah dites |
| Pakai H2 untuk PostgreSQL-specific SQL | Behavior beda, false confidence |
| Test tidak menjalankan migration | Schema test tidak sama dengan deployment |
| Semua test share data tanpa cleanup | Flaky test |
| Random time/UUID tanpa kontrol | Assertion sulit/debug sulit |
| Tidak test rollback | Partial write tidak ketahuan |
| Tidak test affected row = 0 | Lost update tidak ketahuan |
| Tidak test constraint violation | Error mapping salah |
| Concurrency test pakai satu session | Tidak benar-benar concurrent |
| Lock test tanpa timeout | CI menggantung |
| SQL plan test terlalu spesifik | Test brittle |
| Test API semua hal | Lambat dan susah maintain |
| Fixture terlalu magical | Test sulit dipahami |
| Performance test tanpa data realistis | Tidak merepresentasikan production |
| Tidak test idempotency conflict | Same key beda payload bisa lolos |

---

## 31. Production-Grade Testing Checklist

| Checklist | Status Ideal |
|---|---|
| Mapper XML dites terhadap PostgreSQL nyata | Ya |
| Migration dijalankan sebelum mapper test | Ya |
| Testcontainers memakai PostgreSQL, bukan H2 | Ya |
| ResultMap penting punya test eksplisit | Ya |
| TypeHandler custom punya round-trip test | Ya |
| Constraint penting punya negative test | Ya |
| Transaction rollback punya test | Ya |
| Optimistic locking punya affected-row test | Ya |
| Idempotency punya same-key/same-payload dan same-key/different-payload test | Ya |
| Concurrency critical path punya multi-thread test | Ya |
| Pagination punya deterministic order test | Ya |
| Batch behavior punya success dan failure test | Ya |
| Error classifier SQLSTATE punya unit test | Ya |
| HTTP error mapping punya API test | Ya |
| Test data dibersihkan deterministik | Ya |
| CI memisahkan fast tests dan heavy tests | Ya |
| Test tidak log payload sensitif | Ya |

---

## 32. Decision Table

| Problem | Test yang Dibutuhkan |
|---|---|
| Mapper XML baru | Mapper integration test |
| Custom JSONB TypeHandler | Round-trip PostgreSQL test |
| Native enum mapping | Insert/select enum test |
| Migration baru | Empty DB migration test |
| Query report berubah | Golden dataset regression test |
| State transition berubah | Unit + mapper affected-row test |
| Endpoint POST retryable | Idempotency integration + concurrency test |
| Queue worker claim | `FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED` concurrency test |
| Locking behavior | Multi-session test dengan timeout |
| Error mapping berubah | API integration test |
| Batch import berubah | Batch success/failure/chunk test |
| Slow query incident | Add regression test around query shape/data |
| Index/migration for performance | EXPLAIN smoke test if critical |

---

## 33. Roadmap Belajar Singkat

| Urutan | Materi | Target |
|---:|---|---|
| 1 | Unit test domain/service | Business logic cepat dites |
| 2 | Testcontainers PostgreSQL | Test DB nyata di CI |
| 3 | Migration test | Schema terbaru selalu valid |
| 4 | Mapper integration test | SQL/resultMap/type handler terbukti |
| 5 | Constraint dan error classification | Conflict/transient/fatal jelas |
| 6 | Transaction rollback test | Atomicity terbukti |
| 7 | Optimistic locking test | Lost update dicegah |
| 8 | Idempotency test | Retry client aman |
| 9 | Concurrency test | Race condition critical path tertangkap |
| 10 | SQL regression/performance smoke | Query penting tidak regress diam-diam |

## Response:
7/13/2026, 2:34:40 AM

Lanjut ke schema evolution dan deployment compatibility: ini krusial karena mapper MyBatis sangat dekat dengan struktur tabel/kolom, jadi perubahan schema harus aman untuk multi-instance rollout.

# Cheatsheet - Java MyBatis Migration & Schema Evolution Compatibility: Flyway/Liquibase, Backward-Compatible SQL, Expand-Contract, Zero-Downtime Deployment, dan Mapper Compatibility Testing

## 1. Introduction

Di aplikasi **Jersey/JAX-RS + MyBatis + PostgreSQL**, schema migration bukan sekadar “ubah tabel”. Karena MyBatis mapper XML biasanya menulis kolom secara eksplisit, perubahan schema langsung memengaruhi:

```text id="srwshp"
Mapper XML
ResultMap
DTO projection
TypeHandler
Service transaction
Backfill job
Index strategy
Deployment order
Rollback strategy
```

Prinsip paling aman adalah **backward-compatible schema evolution**: versi aplikasi lama dan versi aplikasi baru harus bisa berjalan bersamaan selama rolling deployment. Ini penting di Kubernetes, blue/green deployment, canary release, dan multi-instance on-prem/cloud deployment.

PostgreSQL `ALTER TABLE` punya karakteristik locking dan rewrite yang perlu diperhatikan. Dokumentasi PostgreSQL menyatakan bahwa `ALTER TABLE` dapat mengambil lock berbeda tergantung subform, dan `ACCESS EXCLUSIVE` dipakai kecuali ada catatan khusus; beberapa perubahan seperti tipe kolom tertentu dapat menyebabkan table/index rewrite yang mahal di tabel besar. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-altertable.html))

---

## 2. Mental Model

```mermaid id="mbs1g3"
flowchart TD
    A[Change Requirement] --> B[Schema Compatibility Analysis]
    B --> C{Breaking Change?}

    C -->|No| D[Simple Migration]
    C -->|Yes| E[Expand-Contract Plan]

    E --> F[Expand Schema<br/>add nullable column/table/index]
    F --> G[Deploy App Compatible with Old + New Schema]
    G --> H[Dual Write / Dual Read / Fallback Read]
    H --> I[Backfill Existing Data]
    I --> J[Validate Data]
    J --> K[Switch Read Path]
    K --> L[Stop Old Write]
    L --> M[Contract Schema<br/>drop old column/constraint/path]

    D --> N[Migration Test]
    M --> N
    N --> O[Mapper Compatibility Test]
    O --> P[Deploy]
```

**Core invariant:**

```text id="jjmwzr"
Aplikasi versi lama harus tetap jalan setelah migration expand.
Aplikasi versi baru harus tetap jalan sebelum contract cleanup.
Contract/drop/rename dilakukan paling akhir, setelah semua instance aman.
```

---

## 3. Decomposition Map

| Area | Fokus Praktis | Skill yang Dikuasai | Risiko Jika Salah |
|---|---|---|---|
| **Migration Tooling** | Flyway/Liquibase lifecycle | Versioned migration, checksum, changelog, validate | Schema drift |
| **Backward Compatibility** | App lama + baru bisa coexist | Additive schema, fallback read, dual write | Rolling deploy gagal |
| **Expand-Contract** | Breaking change bertahap | Expand → deploy → backfill → switch → contract | Downtime/data loss |
| **Mapper Compatibility** | XML mapper tetap valid | ResultMap, insert/update SQL, projection test | Runtime SQL error |
| **Column Add** | Tambah kolom aman | nullable/default, backfill, NOT NULL later | Lock/rewrite besar |
| **Column Rename** | Hindari rename langsung | add new column + dual write + contract | App lama broken |
| **Column Drop** | Drop paling akhir | code no longer reads/writes old column | App lama SQL error |
| **Type Change** | Ubah tipe tanpa rewrite berbahaya | parallel column + backfill | Table rewrite lama |
| **Constraint Evolution** | Tambah constraint aman | `NOT VALID`, backfill, `VALIDATE CONSTRAINT` | Blocking update |
| **Index Evolution** | Build index production-safe | `CREATE INDEX CONCURRENTLY` | Write blocked |
| **Backfill Job** | Isi data lama bertahap | chunking, idempotent, resumable | Lock panjang/rollback besar |
| **Dual Write** | Tulis old + new | service/repository consistency | Data divergen |
| **Dual Read/Fallback Read** | Baca new jika ada, fallback old | mapper SQL compatibility | Null/incorrect read |
| **Rollback Strategy** | Aman mundur versi aplikasi | rollback code before contract | Tidak bisa rollback |
| **Migration Testing** | Validasi dari DB kosong dan upgrade | Testcontainers + Flyway/Liquibase | Migration rusak di CI |
| **Deployment Runbook** | Urutan deploy jelas | migration before/after app | Race antar version |

---

## 4. Migration Tooling: Flyway vs Liquibase

| Aspek | Flyway | Liquibase |
|---|---|---|
| Model utama | Versioned SQL migration | Changeset di changelog |
| Gaya umum | SQL-first, sederhana | Declarative/XML/YAML/JSON/SQL |
| Cocok untuk | Tim yang nyaman review SQL langsung | Governance/metadata/precondition/rollback lebih eksplisit |
| Rollback | Umumnya forward-fix; rollback tergantung edition/pola | Bisa mendefinisikan rollback changeset |
| Auditability | Migration history table | Changelog + changeset metadata |
| MyBatis fit | Sangat cocok karena SQL eksplisit | Cocok jika organisasi standardize Liquibase |

Flyway mendukung migration validation; dokumentasi Redgate/Flyway menyebut `validateOnMigrate` mengontrol apakah validasi otomatis dipanggil saat `migrate`. ([Redgate Documentation](https://documentation.red-gate.com/fd/flyway-validate-on-migrate-setting-277579048.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) Liquibase menggunakan **changeset** sebagai unit perubahan yang disimpan di changelog, dan preconditions dapat dipakai untuk mengontrol apakah update dijalankan berdasarkan state database. ([Liquibase Docs](https://docs.liquibase.com/secure/user-guide-5-2/what-is-a-changeset?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 5. Golden Rule untuk MyBatis Schema Evolution

| Rule | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| Jangan rename kolom langsung | Mapper XML app lama akan gagal |
| Jangan drop kolom langsung | App lama masih mungkin `SELECT`/`INSERT` kolom itu |
| Jangan ubah tipe kolom besar langsung | Bisa rewrite table/index dan mengunci lama |
| Tambah kolom secara additive | Kolom baru nullable atau default aman |
| Backfill di luar migration besar | Migration harus cepat dan predictable |
| Validasi constraint bertahap | Pakai `NOT VALID` lalu `VALIDATE CONSTRAINT` |
| Index besar pakai concurrent build | Hindari blocking writes |
| Contract cleanup terakhir | Setelah semua app version tidak memakai schema lama |
| Mapper test wajib setelah migration | SQL dan resultMap harus dibuktikan |

---

## 6. Expand-Contract Pattern

### Phase 1 - Expand schema

Tambahkan struktur baru tanpa memutus app lama.

```sql id="5gw953"
ALTER TABLE orders
ADD COLUMN approval_reason text;
```

PostgreSQL menyatakan bahwa menambahkan kolom tanpa constraint akan mengisi existing row dengan `NULL`, dan menambahkan kolom dengan default non-volatile dapat disimpan di metadata sehingga tidak perlu rewrite table; tetapi default volatile, generated stored column, identity column, atau domain type tertentu dapat menyebabkan table/index rewrite. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/ddl-alter.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

### Phase 2 - Deploy app compatible

Aplikasi baru mulai mengenal kolom baru, tetapi tetap bisa berjalan jika value lama masih `NULL`.

```xml id="rmvpvi"
<select id="findById" resultMap="OrderResultMap">
  SELECT
    id,
    customer_id,
    status,
    approval_reason,
    created_at,
    updated_at
  FROM orders
  WHERE id = #{id}
</select>
```

```xml id="xxi8hd"
<resultMap id="OrderResultMap" type="com.company.app.model.Order">
  <id property="id" column="id"/>
  <result property="customerId" column="customer_id"/>
  <result property="status" column="status"/>
  <result property="approvalReason" column="approval_reason"/>
  <result property="createdAt" column="created_at"/>
  <result property="updatedAt" column="updated_at"/>
</resultMap>
```

---

### Phase 3 - Backfill

Isi data lama secara bertahap.

```sql id="x8kfjd"
UPDATE orders
SET approval_reason = 'LEGACY_APPROVED'
WHERE status = 'APPROVED'
  AND approval_reason IS NULL
LIMIT 1000;
```

PostgreSQL tidak mendukung `LIMIT` langsung pada `UPDATE` seperti contoh konseptual di atas. Untuk PostgreSQL, gunakan CTE berbasis primary key:

```sql id="d4se7m"
WITH batch AS (
    SELECT id
    FROM orders
    WHERE status = 'APPROVED'
      AND approval_reason IS NULL
    ORDER BY id
    LIMIT 1000
)
UPDATE orders o
SET approval_reason = 'LEGACY_APPROVED'
FROM batch
WHERE o.id = batch.id;
```

---

### Phase 4 - Validate

Pastikan tidak ada data lama yang belum selesai.

```sql id="eubtst"
SELECT count(*)
FROM orders
WHERE status = 'APPROVED'
  AND approval_reason IS NULL;
```

---

### Phase 5 - Contract

Setelah semua aman, tambahkan constraint atau hapus schema lama.

```sql id="8fpl0c"
ALTER TABLE orders
ALTER COLUMN approval_reason SET NOT NULL;
```

Untuk tabel besar, jangan langsung lakukan contract tanpa mengerti lock/scan. PostgreSQL menjelaskan bahwa `SET NOT NULL` biasanya perlu memastikan tidak ada row `NULL`; valid `CHECK` constraint dapat membantu melewati scan dalam kondisi tertentu. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-altertable.html))

---

## 7. Pattern: Add New Nullable Column

### Migration

```sql id="7y3x5f"
-- V20260713_01__add_order_approval_reason.sql

ALTER TABLE orders
ADD COLUMN approval_reason text;
```

### Mapper update

```xml id="a08ulp"
<insert id="insertOrder">
  INSERT INTO orders (
    id,
    customer_id,
    status,
    approval_reason,
    created_at,
    updated_at
  )
  VALUES (
    #{id},
    #{customerId},
    #{status},
    #{approvalReason},
    #{createdAt},
    #{updatedAt}
  )
</insert>
```

### Compatibility notes

| App Version | Behavior |
|---|---|
| Old app + new column exists | Aman, old app tidak tahu kolom baru |
| New app + old rows punya `NULL` | Aman jika Java model menerima nullable |
| New app writes new column | Aman |
| Setelah backfill | Bisa tambah constraint |

**Rule:** sebelum kolom menjadi `NOT NULL`, Java model dan DTO harus memperlakukan field sebagai nullable atau punya fallback.

---

## 8. Pattern: Add NOT NULL Column Safely

### Bad

```sql id="hlpcyy"
ALTER TABLE orders
ADD COLUMN risk_score integer NOT NULL;
```

Ini gagal jika tabel sudah berisi row lama karena existing row tidak punya value.

### Safer sequence

```sql id="n8mii6"
-- 1. Expand
ALTER TABLE orders
ADD COLUMN risk_score integer;

-- 2. Optional default for new writes
ALTER TABLE orders
ALTER COLUMN risk_score SET DEFAULT 0;
```

```sql id="nnyxdn"
-- 3. Backfill in chunks
WITH batch AS (
    SELECT id
    FROM orders
    WHERE risk_score IS NULL
    ORDER BY id
    LIMIT 1000
)
UPDATE orders o
SET risk_score = 0
FROM batch
WHERE o.id = batch.id;
```

```sql id="i8gfkw"
-- 4. Validate no null remains
SELECT count(*)
FROM orders
WHERE risk_score IS NULL;
```

```sql id="paxaj7"
-- 5. Contract
ALTER TABLE orders
ALTER COLUMN risk_score SET NOT NULL;
```

---

## 9. Pattern: Rename Column Safely

### Bad

```sql id="0zd3ip"
ALTER TABLE customers
RENAME COLUMN name TO legal_name;
```

Ini breaking untuk mapper lama:

```xml id="lol21u"
SELECT id, name FROM customers WHERE id = #{id}
```

### Safe expand-contract

```sql id="9upcir"
-- V1 expand
ALTER TABLE customers
ADD COLUMN legal_name text;
```

```java id="qut2qt"
public void updateCustomerName(Customer customer) {
    mapper.updateNameAndLegalName(
        customer.id(),
        customer.name(),
        customer.legalName()
    );
}
```

```xml id="st938a"
<update id="updateNameAndLegalName">
  UPDATE customers
  SET
    name = #{name},
    legal_name = #{legalName},
    updated_at = now()
  WHERE id = #{id}
</update>
```

Backfill:

```sql id="x1uz55"
WITH batch AS (
    SELECT id
    FROM customers
    WHERE legal_name IS NULL
    ORDER BY id
    LIMIT 1000
)
UPDATE customers c
SET legal_name = c.name
FROM batch
WHERE c.id = batch.id;
```

Switch read path:

```xml id="co77gq"
<select id="findById" resultMap="CustomerResultMap">
  SELECT
    id,
    COALESCE(legal_name, name) AS legal_name,
    created_at,
    updated_at
  FROM customers
  WHERE id = #{id}
</select>
```

Contract later:

```sql id="qgkcb3"
ALTER TABLE customers
DROP COLUMN name;
```

PostgreSQL `DROP COLUMN` membuat kolom tidak terlihat untuk operasi SQL, tetapi tidak langsung mereclaim disk space; space biasanya direclaim saat row diupdate kemudian, atau melalui table rewrite operation seperti `VACUUM FULL`/rewrite tertentu. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-altertable.html))

---

## 10. Pattern: Change Column Type Safely

### Problem

```sql id="7url9b"
ALTER TABLE orders
ALTER COLUMN external_ref TYPE uuid USING external_ref::uuid;
```

Untuk tabel besar, perubahan tipe bisa menyebabkan rewrite table/index. PostgreSQL menyatakan perubahan tipe kolom biasanya menyebabkan seluruh table dan index direwrite, kecuali kasus tertentu seperti binary coercible atau unconstrained domain yang tidak mengubah isi kolom. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-altertable.html))

### Safe pattern

```sql id="zopg90"
-- 1. Add new column
ALTER TABLE orders
ADD COLUMN external_ref_uuid uuid;
```

```sql id="mqvi9w"
-- 2. Backfill in chunks
WITH batch AS (
    SELECT id
    FROM orders
    WHERE external_ref_uuid IS NULL
      AND external_ref IS NOT NULL
    ORDER BY id
    LIMIT 1000
)
UPDATE orders o
SET external_ref_uuid = o.external_ref::uuid
FROM batch
WHERE o.id = batch.id;
```

```xml id="ppcpxd"
<!-- 3. New mapper reads new column with fallback during transition -->
<select id="findByExternalRef" resultMap="OrderResultMap">
  SELECT
    id,
    customer_id,
    status,
    external_ref,
    external_ref_uuid
  FROM orders
  WHERE external_ref_uuid = #{externalRef}
     OR external_ref = #{externalRefAsText}
</select>
```

```sql id="9re7ea"
-- 4. Add index after backfill or before switch, depending query shape
CREATE INDEX CONCURRENTLY orders_external_ref_uuid_idx
ON orders (external_ref_uuid);
```

```sql id="byzhui"
-- 5. Contract much later
ALTER TABLE orders
DROP COLUMN external_ref;

ALTER TABLE orders
RENAME COLUMN external_ref_uuid TO external_ref;
```

---

## 11. Pattern: Add Index Safely

### Normal index

```sql id="aawf9f"
CREATE INDEX orders_status_created_idx
ON orders (status, created_at DESC, id DESC);
```

Normal `CREATE INDEX` can block writes on the target table while index build is running; PostgreSQL documents that `CREATE INDEX CONCURRENTLY` allows index building without locking out writes, but it performs more work and cannot run inside a transaction block. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-createindex.html))

### Concurrent index

```sql id="yq405s"
CREATE INDEX CONCURRENTLY orders_status_created_idx
ON orders (status, created_at DESC, id DESC);
```

### Flyway caveat

`CREATE INDEX CONCURRENTLY` cannot run inside a transaction block in PostgreSQL, so migration tooling must support non-transactional execution for that migration. With Flyway, this usually means separating concurrent index creation into its own migration and configuring transaction behavior appropriately for that migration/tool version. The PostgreSQL restriction itself is explicit in the official documentation. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-createindex.html))

---

## 12. Pattern: Add Foreign Key / Check Constraint Safely

### Direct constraint

```sql id="3j7cgk"
ALTER TABLE orders
ADD CONSTRAINT orders_customer_fk
FOREIGN KEY (customer_id)
REFERENCES customers(id);
```

On large tables, validating all existing rows can be expensive. PostgreSQL supports `NOT VALID` for foreign-key, check, and not-null constraints; this skips the initial scan, enforces the constraint for subsequent inserts/updates, and allows later `VALIDATE CONSTRAINT`. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-altertable.html))

### Safer sequence

```sql id="ihqjy8"
ALTER TABLE orders
ADD CONSTRAINT orders_customer_fk
FOREIGN KEY (customer_id)
REFERENCES customers(id)
NOT VALID;
```

```sql id="4q0m8a"
ALTER TABLE orders
VALIDATE CONSTRAINT orders_customer_fk;
```

PostgreSQL states that `VALIDATE CONSTRAINT` scans the table for pre-existing rows and uses a `SHARE UPDATE EXCLUSIVE` lock; validation does not need to lock out concurrent updates in the same way as the initial full validation would. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-altertable.html))

---

## 13. Pattern: Add Unique Constraint Safely

For large tables, build a unique index concurrently first, then attach it as a constraint if appropriate.

```sql id="7cgnhk"
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX CONCURRENTLY customers_tenant_email_uq_idx
ON customers (tenant_id, lower(email));
```

Then, if the index is valid and matches constraint requirements, attach as constraint when applicable:

```sql id="upymx8"
ALTER TABLE customers
ADD CONSTRAINT customers_tenant_email_uq
UNIQUE USING INDEX customers_tenant_email_uq_idx;
```

PostgreSQL documents that `ADD table_constraint_using_index` can add a `PRIMARY KEY` or `UNIQUE` constraint based on an existing unique index, with restrictions such as b-tree, default sort ordering, and no expression columns/partial index. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-altertable.html))

**Note:** expression index seperti `lower(email)` tidak bisa langsung dipakai sebagai `UNIQUE USING INDEX` constraint karena restriction tersebut. Untuk case-insensitive uniqueness, gunakan expression unique index sebagai enforcement mechanism, atau gunakan tipe/extension/pattern yang sesuai standar database team.

---

## 14. Pattern: Mapper Compatibility During Migration

### Old schema

```sql id="kn9p2o"
CREATE TABLE orders (
    id uuid PRIMARY KEY,
    status text NOT NULL,
    approved_at timestamp with time zone
);
```

### New schema target

```sql id="85giuf"
CREATE TABLE orders (
    id uuid PRIMARY KEY,
    status text NOT NULL,
    approval_status text NOT NULL,
    approved_at timestamp with time zone
);
```

### Transitional mapper

```xml id="uhomwa"
<select id="findById" resultMap="OrderResultMap">
  SELECT
    id,
    status,
    COALESCE(approval_status, status) AS approval_status,
    approved_at
  FROM orders
  WHERE id = #{id}
</select>
```

### Transitional insert/update

```xml id="81xeyn"
<update id="approveOrder">
  UPDATE orders
  SET
    status = 'APPROVED',
    approval_status = 'APPROVED',
    approved_at = now()
  WHERE id = #{id}
    AND status = 'SUBMITTED'
    AND version = #{version}
</update>
```

**Rule:** selama transition, mapper boleh lebih verbose agar aman. Setelah contract selesai, baru sederhanakan.

---

## 15. Pattern: Backfill Job di Java/MyBatis

### Mapper

```java id="v60akh"
public interface OrderBackfillMapper {

    List<UUID> findOrderIdsMissingApprovalStatus(@Param("limit") int limit);

    int backfillApprovalStatus(@Param("ids") List<UUID> ids);
}
```

### XML

```xml id="3bej8t"
<select id="findOrderIdsMissingApprovalStatus" resultType="java.util.UUID">
  SELECT id
  FROM orders
  WHERE approval_status IS NULL
  ORDER BY id
  LIMIT #{limit}
</select>
```

```xml id="xxc6nd"
<update id="backfillApprovalStatus">
  UPDATE orders
  SET approval_status = status
  WHERE id IN
  <foreach collection="ids"
           item="id"
           open="("
           separator=","
           close=")">
    #{id}
  </foreach>
    AND approval_status IS NULL
</update>
```

### Service

```java id="8655ge"
public BackfillResult backfillApprovalStatus(int batchSize, int maxBatches) {
    int totalUpdated = 0;

    for (int i = 0; i < maxBatches; i++) {
        int updated = unitOfWork.transactional(ctx -> {
            OrderBackfillMapper mapper = ctx.mapper(OrderBackfillMapper.class);

            List<UUID> ids = mapper.findOrderIdsMissingApprovalStatus(batchSize);
            if (ids.isEmpty()) {
                return 0;
            }

            return mapper.backfillApprovalStatus(ids);
        });

        if (updated == 0) {
            break;
        }

        totalUpdated += updated;
    }

    return new BackfillResult(totalUpdated);
}
```

### Backfill checklist

| Checklist | Kenapa |
|---|---|
| Chunk kecil | Mengurangi lock duration |
| Idempotent update | Aman diulang |
| Progress observable | Tahu sisa data |
| Bisa pause/resume | Aman saat incident |
| Tidak satu transaksi raksasa | Mengurangi rollback cost |
| Index mendukung selector | Backfill tidak full scan terus |
| Rate limit | Tidak mengganggu traffic utama |

---

## 16. Deployment Sequence untuk Rolling Release

```text id="6xf7bn"
Release A: Expand Migration
  1. Add new nullable column/table/index/constraint NOT VALID.
  2. Do not break old app.

Release B: Compatible Application
  3. Deploy app that can read old + new schema.
  4. Dual write if needed.

Release C: Backfill
  5. Run resumable backfill job.
  6. Validate data completeness.

Release D: Switch
  7. Change read path to new schema.
  8. Stop writing old schema.

Release E: Contract
  9. Drop old column/table/index.
  10. Remove fallback code.
```

### Compatibility matrix

| Stage | Old App | New App | DB Schema |
|---|---|---|---|
| Before expand | Works | Not deployed | Old |
| After expand | Works | Works if deployed | Old + new |
| During backfill | Works | Works | Mixed data |
| After switch | Not recommended to roll back without compatibility | Works | New path primary |
| After contract | Broken if rolled back too far | Works | New only |

**Rule:** jangan lakukan contract kalau masih ada kemungkinan rollback ke app lama yang membaca/menulis schema lama.

---

## 17. Rollback Strategy

| Situation | Safe Rollback? | Strategy |
|---|---:|---|
| Setelah expand migration | Ya | Rollback app saja; schema additive tidak mengganggu |
| Setelah compatible app deploy | Ya | App lama masih bisa jalan |
| Saat backfill | Ya | Stop job; data baru bisa dibiarkan |
| Setelah switch read path | Tergantung | Pastikan fallback masih ada |
| Setelah stop old write | Tergantung | Re-enable dual write jika rollback |
| Setelah contract/drop | Sulit | Restore backup atau forward fix |

**Production principle:** rollback aplikasi jauh lebih mudah daripada rollback database destructive change. Karena itu, **database contract harus terlambat**, bukan tergesa-gesa.

---

## 18. Flyway Migration Naming Pattern

```text id="ckfx21"
V20260713_0100__add_order_approval_status.sql
V20260713_0200__backfill_order_approval_status_function.sql
V20260713_0300__add_order_approval_status_check_not_valid.sql
V20260713_0400__validate_order_approval_status_check.sql
V20260720_0100__drop_legacy_order_status_dependency.sql
```

### Recommended content split

| Migration | Isi |
|---|---|
| Additive DDL | Add column/table |
| Index migration | `CREATE INDEX CONCURRENTLY` sendiri |
| Constraint migration | `NOT VALID` dulu |
| Validate migration | `VALIDATE CONSTRAINT` sendiri |
| Contract migration | Drop/rename paling akhir |
| Backfill | Prefer job/app script, bukan migration panjang |

**Rule:** migration harus kecil, reviewable, dan punya satu tujuan jelas.

---

## 19. Liquibase Changeset Pattern

### Add column

```yaml id="f7cmbw"
databaseChangeLog:
  - changeSet:
      id: 20260713-0100-add-approval-status
      author: platform-team
      changes:
        - addColumn:
            tableName: orders
            columns:
              - column:
                  name: approval_status
                  type: text
```

### Preconditions

```yaml id="tdph7a"
databaseChangeLog:
  - changeSet:
      id: 20260713-0200-add-orders-approval-status-check
      author: platform-team
      preConditions:
        - onFail: HALT
        - tableExists:
            tableName: orders
        - columnExists:
            tableName: orders
            columnName: approval_status
      changes:
        - sql:
            sql: >
              ALTER TABLE orders
              ADD CONSTRAINT orders_approval_status_chk
              CHECK (approval_status IN ('PENDING', 'APPROVED', 'REJECTED'))
              NOT VALID;
```

Liquibase preconditions are documented as tags added to changelogs or individual changesets to control execution based on database state; if a precondition fails, Liquibase does not deploy that changeset. ([Liquibase Docs](https://docs.liquibase.com/secure/user-guide-5-1/what-are-preconditions?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 20. Migration Testing Strategy

### Test 1 - Empty DB migrate

```java id="y759ny"
@Test
void shouldMigrateEmptyDatabaseToLatestSchema() {
    Flyway flyway = Flyway.configure()
        .dataSource(
            postgres.getJdbcUrl(),
            postgres.getUsername(),
            postgres.getPassword()
        )
        .locations("classpath:db/migration")
        .load();

    MigrateResult result = flyway.migrate();

    assertThat(result.success).isTrue();
}
```

### Test 2 - Mapper works after migration

```java id="bqtpvs"
@Test
void latestSchemaShouldSupportOrderMapper() {
    runMigrations();

    try (SqlSession session = sqlSessionFactory.openSession(false)) {
        OrderMapper mapper = session.getMapper(OrderMapper.class);

        UUID id = UUID.randomUUID();
        mapper.insertOrder(Order.draft(id));
        session.commit();

        Order found = mapper.findById(id);

        assertThat(found).isNotNull();
    }
}
```

MyBatis mapper XML defines `select`, `insert`, `update`, and `delete` mapped statements, and result maps are a core feature for mapping query results back to objects; therefore mapper compatibility tests must validate both statement execution and result mapping after migration. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/sqlmap-xml.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 21. Compatibility Test Matrix

| Migration Type | Test Required |
|---|---|
| Add nullable column | Old mapper still works, new mapper handles null |
| Add NOT NULL column | Backfill + no null validation |
| Rename logical field | Old column + new column coexist test |
| Drop old column | Verify no mapper references old column |
| Change type | Round-trip old/new type during transition |
| Add index | Query still returns same result; optional EXPLAIN smoke |
| Add FK | Existing data valid; insert invalid FK fails |
| Add check constraint | Valid data passes; invalid data rejected |
| Add enum value | Java enum and DB enum synchronized |
| Add JSONB field | TypeHandler round-trip |
| Add table | Migration + mapper insert/select |
| Backfill job | Idempotent rerun test |
| Contract cleanup | App version no longer references old schema |

---

## 22. Detect Mapper References Before Contract

Before dropping or renaming a column, search mapper XML and Java code.

```bash id="4jly71"
grep -R "approval_reason" src/main/resources src/main/java
grep -R "old_status" src/main/resources src/main/java
```

Better CI guard:

```bash id="h77lwk"
if grep -R "old_status" src/main/resources/mapper; then
  echo "Mapper still references old_status"
  exit 1
fi
```

### Contract readiness checklist

| Check | Required |
|---|---:|
| No mapper XML references old column | Ya |
| No Java DTO/model depends on old field | Ya |
| No report/query uses old field | Ya |
| No stored procedure/function uses old field | Ya |
| No view/materialized view uses old field | Ya |
| No downstream consumer expects old field | Ya |
| Backfill complete | Ya |
| Production metrics show new path used | Ya |
| Rollback window closed | Ya |

---

## 23. Safe vs Risky Migration Table

| Change | Risk Level | Safer Pattern |
|---|---:|---|
| Add nullable column | Low | Direct add |
| Add column with constant default | Usually low on modern PostgreSQL | Still review lock and version |
| Add volatile default column | High | Add nullable, backfill, then default |
| Add NOT NULL column | Medium/High | Add nullable → backfill → set not null |
| Rename column | High | Add new → dual write → backfill → switch → drop old |
| Drop column | High | Contract only after code no longer references it |
| Change column type | High | Parallel column → backfill → switch |
| Add index on large table | Medium/High | `CREATE INDEX CONCURRENTLY` |
| Add FK on large table | Medium/High | `NOT VALID` → `VALIDATE CONSTRAINT` |
| Add check constraint on large table | Medium | `NOT VALID` → `VALIDATE CONSTRAINT` |
| Add enum value | Medium | Coordinate DB enum + Java enum deployment |
| Remove enum value | High | Migrate data first, deploy code, contract later |
| Split table | High | Dual write/read + backfill |
| Merge columns | High | New column + backfill + fallback read |

---

## 24. MyBatis Mapper Patterns for Compatibility

### Fallback read

```xml id="pku3ki"
<select id="findCustomer" resultMap="CustomerResultMap">
  SELECT
    id,
    COALESCE(legal_name, name) AS legal_name,
    email,
    created_at
  FROM customers
  WHERE id = #{id}
</select>
```

### Dual write

```xml id="5hitpf"
<update id="updateCustomerLegalName">
  UPDATE customers
  SET
    name = #{legalName},
    legal_name = #{legalName},
    updated_at = now()
  WHERE id = #{id}
</update>
```

### New-column write with old-column fallback

```xml id="plf710"
<insert id="insertCustomer">
  INSERT INTO customers (
    id,
    name,
    legal_name,
    email,
    created_at
  )
  VALUES (
    #{id},
    #{legalName},
    #{legalName},
    #{email},
    #{createdAt}
  )
</insert>
```

### Contract mapper

```xml id="thxl89"
<select id="findCustomer" resultMap="CustomerResultMap">
  SELECT
    id,
    legal_name,
    email,
    created_at
  FROM customers
  WHERE id = #{id}
</select>
```

---

## 25. Backfill Observability

| Metric / Log | Meaning |
|---|---|
| `backfill.rows.updated` | Progress |
| `backfill.batch.duration` | Performance |
| `backfill.remaining.rows` | Completion estimate |
| `backfill.error.count` | Stability |
| `backfill.lock.timeout` | DB contention |
| `backfill.last.processed.id` | Resume checkpoint |
| `backfill.throttle.sleep.ms` | Load control |
| `backfill.validation.failed` | Data quality issue |

### Backfill log example

```java id="1gnjkg"
log.info(
    "Backfill batch completed job={} batch={} updated={} durationMs={}",
    "orders-approval-status",
    batchNumber,
    updated,
    durationMs
);
```

---

## 26. Migration Review Checklist

| Review Question | Why It Matters |
|---|---|
| Is this change additive or breaking? | Determines deployment strategy |
| Will old app still run after migration? | Rolling deployment safety |
| Will new app run before all data is backfilled? | Mixed-state safety |
| Does migration scan/rewrite a large table? | Downtime risk |
| Does migration require strong lock? | Availability risk |
| Does index build block writes? | Production traffic risk |
| Is backfill chunked and resumable? | Operational safety |
| Are constraints validated safely? | Avoid long blocking |
| Are mapper XML/resultMap updated? | Runtime correctness |
| Are TypeHandlers impacted? | Data mapping correctness |
| Is rollback app-only possible? | Recovery |
| Is contract delayed enough? | Avoid breaking old versions |
| Are tests added? | CI safety |
| Are dashboards/alerts ready? | Production monitoring |

---

## 27. Incident Patterns

| Incident | Likely Cause | Prevention |
|---|---|---|
| App fails after deploy: column missing | App deployed before migration | Deploy expand migration first |
| Old app fails after migration | Migration dropped/renamed column too early | Additive migration only |
| Migration locks production table | Large `ALTER`, index, constraint validation | Concurrent index, `NOT VALID`, split phases |
| New field null causes NPE | Backfill incomplete and code assumes non-null | Nullable model/fallback read |
| Constraint validation fails | Existing dirty data | Precheck + cleanup before validate |
| Rollback app fails | Contract migration already ran | Delay contract |
| Query gets slower after migration | New predicate lacks index | Add index before switching read path |
| Backfill overloads DB | Batch too large/no throttle | Chunk + rate limit |
| JSONB migration corrupts data | Bad TypeHandler/conversion | Round-trip test |
| Enum migration fails | DB enum and Java enum out of sync | Deploy ordering + compatibility test |

---

| Urutan | Materi | Target |
|---:|---|---|
| 1 | Flyway/Liquibase basics | Paham migration history dan validation |
| 2 | Backward-compatible schema change | Bisa membedakan additive vs breaking |
| 3 | Expand-contract pattern | Bisa rollout breaking change tanpa downtime |
| 4 | Mapper compatibility | Bisa menjaga XML/resultMap aman selama transition |
| 5 | PostgreSQL lock/rewrite awareness | Bisa menilai risiko migration besar |
| 6 | Concurrent index dan constraint validation | Bisa menambah index/constraint production-safe |
| 7 | Backfill job design | Bisa isi data lama secara chunked/resumable |
| 8 | Contract cleanup | Bisa drop schema lama saat aman |
| 9 | Migration testing | Bisa membuktikan schema + mapper compatible |
| 10 | Deployment runbook | Bisa menjalankan rollout/rollback dengan jelas |
