# Cheatsheet - Java MyBatis Repository Pattern, DAO Boundary, Domain Model vs DTO Projection, Transaction Script vs Domain Service, dan Clean Architecture di Jersey/JAX-RS

## 1. Introduction

Di aplikasi **Jersey/JAX-RS + MyBatis**, masalah desain yang sering muncul adalah mapper MyBatis dipakai langsung dari mana-mana:

```text id="kgrjax"
Resource -> Mapper
Service -> Mapper
Validator -> Mapper
Scheduler -> Mapper
Event handler -> Mapper
```

Ini bekerja pada awalnya, tetapi cepat menjadi sulit dirawat karena SQL detail, transaction boundary, dan domain decision tersebar ke seluruh aplikasi.

MyBatis sendiri memang menyediakan `SqlSession` sebagai interface utama untuk menjalankan command, mengambil mapper, dan mengelola transaksi. Mapper XML juga memang menjadi pusat mapped statement seperti `select`, `insert`, `update`, `delete`, `resultMap`, dan fragment SQL. Artinya, MyBatis sangat powerful, tetapi perlu boundary agar tidak bocor ke seluruh application layer. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/java-api.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 2. Mental Model

```mermaid id="0vvd7v"
flowchart TD
    A[JAX-RS Resource] --> B[Application Service / Use Case]
    B --> C{Use Case Complexity}

    C -->|Simple CRUD / Admin| D[Transaction Script]
    C -->|Stateful Domain Workflow| E[Domain Service / Aggregate Logic]

    D --> F[Repository Interface]
    E --> F

    F --> G[MyBatis Repository Implementation]
    G --> H[MyBatis Mapper Interface]
    H --> I[Mapper XML SQL]
    I --> J[(PostgreSQL)]

    B --> K[Unit of Work / Transaction Boundary]
    K --> G

    L[DTO Projection Query] --> H
    M[Domain Object Load] --> H
```

**Core invariant:**

```text id="v6eflz"
JAX-RS Resource:
  HTTP concern only

Application Service:
  use case orchestration + transaction boundary

Domain Model / Domain Service:
  business rules, state transition, invariants

Repository:
  persistence abstraction

MyBatis Mapper:
  SQL execution contract

Mapper XML:
  SQL and result mapping
```

---

## 3. Decomposition Map

| Area | Fokus Praktis | Skill yang Dikuasai | Risiko Jika Salah |
|---|---|---|---|
| **Resource Boundary** | HTTP contract saja | request/response DTO, status code, validation | Resource berubah jadi god class |
| **Application Service** | Use case orchestration | transaction, authorization check, repository call | Business flow tersebar |
| **Repository Interface** | Abstraksi persistence | domain-oriented methods | Mapper detail bocor |
| **MyBatis Repository Impl** | Adapt repository ke mapper | session context, mapper call, mapping | `SqlSession` salah scope |
| **Mapper Interface** | SQL method contract | query/update method, `@Param` | Method terlalu generic |
| **Mapper XML** | SQL eksplisit | resultMap, dynamic SQL, projection | SQL tidak maintainable |
| **DAO vs Repository** | Bedakan table access vs domain persistence | DAO data-centric, repository domain-centric | Layering berlebihan atau kacau |
| **Domain Model** | Business invariant | state transition, validation | Anemic model berlebihan |
| **DTO Projection** | Read model efisien | list/search/report query | Over-fetching domain aggregate |
| **Transaction Script** | Simple procedural use case | CRUD sederhana, admin operation | Domain complex jadi procedural spaghetti |
| **Domain Service** | Complex workflow logic | cross-aggregate policy, state machine | Logic tersebar di SQL/resource |
| **CQRS-lite** | Pisahkan command/read query | command repository, query mapper | Satu model dipaksa untuk semua |
| **Unit of Work** | Transaction boundary | one command = one transaction | Partial write |
| **Clean Architecture** | Dependency direction | outer layer depends on inner contract | Framework menguasai domain |
| **Testing Boundary** | Mock domain contract, integration test mapper | unit vs integration split | Test lambat atau palsu |

---

## 4. Layering yang Direkomendasikan

```text id="j6div9"
api/
  CaseResource.java
  request/
    ApproveCaseRequest.java
  response/
    CaseResponse.java

application/
  CaseApplicationService.java
  command/
    ApproveCaseCommand.java
    AssignCaseCommand.java

domain/
  Case.java
  CaseStatus.java
  CasePolicy.java
  CaseTransitionException.java

repository/
  CaseRepository.java
  CaseQueryRepository.java

persistence/mybatis/
  MyBatisCaseRepository.java
  MyBatisCaseQueryRepository.java
  mapper/
    CaseMapper.java
    CaseQueryMapper.java

resources/mapper/
  CaseMapper.xml
  CaseQueryMapper.xml
```

**Practical rule:**  
`domain/` tidak boleh import MyBatis, Jersey, JDBC, PostgreSQL driver, atau framework persistence lain.

---

## 5. Dependency Direction

```mermaid id="k4ex7y"
flowchart LR
    A[api / Jersey] --> B[application service]
    B --> C[domain]
    B --> D[repository interface]
    E[persistence / MyBatis] --> D
    E --> F[MyBatis mapper]
    F --> G[Mapper XML]
    G --> H[(PostgreSQL)]
```

```text id="ee2p05"
Allowed:
  api -> application
  application -> domain
  application -> repository interface
  persistence -> repository interface
  persistence -> MyBatis

Not allowed:
  domain -> MyBatis
  domain -> Jersey
  api -> mapper XML
  resource -> SqlSession
```

Jakarta REST/JAX-RS sendiri berperan sebagai API untuk membangun service REST, dengan `@ApplicationPath` sebagai base URI bagi resource URI yang disediakan oleh `@Path`. Jadi, JAX-RS layer sebaiknya tetap berada pada boundary HTTP, bukan menjadi persistence orchestration layer. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/restful-ws/3.1/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 6. DAO vs Repository

| Konsep | DAO | Repository |
|---|---|---|
| Orientasi | Data access/table/query | Domain collection/aggregate |
| Method style | `insert`, `update`, `selectById` | `save`, `findById`, `findForApproval` |
| Return type | Row/entity/DTO | Domain object / aggregate |
| Cocok untuk | Thin CRUD, simple table access | Domain workflow |
| SQL awareness | Lebih dekat ke SQL | Menyembunyikan SQL detail |
| MyBatis fit | Mapper sering mirip DAO | Repository membungkus mapper |
| Risiko | Bocor ke service sebagai table script | Bisa over-abstract kalau domain sederhana |

### Mapper sebagai DAO-like contract

```java id="i09kcr"
public interface CaseMapper {
    CaseRecord findById(@Param("id") UUID id);

    int insert(CaseRecord record);

    int updateStatus(
        @Param("id") UUID id,
        @Param("fromStatus") String fromStatus,
        @Param("toStatus") String toStatus,
        @Param("version") long version
    );
}
```

### Repository sebagai domain-oriented contract

```java id="agpf2g"
public interface CaseRepository {
    Case findById(CaseId id);

    void save(Case aggregate);

    boolean transitionStatus(
        CaseId id,
        CaseStatus from,
        CaseStatus to,
        long expectedVersion
    );
}
```

**Rule:** MyBatis mapper boleh data-oriented. Repository sebaiknya domain-oriented.

---

## 7. Resource Layer Pattern

### Resource hanya HTTP adapter

```java id="uuuzut"
@Path("/cases")
public class CaseResource {

    private final CaseApplicationService caseApplicationService;

    @Inject
    public CaseResource(CaseApplicationService caseApplicationService) {
        this.caseApplicationService = caseApplicationService;
    }

    @POST
    @Path("/{id}/approve")
    public Response approve(@PathParam("id") UUID id,
                            @Valid ApproveCaseRequest request) {

        ApproveCaseCommand command = new ApproveCaseCommand(
            id,
            request.expectedVersion(),
            request.reason()
        );

        CaseResponse response = caseApplicationService.approve(command);

        return Response.ok(response).build();
    }
}
```

### Jangan seperti ini

```java id="ux8a84"
@Path("/cases")
public class CaseResource {

    @Inject
    SqlSessionFactory sqlSessionFactory;

    @POST
    @Path("/{id}/approve")
    public Response approve(@PathParam("id") UUID id) {
        try (SqlSession session = sqlSessionFactory.openSession(false)) {
            CaseMapper mapper = session.getMapper(CaseMapper.class);
            mapper.updateStatus(id, "APPROVED");
            session.commit();
            return Response.ok().build();
        }
    }
}
```

**Masalah:**

```text id="vq4k7b"
- Resource tahu MyBatis
- Resource mengelola transaction
- Tidak ada application service
- Business rule kemungkinan bercampur HTTP
- Sulit test use case tanpa Jersey/MyBatis
```

---

## 8. Application Service Pattern

Application service menjalankan use case. Ia bukan tempat semua business rule detail, tetapi tempat orchestration:

```text id="gtcq2n"
- authorization check
- load aggregate/read state
- call domain behavior
- persist result
- write audit/outbox
- commit transaction
```

```java id="7953yg"
public class CaseApplicationService {

    private final MyBatisUnitOfWorkFactory unitOfWorkFactory;
    private final CaseRepositoryFactory repositoryFactory;
    private final AuthorizationService authorizationService;

    @Inject
    public CaseApplicationService(
        MyBatisUnitOfWorkFactory unitOfWorkFactory,
        CaseRepositoryFactory repositoryFactory,
        AuthorizationService authorizationService
    ) {
        this.unitOfWorkFactory = unitOfWorkFactory;
        this.repositoryFactory = repositoryFactory;
        this.authorizationService = authorizationService;
    }

    public CaseResponse approve(ApproveCaseCommand command) {
        return unitOfWorkFactory.transactional(ctx -> {
            CaseRepository caseRepository =
                repositoryFactory.caseRepository(ctx);

            Case aggregate = caseRepository.findById(
                new CaseId(command.caseId())
            );

            if (aggregate == null) {
                throw new CaseNotFoundException(command.caseId());
            }

            authorizationService.requireCanApprove(aggregate);

            aggregate.approve(command.reason());

            caseRepository.save(aggregate);

            return CaseResponse.from(aggregate);
        });
    }
}
```

**Rule:** application service tahu “urutan use case”, domain model tahu “aturan validitas”.

---

## 9. Domain Model Pattern

```java id="2z6uis"
public final class Case {

    private final CaseId id;
    private CaseStatus status;
    private long version;
    private String approvalReason;

    public Case(CaseId id, CaseStatus status, long version, String approvalReason) {
        this.id = id;
        this.status = status;
        this.version = version;
        this.approvalReason = approvalReason;
    }

    public void approve(String reason) {
        if (status != CaseStatus.SUBMITTED) {
            throw new InvalidCaseTransitionException(status, CaseStatus.APPROVED);
        }

        if (reason == null || reason.isBlank()) {
            throw new InvalidApprovalReasonException();
        }

        this.status = CaseStatus.APPROVED;
        this.approvalReason = reason;
        this.version++;
    }

    public CaseId id() {
        return id;
    }

    public CaseStatus status() {
        return status;
    }

    public long version() {
        return version;
    }

    public String approvalReason() {
        return approvalReason;
    }
}
```

### Domain model cocok jika:

| Kondisi | Domain Model Layak? |
|---|---:|
| Ada state transition | Ya |
| Ada invariant kompleks | Ya |
| Ada behavior selain getter/setter | Ya |
| Ada lifecycle audit/regulatory | Ya |
| CRUD admin sederhana | Tidak wajib |
| Query report/list | Biasanya tidak |

---

## 10. Persistence Record vs Domain Object

Sering lebih sehat memisahkan **persistence record** dari **domain object**.

### Persistence record

```java id="dwiv9q"
public record CaseRecord(
    UUID id,
    String status,
    long version,
    String approvalReason,
    Instant createdAt,
    Instant updatedAt
) {}
```

### Domain object

```java id="y3oykj"
public final class Case {
    private final CaseId id;
    private CaseStatus status;
    private long version;
    private String approvalReason;

    // domain behavior
}
```

### Mapper converter

```java id="toz31l"
public final class CasePersistenceMapper {

    public Case toDomain(CaseRecord record) {
        return new Case(
            new CaseId(record.id()),
            CaseStatus.valueOf(record.status()),
            record.version(),
            record.approvalReason()
        );
    }

    public CaseRecord toRecord(Case aggregate) {
        return new CaseRecord(
            aggregate.id().value(),
            aggregate.status().name(),
            aggregate.version(),
            aggregate.approvalReason(),
            null,
            null
        );
    }
}
```

| Approach | Kelebihan | Kekurangan |
|---|---|---|
| Mapper langsung ke domain | Simpel | Domain harus persistence-friendly |
| Mapper ke record lalu convert | Boundary bersih | Tambah mapping code |
| Mapper ke DTO untuk read | Efisien | Bukan domain behavior |
| Satu class untuk semua | Cepat awal | Cepat kacau saat domain tumbuh |

---

## 11. Repository Implementation dengan MyBatis

```java id="6cp47p"
public class MyBatisCaseRepository implements CaseRepository {

    private final MyBatisSessionContext context;
    private final CasePersistenceMapper persistenceMapper;

    public MyBatisCaseRepository(
        MyBatisSessionContext context,
        CasePersistenceMapper persistenceMapper
    ) {
        this.context = context;
        this.persistenceMapper = persistenceMapper;
    }

    @Override
    public Case findById(CaseId id) {
        CaseMapper mapper = context.mapper(CaseMapper.class);

        CaseRecord record = mapper.findById(id.value());

        return record == null ? null : persistenceMapper.toDomain(record);
    }

    @Override
    public void save(Case aggregate) {
        CaseMapper mapper = context.mapper(CaseMapper.class);

        int updated = mapper.updateCase(
            aggregate.id().value(),
            aggregate.status().name(),
            aggregate.approvalReason(),
            aggregate.version() - 1
        );

        if (updated != 1) {
            throw new ConcurrentCaseModificationException(aggregate.id().value());
        }
    }
}
```

**Important:** repository implementation ini **session-scoped**, karena menyimpan `MyBatisSessionContext`. Jangan jadikan singleton global.

---

## 12. Repository Factory Pattern

Agar repository tidak singleton dengan session stale, buat factory.

```java id="j1gu3o"
public class CaseRepositoryFactory {

    private final CasePersistenceMapper persistenceMapper;

    @Inject
    public CaseRepositoryFactory(CasePersistenceMapper persistenceMapper) {
        this.persistenceMapper = persistenceMapper;
    }

    public CaseRepository caseRepository(MyBatisSessionContext context) {
        return new MyBatisCaseRepository(context, persistenceMapper);
    }

    public CaseQueryRepository caseQueryRepository(MyBatisSessionContext context) {
        return new MyBatisCaseQueryRepository(context);
    }
}
```

Usage:

```java id="iy7t5y"
return unitOfWorkFactory.transactional(ctx -> {
    CaseRepository repository =
        repositoryFactory.caseRepository(ctx);

    Case aggregate = repository.findById(caseId);
    aggregate.approve(reason);
    repository.save(aggregate);

    return CaseResponse.from(aggregate);
});
```

---

## 13. Mapper XML Tetap SQL-Oriented

```xml id="qwd3lr"
<mapper namespace="com.company.app.persistence.mybatis.mapper.CaseMapper">

  <resultMap id="CaseRecordResultMap"
             type="com.company.app.persistence.mybatis.record.CaseRecord">
    <constructor>
      <idArg column="id" javaType="java.util.UUID"/>
      <arg column="status" javaType="string"/>
      <arg column="version" javaType="long"/>
      <arg column="approval_reason" javaType="string"/>
      <arg column="created_at" javaType="java.time.Instant"/>
      <arg column="updated_at" javaType="java.time.Instant"/>
    </constructor>
  </resultMap>

  <select id="findById" resultMap="CaseRecordResultMap">
    SELECT
      id,
      status,
      version,
      approval_reason,
      created_at,
      updated_at
    FROM cases
    WHERE id = #{id}
  </select>

  <update id="updateCase">
    UPDATE cases
    SET
      status = #{status},
      approval_reason = #{approvalReason},
      version = version + 1,
      updated_at = now()
    WHERE id = #{id}
      AND version = #{expectedVersion}
  </update>

</mapper>
```

MyBatis Mapper XML memang dirancang untuk mendefinisikan mapped statement dan `resultMap` sebagai elemen kuat untuk mapping hasil database ke object. Jadi biarkan XML fokus pada SQL dan mapping, bukan business orchestration. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/sqlmap-xml.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 14. Domain Model vs DTO Projection

### Jangan pakai domain aggregate untuk semua query

```java id="p6zlzs"
List<Case> cases = caseRepository.searchOpenCases(team);
```

Masalah:

```text id="e2ejag"
- Over-fetching
- Domain object terlalu berat
- Query list butuh field berbeda dari command object
- ResultMap aggregate kompleks
```

### Gunakan DTO projection untuk read/list/report

```java id="tl6c17"
public record CaseQueueItemDto(
    UUID id,
    String caseNumber,
    String status,
    String priority,
    Instant dueAt
) {}
```

```java id="njcu41"
public interface CaseQueryRepository {
    List<CaseQueueItemDto> findQueueItems(CaseQueueQuery query);
}
```

```xml id="3hn9ov"
<select id="findQueueItems"
        resultType="com.company.app.api.response.CaseQueueItemDto">
  SELECT
    id,
    case_number AS caseNumber,
    status,
    priority,
    due_at AS dueAt
  FROM cases
  WHERE assigned_team = #{query.team}
    AND status IN ('OPEN', 'IN_REVIEW')
  ORDER BY due_at ASC, id ASC
  LIMIT #{query.limit}
</select>
```

| Use Case | Return Type |
|---|---|
| Approve/reject/assign case | Domain aggregate |
| Search queue | DTO projection |
| Dashboard count | Scalar/summary DTO |
| Export audit log | Streaming DTO |
| Reference data dropdown | Lightweight DTO |
| State transition validation | Minimal domain state |
| Full detail page | Detail DTO or aggregate + child DTO |

---

## 15. CQRS-lite dengan MyBatis

Tidak perlu full CQRS/event sourcing. Cukup pisahkan **command repository** dan **query repository**.

```text id="f34jhb"
Command side:
  CaseRepository
  load domain aggregate
  enforce invariant
  save with optimistic locking

Query side:
  CaseQueryRepository
  projection SQL
  joins optimized for screen/report
  no domain behavior
```

### Command repository

```java id="viffpy"
public interface CaseRepository {
    Case findById(CaseId id);

    void save(Case aggregate);
}
```

### Query repository

```java id="j0t0h8"
public interface CaseQueryRepository {
    List<CaseQueueItemDto> findQueueItems(CaseQueueQuery query);

    CaseDetailDto findDetail(UUID caseId);

    CaseDashboardSummaryDto summarizeByTeam(String team);
}
```

### Why MyBatis cocok untuk CQRS-lite?

Karena MyBatis memberi kontrol SQL eksplisit. Query read model bisa dioptimalkan untuk layar/report tertentu tanpa memaksa object graph domain. MyBatis juga memang lebih sederhana sebagai SQL mapper dibanding full ORM, dengan mapped SQL melalui XML atau annotations. ([GitHub](https://github.com/mybatis/mybatis-3?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 16. Transaction Script vs Domain Service

### Transaction Script

Transaction script cocok untuk use case sederhana: langkah procedural langsung.

```java id="s2ho10"
public void archiveCustomer(UUID customerId) {
    unitOfWork.transactional(ctx -> {
        CustomerMapper mapper = ctx.mapper(CustomerMapper.class);

        int updated = mapper.archive(customerId);

        if (updated != 1) {
            throw new CustomerNotFoundException(customerId);
        }

        return null;
    });
}
```

### Domain Service / Domain Model

Cocok untuk workflow yang punya invariant dan lifecycle.

```java id="5fmnl6"
public void approveCase(ApproveCaseCommand command) {
    unitOfWork.transactional(ctx -> {
        CaseRepository repository = repositoryFactory.caseRepository(ctx);

        Case aggregate = repository.findById(new CaseId(command.caseId()));

        caseApprovalPolicy.assertCanApprove(aggregate, command.actor());

        aggregate.approve(command.reason());

        repository.save(aggregate);

        return null;
    });
}
```

| Kondisi | Transaction Script | Domain Service / Model |
|---|---:|---:|
| CRUD sederhana | Cocok | Bisa overkill |
| Admin maintenance | Cocok | Tidak wajib |
| State machine | Kurang cocok | Cocok |
| Regulatory workflow | Kurang defensible | Cocok |
| Cross-entity invariant | Berisiko | Cocok |
| Reporting query | Cocok via query repo | Domain model tidak perlu |
| Complex approval/escalation | Tidak ideal | Cocok |
| High throughput queue update sederhana | Cocok | Tergantung invariant |

---

## 17. Domain Service vs Application Service

| Layer | Tanggung Jawab | Contoh |
|---|---|---|
| Application Service | Orkestrasi use case dan transaction | `approveCase(command)` |
| Domain Service | Business rule yang tidak natural di satu aggregate | `CaseApprovalPolicy` |
| Repository | Simpan/ambil aggregate/read model | `caseRepository.save(case)` |
| Mapper | SQL statement | `updateCase(...)` |
| Resource | HTTP adapter | `POST /cases/{id}/approve` |

### Domain service example

```java id="7vafri"
public class CaseApprovalPolicy {

    public void assertCanApprove(Case aggregate, Actor actor) {
        if (!actor.hasPermission("CASE_APPROVE")) {
            throw new PermissionDeniedException();
        }

        if (aggregate.status() != CaseStatus.SUBMITTED) {
            throw new InvalidCaseTransitionException(
                aggregate.status(),
                CaseStatus.APPROVED
            );
        }

        if (aggregate.isAssignedTo(actor.id())) {
            throw new SelfApprovalNotAllowedException();
        }
    }
}
```

**Rule:** application service bertanya ke domain service; domain service tidak membuka `SqlSession`.

---

## 18. Repository Method Naming

| Kurang Bagus | Lebih Bagus | Alasan |
|---|---|---|
| `update(Case c)` | `save(Case aggregate)` | Domain-oriented |
| `selectByStatus` | `findCasesEligibleForEscalation` | Intent jelas |
| `deleteById` | `archive` | Sesuai domain behavior |
| `getData` | `findQueueItems` | Query purpose jelas |
| `executeQuery` | Jangan | Hilang type safety |
| `changeStatus` | `transitionStatus` | Menunjukkan lifecycle |
| `insertAudit` | `recordAuditEvent` | Intent domain/audit jelas |

### Mapper boleh lebih teknis

```java id="8br91g"
int updateStatusWithVersion(
    @Param("id") UUID id,
    @Param("fromStatus") String fromStatus,
    @Param("toStatus") String toStatus,
    @Param("expectedVersion") long expectedVersion
);
```

### Repository lebih semantik

```java id="osp96p"
boolean transitionStatus(
    CaseId id,
    CaseStatus from,
    CaseStatus to,
    long expectedVersion
);
```

---

## 19. Boundary untuk Transaction

### Good

```text id="g7akbz"
ApplicationService
  -> unitOfWork.transactional
    -> repository
      -> mapper
```

### Bad

```text id="iamntj"
Repository
  -> opens session
  -> commits transaction

Service
  -> calls multiple repositories
  -> partial commit risk
```

Repository sebaiknya tidak diam-diam membuka dan commit transaksi jika use case bisa memanggil beberapa repository.

```java id="dciq2h"
public void submitOrder(SubmitOrderCommand command) {
    unitOfWork.transactional(ctx -> {
        OrderRepository orderRepository =
            repositoryFactory.orderRepository(ctx);

        OutboxRepository outboxRepository =
            repositoryFactory.outboxRepository(ctx);

        Order order = orderRepository.findById(command.orderId());
        order.submit();

        orderRepository.save(order);
        outboxRepository.add(OrderSubmittedEvent.from(order));

        return null;
    });
}
```

**Invariant:** business data dan outbox event commit bersama.

---

## 20. Anti-Corruption Layer untuk Legacy Schema

Jika schema lama tidak cocok dengan domain, jangan paksa domain mengikuti schema.

### Legacy table

```sql id="xj8t7y"
CREATE TABLE t_case_master (
    case_id varchar(50) PRIMARY KEY,
    stat_cd varchar(20),
    aprv_flg char(1),
    upd_ts timestamp
);
```

### Persistence record

```java id="bduz6o"
public record LegacyCaseRow(
    String caseId,
    String statusCode,
    String approvalFlag,
    Instant updatedAt
) {}
```

### Adapter mapper

```java id="a3hc36"
public final class LegacyCaseAdapter {

    public Case toDomain(LegacyCaseRow row) {
        CaseStatus status = switch (row.statusCode()) {
            case "SUB" -> CaseStatus.SUBMITTED;
            case "APR" -> CaseStatus.APPROVED;
            case "REJ" -> CaseStatus.REJECTED;
            default -> throw new UnknownLegacyStatusException(row.statusCode());
        };

        return new Case(
            new CaseId(UUID.fromString(row.caseId())),
            status,
            0L,
            null
        );
    }
}
```

**Rule:** domain naming tidak harus mengikuti column naming legacy.

---

## 21. Handling Cross-Repository Use Case

Contoh: approve case + write audit + create outbox.

```java id="4f6q3d"
public CaseResponse approve(ApproveCaseCommand command) {
    return unitOfWork.transactional(ctx -> {
        CaseRepository caseRepository =
            repositoryFactory.caseRepository(ctx);

        AuditRepository auditRepository =
            repositoryFactory.auditRepository(ctx);

        OutboxRepository outboxRepository =
            repositoryFactory.outboxRepository(ctx);

        Case aggregate = caseRepository.findById(new CaseId(command.caseId()));

        aggregate.approve(command.reason());

        caseRepository.save(aggregate);

        auditRepository.record(AuditEntry.caseApproved(aggregate));

        outboxRepository.add(OutboxEvent.caseApproved(aggregate));

        return CaseResponse.from(aggregate);
    });
}
```

**Good sign:** semua repository memakai `ctx` yang sama, jadi satu `SqlSession` dan satu transaction boundary.

---

## 22. Read Model Repository

```java id="730y7j"
public class MyBatisCaseQueryRepository implements CaseQueryRepository {

    private final MyBatisSessionContext context;

    public MyBatisCaseQueryRepository(MyBatisSessionContext context) {
        this.context = context;
    }

    @Override
    public List<CaseQueueItemDto> findQueueItems(CaseQueueQuery query) {
        return context.mapper(CaseQueryMapper.class)
            .findQueueItems(query);
    }

    @Override
    public CaseDashboardSummaryDto summarizeByTeam(String team) {
        return context.mapper(CaseQueryMapper.class)
            .summarizeByTeam(team);
    }
}
```

### Query mapper

```java id="9gpu26"
public interface CaseQueryMapper {

    List<CaseQueueItemDto> findQueueItems(@Param("query") CaseQueueQuery query);

    CaseDashboardSummaryDto summarizeByTeam(@Param("team") String team);
}
```

**Rule:** query repository tidak perlu load domain aggregate kalau tujuannya hanya read projection.

---

## 23. DTO Boundary

### Request DTO

```java id="fawz1k"
public record ApproveCaseRequest(
    @NotNull Long expectedVersion,
    @NotBlank String reason
) {}
```

### Command

```java id="gr0g4i"
public record ApproveCaseCommand(
    UUID caseId,
    long expectedVersion,
    String reason
) {}
```

### Response DTO

```java id="tmh2ll"
public record CaseResponse(
    UUID id,
    String status,
    long version
) {
    public static CaseResponse from(Case aggregate) {
        return new CaseResponse(
            aggregate.id().value(),
            aggregate.status().name(),
            aggregate.version()
        );
    }
}
```

| Object | Layer | Purpose |
|---|---|---|
| Request DTO | API | Input validation/JSON binding |
| Command | Application | Use case input |
| Domain object | Domain | Behavior/invariant |
| Persistence record | Persistence | DB row shape |
| Query DTO | Query/API | Read projection |
| Response DTO | API | Output contract |

**Rule:** jangan pakai JAX-RS request DTO langsung sebagai mapper parameter untuk domain write kalau field-nya bukan persistence contract.

---

## 24. Mapping Strategy

| Mapping | Di Mana | Kenapa |
|---|---|---|
| Request DTO → Command | Resource | HTTP ke use case |
| Command → Domain behavior | Application/domain | Business logic |
| Domain → Persistence record | Repository adapter | Persistence boundary |
| Persistence record → Domain | Repository adapter | Reconstruct aggregate |
| Query row → DTO | Mapper XML / query repo | Efficient read |
| Domain → Response DTO | Resource/application mapper | API contract |

### Jangan campur seperti ini

```java id="9rxoc1"
mapper.insert(requestDto);
```

Karena request DTO adalah kontrak HTTP, bukan kontrak database.

---

## 25. Clean Architecture Fit

| Clean Architecture Concept | Implementasi di MyBatis/Jersey |
|---|---|
| Entity | Domain object seperti `Case`, `Order` |
| Use Case | Application service seperti `ApproveCaseService` |
| Interface Adapter | JAX-RS Resource, repository implementation |
| Framework/Driver | Jersey, MyBatis, PostgreSQL |
| Gateway Interface | Repository interface |
| Gateway Implementation | MyBatis repository |
| Presenter/ViewModel | Response DTO |
| Data Mapper | MyBatis Mapper XML |

**Practical point:** tidak perlu terlalu dogmatis. Tujuannya bukan banyak layer, tetapi dependency dan responsibility tetap jelas.

---

## 26. Folder Structure - Pragmatic Enterprise

```text id="a5oxgb"
com.company.caseapp
  api
    CaseResource
    request
    response
    mapper  // API DTO mapper, not MyBatis mapper

  application
    CaseApplicationService
    command
    result

  domain
    model
      Case
      CaseId
      CaseStatus
    policy
      CaseApprovalPolicy
    exception

  repository
    CaseRepository
    CaseQueryRepository
    AuditRepository
    OutboxRepository

  persistence
    mybatis
      MyBatisUnitOfWorkFactory
      MyBatisSessionContext
      RepositoryFactory
      repository
        MyBatisCaseRepository
        MyBatisCaseQueryRepository
      mapper
        CaseMapper
        CaseQueryMapper
      record
        CaseRecord
      typehandler
```

## 27. Testing Boundary

| Layer | Test Type | Mock? |
|---|---|---|
| Domain model | Unit test | No |
| Domain service/policy | Unit test | Fake object okay |
| Application service | Unit test + integration | Mock/fake repository for unit |
| Repository interface contract | Integration | Real MyBatis/PostgreSQL |
| Mapper XML | Integration | No mock |
| Resource | API integration | Real/fake app service depending test |
| Error mapper | Unit/API test | Depends |

### Domain unit test

```java id="m9bp5d"
@Test
void submittedCaseCanBeApproved() {
    Case aggregate = CaseFixture.submitted();

    aggregate.approve("Valid reason");

    assertThat(aggregate.status()).isEqualTo(CaseStatus.APPROVED);
}
```

### Repository integration test

```java id="3e9mnp"
@Test
void shouldLoadAndSaveCaseAggregate() {
    UUID id = fixture.insertSubmittedCase();

    try (SqlSession session = openSession()) {
        MyBatisSessionContext ctx = new MyBatisSessionContext(session);
        CaseRepository repository = repositoryFactory.caseRepository(ctx);

        Case aggregate = repository.findById(new CaseId(id));
        aggregate.approve("Valid reason");

        repository.save(aggregate);

        session.commit();
    }

    assertThat(fixture.findCaseStatus(id)).isEqualTo("APPROVED");
}
```

---

## 28. When to Skip Repository Layer

Repository layer bukan kewajiban mutlak.

| Kondisi | Boleh Langsung Mapper dari Service? |
|---|---:|
| Aplikasi kecil | Bisa |
| CRUD sederhana | Bisa |
| Tidak ada domain behavior | Bisa |
| Query/report only | Bisa via query mapper |
| Complex state machine | Sebaiknya repository/domain |
| Regulatory audit workflow | Sebaiknya repository/domain |
| Banyak persistence detail | Sebaiknya repository |
| Perlu test domain isolated | Sebaiknya repository interface |

### Pragmatic compromise

```text id="mve58v"
Command/write side:
  ApplicationService -> Repository -> Mapper

Read/query side:
  ApplicationService -> QueryRepository/Mapper -> DTO
```

Ini biasanya cukup bersih tanpa overengineering.

---

## 29. Smell Detector

| Smell | Arti |
|---|---|
| Resource membuka `SqlSession` | HTTP layer bocor ke persistence |
| Domain class import `org.apache.ibatis.*` | Domain terikat framework |
| Mapper dipakai dari 10 service berbeda | Query contract terlalu global |
| Mapper method `execute(String sql)` | SQL injection dan auditability risk |
| Service berisi SQL fragments | Persistence concern bocor |
| ResultMap langsung ke API response untuk command | API dan persistence coupling |
| Satu class `Order` dipakai request, domain, DB, response | Model terlalu banyak tanggung jawab |
| Repository commit sendiri | Transaction boundary pecah |
| Query list load aggregate penuh | Over-fetching |
| Business invariant hanya di resource | Worker/scheduler bisa bypass |
| Business invariant hanya di SQL | Sulit unit test/domain reasoning |
| DTO punya behavior domain penting | Boundary kabur |
| Domain object hanya getter/setter | Mungkin anemic atau memang CRUD sederhana |

---

## 30. Anti-Pattern

| Anti-Pattern | Contoh | Dampak |
|---|---|---|
| Resource → Mapper langsung | `resource.approve()` buka mapper | Sulit test dan transaction kacau |
| Singleton repository menyimpan session | `private SqlSession session;` | Thread-safety bug |
| Domain object adalah DB row mentah | `statusCode`, `aprvFlg` bocor | Domain sulit dipahami |
| Mapper sebagai god interface | `ApplicationMapper` 200 method | Sulit maintain |
| Repository terlalu generic | `save(Object)` | Hilang type safety |
| Semua query lewat aggregate | List page load object penuh | Performa buruk |
| Semua logic di service | 1000-line transaction script | Sulit reasoning |
| Semua logic di SQL | Java hanya pass-through | Sulit test business rule |
| Banyak layer untuk CRUD trivial | Boilerplate tinggi | Tim lambat |
| Contract DTO = table schema | API breaking saat schema berubah | Coupling eksternal |
| Repository membuka transaksi sendiri | Multi-repository use case partial commit | Atomicity rusak |

---

## 31. Decision Table

| Problem | Pattern |
|---|---|
| Simple CRUD endpoint | Transaction script + mapper/query repository |
| Complex state transition | Application service + domain model + repository |
| Report/dashboard query | Query repository + DTO projection |
| Legacy schema naming buruk | Persistence record + anti-corruption mapper |
| Need audit + outbox same transaction | Application service owns unit-of-work |
| Need high-performance list | DTO projection mapper, not aggregate |
| Need reusable domain logic | Domain model/domain service |
| Need test business rule without DB | Repository interface + domain unit test |
| Mapper leaking everywhere | Introduce repository boundary |
| Too much boilerplate | Keep repository only for command side |
| Multiple DB operations atomic | One unit-of-work in application service |
| Read model very different from write model | CQRS-lite split |

---

## 32. Production Checklist

| Checklist | Status Ideal |
|---|---|
| JAX-RS resource tidak membuka `SqlSession` | Ya |
| Transaction boundary ada di application service | Ya |
| Domain layer bebas dari MyBatis/Jersey | Ya |
| Repository interface domain-oriented | Ya |
| MyBatis mapper tetap SQL-oriented | Ya |
| Repository implementation tidak singleton dengan session field | Ya |
| Query/list endpoint memakai DTO projection | Ya |
| Command workflow memakai domain model jika invariant kompleks | Ya |
| Affected row/concurrency check dilakukan di repository/service | Ya |
| Outbox/audit ditulis dalam unit-of-work yang sama | Ya |
| Request DTO tidak langsung masuk mapper write | Ya |
| Persistence record dipisah jika schema tidak cocok dengan domain | Ya |
| Mapper XML punya namespace dan statement naming jelas | Ya |
| Unit test domain tidak butuh DB | Ya |
| Mapper integration test tetap pakai PostgreSQL nyata | Ya |
| Layering tidak dibuat berlebihan untuk CRUD trivial | Ya |

---

## 33. Roadmap Belajar Singkat

| Urutan | Materi | Target |
|---:|---|---|
| 1 | Resource vs service boundary | Resource hanya HTTP adapter |
| 2 | Unit of Work di application service | Transaction tidak tersebar |
| 3 | Mapper vs repository | Tahu kapan membungkus mapper |
| 4 | Domain model vs persistence record | Domain tidak dikendalikan schema |
| 5 | DTO projection | Read endpoint efisien |
| 6 | Transaction script vs domain service | Pilih kompleksitas yang tepat |
| 7 | CQRS-lite | Pisahkan command dan query model |
| 8 | Anti-corruption layer | Legacy schema tidak merusak domain |
| 9 | Testing boundary | Unit test domain, integration test mapper |
| 10 | Refactoring smells | Deteksi mapper/resource/domain coupling |

## Response:
7/13/2026, 2:44:36 AM

# Cheatsheet - Java MyBatis Security & Data Protection: SQL Injection Prevention, Dynamic SQL Whitelisting, Least-Privilege DB User, PII Masking, Row/Tenant Filtering, Audit Trail, dan Secrets Management di Jersey/JAX-RS

## 1. Introduction

Di aplikasi **Jersey/JAX-RS + MyBatis + PostgreSQL**, security persistence layer bukan hanya soal “jangan SQL injection”. Area yang harus dijaga meliputi:

```text
HTTP input
  -> validation
  -> authorization / tenant context
  -> service use case
  -> MyBatis mapper parameter binding
  -> PostgreSQL role privilege
  -> row filtering / tenant filtering
  -> audit trail
  -> logging / masking
  -> secrets handling
```

MyBatis aman jika value user dipasang lewat `#{}` karena akan diperlakukan sebagai parameter prepared statement. Namun MyBatis juga punya `${}` untuk string substitution, dan dokumentasi MyBatis memperingatkan bahwa menerima input user lalu memasukkannya tanpa modifikasi ke `${}` dapat menyebabkan SQL injection. OWASP juga menempatkan prepared statements/parameterized queries sebagai pertahanan utama terhadap SQL injection, serta allow-list validation untuk bagian SQL yang memang tidak bisa dibind sebagai parameter, seperti nama kolom atau arah sorting. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/sqlmap-xml.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 2. Mental Model

```mermaid
flowchart TD
    A[HTTP Request] --> B[JAX-RS Resource]
    B --> C[Input Validation]
    C --> D[AuthN / AuthZ / Tenant Context]
    D --> E[Application Service]
    E --> F[Repository / MyBatis Mapper]
    F --> G{SQL Construction}

    G -->|Safe value binding| H[#{parameter}]
    G -->|Identifier substitution only| I[${whitelistedIdentifier}]

    H --> J[PreparedStatement]
    I --> J

    J --> K[(PostgreSQL)]
    K --> L[Role Privileges]
    K --> M[Row / Tenant Filtering]
    K --> N[Constraints]
    K --> O[Audit Tables]

    E --> P[Structured Logs]
    P --> Q[PII Masking]

    R[Secrets Manager / K8s Secret] --> S[Runtime Env / Mounted Secret]
    S --> T[DataSource Config]
```

**Core invariant:**

```text
User-controlled value:
  use #{}

User-controlled SQL identifier:
  never raw string
  convert to enum/allow-list first
  only then use ${}

Tenant/user access:
  enforce in service authorization
  enforce in SQL predicate
  optionally enforce again using PostgreSQL RLS

Secrets:
  never hardcode
  never log
  rotate
```

---

## 3. Decomposition Map

| Area | Fokus Praktis | Skill yang Dikuasai | Risiko Jika Salah |
|---|---|---|---|
| **SQL Injection Prevention** | Parameter binding aman | `#{}` vs `${}`, prepared statement | Data leak, data tampering |
| **Dynamic SQL Whitelisting** | Sorting/filter dynamic aman | enum column whitelist, direction whitelist | Injection via `ORDER BY`, column, table |
| **Input Validation** | Validasi sebelum service | Bean Validation, canonicalization, size limit | Payload abnormal masuk DB |
| **Authorization Boundary** | User boleh melakukan action? | permission check di service | Horizontal privilege escalation |
| **Tenant Filtering** | User hanya lihat tenant sendiri | `tenant_id` predicate, context binding | Cross-tenant data leak |
| **PostgreSQL RLS** | Defense-in-depth row filtering | `ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY`, policy | Salah konfigurasi bisa block/allow salah |
| **Least Privilege DB User** | DB role terbatas | `GRANT`, `REVOKE`, role separation | App bisa drop/alter/read semua |
| **Sensitive Data Masking** | Log aman | mask PII/token/JSONB payload | Compliance incident |
| **Audit Trail** | Siapa melakukan apa, kapan, dari mana | append-only audit, request correlation | Tidak defensible saat investigasi |
| **Secrets Management** | Password/token tidak hardcoded | env, K8s Secret, external secret manager | Secret leak di repo/image/log |
| **Credential Rotation** | Ganti password/key aman | rolling deploy, dual credential | Downtime saat rotate |
| **Constraint Enforcement** | DB ikut menjaga invariant | FK, CHECK, UNIQUE, NOT NULL | App bug merusak data |
| **Data Minimization** | Query hanya kolom perlu | DTO projection, no `SELECT *` | Overexposure data |
| **Encryption Awareness** | Data in transit/at rest | TLS DB, disk/KMS/secrets encryption | Sniffing / disk leak |
| **Security Testing** | Buktikan boundary aman | injection tests, authz tests, tenant tests | Security regression |

---

## 4. `#{}` vs `${}`

| Syntax | Cara Kerja | Aman untuk User Value? | Contoh |
|---|---|---:|---|
| `#{value}` | Prepared statement parameter | Ya | `WHERE id = #{id}` |
| `${value}` | String substitution langsung | Tidak, kecuali allow-list | `ORDER BY ${sortColumn}` |

### Aman

```xml
<select id="findByEmail" resultMap="UserResultMap">
  SELECT
    id,
    email,
    display_name,
    status
  FROM users
  WHERE email = #{email}
</select>
```

### Berbahaya

```xml
<select id="searchUsers" resultMap="UserResultMap">
  SELECT
    id,
    email,
    display_name,
    status
  FROM users
  WHERE email = '${email}'
</select>
```

### Berbahaya untuk sorting

```xml
ORDER BY ${sort}
```

Jika `sort` berasal dari query parameter seperti:

```text
created_at desc; drop table users; --
```

maka SQL bisa rusak atau dieksploitasi.

**Rule:** `${}` bukan untuk data value. `${}` hanya boleh untuk SQL identifier yang sudah diubah dari input user menjadi enum/allow-list internal. Dokumentasi MyBatis secara eksplisit memberi peringatan SQL injection pada string substitution `${}` jika input user disuplai tanpa kontrol. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/sqlmap-xml.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 5. Dynamic Sorting Whitelist Pattern

### API input

```text
GET /cases?sort=dueAt&direction=asc
```

### Enum whitelist

```java
public enum CaseSortField {
    DUE_AT("due_at"),
    CREATED_AT("created_at"),
    PRIORITY("priority"),
    STATUS("status");

    private final String column;

    CaseSortField(String column) {
        this.column = column;
    }

    public String column() {
        return column;
    }

    public static CaseSortField fromApiValue(String value) {
        return switch (value) {
            case "dueAt" -> DUE_AT;
            case "createdAt" -> CREATED_AT;
            case "priority" -> PRIORITY;
            case "status" -> STATUS;
            default -> throw new InvalidSortFieldException(value);
        };
    }
}
```

```java
public enum SortDirection {
    ASC,
    DESC;

    public static SortDirection fromApiValue(String value) {
        if (value == null || value.isBlank()) {
            return DESC;
        }

        return switch (value.toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT)) {
            case "asc" -> ASC;
            case "desc" -> DESC;
            default -> throw new InvalidSortDirectionException(value);
        };
    }
}
```

### Criteria

```java
public record CaseSearchQuery(
    UUID tenantId,
    String status,
    CaseSortField sortField,
    SortDirection direction,
    int limit,
    int offset
) {}
```

### Mapper XML

```xml
<select id="searchCases" resultType="com.company.app.dto.CaseQueueItemDto">
  SELECT
    id,
    case_number AS caseNumber,
    status,
    priority,
    due_at AS dueAt
  FROM cases
  WHERE tenant_id = #{query.tenantId}

  <if test="query.status != null">
    AND status = #{query.status}
  </if>

  ORDER BY ${query.sortField.column} ${query.direction}, id DESC
  LIMIT #{query.limit}
  OFFSET #{query.offset}
</select>
```

**Security invariant:** raw query parameter tidak pernah langsung masuk `${}`.

---

## 6. Dynamic Filter Whitelist Pattern

### Jangan expose arbitrary field search

Bad:

```text
GET /cases?field=status&op==&value=OPEN
GET /cases?field=tenant_id&op=!=&value=...
```

Ini membuat API menjadi mini SQL engine.

### Better: explicit supported filters

```java
public record CaseSearchRequest(
    String status,
    String priority,
    Instant dueFrom,
    Instant dueTo,
    String sort,
    String direction,
    Integer limit
) {}
```

```java
public record CaseSearchQuery(
    UUID tenantId,
    Optional<CaseStatus> status,
    Optional<CasePriority> priority,
    Optional<Instant> dueFrom,
    Optional<Instant> dueTo,
    CaseSortField sortField,
    SortDirection direction,
    int limit
) {}
```

```xml
<where>
  tenant_id = #{query.tenantId}

  <if test="query.status.present">
    AND status = #{query.status.get().name()}
  </if>

  <if test="query.priority.present">
    AND priority = #{query.priority.get().name()}
  </if>

  <if test="query.dueFrom.present">
    AND due_at &gt;= #{query.dueFrom.get()}
  </if>

  <if test="query.dueTo.present">
    AND due_at &lt; #{query.dueTo.get()}
  </if>
</where>
```

**Rule:** filter yang didukung harus eksplisit. Jangan membangun generic arbitrary SQL filter dari request.

---

## 7. Input Validation Boundary

Resource layer harus memvalidasi bentuk input; service layer memvalidasi business rule.

```java
public record SearchCasesRequest(
    @Size(max = 30) String status,
    @Size(max = 30) String priority,
    @Min(1) @Max(200) Integer limit,
    @Min(0) Integer offset
) {}
```

```java
@Path("/cases")
public class CaseResource {

    private final CaseApplicationService service;

    @GET
    public Response search(@BeanParam @Valid SearchCasesRequest request) {
        CaseSearchQuery query = CaseSearchQueryFactory.fromRequest(
            request,
            SecurityContext.currentTenantId()
        );

        return Response.ok(service.searchCases(query)).build();
    }
}
```

| Validation Type | Layer | Contoh |
|---|---|---|
| Shape validation | Resource DTO | `@NotNull`, `@Size`, `@Pattern` |
| Canonicalization | Resource/application | trim, lowercase email |
| Enum mapping | Application | API string → enum |
| Permission validation | Application service | can approve? can view? |
| State validation | Domain | valid transition? |
| Persistence constraint | Database | unique, FK, check |

OWASP recommends prepared statements as a primary defense, and allow-list validation is the appropriate option when parts of SQL cannot be parameterized, such as table names, column names, or sort direction. ([OWASP Cheat Sheet Series](https://cheatsheetseries.owasp.org/cheatsheets/SQL_Injection_Prevention_Cheat_Sheet.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 8. Tenant Filtering Pattern

Multi-tenant security harus dipikirkan dari awal. Jangan hanya filter tenant di UI.

### Tenant context

```java
public record TenantContext(
    UUID tenantId,
    UUID userId,
    Set<String> permissions
) {}
```

### Resource/application service

```java
public List<CaseQueueItemDto> searchCases(SearchCasesRequest request) {
    TenantContext tenantContext = securityContext.currentTenant();

    CaseSearchQuery query = CaseSearchQueryFactory.fromRequest(
        request,
        tenantContext.tenantId()
    );

    authorizationService.requirePermission(
        tenantContext,
        "CASE_READ"
    );

    return unitOfWork.readOnly(ctx -> {
        CaseQueryRepository repository =
            repositoryFactory.caseQueryRepository(ctx);

        return repository.search(query);
    });
}
```

### Mapper XML

```xml
<select id="search" resultType="com.company.app.dto.CaseQueueItemDto">
  SELECT
    id,
    case_number AS caseNumber,
    status,
    priority,
    due_at AS dueAt
  FROM cases
  WHERE tenant_id = #{query.tenantId}

  <if test="query.status != null">
    AND status = #{query.status}
  </if>

  ORDER BY due_at ASC, id ASC
  LIMIT #{query.limit}
</select>
```

**Invariant:** setiap query tenant-owned table harus memiliki `tenant_id = ?`, kecuali query admin yang benar-benar eksplisit dan diaudit.

---

## 9. Tenant Predicate Guard

Untuk mencegah mapper lupa tenant filter, gunakan konvensi dan test.

### Naming convention

| Mapper Method | Tenant Required? |
|---|---:|
| `findById(tenantId, id)` | Ya |
| `search(tenantId, criteria)` | Ya |
| `adminFindById(id)` | Admin only, audited |
| `systemFindForBackfill(limit)` | System job only |
| `findReferenceData()` | Tidak jika global reference |

### Better method signature

```java
CaseRecord findById(
    @Param("tenantId") UUID tenantId,
    @Param("id") UUID id
);
```

Bukan:

```java
CaseRecord findById(@Param("id") UUID id);
```

### XML

```xml
<select id="findById" resultMap="CaseRecordResultMap">
  SELECT
    id,
    tenant_id,
    case_number,
    status,
    version
  FROM cases
  WHERE tenant_id = #{tenantId}
    AND id = #{id}
</select>
```

---

## 10. PostgreSQL Row-Level Security / RLS

PostgreSQL mendukung **row security policies**. Policy dibuat dengan `CREATE POLICY`, diaktifkan/dinonaktifkan pada table dengan `ALTER TABLE`, dan setiap policy bersifat table-specific. RLS bisa menjadi defense-in-depth untuk multi-tenant filtering, tetapi tetap perlu dipahami benar karena table owner dan role dengan `BYPASSRLS` punya aturan khusus. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/ddl-rowsecurity.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Enable RLS

```sql
ALTER TABLE cases ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
```

### Policy dengan session setting

```sql
CREATE POLICY tenant_isolation_policy
ON cases
USING (tenant_id = current_setting('app.tenant_id')::uuid)
WITH CHECK (tenant_id = current_setting('app.tenant_id')::uuid);
```

### Set tenant per transaction

```xml
<update id="setTenantContext">
  SELECT set_config('app.tenant_id', #{tenantId}, true)
</update>
```

`true` pada `set_config` membuat setting bersifat local untuk transaksi saat ini.

### Unit of Work

```java
public <T> T tenantTransactional(UUID tenantId, MyBatisWork<T> work) {
    return transactional(ctx -> {
        RuntimeMapper runtimeMapper = ctx.mapper(RuntimeMapper.class);

        runtimeMapper.setTenantContext(tenantId);

        return work.execute(ctx);
    });
}
```

### Mapper tetap pakai tenant predicate?

Ya, untuk defense-in-depth. RLS bukan alasan untuk menghapus tenant predicate dari SQL aplikasi.

| Layer | Fungsi |
|---|---|
| Service authz | User boleh mengakses tenant ini? |
| SQL predicate | Query jelas, index-friendly, reviewable |
| PostgreSQL RLS | Defense-in-depth jika query lupa predicate |
| Audit log | Bukti akses dan perubahan |

---

## 11. Least-Privilege DB User

Aplikasi tidak boleh login ke database sebagai owner/schema admin.

PostgreSQL memakai privilege model berbasis role dan `GRANT`; `GRANT` memberikan privilege tertentu pada object database kepada satu atau lebih role. PostgreSQL juga punya konsep role membership untuk mengelola privilege melalui group role. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-grant.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Role split

```text
app_owner
  owns schema/tables
  used only by migration tool

app_runtime
  used by Jersey/MyBatis application
  limited SELECT/INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE

app_readonly
  used by reporting/read-only service

app_backfill
  limited update rights for backfill job

app_audit_reader
  read audit tables only
```

### Example grants

```sql
-- Schema usage
GRANT USAGE ON SCHEMA public TO app_runtime;

-- Table privileges
GRANT SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE, DELETE
ON TABLE cases, orders, order_items, outbox_events
TO app_runtime;

-- Sequence privileges if using sequences
GRANT USAGE, SELECT
ON ALL SEQUENCES IN SCHEMA public
TO app_runtime;

-- Read-only role
GRANT USAGE ON SCHEMA public TO app_readonly;

GRANT SELECT
ON TABLE cases, orders, order_items
TO app_readonly;
```

### Do not

```sql
GRANT ALL PRIVILEGES ON DATABASE appdb TO app_runtime;
GRANT app_owner TO app_runtime;
```

OWASP’s SQL injection guidance also recommends least privilege to reduce blast radius if injection or application compromise occurs. ([GitHub](https://github.com/nokia/OWASP-CheatSheetSeries/blob/master/cheatsheets/SQL_Injection_Prevention_Cheat_Sheet.md?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 12. DB Privilege Matrix

| DB Role | Used By | Allowed | Not Allowed |
|---|---|---|---|
| `app_owner` | Migration pipeline | DDL, ownership | Runtime app login |
| `app_runtime` | Jersey/MyBatis app | CRUD required tables | `DROP`, `ALTER`, superuser |
| `app_readonly` | Reports | `SELECT` only | Write |
| `app_backfill` | Controlled jobs | Specific update/select | Broad DDL/admin |
| `app_audit_reader` | Audit tooling | Select audit views | Modify audit |
| `app_admin_runtime` | Rare admin service | Explicit admin tables | Global superuser |

**Rule:** production runtime credential should not be able to migrate schema.

---

## 13. Securing Admin / System Queries

Admin/system queries sering butuh akses lintas tenant. Itu harus eksplisit.

### Admin method naming

```java
List<CaseRecord> adminSearchAcrossTenants(AdminCaseSearchQuery query);
```

### Mapper SQL comment

```xml
<select id="adminSearchAcrossTenants" resultMap="CaseRecordResultMap">
  /* app=case-service purpose=admin-cross-tenant-search */
  SELECT
    tenant_id,
    id,
    case_number,
    status,
    priority,
    created_at
  FROM cases

  <where>
    <if test="query.tenantId != null">
      tenant_id = #{query.tenantId}
    </if>

    <if test="query.status != null">
      AND status = #{query.status}
    </if>
  </where>

  ORDER BY created_at DESC, id DESC
  LIMIT #{query.limit}
</select>
```

### Service guard

```java
authorizationService.requirePermission(
    securityContext.currentUser(),
    "CASE_ADMIN_CROSS_TENANT_SEARCH"
);
```

### Audit

```java
auditRepository.record(AuditEntry.adminSearch(
    actorId,
    "CASE_ADMIN_CROSS_TENANT_SEARCH",
    query.safeSummary()
));
```

**Rule:** cross-tenant access harus punya permission khusus, log khusus, dan review khusus.

---

## 14. Data Minimization di Mapper

Security bukan hanya mencegah write ilegal. Security juga membatasi data yang tidak perlu keluar.

### Bad

```xml
<select id="searchCustomers" resultMap="CustomerResultMap">
  SELECT *
  FROM customers
  WHERE tenant_id = #{tenantId}
</select>
```

### Good

```xml
<select id="searchCustomers"
        resultType="com.company.app.dto.CustomerListItemDto">
  SELECT
    id,
    customer_number AS customerNumber,
    display_name AS displayName,
    status
  FROM customers
  WHERE tenant_id = #{tenantId}
  ORDER BY created_at DESC, id DESC
  LIMIT #{limit}
</select>
```

| Endpoint | Jangan Ambil |
|---|---|
| Customer list | address, identity number, internal notes |
| Case queue | evidence payload, legal narrative |
| Order list | payment details |
| User list | password hash, MFA secret |
| Audit summary | full request body |
| Dashboard | row-level sensitive detail |

**Rule:** DTO projection adalah security control, bukan hanya performance optimization.

---

## 15. PII Masking in Logs

### Data yang tidak boleh dilog mentah

```text
password
token
authorization header
session id
refresh token
MFA secret
private key
database password
KYC identity number
full address
phone number
email, tergantung policy
full JSONB payload
payment data
legal/enforcement evidence
```

### Masking helper

```java
public final class SensitiveLogMasker {

    private SensitiveLogMasker() {}

    public static String maskEmail(String email) {
        if (email == null || !email.contains("@")) {
            return "***";
        }

        String[] parts = email.split("@", 2);
        String local = parts[0];

        if (local.length() <= 2) {
            return "***@" + parts[1];
        }

        return local.charAt(0) + "***" + local.charAt(local.length() - 1)
            + "@" + parts[1];
    }

    public static String maskToken(String token) {
        if (token == null || token.length() < 8) {
            return "***";
        }

        return token.substring(0, 4) + "..." + token.substring(token.length() - 4);
    }

    public static String safeId(UUID id) {
        return id == null ? "null" : id.toString();
    }
}
```

### Logging pattern

```java
log.info(
    "Case approved caseId={} tenantId={} actorId={} correlationId={}",
    caseId,
    tenantId,
    actorId,
    correlationId
);
```

Bukan:

```java
log.info("Approve request body={}", request);
log.info("Outbox payload={}", payloadJson);
log.info("DB password={}", password);
```

---

## 16. MyBatis SQL Logging Policy

| Environment | SQL Log Level | Parameter Logging |
|---|---|---|
| Local | DEBUG boleh | Boleh dengan dummy data |
| Test | DEBUG selektif | Hindari real secrets |
| Staging | DEBUG sementara | Masking jika data realistis |
| Production normal | INFO/WARN | Tidak log full parameter |
| Production incident | DEBUG sangat selektif dan terbatas waktu | Review security/compliance |

### MyBatis log config

```xml
<settings>
  <setting name="logImpl" value="SLF4J"/>
</settings>
```

MyBatis supports multiple logging implementations, including SLF4J, Log4j2, JDK logging, stdout logging, no logging, or a custom implementation; production systems should route this through a managed logging framework rather than raw stdout debugging. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/sqlmap-xml.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 17. Audit Trail Pattern

Audit trail harus menjawab:

```text
Who did what?
To which entity?
When?
From where?
Under which tenant?
With what result?
Why was it allowed?
What changed?
```

### Audit table

```sql
CREATE TABLE audit_events (
    id uuid PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id uuid NOT NULL,
    actor_id uuid NOT NULL,
    action text NOT NULL,
    aggregate_type text NOT NULL,
    aggregate_id text NOT NULL,
    before_state jsonb,
    after_state jsonb,
    reason text,
    correlation_id text,
    request_ip inet,
    user_agent text,
    created_at timestamp with time zone NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);
```

### Insert mapper

```xml
<insert id="insertAuditEvent">
  INSERT INTO audit_events (
    id,
    tenant_id,
    actor_id,
    action,
    aggregate_type,
    aggregate_id,
    before_state,
    after_state,
    reason,
    correlation_id,
    request_ip,
    user_agent,
    created_at
  )
  VALUES (
    #{id},
    #{tenantId},
    #{actorId},
    #{action},
    #{aggregateType},
    #{aggregateId},
    #{beforeState, jdbcType=OTHER,
      typeHandler=com.company.app.persistence.typehandler.JsonNodeJsonbTypeHandler},
    #{afterState, jdbcType=OTHER,
      typeHandler=com.company.app.persistence.typehandler.JsonNodeJsonbTypeHandler},
    #{reason},
    #{correlationId},
    CAST(#{requestIp} AS inet),
    #{userAgent},
    #{createdAt}
  )
</insert>
```

### Service usage

```java
unitOfWork.transactional(ctx -> {
    CaseRepository caseRepository = repositoryFactory.caseRepository(ctx);
    AuditRepository auditRepository = repositoryFactory.auditRepository(ctx);

    Case before = caseRepository.findById(command.caseId());
    Case after = before.approve(command.reason());

    caseRepository.save(after);

    auditRepository.record(AuditEvent.caseApproved(
        tenantId,
        actorId,
        before,
        after,
        command.reason(),
        correlationId
    ));

    return CaseResponse.from(after);
});
```

**Rule:** audit event untuk business command penting harus commit dalam transaksi yang sama dengan perubahan data utama.

---

## 18. Audit Data Minimization

Audit bukan berarti simpan semua payload mentah.

| Field | Simpan? | Catatan |
|---|---:|---|
| `actor_id` | Ya | Siapa |
| `tenant_id` | Ya | Scope |
| `action` | Ya | Apa |
| `aggregate_id` | Ya | Target |
| `before/after state` | Tergantung | Mask sensitive field |
| full request body | Hindari | Risiko PII/secrets |
| authorization header | Tidak | Secret |
| password/token | Tidak | Secret |
| IP/user-agent | Bisa | Perlu retention policy |
| reason/comment | Ya, jika business-critical | Bisa mengandung sensitive text |
| evidence payload | Hati-hati | Biasanya referensi, bukan full copy |

---

## 19. Secrets Management

Kubernetes Secret adalah object untuk menyimpan sejumlah kecil data sensitif seperti password, token, atau key, agar tidak perlu menaruh data rahasia langsung di Pod spec atau container image. Namun Kubernetes juga memperingatkan bahwa Secret di etcd tidak otomatis terenkripsi secara default dan cluster administrator harus mengaktifkan encryption at rest untuk Secret data. ([Kubernetes](https://kubernetes.io/docs/concepts/configuration/secret/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Jangan hardcode

Bad:

```java
config.setPassword("ProdPassword123!");
```

Good:

```java
config.setPassword(requiredEnv("DB_PASSWORD"));
```

### Runtime config

```java
public final class DatabaseConfig {

    public static HikariDataSource dataSource() {
        HikariConfig config = new HikariConfig();

        config.setJdbcUrl(requiredEnv("DB_URL"));
        config.setUsername(requiredEnv("DB_USERNAME"));
        config.setPassword(requiredEnv("DB_PASSWORD"));
        config.setMaximumPoolSize(intEnv("DB_POOL_MAX_SIZE", 20));

        return new HikariDataSource(config);
    }

    private static String requiredEnv(String name) {
        String value = System.getenv(name);

        if (value == null || value.isBlank()) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("Missing required environment variable: " + name);
        }

        return value;
    }

    private static int intEnv(String name, int defaultValue) {
        String value = System.getenv(name);
        return value == null ? defaultValue : Integer.parseInt(value);
    }
}
```

---

## 20. Kubernetes Secret Usage

### Secret manifest

```yaml
apiVersion: v1
kind: Secret
metadata:
  name: case-service-db
type: Opaque
stringData:
  DB_URL: jdbc:postgresql://postgres.example.svc:5432/case_db
  DB_USERNAME: app_runtime
  DB_PASSWORD: change-me
```

### Deployment env

```yaml
apiVersion: apps/v1
kind: Deployment
metadata:
  name: case-service
spec:
  template:
    spec:
      containers:
        - name: case-service
          image: registry.example.com/case-service:1.0.0
          envFrom:
            - secretRef:
                name: case-service-db
```

### Better production pattern

```text
External secret manager
  -> External Secrets Operator / CSI driver / platform integration
    -> Kubernetes Secret or mounted secret
      -> application environment/config
```

| Risk | Mitigation |
|---|---|
| Secret stored unencrypted in etcd | Enable encryption at rest |
| Any pod can read secret | RBAC least privilege |
| Secret printed in logs | Masking/log policy |
| Secret in image | Never bake secret into image |
| Secret in Git | Use sealed/encrypted/external secret workflow |
| No rotation | Rotation runbook |
| One credential for all services | Per-service DB role |

Kubernetes official good practices recommend encryption at rest for Secret data and least-privilege access controls around Secrets. ([Kubernetes](https://kubernetes.io/docs/concepts/security/secrets-good-practices/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 21. Credential Rotation Pattern

### Goal

Rotate DB password without downtime.

### Strategy

```text
1. Create new DB credential / password.
2. Add new secret version.
3. Deploy app instances using new secret.
4. Confirm new connections work.
5. Drain old app instances.
6. Revoke old credential/password.
7. Monitor authentication failures.
```

### DB role approach

```sql
-- Existing runtime role
CREATE ROLE app_runtime_v2 LOGIN PASSWORD 'new-password';

GRANT USAGE ON SCHEMA public TO app_runtime_v2;
GRANT SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE, DELETE ON TABLE cases TO app_runtime_v2;
```

Then deploy app with:

```text
DB_USERNAME=app_runtime_v2
DB_PASSWORD=...
```

After rollout:

```sql
REVOKE ALL PRIVILEGES ON TABLE cases FROM app_runtime_v1;
DROP ROLE app_runtime_v1;
```

**Rule:** password rotation should be tested in staging. Connection pools can keep old connections alive until recycled.

---

## 22. Encryption in Transit

For PostgreSQL connection, require TLS in production where network trust is not guaranteed.

### JDBC URL example

```text
jdbc:postgresql://postgres.example.com:5432/case_db?sslmode=require
```

Stronger options depend on cert management:

```text
sslmode=verify-full
sslrootcert=/etc/certs/root.crt
```

### Practical checklist

| Checklist | Status |
|---|---|
| DB traffic encrypted across network boundary | Required |
| Certificate validation enabled where possible | Preferred |
| No DB password in URL logs | Required |
| JDBC URL not printed at INFO with password | Required |
| Secrets mounted/env securely | Required |
| TLS setting tested in staging | Required |

---

## 23. Row/Tenant Filtering Testing

### Mapper test

```java
@Test
void shouldNotReturnCaseFromDifferentTenant() {
    UUID tenantA = UUID.randomUUID();
    UUID tenantB = UUID.randomUUID();

    UUID caseA = fixture.insertCase(tenantA, "CASE-A");
    UUID caseB = fixture.insertCase(tenantB, "CASE-B");

    try (SqlSession session = openSession()) {
        CaseMapper mapper = session.getMapper(CaseMapper.class);

        CaseRecord found = mapper.findById(tenantA, caseB);

        assertThat(found).isNull();
    }
}
```

### API test

```java
@Test
void userShouldNotAccessCaseFromAnotherTenant() {
    UUID tenantA = fixture.createTenant();
    UUID tenantB = fixture.createTenant();

    UUID caseInTenantB = fixture.insertCase(tenantB);

    Response response = target("/cases/" + caseInTenantB)
        .request()
        .header("Authorization", tokenForTenant(tenantA))
        .get();

    assertThat(response.getStatus()).isIn(403, 404);
}
```

### Decision: 403 vs 404

| Response | Kapan |
|---|---|
| `403 Forbidden` | User tahu resource ada tapi tidak boleh |
| `404 Not Found` | Hindari resource enumeration |
| `401 Unauthorized` | Belum login/token invalid |
| `409 Conflict` | State/permission business conflict |

---

## 24. SQL Injection Testing

### Sorting injection test

```java
@Test
void shouldRejectInvalidSortField() {
    SearchCasesRequest request = new SearchCasesRequest(
        "OPEN",
        "created_at desc; drop table cases; --",
        "desc",
        50
    );

    assertThatThrownBy(() -> CaseSearchQueryFactory.fromRequest(request, tenantId))
        .isInstanceOf(InvalidSortFieldException.class);
}
```

### Mapper safe value test

```java
@Test
void emailSearchShouldTreatInjectedTextAsLiteralValue() {
    String maliciousEmail = "a@example.com' OR '1'='1";

    try (SqlSession session = openSession()) {
        UserMapper mapper = session.getMapper(UserMapper.class);

        List<UserRecord> users = mapper.findByEmail(maliciousEmail);

        assertThat(users).isEmpty();
    }
}
```

### Static grep guard

```bash
grep -R "\${" src/main/resources/mapper
```

Every `${}` occurrence should be reviewed and justified.

---

## 25. Static Review Rule for `${}`

Create a small review table in PR description whenever `${}` is used.

| Mapper | Statement | `${}` Usage | Source | Safety Control |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `CaseQueryMapper` | `searchCases` | sort column | enum `CaseSortField` | allow-list |
| `CaseQueryMapper` | `searchCases` | direction | enum `SortDirection` | allow-list |
| `AdminReportMapper` | `groupBy` | report dimension | enum `ReportDimension` | allow-list |

**Rule:** no raw `${requestParam}`. Ever.

---

## 26. Database Constraints as Security Boundary

Application validation is not enough.

```sql
ALTER TABLE cases
ADD CONSTRAINT cases_status_chk
CHECK (status IN ('DRAFT', 'SUBMITTED', 'APPROVED', 'REJECTED'));

ALTER TABLE cases
ADD CONSTRAINT cases_tenant_case_number_uq
UNIQUE (tenant_id, case_number);

ALTER TABLE cases
ADD CONSTRAINT cases_tenant_fk
FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id)
REFERENCES tenants(id);
```

| Constraint | Security/Integrity Benefit |
|---|---|
| `NOT NULL` | Prevent incomplete security-critical fields |
| `CHECK` | Prevent invalid state/code |
| `UNIQUE` | Prevent duplicate business identity/idempotency |
| `FOREIGN KEY` | Prevent orphan/cross-reference errors |
| composite unique `(tenant_id, external_ref)` | Prevent cross-tenant collision |
| trigger/audit constraint | Prevent missing audit metadata, if used carefully |

---

## 27. Idempotency as Security Control

Idempotency is also a protection against accidental or malicious duplicate command submission.

```sql
CREATE TABLE idempotency_keys (
    tenant_id uuid NOT NULL,
    key text NOT NULL,
    request_hash text NOT NULL,
    status text NOT NULL,
    response_body jsonb,
    created_at timestamp with time zone NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    completed_at timestamp with time zone,
    PRIMARY KEY (tenant_id, key)
);
```

```xml
<insert id="insertIdempotencyKey">
  INSERT INTO idempotency_keys (
    tenant_id,
    key,
    request_hash,
    status
  )
  VALUES (
    #{tenantId},
    #{key},
    #{requestHash},
    'PROCESSING'
  )
  ON CONFLICT (tenant_id, key) DO NOTHING
</insert>
```

**Rule:** idempotency key harus scoped by tenant/user/client. Jangan global tanpa alasan.

---

## 28. Secure Error Handling

### Bad

```json
{
  "error": "ERROR: duplicate key value violates unique constraint cases_tenant_case_number_uq Detail: Key (tenant_id, case_number)=(..., CASE-001) already exists."
}
```

### Good

```json
{
  "code": "DUPLICATE_CASE_NUMBER",
  "message": "A case with this case number already exists."
}
```

### Exception mapper

```java
@Provider
public class PersistenceExceptionMapper
    implements ExceptionMapper<PersistenceException> {

    @Override
    public Response toResponse(PersistenceException exception) {
        PersistenceErrorKind kind = PostgresErrorClassifier.classify(exception);

        return switch (kind) {
            case UNIQUE_VIOLATION ->
                Response.status(Response.Status.CONFLICT)
                    .entity(ApiError.of(
                        "DUPLICATE_RESOURCE",
                        "Resource already exists."
                    ))
                    .build();

            case FOREIGN_KEY_VIOLATION, CHECK_VIOLATION ->
                Response.status(Response.Status.CONFLICT)
                    .entity(ApiError.of(
                        "CONSTRAINT_VIOLATION",
                        "Request conflicts with current data state."
                    ))
                    .build();

            default ->
                Response.status(Response.Status.INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR)
                    .entity(ApiError.of(
                        "PERSISTENCE_ERROR",
                        "Unexpected persistence error."
                    ))
                    .build();
        };
    }
}
```

**Rule:** log internal detail securely; return sanitized error to client.

---

## 29. Secure Mapper XML Review Checklist

| Review Item | Required |
|---|---:|
| No user value in `${}` | Ya |
| Every `${}` backed by enum/allow-list | Ya |
| Tenant-owned table has tenant predicate | Ya |
| Query projection excludes sensitive columns | Ya |
| Admin query explicitly named and guarded | Ya |
| `SELECT *` avoided | Ya |
| Pagination has bounded limit | Ya |
| Update/delete has tenant predicate or admin guard | Ya |
| State update checks status/version | Ya |
| Insert/update does not accept forbidden client fields | Ya |
| Audit insert exists for sensitive command | Ya |
| Error detail not exposed to API | Ya |
| SQL logging safe for production | Ya |

---

## 30. Security Anti-Pattern

| Anti-Pattern | Example | Impact |
|---|---|---|
| Raw `${}` from request | `ORDER BY ${sort}` | SQL injection |
| Generic SQL endpoint | `POST /query {sql: ...}` | Full DB exposure |
| Mapper without tenant predicate | `WHERE id = #{id}` | Cross-tenant leak |
| Runtime app uses DB owner | `app_owner` credential | Drop/alter/data breach blast radius |
| Secrets in Git | `application.properties` with password | Credential leak |
| Secrets in Docker image | `ENV DB_PASSWORD=...` | Image leak |
| SQL DEBUG always on | Full parameter logs | PII/secrets exposure |
| `SELECT *` for list | Pulls sensitive fields | Data overexposure |
| Audit stores full request | Token/PII captured | Compliance incident |
| No idempotency | Duplicate POST | Duplicate order/payment/case |
| Error exposes SQL detail | raw DB exception response | Information disclosure |
| In-memory tenant filtering only | Java filters after query | Over-fetch and leak risk |
| Admin query reused by normal user | same mapper method | Privilege escalation |
| One DB user for all services | shared credential | Hard to rotate/audit |

---

## 31. Production Checklist

| Checklist | Status Ideal |
|---|---|
| All user values use `#{}` | Ya |
| Every `${}` has documented allow-list | Ya |
| Sorting/filtering uses enum mapping | Ya |
| Tenant context is required for tenant-owned queries | Ya |
| Cross-tenant/admin queries are explicitly named and permission-guarded | Ya |
| Runtime DB user is not schema owner | Ya |
| DB privileges are least-privilege | Ya |
| Migration DB user separate from runtime DB user | Ya |
| Sensitive fields are excluded from list projections | Ya |
| SQL logging production default is not DEBUG | Ya |
| PII/secrets masking is implemented | Ya |
| Secrets not hardcoded, not in image, not in Git | Ya |
| Kubernetes Secret encryption at rest configured | Ya |
| Secret access controlled by RBAC | Ya |
| Credential rotation runbook exists | Ya |
| Audit trail exists for sensitive business commands | Ya |
| Audit payload is minimized/masked | Ya |
| Error responses are sanitized | Ya |
| Injection and tenant isolation tests exist | Ya |
| Security review required for mapper XML changes | Ya |

---

## 32. Decision Table

| Problem | Pattern |
|---|---|
| User search value | `#{}` |
| Dynamic sort column | enum allow-list + `${enum.column}` |
| Dynamic sort direction | enum allow-list + `${direction}` |
| Tenant-owned read | `WHERE tenant_id = #{tenantId}` |
| Cross-tenant admin read | special permission + audit + explicit mapper |
| Prevent forgotten tenant filter | mapper signature requires tenantId + tests + optional RLS |
| Runtime DB access | least-privilege role |
| Schema migration | separate owner/migration role |
| Sensitive command | audit event in same transaction |
| Log sensitive data | mask or do not log |
| Store DB password | secret manager/K8s Secret, not code |
| Rotate credential | new credential → rolling deploy → revoke old |
| Duplicate POST | idempotency key scoped by tenant |
| DB constraint error | sanitized HTTP error mapping |
| Security regression | integration test + static mapper review |

---

| Urutan | Materi | Target |
|---:|---|---|
| 1 | `#{}` vs `${}` | Bisa mencegah SQL injection dasar |
| 2 | Dynamic SQL whitelist | Sorting/filtering aman |
| 3 | Tenant filtering | Tidak ada cross-tenant leak |
| 4 | Authorization boundary | Permission dicek di service |
| 5 | Least-privilege DB role | Blast radius terbatas |
| 6 | PostgreSQL RLS | Defense-in-depth row filtering |
| 7 | PII masking/log policy | Tidak bocor lewat observability |
| 8 | Audit trail | Perubahan sensitif defensible |
| 9 | Secrets management | Credential aman di runtime |
| 10 | Security testing | Injection, tenant, authz regression tertangkap |
