## Response:
7/13/2026, 2:50:24 AM

Lanjut ke advanced dynamic SQL dan query builder strategy: fokusnya bagaimana membuat search/filter yang fleksibel tanpa membuka injection risk, tanpa mapper XML menjadi spaghetti, dan tanpa API berubah menjadi SQL engine.

# Cheatsheet - Java MyBatis Advanced Dynamic SQL & Query Builder Strategy: XML Dynamic SQL vs MyBatis Dynamic SQL Library, Criteria Object, Search API Design, Safe Filtering, dan Composable Query Pattern

## 1. Introduction

Dynamic SQL di MyBatis biasanya mulai sederhana:

```xml id="1q5nb9"
<if test="status != null">
  AND status = #{status}
</if>
```

Tetapi di aplikasi enterprise, query search bisa berkembang menjadi:

```text id="jhvd6d"
status
priority
tenant
assigned team
date range
keyword
visibility
permission scope
sort
pagination
include archived
case lifecycle state
SLA state
escalation candidate
```

Kalau tidak dikontrol, mapper XML berubah menjadi ratusan baris dynamic SQL yang sulit dites, sulit di-index, rawan injection, dan sulit direview.

MyBatis menyediakan dynamic SQL XML seperti `if`, `choose`, `where`, `trim`, `set`, dan `foreach`. MyBatis Dynamic SQL Library adalah library terpisah untuk membangun SQL secara type-safe di Java; dokumentasi resminya menyebut library ini sebagai **typesafe SQL templating library** dengan dukungan untuk MyBatis3, dan versi **2.0.0** memindahkan minimum Java ke **Java 17**. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-dynamic-sql/docs/introduction.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 2. Mental Model

```mermaid id="7w3ubs"
flowchart TD
    A[HTTP Search Request] --> B[Request DTO Validation]
    B --> C[Criteria Object]
    C --> D[Normalize / Whitelist / Defaults]
    D --> E{Query Strategy}

    E -->|Stable search shape| F[MyBatis XML Dynamic SQL]
    E -->|Highly composable query| G[MyBatis Dynamic SQL Library]
    E -->|Complex report/search| H[Dedicated Query Object / Repository]
    E -->|Unsafe arbitrary query| I[Reject / Do Not Build SQL Engine]

    F --> J[Mapper XML]
    G --> K[Java SQL DSL]
    H --> L[Query Repository]
    J --> M[(PostgreSQL)]
    K --> M
    L --> M

    D --> N[Security Controls]
    N --> O[Allowed Filters]
    N --> P[Allowed Sort Fields]
    N --> Q[Tenant Predicate]
    N --> R[Limit Bound]
```

**Core invariant:**

```text id="n1kvst"
Search API tidak boleh menjadi SQL API.

User boleh memilih filter yang disediakan aplikasi.
User tidak boleh memilih arbitrary column, operator, table, join, expression, atau raw SQL.
```

---

## 3. Decomposition Map

| Area | Fokus Praktis | Skill yang Dikuasai | Risiko Jika Salah |
|---|---|---|---|
| **Criteria Object** | Representasi query yang sudah aman | DTO → validated criteria | Raw request masuk mapper |
| **Search API Design** | Filter eksplisit, bukan SQL bebas | allowed filters, allowed sort, bounded limit | API berubah jadi mini SQL engine |
| **XML Dynamic SQL** | Dynamic condition di mapper XML | `if`, `choose`, `where`, `trim`, `set`, `foreach` | XML spaghetti |
| **MyBatis Dynamic SQL Library** | Query DSL type-safe di Java | `select`, `where`, `orderBy`, rendering strategy | SQL tersebar di Java terlalu kompleks |
| **Safe Filtering** | Filter allow-list | enum field/operator, parameter binding | SQL injection |
| **Safe Sorting** | Sort allow-list | enum column + direction | Injection via `ORDER BY` |
| **Composable Query Pattern** | Filter bisa digabung | filter object, specification-like composition | Banyak kombinasi tidak tertes |
| **Tenant Predicate** | Multi-tenant safety | `tenant_id` selalu ada | Cross-tenant leak |
| **Authorization-aware Query** | Query dibatasi permission | team scope, ownership scope, role scope | Horizontal privilege escalation |
| **Pagination Strategy** | Offset/keyset | deterministic ordering | duplicate/missing row |
| **Index-aware Filter Design** | Filter sesuai index | selective predicates, composite index | query lambat |
| **Query Complexity Budget** | Batasi join/filter | max filters, max range, max limit | DoS via expensive query |
| **Testing Matrix** | Kombinasi filter penting | golden dataset, generated cases | regression tidak terlihat |
| **Observability** | Query bisa didiagnosis | mapper id, duration, rows | bottleneck kabur |

---

## 4. XML Dynamic SQL vs MyBatis Dynamic SQL Library

| Aspek | XML Dynamic SQL | MyBatis Dynamic SQL Library |
|---|---|---|
| Lokasi query | XML mapper | Java code DSL |
| Cocok untuk | Query shape relatif stabil | Query sangat composable |
| Review SQL | Mudah oleh DBA/reviewer SQL | Perlu render/mental model DSL |
| Type safety column | Rendah | Lebih tinggi |
| Dynamic branching | `if`, `choose`, `where`, `foreach` | Java conditional composition |
| Query readability | Bagus jika tidak terlalu banyak branch | Bagus jika DSL tidak over-abstract |
| Risk | XML spaghetti | Java query builder spaghetti |
| Testing | Mapper integration test | Unit test builder + mapper integration |
| Java version | Tergantung MyBatis core | Dynamic SQL 2.0 minimum Java 17 ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-dynamic-sql/docs/CHANGELOG.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) |
| Best use | Search endpoint umum, update patch, IN list | Advanced search, reusable predicates, type-safe generated table support |

MyBatis XML dynamic SQL mendukung elemen `if`, `choose`, `trim`, `where`, `set`, dan `foreach`, yang memang ditujukan untuk mengurangi kebutuhan menyusun SQL string manual. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/dynamic-sql.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 5. Search API Design: Jangan Membuat SQL Engine

### Bad API

```text id="jpwoao"
GET /cases?where=status='OPEN' and priority='HIGH'
GET /cases?field=created_at&operator=>&value=2026-01-01
GET /cases?orderBy=created_at desc; drop table cases
```

Masalah:

```text id="s6gv7x"
- injection risk
- authorization sulit
- index strategy kacau
- query cost tidak terkendali
- impossible to reason about
```

### Good API

```text id="9wp2h5"
GET /cases?status=OPEN&priority=HIGH&dueBefore=2026-07-20&sort=dueAt&direction=asc&limit=50
```

### Supported contract

| Filter | Type | Allowed? | Notes |
|---|---|---:|---|
| `status` | enum | Ya | `OPEN`, `IN_REVIEW`, `ESCALATED` |
| `priority` | enum | Ya | `LOW`, `MEDIUM`, `HIGH` |
| `assignedTeam` | string/enum | Ya | permission-scoped |
| `dueBefore` | date/time | Ya | bounded range |
| `createdFrom/createdTo` | instant | Ya | max range optional |
| `keyword` | text | Ya, bounded | length limit, specific columns |
| `sort` | enum | Ya | allow-list |
| `direction` | enum | Ya | `asc`, `desc` only |
| `table` | raw | Tidak | never |
| `join` | raw | Tidak | never |
| `operator` arbitrary | raw | Tidak | avoid |
| `select` columns | raw | Tidak | projection controlled by API |

---

## 6. Criteria Object Pattern

### Request DTO

```java id="a6bdfu"
public record CaseSearchRequest(
    String status,
    String priority,
    String assignedTeam,
    Instant dueBefore,
    Instant createdFrom,
    Instant createdTo,
    String keyword,
    String sort,
    String direction,
    Integer limit,
    String cursor
) {}
```

### Safe criteria

```java id="84y6g2"
public record CaseSearchCriteria(
    UUID tenantId,
    Optional<CaseStatus> status,
    Optional<CasePriority> priority,
    Optional<String> assignedTeam,
    Optional<Instant> dueBefore,
    Optional<Instant> createdFrom,
    Optional<Instant> createdTo,
    Optional<String> keyword,
    CaseSortField sortField,
    SortDirection direction,
    int limit,
    Optional<CaseCursor> cursor
) {}
```

### Factory

```java id="3xmltv"
public final class CaseSearchCriteriaFactory {

    private static final int DEFAULT_LIMIT = 50;
    private static final int MAX_LIMIT = 200;
    private static final int MAX_KEYWORD_LENGTH = 100;

    private CaseSearchCriteriaFactory() {}

    public static CaseSearchCriteria from(
        CaseSearchRequest request,
        UUID tenantId
    ) {
        int limit = normalizeLimit(request.limit());

        Optional<String> keyword = normalizeKeyword(request.keyword());

        return new CaseSearchCriteria(
            tenantId,
            optionalEnum(request.status(), CaseStatus.class),
            optionalEnum(request.priority(), CasePriority.class),
            normalizeAssignedTeam(request.assignedTeam()),
            Optional.ofNullable(request.dueBefore()),
            Optional.ofNullable(request.createdFrom()),
            Optional.ofNullable(request.createdTo()),
            keyword,
            CaseSortField.fromApiValue(request.sort()),
            SortDirection.fromApiValue(request.direction()),
            limit,
            CaseCursor.decode(request.cursor())
        );
    }

    private static int normalizeLimit(Integer requestedLimit) {
        if (requestedLimit == null) {
            return DEFAULT_LIMIT;
        }

        if (requestedLimit < 1 || requestedLimit > MAX_LIMIT) {
            throw new InvalidLimitException(requestedLimit);
        }

        return requestedLimit;
    }

    private static Optional<String> normalizeKeyword(String keyword) {
        if (keyword == null || keyword.isBlank()) {
            return Optional.empty();
        }

        String normalized = keyword.trim();

        if (normalized.length() > MAX_KEYWORD_LENGTH) {
            throw new InvalidKeywordException();
        }

        return Optional.of(normalized);
    }
}
```

**Rule:** mapper hanya menerima `CaseSearchCriteria`, bukan raw request DTO.

---

## 7. Safe Sort Field Pattern

```java id="n2wj0z"
public enum CaseSortField {
    DUE_AT("due_at"),
    CREATED_AT("created_at"),
    UPDATED_AT("updated_at"),
    PRIORITY("priority");

    private final String column;

    CaseSortField(String column) {
        this.column = column;
    }

    public String column() {
        return column;
    }

    public static CaseSortField fromApiValue(String value) {
        if (value == null || value.isBlank()) {
            return DUE_AT;
        }

        return switch (value) {
            case "dueAt" -> DUE_AT;
            case "createdAt" -> CREATED_AT;
            case "updatedAt" -> UPDATED_AT;
            case "priority" -> PRIORITY;
            default -> throw new InvalidSortFieldException(value);
        };
    }
}
```

```java id="8dx5ow"
public enum SortDirection {
    ASC,
    DESC;

    public static SortDirection fromApiValue(String value) {
        if (value == null || value.isBlank()) {
            return ASC;
        }

        return switch (value.toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT)) {
            case "asc" -> ASC;
            case "desc" -> DESC;
            default -> throw new InvalidSortDirectionException(value);
        };
    }
}
```

### XML usage

```xml id="nm5uzf"
ORDER BY ${criteria.sortField.column} ${criteria.direction}, id ASC
```

**Invariant:** `${}` hanya menerima enum internal, bukan raw string user.

---

## 8. XML Dynamic SQL: Search Query Template

```java id="4fc2gr"
public interface CaseQueryMapper {

    List<CaseQueueItemDto> searchCases(
        @Param("criteria") CaseSearchCriteria criteria
    );
}
```

```xml id="ahw3vg"
<select id="searchCases"
        resultType="com.company.app.dto.CaseQueueItemDto"
        timeout="5">
  SELECT
    id,
    case_number AS caseNumber,
    status,
    priority,
    assigned_team AS assignedTeam,
    due_at AS dueAt,
    created_at AS createdAt
  FROM cases

  <where>
    tenant_id = #{criteria.tenantId}

    <if test="criteria.status.present">
      AND status = #{criteria.status.get().name()}
    </if>

    <if test="criteria.priority.present">
      AND priority = #{criteria.priority.get().name()}
    </if>

    <if test="criteria.assignedTeam.present">
      AND assigned_team = #{criteria.assignedTeam.get()}
    </if>

    <if test="criteria.dueBefore.present">
      AND due_at &lt; #{criteria.dueBefore.get()}
    </if>

    <if test="criteria.createdFrom.present">
      AND created_at &gt;= #{criteria.createdFrom.get()}
    </if>

    <if test="criteria.createdTo.present">
      AND created_at &lt; #{criteria.createdTo.get()}
    </if>

    <if test="criteria.keyword.present">
      AND (
        case_number ILIKE #{criteria.keywordPattern}
        OR title ILIKE #{criteria.keywordPattern}
      )
    </if>
  </where>

  ORDER BY ${criteria.sortField.column} ${criteria.direction}, id ASC
  LIMIT #{criteria.limit}
</select>
```

### Catatan untuk `Optional`

OGNL terhadap `Optional` kadang terasa tidak nyaman dan bisa berbeda tergantung style project. Alternatif yang lebih sederhana untuk mapper adalah criteria dengan nullable field biasa, tetapi raw request tetap jangan langsung masuk mapper.

```java id="c0tc8g"
public record CaseSearchCriteria(
    UUID tenantId,
    CaseStatus status,
    CasePriority priority,
    String assignedTeam,
    Instant dueBefore,
    Instant createdFrom,
    Instant createdTo,
    String keywordPattern,
    CaseSortField sortField,
    SortDirection direction,
    int limit
) {}
```

Untuk mapper XML, nullable fields sering lebih praktis daripada `Optional`.

---

## 9. XML Dynamic SQL Style Guide

| Rule | Contoh |
|---|---|
| Gunakan `<where>` untuk dynamic predicates | Hindari `WHERE 1=1` jika bisa |
| Gunakan `<set>` untuk patch update | Hindari trailing comma |
| Gunakan `<choose>` jika branch mutually exclusive | Search by ID atau external ref |
| Gunakan `<foreach>` untuk `IN` list | Validasi list kosong di service |
| Jangan dynamic-kan table name dari user | Hardcode table |
| Jangan dynamic-kan join dari user | Buat endpoint/query khusus |
| Batasi jumlah filter | Hindari query cost liar |
| Selalu tenant predicate untuk tenant-owned table | `tenant_id = #{tenantId}` |
| Selalu deterministic order | `ORDER BY due_at, id` |
| Selalu bounded limit | max 100/200 |
| Dynamic sort hanya enum whitelist | `${criteria.sortField.column}` |
| Raw user value selalu `#{}` | Tidak ada pengecualian |

---

## 10. `<choose>` Pattern: Mutually Exclusive Search

Gunakan ketika search mode hanya boleh salah satu.

```java id="dzjl4k"
public enum CaseLookupMode {
    ID,
    CASE_NUMBER,
    EXTERNAL_REFERENCE
}
```

```java id="cifvzv"
public record CaseLookupCriteria(
    UUID tenantId,
    CaseLookupMode mode,
    UUID id,
    String caseNumber,
    String externalReference
) {}
```

```xml id="uutp49"
<select id="lookupCase" resultMap="CaseRecordResultMap">
  SELECT
    id,
    tenant_id,
    case_number,
    external_reference,
    status,
    version
  FROM cases

  <where>
    tenant_id = #{criteria.tenantId}

    <choose>
      <when test="criteria.mode.name() == 'ID'">
        AND id = #{criteria.id}
      </when>
      <when test="criteria.mode.name() == 'CASE_NUMBER'">
        AND case_number = #{criteria.caseNumber}
      </when>
      <when test="criteria.mode.name() == 'EXTERNAL_REFERENCE'">
        AND external_reference = #{criteria.externalReference}
      </when>
      <otherwise>
        AND 1 = 0
      </otherwise>
    </choose>
  </where>
</select>
```

**Why:** lebih aman daripada menerima banyak parameter lookup sekaligus lalu ambiguity terjadi.

---

## 11. `<trim>` Pattern: Advanced WHERE / Prefix Control

`<where>` cukup untuk mayoritas kasus. `<trim>` berguna jika perlu kontrol prefix/suffix yang lebih eksplisit.

```xml id="mrfycq"
<trim prefix="WHERE" prefixOverrides="AND |OR ">
  tenant_id = #{criteria.tenantId}

  <if test="criteria.status != null">
    AND status = #{criteria.status}
  </if>

  <if test="criteria.priority != null">
    AND priority = #{criteria.priority}
  </if>
</trim>
```

Untuk update:

```xml id="msmr0w"
<trim prefix="SET" suffixOverrides=",">
  <if test="patch.title != null">
    title = #{patch.title},
  </if>
  <if test="patch.priority != null">
    priority = #{patch.priority},
  </if>
  updated_at = now(),
</trim>
```

---

## 12. `<foreach>` Pattern: IN Query dan Empty List

### Mapper

```java id="yjfiah"
List<CaseRecord> findByIds(
    @Param("tenantId") UUID tenantId,
    @Param("ids") List<UUID> ids
);
```

### XML

```xml id="6ki8po"
<select id="findByIds" resultMap="CaseRecordResultMap">
  SELECT
    id,
    tenant_id,
    case_number,
    status,
    version
  FROM cases
  WHERE tenant_id = #{tenantId}
    AND id IN
    <foreach collection="ids"
             item="id"
             open="("
             separator=","
             close=")">
      #{id}
    </foreach>
</select>
```

### Service guard

```java id="ycdpud"
public List<CaseRecord> findByIds(UUID tenantId, List<UUID> ids) {
    if (ids == null || ids.isEmpty()) {
        return List.of();
    }

    if (ids.size() > 500) {
        throw new TooManyIdsException(ids.size());
    }

    return mapper.findByIds(tenantId, ids);
}
```

**Rule:** list kosong dan list terlalu besar harus dicegah sebelum mapper.

---

## 13. Dynamic Patch Update Pattern

### Request

```java id="z2hft4"
public record UpdateCasePatchRequest(
    String title,
    CasePriority priority,
    String assignedTeam,
    Long expectedVersion
) {}
```

### Criteria/Patch object

```java id="lix4gz"
public record CasePatch(
    UUID tenantId,
    UUID caseId,
    String title,
    CasePriority priority,
    String assignedTeam,
    long expectedVersion
) {
    public boolean isEmpty() {
        return title == null
            && priority == null
            && assignedTeam == null;
    }
}
```

### Service guard

```java id="h6s8f5"
if (patch.isEmpty()) {
    throw new EmptyPatchException();
}
```

### XML

```xml id="mx5ydz"
<update id="patchCase">
  UPDATE cases

  <set>
    <if test="patch.title != null">
      title = #{patch.title},
    </if>

    <if test="patch.priority != null">
      priority = #{patch.priority.name()},
    </if>

    <if test="patch.assignedTeam != null">
      assigned_team = #{patch.assignedTeam},
    </if>

    version = version + 1,
    updated_at = now()
  </set>

  WHERE tenant_id = #{patch.tenantId}
    AND id = #{patch.caseId}
    AND version = #{patch.expectedVersion}
</update>
```

**Security/correctness:** patch update tetap perlu tenant predicate dan optimistic locking.

---

## 14. MyBatis Dynamic SQL Library: Kapan Dipakai

MyBatis Dynamic SQL Library cocok saat query sangat composable dan kita ingin type-safe SQL builder di Java. Dokumentasi resminya menyatakan library ini menghasilkan dynamic SQL statement dan menyediakan dukungan untuk MyBatis3; quick start library juga menggambarkan langkah seperti membuat table/column support object, mapper, dan menggunakan builder untuk CRUD. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-dynamic-sql/docs/introduction.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Cocok

| Use Case | Kenapa |
|---|---|
| Banyak kombinasi filter reusable | Predicate bisa dikomposisi di Java |
| Query builder internal, bukan user SQL | Type-safe composition |
| Generated table support | Mengurangi typo column |
| Need optional conditions | Java conditionals lebih jelas |
| Multi-module shared query fragments | Bisa encapsulate specifications |
| Java 17 project | Cocok dengan Dynamic SQL 2.0 requirement |

### Kurang cocok

| Use Case | Kenapa |
|---|---|
| Query SQL jelas dan stabil | XML lebih mudah direview |
| DBA-heavy review process | SQL XML lebih transparan |
| Query PostgreSQL-specific kompleks | DSL bisa kurang expressive |
| Tim belum familiar DSL | Learning curve |
| Query report panjang | SQL eksplisit sering lebih baik |
| Butuh optimize with handcrafted SQL | XML/native SQL lebih jelas |

---

## 15. MyBatis Dynamic SQL Basic Shape

Contoh konseptual dengan style MyBatis Dynamic SQL:

```java id="5j6f1c"
public final class CaseDynamicSqlSupport {
    public static final CaseTable cases = new CaseTable();

    public static final SqlColumn<UUID> id = cases.id;
    public static final SqlColumn<UUID> tenantId = cases.tenantId;
    public static final SqlColumn<String> caseNumber = cases.caseNumber;
    public static final SqlColumn<String> status = cases.status;
    public static final SqlColumn<String> priority = cases.priority;
    public static final SqlColumn<Instant> dueAt = cases.dueAt;

    public static final class CaseTable extends SqlTable {
        public final SqlColumn<UUID> id = column("id");
        public final SqlColumn<UUID> tenantId = column("tenant_id");
        public final SqlColumn<String> caseNumber = column("case_number");
        public final SqlColumn<String> status = column("status");
        public final SqlColumn<String> priority = column("priority");
        public final SqlColumn<Instant> dueAt = column("due_at");

        public CaseTable() {
            super("cases");
        }
    }
}
```

```java id="8g6qzi"
public List<CaseQueueItemDto> search(CaseSearchCriteria criteria) {
    SelectStatementProvider selectStatement =
        select(id, caseNumber, status, priority, dueAt)
            .from(cases)
            .where(tenantId, isEqualTo(criteria.tenantId()))
            .and(status, isEqualToWhenPresent(criteria.statusName()))
            .and(priority, isEqualToWhenPresent(criteria.priorityName()))
            .and(dueAt, isLessThanWhenPresent(criteria.dueBefore()))
            .orderBy(criteria.sortField().toSortSpecification())
            .limit(criteria.limit())
            .build()
            .render(RenderingStrategies.MYBATIS3);

    return mapper.selectMany(selectStatement);
}
```

**Catatan:** detail class/import bisa mengikuti setup project/generator. Yang penting adalah pattern: criteria aman → DSL build → render MyBatis3 → mapper execute.

---

## 16. MyBatis Dynamic SQL Mapper

```java id="n049ej"
public interface CaseDynamicMapper {

    @SelectProvider(type = SqlProviderAdapter.class, method = "select")
    @Results(id = "CaseQueueItemResult", value = {
        @Result(column = "id", property = "id"),
        @Result(column = "case_number", property = "caseNumber"),
        @Result(column = "status", property = "status"),
        @Result(column = "priority", property = "priority"),
        @Result(column = "due_at", property = "dueAt")
    })
    List<CaseQueueItemDto> selectMany(SelectStatementProvider selectStatement);
}
```

### Boundary warning

Jangan biarkan controller/resource membangun `SelectStatementProvider`.

```text id="oz3orj"
Good:
  Resource -> Criteria -> QueryRepository -> Dynamic SQL Builder -> Mapper

Bad:
  Resource -> Dynamic SQL Builder -> Mapper
```

---

## 17. Query Specification Pattern

Agar builder tidak menjadi satu method raksasa, buat reusable specification internal.

```java id="u9ntxx"
public interface CaseQuerySpec {
    QueryExpressionDSL<SelectModel>.QueryExpressionWhereBuilder apply(
        QueryExpressionDSL<SelectModel>.QueryExpressionWhereBuilder where
    );
}
```

Namun generic type DSL bisa verbose. Alternatif yang lebih praktis: helper method.

```java id="3fp5kc"
public final class CasePredicates {

    private CasePredicates() {}

    public static void applyStatus(
        QueryExpressionDSL<SelectModel>.QueryExpressionWhereBuilder where,
        CaseStatus statusFilter
    ) {
        if (statusFilter != null) {
            where.and(status, isEqualTo(statusFilter.name()));
        }
    }
}
```

**Pragmatic warning:** jangan over-engineer specification abstraction kalau 5-10 `<if>` di XML sudah cukup jelas.

---

## 18. Criteria Complexity Budget

Setiap search endpoint butuh batas kompleksitas.

| Control | Rekomendasi |
|---|---|
| Max `limit` | 100/200 untuk API umum |
| Max date range | Misalnya 90 hari untuk query mahal |
| Max IDs in `IN` | 500/1000 tergantung workload |
| Max keyword length | 50-100 chars |
| Allowed sort fields | 3-6 field, bukan semua column |
| Allowed filters | Sesuai use case, bukan schema |
| Default filter | Exclude archived/deleted by default |
| Tenant predicate | Mandatory |
| Query timeout | 3-10 detik sesuai endpoint |
| Export | Async job untuk data besar |
| Count query | Optional, jangan default untuk high-volume |

### Criteria validation

```java id="u3jcaw"
public void validate(CaseSearchCriteria criteria) {
    if (criteria.limit() > 200) {
        throw new InvalidLimitException();
    }

    if (criteria.createdFrom() != null && criteria.createdTo() != null) {
        Duration range = Duration.between(
            criteria.createdFrom(),
            criteria.createdTo()
        );

        if (range.compareTo(Duration.ofDays(90)) > 0) {
            throw new DateRangeTooLargeException();
        }
    }
}
```

---

## 19. Authorization-Aware Query Composition

Search result sering tergantung permission.

### Permission scope

```java id="421qlo"
public enum CaseVisibilityScope {
    OWNED,
    TEAM,
    TENANT,
    CROSS_TENANT_ADMIN
}
```

### Criteria

```java id="55ezxk"
public record CaseSearchCriteria(
    UUID tenantId,
    UUID actorId,
    String actorTeam,
    CaseVisibilityScope visibilityScope,
    CaseStatus status,
    int limit
) {}
```

### XML

```xml id="j8f9pp"
<where>
  tenant_id = #{criteria.tenantId}

  <choose>
    <when test="criteria.visibilityScope.name() == 'OWNED'">
      AND assignee_id = #{criteria.actorId}
    </when>

    <when test="criteria.visibilityScope.name() == 'TEAM'">
      AND assigned_team = #{criteria.actorTeam}
    </when>

    <when test="criteria.visibilityScope.name() == 'TENANT'">
      <!-- tenant predicate above is enough -->
    </when>

    <otherwise>
      AND 1 = 0
    </otherwise>
  </choose>

  <if test="criteria.status != null">
    AND status = #{criteria.status.name()}
  </if>
</where>
```

**Rule:** permission scope sebaiknya dihitung di service/authorization layer, bukan dari raw request.

---

## 20. Keyword Search Pattern

### Simple ILIKE

```xml id="nr0ves"
<if test="criteria.keywordPattern != null">
  AND (
    case_number ILIKE #{criteria.keywordPattern}
    OR title ILIKE #{criteria.keywordPattern}
  )
</if>
```

Criteria factory:

```java id="td2wnu"
private static String toKeywordPattern(String keyword) {
    String normalized = keyword.trim();

    if (normalized.length() > 100) {
        throw new InvalidKeywordException();
    }

    return "%" + escapeLike(normalized) + "%";
}

private static String escapeLike(String value) {
    return value
        .replace("\\", "\\\\")
        .replace("%", "\\%")
        .replace("_", "\\_");
}
```

XML with escape:

```xml id="u6fp5x"
AND (
  case_number ILIKE #{criteria.keywordPattern} ESCAPE '\'
  OR title ILIKE #{criteria.keywordPattern} ESCAPE '\'
)
```

### Warning

`ILIKE '%keyword%'` sulit memakai index B-tree biasa. Untuk search serius, pertimbangkan PostgreSQL full-text search, trigram index, atau dedicated search engine. Jangan membuat keyword search bebas tanpa cost control.

---

## 21. Date Range Pattern

### Criteria normalization

```java id="gt4t13"
public record DateRange(
    Instant fromInclusive,
    Instant toExclusive
) {
    public DateRange {
        if (fromInclusive != null && toExclusive != null
            && !fromInclusive.isBefore(toExclusive)) {
            throw new InvalidDateRangeException();
        }
    }
}
```

### XML

```xml id="e48kab"
<if test="criteria.createdFrom != null">
  AND created_at &gt;= #{criteria.createdFrom}
</if>

<if test="criteria.createdTo != null">
  AND created_at &lt; #{criteria.createdTo}
</if>
```

**Rule:** gunakan `>= from` dan `< to` untuk range waktu. Hindari `BETWEEN` untuk timestamp jika batas atas dimaksudkan sebagai exclusive boundary.

---

## 22. Keyset Pagination with Dynamic Sort

Keyset pagination dengan dynamic sort lebih kompleks daripada offset. Jangan sembarang membolehkan banyak sort field jika butuh cursor.

### Simpler rule

```text id="6h3k7g"
Untuk cursor/keyset endpoint:
  sort field fixed atau sangat terbatas.

Untuk arbitrary sort:
  gunakan offset dengan limit kecil, atau buat endpoint khusus.
```

### Fixed keyset by due date

```xml id="t7ktjt"
<select id="findQueueAfter" resultType="com.company.app.dto.CaseQueueItemDto">
  SELECT
    id,
    case_number AS caseNumber,
    priority,
    due_at AS dueAt
  FROM cases
  WHERE tenant_id = #{criteria.tenantId}
    AND status IN ('OPEN', 'IN_REVIEW')

  <if test="criteria.cursor != null">
    AND (
      due_at &gt; #{criteria.cursor.dueAt}
      OR (due_at = #{criteria.cursor.dueAt} AND id &gt; #{criteria.cursor.id})
    )
  </if>

  ORDER BY due_at ASC, id ASC
  LIMIT #{criteria.limit}
</select>
```

### Cursor object

```java id="ohjskz"
public record CaseCursor(
    Instant dueAt,
    UUID id
) {}
```

---

## 23. Count Query Strategy

### Avoid default count

```text id="0wy7jl"
Search response:
  items
  nextCursor
  hasMore

Not always:
  totalCount
```

### `limit + 1` pattern

```java id="ahhqwa"
int requestedLimit = criteria.limit();
int queryLimit = requestedLimit + 1;

List<CaseQueueItemDto> rows = mapper.search(criteria.withLimit(queryLimit));

boolean hasMore = rows.size() > requestedLimit;

List<CaseQueueItemDto> items = hasMore
    ? rows.subList(0, requestedLimit)
    : rows;
```

### Count only if requested

```xml id="67v8a1"
<select id="countCases" resultType="long">
  SELECT count(*)
  FROM cases
  <where>
    tenant_id = #{criteria.tenantId}

    <if test="criteria.status != null">
      AND status = #{criteria.status.name()}
    </if>
  </where>
</select>
```

**Rule:** `count(*)` bisa mahal; jangan selalu jalankan untuk list endpoint high-volume.

---

## 24. Index-Aware Filter Design

Dynamic filter fleksibel harus tetap mempertimbangkan index.

| Query Pattern | Index Candidate |
|---|---|
| `tenant_id + status + due_at order` | `(tenant_id, status, due_at, id)` |
| `tenant_id + assigned_team + status + created_at` | `(tenant_id, assigned_team, status, created_at DESC, id DESC)` |
| `tenant_id + case_number exact` | unique `(tenant_id, case_number)` |
| `tenant_id + external_ref exact` | unique `(tenant_id, external_ref)` |
| `status='PENDING' queue` | partial index `WHERE status='PENDING'` |
| `keyword ILIKE '%x%'` | trigram/full-text strategy |
| `jsonb @>` | GIN index |
| many optional filters | maybe multiple targeted indexes, not every combination |

### Design principle

```text id="z6aqvn"
API filter design should follow supported access patterns,
not expose every database column.
```

---

## 25. Query Builder Anti-Corruption

Jika product ingin “advanced search builder”, jangan langsung translate UI rules ke arbitrary SQL.

### Internal DSL

```java id="4npf35"
public sealed interface CaseFilter
    permits StatusFilter, PriorityFilter, DueBeforeFilter, AssignedTeamFilter {}

public record StatusFilter(CaseStatus status) implements CaseFilter {}
public record PriorityFilter(CasePriority priority) implements CaseFilter {}
public record DueBeforeFilter(Instant dueBefore) implements CaseFilter {}
public record AssignedTeamFilter(String team) implements CaseFilter {}
```

### Validator

```java id="lav4z7"
public final class CaseFilterValidator {

    public void validate(List<CaseFilter> filters) {
        if (filters.size() > 10) {
            throw new TooManyFiltersException();
        }

        boolean hasTenantEscapingFilter = false;

        for (CaseFilter filter : filters) {
            if (filter instanceof AssignedTeamFilter assignedTeamFilter
                && assignedTeamFilter.team().length() > 50) {
                throw new InvalidFilterException();
            }
        }

        if (hasTenantEscapingFilter) {
            throw new InvalidFilterException();
        }
    }
}
```

### Mapper criteria remains safe

```java id="cbia9p"
public record CaseAdvancedSearchCriteria(
    UUID tenantId,
    List<CaseFilter> filters,
    CaseSortField sortField,
    SortDirection direction,
    int limit
) {}
```

**Rule:** bahkan advanced search tetap memakai tipe filter yang didukung aplikasi, bukan raw SQL.

---

## 26. XML vs DSL Decision Table

| Situation | Prefer |
|---|---|
| 5-10 optional filters | XML Dynamic SQL |
| Query SQL perlu DBA review | XML |
| PostgreSQL-specific syntax kompleks | XML |
| Highly composable internal query builder | MyBatis Dynamic SQL |
| Generated table/column support tersedia | MyBatis Dynamic SQL |
| Need reusable predicate fragments in Java | MyBatis Dynamic SQL |
| Report query panjang dengan CTE/window function | XML/native SQL |
| Simple CRUD | XML/annotation |
| Patch update sederhana | XML `<set>` |
| Many dynamic insert/update variants | Dynamic SQL Library bisa membantu |
| Team lebih kuat SQL daripada DSL | XML |
| Team lebih kuat Java type-safe builder | Dynamic SQL Library |

---

## 27. Testing Strategy for Dynamic SQL

### Unit test criteria factory

```java id="hy26rm"
@Test
void shouldRejectInvalidSortField() {
    CaseSearchRequest request = new CaseSearchRequest(
        "OPEN",
        "HIGH",
        null,
        null,
        null,
        null,
        null,
        "created_at desc; drop table cases; --",
        "desc",
        50,
        null
    );

    assertThatThrownBy(() ->
        CaseSearchCriteriaFactory.from(request, tenantId)
    ).isInstanceOf(InvalidSortFieldException.class);
}
```

### Mapper integration test: filter combination

```java id="80w8td"
@Test
void shouldFilterByStatusPriorityAndTenant() {
    fixture.insertCase(tenantA, "CASE-A", "OPEN", "HIGH");
    fixture.insertCase(tenantA, "CASE-B", "OPEN", "LOW");
    fixture.insertCase(tenantB, "CASE-C", "OPEN", "HIGH");

    CaseSearchCriteria criteria = new CaseSearchCriteria(
        tenantA,
        CaseStatus.OPEN,
        CasePriority.HIGH,
        null,
        null,
        null,
        null,
        null,
        CaseSortField.DUE_AT,
        SortDirection.ASC,
        50
    );

    List<CaseQueueItemDto> result = mapper.searchCases(criteria);

    assertThat(result)
        .extracting(CaseQueueItemDto::caseNumber)
        .containsExactly("CASE-A");
}
```

### Test matrix

| Case | Test |
|---|---|
| No optional filter | Returns tenant-scoped default |
| Each filter alone | Correct subset |
| Common filter combinations | Correct subset |
| Invalid enum | Rejected before mapper |
| Invalid sort | Rejected before mapper |
| Limit too high | Rejected |
| Empty `IN` list | Returns empty or rejected |
| Cross-tenant data | Not returned |
| Cursor pagination | No duplicate/missing |
| Keyword wildcard | Escaped and safe |
| Dynamic SQL generated invalid | Caught by mapper integration test |

---

## 28. Observability for Dynamic Queries

Dynamic query harus mudah didiagnosis tanpa melog raw payload.

### Log safe summary

```java id="1b4cma"
log.info(
    "Case search criteria tenantId={} status={} priority={} sort={} direction={} limit={} hasKeyword={}",
    criteria.tenantId(),
    criteria.status(),
    criteria.priority(),
    criteria.sortField(),
    criteria.direction(),
    criteria.limit(),
    criteria.keywordPattern() != null
);
```

### Metric tags

| Tag | Aman? |
|---|---:|
| mapper statement id | Ya |
| endpoint template | Ya |
| sort field enum | Ya |
| has keyword true/false | Ya |
| filter count | Ya |
| tenantId | Hati-hati |
| keyword value | Tidak |
| raw SQL | Tidak |
| userId | Tidak sebagai metric tag |

### Useful metrics

```text id="tcnyru"
search.duration
search.result.count
search.limit.requested
search.has.keyword
search.filter.count
search.timeout.count
search.rejected.invalid_filter
```

---

## 29. Dynamic SQL Smell Detector

| Smell | Artinya | Perbaikan |
|---|---|---|
| Mapper XML > 300 lines untuk satu select | Query terlalu kompleks | Split query/use case |
| Banyak nested `<choose>` | Terlalu banyak mode | Separate mapper methods |
| `${}` lebih dari sort/direction | Injection risk | Whitelist or redesign |
| Query punya 15 optional filters | Index strategy tidak jelas | Limit supported filters |
| Endpoint menerima `field/operator/value` raw | SQL engine liar | Typed filters |
| Count selalu jalan | Cost tersembunyi | Optional count / limit+1 |
| `IN` list tanpa max size | DoS risk | Bound list size |
| Keyword unrestricted | Expensive scan | Limit length/range/index |
| Tenant predicate conditional | Security bug | Make mandatory |
| Dynamic table name | Design smell | Separate mapper/query |
| Service membangun SQL string | Boundary rusak | Criteria + mapper/DSL |
| Sort arbitrary field | Injection/index chaos | Sort enum |

---

## 30. Anti-Pattern

| Anti-Pattern | Contoh | Dampak |
|---|---|---|
| Raw SQL API | `/search?where=...` | Injection, DoS, authz bypass |
| User field to `${}` | `${request.sort}` | SQL injection |
| Dynamic table name | `${tableName}` | Injection/schema coupling |
| One mega search endpoint | Semua filter semua entity | Impossible to optimize |
| Mapper accepts request DTO | HTTP contract masuk persistence | Coupling/security issue |
| Criteria without tenant | Cross-tenant leak |
| No max limit | Massive result set |
| Keyword `%...%` everywhere | Full scan |
| Optional filters no tests | Broken branch silent |
| Dynamic DSL over-abstracted | Harder than SQL | Maintainability drop |
| Count every search | Latency hidden |
| Sort without tie-breaker | Pagination unstable |
| Keyset with arbitrary sort | Cursor bug | Limit sort modes |
| Generic operator support | `=`, `!=`, `>`, `like` from user | Query engine chaos |

---

## 31. Production Checklist

| Checklist | Status Ideal |
|---|---|
| Request DTO converted to safe criteria | Ya |
| Mapper never receives raw unvalidated request | Ya |
| Dynamic sort uses enum allow-list | Ya |
| Dynamic direction uses enum allow-list | Ya |
| `${}` occurrences are reviewed and documented | Ya |
| Tenant predicate mandatory for tenant data | Ya |
| Limit bounded | Ya |
| Date range bounded for expensive endpoints | Ya |
| `IN` list max size enforced | Ya |
| Keyword length and escaping enforced | Ya |
| Default query excludes archived/deleted unless explicit | Ya |
| Query has deterministic `ORDER BY` | Ya |
| Count query optional | Ya |
| Keyset pagination uses stable fixed ordering | Ya |
| Query shape has supporting index strategy | Ya |
| XML dynamic SQL branches covered by tests | Ya |
| Dynamic SQL Library usage hidden behind repository | Ya |
| No arbitrary raw SQL API | Ya |
| Observability has safe criteria summary | Ya |

---

## 32. Decision Table

| Problem | Recommended Pattern |
|---|---|
| Optional filters sederhana | XML `<where>` + `<if>` |
| Patch update | XML `<set>` |
| Mutually exclusive lookup | XML `<choose>` |
| IN query | XML `<foreach>` + service guard |
| Dynamic sort | enum allow-list + `${}` |
| Search API request | request DTO → criteria object |
| Highly composable internal query | MyBatis Dynamic SQL Library |
| User-defined report dimensions | enum dimensions, not raw column |
| Advanced search builder | typed filter model |
| Cross-tenant filtering | mandatory tenant criteria + optional RLS |
| Large result browsing | keyset pagination |
| Need total count | optional count endpoint/query |
| Query too complex | split endpoint/query or materialized read model |
| Need DBA SQL review | prefer XML/native SQL |
| Need type-safe column composition | consider Dynamic SQL Library |

---

## 33. Roadmap Belajar Singkat

| Urutan | Materi | Target |
|---:|---|---|
| 1 | XML dynamic SQL basics | `if`, `where`, `set`, `foreach` |
| 2 | Criteria object | Raw request tidak masuk mapper |
| 3 | Sort/filter allow-list | Dynamic SQL aman |
| 4 | Search API design | Tidak membuat SQL engine |
| 5 | Pagination strategy | Offset vs keyset |
| 6 | Index-aware filters | Query tetap scalable |
| 7 | MyBatis Dynamic SQL Library | Type-safe builder saat perlu |
| 8 | Authorization-aware query | Scope query sesuai permission |
| 9 | Dynamic query testing | Branch dan kombinasi penting aman |
| 10 | Observability | Query dynamic bisa didiagnosis |

---

**Sources:**

- [MyBatis Dynamic SQL](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-dynamic-sql/docs/introduction.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com)

## Response:
7/13/2026, 2:55:33 AM

Lanjut ke stored procedure/function integration: fokusnya boundary yang benar antara Java service, MyBatis mapper, dan PL/pgSQL agar database-side logic tetap terkontrol, testable, dan tidak menjadi “hidden application layer”.

# Cheatsheet - Java MyBatis Stored Procedure, PostgreSQL Function/Procedure Integration, Callable Statement, OUT Parameters, Refcursor, Transaction Semantics, dan PL/pgSQL Boundary Design

## 1. Introduction

Dalam aplikasi **Jersey/JAX-RS + MyBatis + PostgreSQL**, stored function/procedure sering muncul untuk kebutuhan seperti:

```text id="w39k3s"
complex validation close to data
bulk processing
legacy database logic
workflow escalation
audit mutation
reporting helper
queue claiming
database-side security boundary
integration with existing PL/pgSQL
```

Tetapi stored procedure/function juga berisiko membuat database menjadi **application layer tersembunyi**. Jadi desain boundary-nya harus jelas:

```text id="urhwsv"
Java service:
  orchestrates use case, transaction, authorization, API semantics

MyBatis:
  maps Java parameters/result to SQL call

PL/pgSQL:
  executes data-local logic that benefits from being near data
```

MyBatis mendukung stored procedure call melalui mapped statement dengan `statementType="CALLABLE"` dan parameter `mode=IN`, `OUT`, atau `INOUT`. Untuk OUT/INOUT, MyBatis akan mengubah nilai property pada parameter object/map setelah call selesai. MyBatis juga mendokumentasikan bahwa OUT cursor tertentu membutuhkan `resultMap` untuk mapping result set. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/sqlmap-xml.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 2. Function vs Procedure di PostgreSQL

PostgreSQL punya **function** dan **procedure**. Function dibuat dengan `CREATE FUNCTION` dan biasanya dipanggil via `SELECT`. Procedure dibuat dengan `CREATE PROCEDURE` dan dipanggil via `CALL`. PostgreSQL mendokumentasikan bahwa `CREATE PROCEDURE` mendefinisikan procedure baru, sedangkan `CREATE FUNCTION` mendefinisikan function baru. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-createprocedure.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

| Aspek | Function | Procedure |
|---|---|---|
| Dibuat dengan | `CREATE FUNCTION` | `CREATE PROCEDURE` |
| Dipanggil dengan | `SELECT function(...)` | `CALL procedure(...)` |
| Return value | Bisa return scalar/table/refcursor/etc | Tidak return seperti function, tapi bisa OUT/INOUT |
| Cocok untuk | Query helper, calculation, set-returning result | Operation/proses side-effect |
| Transaction control internal | Umumnya tidak untuk commit/rollback manual | Bisa dalam kondisi tertentu |
| Dipakai di SQL expression | Ya | Tidak seperti function |
| MyBatis call style | `<select>` atau callable | callable |

**Rule praktis:**  
Gunakan **function** untuk query/calculation/result. Gunakan **procedure** untuk operation yang secara semantik melakukan proses, terutama jika memang ingin dipanggil sebagai `CALL`.

---

## 3. Mental Model Integration

```mermaid id="i5upw0"
flowchart TD
    A[JAX-RS Resource] --> B[Application Service]
    B --> C[Unit of Work / Transaction Boundary]
    C --> D[Repository]
    D --> E[MyBatis Mapper]
    E --> F{Call Type}

    F -->|SELECT function| G[PostgreSQL Function]
    F -->|CALL procedure| H[PostgreSQL Procedure]
    F -->|CALLABLE statement| I[IN / OUT / INOUT Params]

    G --> J[(PostgreSQL Tables)]
    H --> J
    I --> J

    J --> K[Result / OUT Params / Refcursor]
    K --> E
    E --> D
    D --> B
    B --> L[HTTP Response]

    G --> M[PL/pgSQL Exception]
    H --> M
    M --> N[SQLSTATE / SQLException]
    N --> B
```

**Core invariant:**

```text id="kkebsq"
Stored function/procedure boleh menjalankan data-local logic.

Tapi:
- authorization tetap dimulai di application service
- transaction boundary harus jelas
- error harus diklasifikasi
- result contract harus dites
- jangan sembunyikan seluruh business workflow di database tanpa ownership/test/versioning
```

---

## 4. Decomposition Map

| Area | Fokus Praktis | Skill yang Dikuasai | Risiko Jika Salah |
|---|---|---|---|
| **Function Call** | Panggil PostgreSQL function dari mapper | `SELECT function(...)`, scalar/table result | Function jadi hidden service |
| **Procedure Call** | Panggil `CALL procedure(...)` | `statementType=CALLABLE` | Transaction boundary kacau |
| **Callable Statement** | IN/OUT/INOUT mapping | `mode`, `jdbcType`, parameter map/object | OUT value tidak terbaca |
| **Refcursor** | Cursor result dari PL/pgSQL | transaction/session lifecycle | Cursor hilang karena transaction closed |
| **Set-Returning Function** | Function return table | `SELECT * FROM function(...)` | Mapping resultMap salah |
| **Error Propagation** | PL/pgSQL exception ke Java | SQLSTATE, exception mapper | Semua error jadi HTTP 500 |
| **Transaction Semantics** | Siapa commit/rollback? | service-managed vs procedure-managed | Partial commit tersembunyi |
| **Boundary Design** | Logic mana di DB vs Java | data-local vs workflow orchestration | DB jadi monolith app |
| **Security Definer** | DB-side privileged function | least privilege, safe search_path | Privilege escalation |
| **Volatility** | Function behavior declaration | `IMMUTABLE`, `STABLE`, `VOLATILE` | Planner assumption salah |
| **Migration Versioning** | Version PL/pgSQL code | Flyway/Liquibase function migration | Prod DB logic drift |
| **Testing** | Function/procedure integration test | Testcontainers PostgreSQL | Bug hanya muncul runtime |
| **Observability** | Log mapper/function duration | statement id, SQLSTATE, DB logs | Sulit debug |
| **Performance** | Avoid row-by-row DB loops | set-based SQL, EXPLAIN | Lambat tersembunyi |

---

## 5. Function Call Pattern - Scalar Result

### PostgreSQL function

```sql id="8p57sd"
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION calculate_case_age_days(
    p_created_at timestamptz
)
RETURNS integer
LANGUAGE sql
STABLE
AS $$
    SELECT GREATEST(0, (CURRENT_DATE - p_created_at::date)::integer);
$$;
```

### Mapper

```java id="8w97x2"
public interface CaseFunctionMapper {

    int calculateCaseAgeDays(@Param("createdAt") Instant createdAt);
}
```

### XML

```xml id="qhelj1"
<select id="calculateCaseAgeDays" resultType="int">
  SELECT calculate_case_age_days(#{createdAt})
</select>
```

**Kapan layak:** logic benar-benar data/time/database-specific atau sudah menjadi standard DB function. Kalau calculation murni domain dan mudah di-Java, sebaiknya tetap di Java agar unit-test cepat dan tidak perlu DB call.

---

## 6. Function Call Pattern - Table Result

### PostgreSQL function

```sql id="ansgh7"
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION find_cases_eligible_for_escalation(
    p_tenant_id uuid,
    p_limit integer
)
RETURNS TABLE (
    id uuid,
    case_number text,
    status text,
    priority text,
    due_at timestamptz
)
LANGUAGE sql
STABLE
AS $$
    SELECT
        c.id,
        c.case_number,
        c.status,
        c.priority,
        c.due_at
    FROM cases c
    WHERE c.tenant_id = p_tenant_id
      AND c.status IN ('OPEN', 'IN_REVIEW')
      AND c.due_at < now()
    ORDER BY c.due_at ASC, c.id ASC
    LIMIT p_limit;
$$;
```

### DTO

```java id="ndg1ek"
public record EscalationCandidateDto(
    UUID id,
    String caseNumber,
    String status,
    String priority,
    Instant dueAt
) {}
```

### Mapper

```java id="24y16l"
public interface CaseFunctionMapper {

    List<EscalationCandidateDto> findCasesEligibleForEscalation(
        @Param("tenantId") UUID tenantId,
        @Param("limit") int limit
    );
}
```

### XML

```xml id="cn6gem"
<select id="findCasesEligibleForEscalation"
        resultType="com.company.app.dto.EscalationCandidateDto">
  SELECT
    id,
    case_number AS caseNumber,
    status,
    priority,
    due_at AS dueAt
  FROM find_cases_eligible_for_escalation(
    #{tenantId},
    #{limit}
  )
</select>
```

**Rule:** set-returning function cocok jika function adalah query contract yang reusable dan stabil. Untuk query yang sering berubah mengikuti UI/API, XML mapper biasa sering lebih mudah di-review.

---

## 7. Procedure Call Pattern - Simple IN Parameters

### PostgreSQL procedure

```sql id="7hrdgv"
CREATE OR REPLACE PROCEDURE archive_old_audit_logs(
    p_before timestamptz,
    p_limit integer
)
LANGUAGE plpgsql
AS $$
BEGIN
    WITH batch AS (
        SELECT id
        FROM audit_logs
        WHERE created_at < p_before
          AND archived = false
        ORDER BY id
        LIMIT p_limit
    )
    UPDATE audit_logs a
    SET archived = true,
        archived_at = now()
    FROM batch
    WHERE a.id = batch.id;
END;
$$;
```

### Mapper

```java id="6ofc6c"
public interface MaintenanceProcedureMapper {

    void archiveOldAuditLogs(
        @Param("before") Instant before,
        @Param("limit") int limit
    );
}
```

### XML

```xml id="4kthfg"
<update id="archiveOldAuditLogs" statementType="CALLABLE">
  { CALL archive_old_audit_logs(
      #{before, mode=IN, jdbcType=TIMESTAMP},
      #{limit, mode=IN, jdbcType=INTEGER}
    )
  }
</update>
```

**Catatan:** walaupun statement XML memakai `<update>`, secara konseptual ini call operation yang memiliki side-effect. Pastikan service yang memanggil tetap jelas transaction boundary-nya.

---

## 8. Procedure Call dengan OUT Parameter

### PostgreSQL procedure

```sql id="whc02t"
CREATE OR REPLACE PROCEDURE approve_case_proc(
    IN p_tenant_id uuid,
    IN p_case_id uuid,
    IN p_actor_id uuid,
    IN p_expected_version bigint,
    OUT p_updated integer,
    OUT p_new_version bigint
)
LANGUAGE plpgsql
AS $$
BEGIN
    UPDATE cases
    SET status = 'APPROVED',
        version = version + 1,
        approved_by = p_actor_id,
        approved_at = now()
    WHERE tenant_id = p_tenant_id
      AND id = p_case_id
      AND status = 'SUBMITTED'
      AND version = p_expected_version;

    GET DIAGNOSTICS p_updated = ROW_COUNT;

    IF p_updated = 1 THEN
        SELECT version
        INTO p_new_version
        FROM cases
        WHERE tenant_id = p_tenant_id
          AND id = p_case_id;
    ELSE
        p_new_version := NULL;
    END IF;
END;
$$;
```

### Parameter object

```java id="37qcs8"
public class ApproveCaseProcedureParams {

    private UUID tenantId;
    private UUID caseId;
    private UUID actorId;
    private long expectedVersion;

    private Integer updated;
    private Long newVersion;

    // getters and setters required by MyBatis

    public UUID getTenantId() {
        return tenantId;
    }

    public void setTenantId(UUID tenantId) {
        this.tenantId = tenantId;
    }

    public UUID getCaseId() {
        return caseId;
    }

    public void setCaseId(UUID caseId) {
        this.caseId = caseId;
    }

    public UUID getActorId() {
        return actorId;
    }

    public void setActorId(UUID actorId) {
        this.actorId = actorId;
    }

    public long getExpectedVersion() {
        return expectedVersion;
    }

    public void setExpectedVersion(long expectedVersion) {
        this.expectedVersion = expectedVersion;
    }

    public Integer getUpdated() {
        return updated;
    }

    public void setUpdated(Integer updated) {
        this.updated = updated;
    }

    public Long getNewVersion() {
        return newVersion;
    }

    public void setNewVersion(Long newVersion) {
        this.newVersion = newVersion;
    }
}
```

### Mapper

```java id="fulpbl"
public interface CaseProcedureMapper {

    void approveCase(ApproveCaseProcedureParams params);
}
```

### XML

```xml id="it6rfi"
<update id="approveCase"
        parameterType="com.company.app.persistence.proc.ApproveCaseProcedureParams"
        statementType="CALLABLE">
  { CALL approve_case_proc(
      #{tenantId, mode=IN, jdbcType=OTHER},
      #{caseId, mode=IN, jdbcType=OTHER},
      #{actorId, mode=IN, jdbcType=OTHER},
      #{expectedVersion, mode=IN, jdbcType=BIGINT},
      #{updated, mode=OUT, jdbcType=INTEGER},
      #{newVersion, mode=OUT, jdbcType=BIGINT}
    )
  }
</update>
```

### Service usage

```java id="yy52ti"
public CaseResponse approve(ApproveCaseCommand command) {
    return unitOfWork.transactional(ctx -> {
        CaseProcedureMapper mapper = ctx.mapper(CaseProcedureMapper.class);

        ApproveCaseProcedureParams params = new ApproveCaseProcedureParams();
        params.setTenantId(command.tenantId());
        params.setCaseId(command.caseId());
        params.setActorId(command.actorId());
        params.setExpectedVersion(command.expectedVersion());

        mapper.approveCase(params);

        if (!Integer.valueOf(1).equals(params.getUpdated())) {
            throw new InvalidCaseTransitionException(command.caseId());
        }

        return new CaseResponse(
            command.caseId(),
            "APPROVED",
            params.getNewVersion()
        );
    });
}
```

**MyBatis behavior:** OUT/INOUT values are written back into the parameter object or map property, not returned as the Java method return value. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/sqlmap-xml.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 9. OUT Parameter dengan `Map`

### Mapper

```java id="op5ewo"
void approveCase(Map<String, Object> params);
```

### Java

```java id="9gyf00"
Map<String, Object> params = new HashMap<>();
params.put("tenantId", tenantId);
params.put("caseId", caseId);
params.put("actorId", actorId);
params.put("expectedVersion", expectedVersion);

mapper.approveCase(params);

Integer updated = (Integer) params.get("updated");
Long newVersion = (Long) params.get("newVersion");
```

### XML

```xml id="4so60v"
<update id="approveCase"
        parameterType="map"
        statementType="CALLABLE">
  { CALL approve_case_proc(
      #{tenantId, mode=IN, jdbcType=OTHER},
      #{caseId, mode=IN, jdbcType=OTHER},
      #{actorId, mode=IN, jdbcType=OTHER},
      #{expectedVersion, mode=IN, jdbcType=BIGINT},
      #{updated, mode=OUT, jdbcType=INTEGER},
      #{newVersion, mode=OUT, jdbcType=BIGINT}
    )
  }
</update>
```

| Parameter Object | Map |
|---|---|
| Type-safe-ish | Flexible |
| Better refactor support | Faster for quick integration |
| More boilerplate | More casting |
| Better for production contracts | Risk typo keys |

**Recommendation:** gunakan parameter object untuk procedure contract yang penting.

---

## 10. PostgreSQL Refcursor Pattern

PostgreSQL PL/pgSQL cursor variables are of special data type `refcursor`; the docs state all cursor access in PL/pgSQL goes through cursor variables of type `refcursor`. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/plpgsql-cursors.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Function returning refcursor

```sql id="v6bol7"
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION open_case_cursor(
    p_tenant_id uuid,
    p_status text
)
RETURNS refcursor
LANGUAGE plpgsql
AS $$
DECLARE
    case_cursor refcursor;
BEGIN
    OPEN case_cursor FOR
        SELECT
            id,
            case_number,
            status,
            priority,
            due_at
        FROM cases
        WHERE tenant_id = p_tenant_id
          AND status = p_status
        ORDER BY due_at ASC, id ASC;

    RETURN case_cursor;
END;
$$;
```

### Practical warning

Refcursor lifecycle is transaction-bound in practice because the cursor portal lives in the current session/transaction context. If the transaction is committed/closed before fetching, cursor usage can fail. PostgreSQL docs emphasize cursor variables and cursor access semantics in PL/pgSQL, and transaction semantics around cursors need careful handling. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/plpgsql-cursors.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Better alternative for most MyBatis apps

Instead of refcursor, prefer:

```sql id="8qmoi5"
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION find_cases_for_export(
    p_tenant_id uuid,
    p_status text
)
RETURNS TABLE (
    id uuid,
    case_number text,
    status text,
    priority text,
    due_at timestamptz
)
LANGUAGE sql
STABLE
AS $$
    SELECT
        id,
        case_number,
        status,
        priority,
        due_at
    FROM cases
    WHERE tenant_id = p_tenant_id
      AND status = p_status
    ORDER BY due_at ASC, id ASC;
$$;
```

Then map as normal `SELECT`.

**Rule praktis:** gunakan `RETURNS TABLE` atau normal `SELECT` + MyBatis cursor/fetchSize untuk Java apps. Refcursor lebih sering cocok untuk legacy DB API atau driver/procedure contract tertentu.

---

## 11. Callable Refcursor - Caution Pattern

MyBatis docs mention `jdbcType=CURSOR` primarily in the context of databases such as Oracle REF CURSOR and require a `resultMap` for mapping cursor result sets. PostgreSQL refcursor integration can be driver/tooling-sensitive, so for PostgreSQL prefer set-returning functions unless project already has a proven refcursor pattern. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/sqlmap-xml.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### If legacy requires refcursor

Treat this as a special integration requiring:

```text id="206hm8"
- one open transaction
- session kept open while fetching
- explicit integration test against PostgreSQL
- driver behavior verified
- no HTTP long-running cursor unless carefully controlled
```

**Recommended fallback:** convert refcursor procedure to `RETURNS TABLE` wrapper function if possible.

---

## 12. Transaction Semantics: Java vs Procedure

PostgreSQL PL/pgSQL transaction control is only possible in `CALL` or `DO` invocations from top level or nested `CALL`/`DO` invocations without intervening commands; if the call stack includes `SELECT func2()` between `CALL`s, later procedure transaction control is not allowed. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/plpgsql-transactions.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Safer service-managed transaction

```text id="xzcf9u"
Java service opens transaction
  -> CALL procedure / SELECT function
  -> insert audit/outbox if needed
  -> commit/rollback in Java
```

### Riskier procedure-managed transaction

```text id="rnuu1u"
CALL procedure
  -> procedure commits/rollbacks internally
  -> Java service may not control atomicity
```

| Pattern | Recommended? | Notes |
|---|---:|---|
| Java service owns transaction | Ya | Paling predictable untuk JAX-RS service |
| Procedure performs data mutation only | Ya | No internal commit/rollback |
| Procedure performs internal commit | Jarang | Use for top-level maintenance procedures only |
| Function performs side-effect | Hindari | Function should be query/calculation-like where possible |
| Procedure called inside service transaction then commits internally | Hindari | Boundary confusing |
| Outbox/audit outside same transaction | Hindari | Consistency risk |

---

## 13. Transaction Boundary Decision Table

| Use Case | Recommended Boundary |
|---|---|
| Approve case + audit + outbox | Java service transaction |
| Bulk maintenance job standalone | Procedure can own process if called top-level |
| Report function | No mutation; read-only query |
| Queue claim | Java transaction + SQL/mapper, or function without internal commit |
| Backfill chunks | Java job controls chunk transaction |
| Legacy procedure already commits | Isolate behind adapter; do not combine with other writes |
| Stored procedure mutates multiple tables | Java still controls outer transaction unless procedure is top-level maintenance |
| Function used in SELECT projection | Must be side-effect safe |

---

## 14. PL/pgSQL Exception → Java Error Mapping

### PostgreSQL function/procedure

```sql id="sezihv"
CREATE OR REPLACE PROCEDURE submit_case_proc(
    IN p_tenant_id uuid,
    IN p_case_id uuid,
    IN p_expected_version bigint
)
LANGUAGE plpgsql
AS $$
DECLARE
    v_updated integer;
BEGIN
    UPDATE cases
    SET status = 'SUBMITTED',
        version = version + 1,
        submitted_at = now()
    WHERE tenant_id = p_tenant_id
      AND id = p_case_id
      AND status = 'DRAFT'
      AND version = p_expected_version;

    GET DIAGNOSTICS v_updated = ROW_COUNT;

    IF v_updated <> 1 THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Invalid case transition'
            USING ERRCODE = 'P0001',
                  DETAIL = 'Case not in DRAFT or version mismatch',
                  HINT = 'Reload case and retry';
    END IF;
END;
$$;
```

### Java classifier

```java id="7az8ta"
public enum PersistenceErrorKind {
    UNIQUE_VIOLATION,
    FOREIGN_KEY_VIOLATION,
    CHECK_VIOLATION,
    DEADLOCK,
    SERIALIZATION_FAILURE,
    PROCEDURE_RAISED_EXCEPTION,
    UNKNOWN
}
```

```java id="9c8epi"
public final class PostgresErrorClassifier {

    public static PersistenceErrorKind classify(Throwable throwable) {
        SQLException sqlException = findSqlException(throwable);

        if (sqlException == null) {
            return PersistenceErrorKind.UNKNOWN;
        }

        return switch (sqlException.getSQLState()) {
            case "23505" -> PersistenceErrorKind.UNIQUE_VIOLATION;
            case "23503" -> PersistenceErrorKind.FOREIGN_KEY_VIOLATION;
            case "23514" -> PersistenceErrorKind.CHECK_VIOLATION;
            case "40P01" -> PersistenceErrorKind.DEADLOCK;
            case "40001" -> PersistenceErrorKind.SERIALIZATION_FAILURE;
            case "P0001" -> PersistenceErrorKind.PROCEDURE_RAISED_EXCEPTION;
            default -> PersistenceErrorKind.UNKNOWN;
        };
    }

    private static SQLException findSqlException(Throwable throwable) {
        Throwable current = throwable;

        while (current != null) {
            if (current instanceof SQLException sqlException) {
                return sqlException;
            }
            current = current.getCause();
        }

        return null;
    }
}
```

**Rule:** PL/pgSQL exception harus punya SQLSTATE/contract yang bisa diklasifikasi. Jangan hanya mengandalkan string message.

---

## 15. Function Volatility

PostgreSQL function definitions include function attributes such as volatility categories. `CREATE FUNCTION` supports attributes including `IMMUTABLE`, `STABLE`, and `VOLATILE`; this informs the planner about function behavior. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-createfunction.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

| Volatility | Meaning Praktis | Contoh |
|---|---|---|
| `IMMUTABLE` | Same input always same output | pure normalization/math |
| `STABLE` | Same within one statement; reads DB/time-ish stable enough | lookup/config read |
| `VOLATILE` | Can change anytime or has side effects | `now()`, mutation, random, sequence |

### Examples

```sql id="wk17j7"
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION normalize_case_number(p_case_number text)
RETURNS text
LANGUAGE sql
IMMUTABLE
AS $$
    SELECT upper(trim(p_case_number));
$$;
```

```sql id="xf6bag"
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION find_case_count_by_status(p_tenant_id uuid)
RETURNS TABLE(status text, total bigint)
LANGUAGE sql
STABLE
AS $$
    SELECT status, count(*)
    FROM cases
    WHERE tenant_id = p_tenant_id
    GROUP BY status;
$$;
```

```sql id="6b0c38"
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION create_audit_id()
RETURNS uuid
LANGUAGE sql
VOLATILE
AS $$
    SELECT gen_random_uuid();
$$;
```

**Warning:** jangan menandai function sebagai `IMMUTABLE` jika membaca tabel, waktu, sequence, setting session, atau data eksternal.

---

## 16. SECURITY DEFINER Boundary

PostgreSQL `CREATE FUNCTION` supports `SECURITY INVOKER` and `SECURITY DEFINER`; `SECURITY DEFINER` executes with privileges of the function owner rather than the caller. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-createfunction.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Use case

```text id="pui9gd"
app_runtime tidak punya direct update ke sensitive table,
tetapi boleh memanggil function/procedure terbatas yang melakukan validasi dan update tertentu.
```

### Example

```sql id="npnr47"
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION approve_case_secure(
    p_tenant_id uuid,
    p_case_id uuid,
    p_actor_id uuid
)
RETURNS void
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = public
AS $$
BEGIN
    UPDATE cases
    SET status = 'APPROVED',
        approved_by = p_actor_id,
        approved_at = now()
    WHERE tenant_id = p_tenant_id
      AND id = p_case_id
      AND status = 'SUBMITTED';

    IF NOT FOUND THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'case cannot be approved'
            USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';
    END IF;
END;
$$;
```

### Security checklist

| Checklist | Required |
|---|---:|
| Function owner is not superuser unless unavoidable | Ya |
| `search_path` fixed inside function | Ya |
| Function validates tenant and input | Ya |
| Caller only granted `EXECUTE` | Ya |
| Direct table privilege restricted if intended | Ya |
| Function audited/test-covered | Ya |
| No dynamic SQL from user input | Ya |
| SQL injection reviewed inside PL/pgSQL | Ya |

**Rule:** `SECURITY DEFINER` is powerful and dangerous. Treat it as privileged API inside the database.

---

## 17. Grant EXECUTE Only

```sql id="f87zin"
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION approve_case_secure(uuid, uuid, uuid)
FROM PUBLIC;

GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION approve_case_secure(uuid, uuid, uuid)
TO app_runtime;
```

For procedures:

```sql id="sv8t80"
REVOKE ALL ON PROCEDURE archive_old_audit_logs(timestamptz, integer)
FROM PUBLIC;

GRANT EXECUTE ON PROCEDURE archive_old_audit_logs(timestamptz, integer)
TO app_backfill;
```

**Design idea:** app role may not need broad table update privileges if all mutation happens through reviewed DB routines. But this increases PL/pgSQL ownership burden, so use selectively.

---

## 18. Dynamic SQL Inside PL/pgSQL

### Bad

```sql id="h1cleu"
EXECUTE 'SELECT * FROM ' || p_table_name || ' WHERE id = ' || p_id;
```

### Better with format and safe identifier/value

```sql id="zrm96h"
EXECUTE format(
    'SELECT count(*) FROM %I WHERE tenant_id = $1',
    p_table_name
)
USING p_tenant_id;
```

But better still: avoid dynamic table names unless internal and whitelisted.

### Whitelist table

```sql id="jv1i9h"
IF p_table_name NOT IN ('cases', 'orders') THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'invalid table name'
        USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';
END IF;
```

**Rule:** dynamic SQL risk exists in PL/pgSQL too, not only in MyBatis XML.

---

## 19. Boundary Design: What Belongs in PL/pgSQL?

| Logic | Java Service | PL/pgSQL |
|---|---:|---:|
| HTTP validation | Ya | Tidak |
| Authorization from JWT/claims | Ya | Optional DB defense |
| State transition orchestration | Usually | Sometimes if data-local |
| Multi-table data mutation | Ya or PL/pgSQL | Good candidate if atomic/data-local |
| Complex set-based update | Maybe | Good candidate |
| Report query helper | Maybe | Good candidate |
| External API call | Ya, not DB | Tidak |
| Kafka/outbox publish | Java/worker | DB only inserts outbox row |
| UI-specific filtering | Java/MyBatis | Usually no |
| DB constraint enforcement | No | Ya |
| Security definer privileged mutation | Maybe | Good candidate but risky |
| Long-running maintenance | Java job + SQL/procedure | Procedure okay if controlled |
| Business policy changing often | Java | Usually no |
| Legacy packaged DB logic | Adapter in Java | Existing PL/pgSQL |

---

## 20. PL/pgSQL Boundary Smells

| Smell | Meaning |
|---|---|
| Procedure sends HTTP/email | DB doing application integration |
| Procedure commits internally while Java expects transaction | Boundary conflict |
| Function mutates data but called in SELECT | Hidden side effect |
| All business rules only in PL/pgSQL | Java service becomes pass-through |
| Java service duplicates same logic as PL/pgSQL | Divergent rules |
| Procedure returns loosely structured text JSON for everything | Contract unclear |
| Procedure uses dynamic SQL with raw input | Injection risk |
| No migration/versioning for functions | DB drift |
| No integration tests | Behavior untrusted |
| Refcursor returned to long HTTP response | Connection/transaction risk |
| SECURITY DEFINER without fixed search_path | Privilege escalation risk |
| OUT params not documented | Mapper contract fragile |

---

## 21. MyBatis Mapper Style Guide for Procedures

| Rule | Example |
|---|---|
| Suffix mapper clearly | `CaseProcedureMapper` |
| Suffix parameter object | `ApproveCaseProcedureParams` |
| Use parameter object for OUT | Avoid raw Map for important calls |
| Explicit `jdbcType` | Especially UUID/JSONB/OUT |
| Do not expose procedure mapper to resource | Only repository/service |
| One procedure call per method | Avoid generic `call(String)` |
| Check OUT status/count | Do not ignore procedure result |
| Classify SQLSTATE | Map to business/transient errors |
| Test with PostgreSQL | No mock-only procedure tests |

---

## 22. Repository Adapter for Procedure

```java id="c3o8iv"
public class MyBatisCaseProcedureRepository {

    private final MyBatisSessionContext context;

    public MyBatisCaseProcedureRepository(MyBatisSessionContext context) {
        this.context = context;
    }

    public CaseApprovalResult approveCase(
        UUID tenantId,
        UUID caseId,
        UUID actorId,
        long expectedVersion
    ) {
        CaseProcedureMapper mapper = context.mapper(CaseProcedureMapper.class);

        ApproveCaseProcedureParams params = new ApproveCaseProcedureParams();
        params.setTenantId(tenantId);
        params.setCaseId(caseId);
        params.setActorId(actorId);
        params.setExpectedVersion(expectedVersion);

        mapper.approveCase(params);

        if (!Integer.valueOf(1).equals(params.getUpdated())) {
            return CaseApprovalResult.conflict();
        }

        return CaseApprovalResult.approved(params.getNewVersion());
    }
}
```

**Rule:** service should consume domain result, not raw procedure param object if possible.

---

## 23. Testing Strategy

### Migration test

```java id="sv904r"
@Test
void shouldCreateProcedureInMigration() {
    runMigrations();

    try (SqlSession session = openSession()) {
        CaseProcedureMapper mapper =
            session.getMapper(CaseProcedureMapper.class);

        assertThat(mapper).isNotNull();
    }
}
```

### Procedure happy path

```java id="0ul6wb"
@Test
void approveCaseProcedureShouldApproveSubmittedCase() {
    UUID tenantId = UUID.randomUUID();
    UUID caseId = fixture.insertSubmittedCase(tenantId, 0L);
    UUID actorId = UUID.randomUUID();

    try (SqlSession session = openSession()) {
        CaseProcedureMapper mapper =
            session.getMapper(CaseProcedureMapper.class);

        ApproveCaseProcedureParams params = new ApproveCaseProcedureParams();
        params.setTenantId(tenantId);
        params.setCaseId(caseId);
        params.setActorId(actorId);
        params.setExpectedVersion(0L);

        mapper.approveCase(params);
        session.commit();

        assertThat(params.getUpdated()).isEqualTo(1);
        assertThat(params.getNewVersion()).isEqualTo(1L);
    }

    assertThat(fixture.findCaseStatus(tenantId, caseId))
        .isEqualTo("APPROVED");
}
```

### Procedure conflict path

```java id="hwtp2z"
@Test
void approveCaseProcedureShouldReturnZeroForWrongVersion() {
    UUID tenantId = UUID.randomUUID();
    UUID caseId = fixture.insertSubmittedCase(tenantId, 1L);

    try (SqlSession session = openSession()) {
        CaseProcedureMapper mapper =
            session.getMapper(CaseProcedureMapper.class);

        ApproveCaseProcedureParams params = new ApproveCaseProcedureParams();
        params.setTenantId(tenantId);
        params.setCaseId(caseId);
        params.setActorId(UUID.randomUUID());
        params.setExpectedVersion(0L);

        mapper.approveCase(params);
        session.commit();

        assertThat(params.getUpdated()).isEqualTo(0);
        assertThat(params.getNewVersion()).isNull();
    }
}
```

### Error SQLSTATE test

```java id="2x329i"
@Test
void shouldClassifyProcedureRaisedException() {
    Throwable thrown = catchThrowable(() -> {
        try (SqlSession session = openSession()) {
            CaseProcedureMapper mapper =
                session.getMapper(CaseProcedureMapper.class);

            mapper.submitInvalidCase(...);
            session.commit();
        }
    });

    assertThat(PostgresErrorClassifier.classify(thrown))
        .isEqualTo(PersistenceErrorKind.PROCEDURE_RAISED_EXCEPTION);
}
```

## 24. Performance Notes

| Pattern | Risk | Recommendation |
|---|---|---|
| PL/pgSQL row-by-row loop | Slow for many rows | Prefer set-based SQL |
| Procedure hides expensive query | Hard to diagnose | Log statement + use `pg_stat_statements` |
| Function called per row in large query | CPU explosion | Check volatility/cost; avoid if expensive |
| Refcursor long-lived | Holds transaction/session | Prefer streaming query/cursor in MyBatis |
| Dynamic SQL in procedure | Plan/cache/injection risk | Use safely and sparingly |
| Procedure doing huge transaction | Lock/rollback impact | Chunk from Java job or procedure loop carefully |
| No EXPLAIN on function query | Blind tuning | Extract inner SQL and EXPLAIN |

### Set-based update preferred

```sql id="lx07ek"
UPDATE cases
SET status = 'ESCALATED',
    escalated_at = now()
WHERE status IN ('OPEN', 'IN_REVIEW')
  AND due_at < now();
```

Instead of:

```sql id="fjrddf"
FOR v_case IN SELECT id FROM cases WHERE ... LOOP
    UPDATE cases SET ... WHERE id = v_case.id;
END LOOP;
```

---

## 25. Observability Pattern

### SQL comment in mapper

```xml id="eyp5h9"
<update id="approveCase"
        parameterType="com.company.app.persistence.proc.ApproveCaseProcedureParams"
        statementType="CALLABLE">
  { CALL approve_case_proc(
      #{tenantId, mode=IN, jdbcType=OTHER},
      #{caseId, mode=IN, jdbcType=OTHER},
      #{actorId, mode=IN, jdbcType=OTHER},
      #{expectedVersion, mode=IN, jdbcType=BIGINT},
      #{updated, mode=OUT, jdbcType=INTEGER},
      #{newVersion, mode=OUT, jdbcType=BIGINT}
    )
  }
</update>
```

### App metric

```text id="j703jf"
db.procedure.duration{procedure="approve_case_proc"}
db.procedure.error{procedure="approve_case_proc", sqlstate="P0001"}
db.procedure.conflict{procedure="approve_case_proc"}
```

### Log safe result

```java id="9ezhqr"
log.info(
    "Procedure approve_case_proc completed tenantId={} caseId={} updated={} newVersion={}",
    tenantId,
    caseId,
    params.getUpdated(),
    params.getNewVersion()
);
```

**Do not log:** full payload, sensitive JSON, DB credentials, raw exception detail to client.

---

## 26. Migration Versioning for PL/pgSQL

### Flyway file

```text id="f8pvbu"
V20260713_0100__create_approve_case_proc.sql
V20260720_0100__replace_approve_case_proc_add_audit.sql
V20260727_0100__drop_legacy_approve_case_proc.sql
```

### Procedure migration

```sql id="1m5n4f"
CREATE OR REPLACE PROCEDURE approve_case_proc(
    IN p_tenant_id uuid,
    IN p_case_id uuid,
    IN p_actor_id uuid,
    IN p_expected_version bigint,
    OUT p_updated integer,
    OUT p_new_version bigint
)
LANGUAGE plpgsql
AS $$
BEGIN
    -- implementation
END;
$$;
```

**Compatibility rule:** changing parameter order/type/name can break MyBatis call. Treat procedure signature as API contract.

---

## 27. Versioned Procedure Contract Strategy

| Change | Safe Strategy |
|---|---|
| Internal SQL change only | `CREATE OR REPLACE` same signature |
| Add optional behavior | Add new parameter? Prefer new procedure version if call contract changes |
| Change OUT params | New procedure name/version |
| Remove parameter | New procedure; migrate Java mapper |
| Rename procedure | Deploy new procedure, update app, drop old later |
| Change error SQLSTATE | Update classifier/tests |
| Change result shape | Update resultMap/tests |
| Add security definer | Review grants/search_path |
| Drop old procedure | Only after no app version calls it |

### Example

```sql id="94ljvx"
CREATE OR REPLACE PROCEDURE approve_case_proc_v2(...)
LANGUAGE plpgsql
AS $$
BEGIN
    -- new contract
END;
$$;
```

```java id="duvb68"
void approveCaseV2(ApproveCaseProcedureV2Params params);
```

---

## 28. Security Checklist

| Item | Required |
|---|---:|
| Procedure validates tenant where applicable | Ya |
| Procedure does not trust caller blindly | Ya |
| Dynamic SQL uses `format('%I')` and `USING` | Ya |
| No raw user input concatenation | Ya |
| SECURITY DEFINER has fixed `search_path` | Ya |
| EXECUTE grants are minimal | Ya |
| Function/procedure owner reviewed | Ya |
| Error messages do not leak sensitive detail | Ya |
| Audit generated for sensitive mutation | Ya |
| Java service still performs authorization | Ya |
| Migration grants reviewed | Ya |

---

## 29. Anti-Pattern

| Anti-Pattern | Example | Impact |
|---|---|---|
| Resource calls procedure mapper directly | HTTP layer bypasses service | Auth/transaction scattered |
| Procedure commits internally inside app transaction | Hidden partial commit | Atomicity broken |
| Function mutates data | `SELECT approve_case(...)` | Side-effect hidden |
| Refcursor for normal list API | Cursor lifecycle complexity | Connection leaks/timeouts |
| OUT params ignored | Call result unchecked | Invalid state accepted |
| Procedure returns magic strings | `"OK"`, `"ERR1"` | Fragile contract |
| Dynamic SQL concat in PL/pgSQL | `'... ' || user_input` | SQL injection |
| SECURITY DEFINER without `search_path` | Object hijacking risk | Privilege escalation |
| No integration test | Procedure may break silently | Runtime failures |
| Signature changed in-place | Old app mapper breaks | Deployment incident |
| Business rules split Java/PLpgSQL inconsistently | Divergent behavior | Defensibility issue |
| Long procedure transaction | Locks/rollback large | Production contention |

---

## 30. Decision Table

| Problem | Recommended Pattern |
|---|---|
| Query helper returns rows | PostgreSQL function `RETURNS TABLE` + MyBatis `<select>` |
| Operation with side effect | Procedure `CALL` via `statementType=CALLABLE` |
| Need OUT status/count | Procedure OUT params + parameter object |
| Need list result | Prefer `RETURNS TABLE` over refcursor |
| Legacy refcursor exists | Keep transaction/session open; test carefully |
| Need privileged DB-side operation | SECURITY DEFINER function/procedure with strict safeguards |
| Need complex set-based update | PL/pgSQL or SQL procedure can be good |
| Need orchestration with audit/outbox | Java service transaction preferred |
| Need transaction control inside DB | Top-level procedure only, isolated use case |
| Need dynamic query | Prefer MyBatis dynamic SQL unless DB-side is clearly better |
| Need rollback with app changes | Avoid procedure internal commit |
| Need external side effect | Not PL/pgSQL; use outbox/worker |

---

## 31. Production Checklist

| Checklist | Status Ideal |
|---|---|
| Procedure/function signatures treated as API contracts | Ya |
| MyBatis callable params have explicit `mode` and `jdbcType` | Ya |
| OUT/INOUT params read from parameter object/map | Ya |
| Procedure result/conflict status checked | Ya |
| Java service owns transaction unless procedure is standalone maintenance | Ya |
| No hidden internal commit in business procedure | Ya |
| Refcursor avoided unless required | Ya |
| Set-returning functions preferred for result lists | Ya |
| PL/pgSQL exceptions use classifiable SQLSTATE | Ya |
| Security definer functions have fixed search_path | Ya |
| EXECUTE privileges are least-privilege | Ya |
| Dynamic SQL inside PL/pgSQL is whitelisted/parameterized | Ya |
| Function volatility declared correctly | Ya |
| Migration tests create/update routines | Ya |
| Integration tests call routines through MyBatis | Ya |
| Observability tracks routine duration/errors | Ya |
| Procedure versioning plan exists | Ya |

---

|---:|---|---|
| 1 | PostgreSQL function vs procedure | Tahu kapan `SELECT` vs `CALL` |
| 2 | MyBatis `statementType=CALLABLE` | Bisa call procedure |
