**Sources:**

- [MyBatis 3 | Mapper XML Files](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/sqlmap-xml.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com)
- [Documentation: 18: CREATE PROCEDURE](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-createprocedure.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com)

## Response:
7/13/2026, 3:00:39 AM

Lanjut ke multi-database dan deployment topology: fokusnya memastikan datasource, transaction boundary, tenant routing, read replica, dan deployment cloud/on-prem tidak membuat data inconsistency atau routing bug.

# Cheatsheet - Java MyBatis Multi-Database, Read/Write Splitting, Multi-Tenant Datasource, Transaction Boundary Across Datasources, dan Cloud/On-Prem Deployment Concerns

## 1. Introduction

Dalam aplikasi **Jersey/JAX-RS + MyBatis + PostgreSQL**, multi-database biasanya muncul dalam beberapa bentuk:

```text id="d8q71g"
1. Primary + read replica
2. Multi-tenant database per tenant
3. Multi-schema per tenant
4. Separate database per bounded context
5. Legacy database + new database
6. Reporting database / replica / warehouse
7. On-prem + cloud hybrid
8. Blue/green database topology
```

MyBatis sendiri mendukung konfigurasi **environment** yang berisi `TransactionManager` dan `DataSource`; setiap `SqlSessionFactory` dibangun dari environment tertentu. Artinya, untuk banyak topology production, pola yang paling jelas adalah **lebih dari satu `SqlSessionFactory`**, masing-masing untuk datasource/topology yang berbeda. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/configuration.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 2. Mental Model

```mermaid id="9a22k1"
flowchart TD
    A[JAX-RS Resource] --> B[Application Service]
    B --> C{Operation Type}

    C -->|Command / Write| D[Primary UnitOfWork]
    C -->|Strong Read After Write| D
    C -->|Eventually Consistent Read| E[Read Replica UnitOfWork]
    C -->|Tenant-specific DB| F[Tenant Routing UnitOfWork]
    C -->|Legacy Integration| G[Legacy DB UnitOfWork]

    D --> H[Primary SqlSessionFactory]
    E --> I[Replica SqlSessionFactory]
    F --> J[Tenant SqlSessionFactory Resolver]
    G --> K[Legacy SqlSessionFactory]

    H --> L[(PostgreSQL Primary)]
    I --> M[(PostgreSQL Standby / Read Replica)]
    J --> N[(Tenant DB / Schema)]
    K --> O[(Legacy DB)]

    B --> P[Transaction Boundary]
    P --> Q[Commit / Rollback Policy]
```

**Core invariant:**

```text id="tnsqwc"
Jangan sembunyikan routing database.

Service harus tahu secara eksplisit:
- apakah operasi butuh primary
- apakah read boleh dari replica
- apakah data tenant-specific
- apakah transaksi melibatkan lebih dari satu datasource
- apakah konsistensi strong atau eventual
```

---

## 3. Decomposition Map

| Area | Fokus Praktis | Pattern Utama | Risiko Jika Salah |
|---|---|---|---|
| **Multiple SqlSessionFactory** | Satu factory per datasource | primary, replica, legacy, tenant | Mapper salah DB |
| **Read/Write Splitting** | Write ke primary, read tertentu ke replica | explicit read policy | Stale read |
| **Read-After-Write** | Setelah write harus baca data terbaru | force primary | User lihat data lama |
| **Replica Lag Awareness** | Replica asynchronous | lag metric, fallback primary | Data inconsistent |
| **Multi-Tenant Routing** | DB/schema per tenant | tenant resolver | Cross-tenant leak |
| **Transaction Boundary** | Satu transaksi per datasource | local transaction | Partial commit antar DB |
| **Cross-DB Transaction** | Operasi lintas datasource | saga/outbox, avoid 2PC if possible | Half-commit |
| **Legacy DB Integration** | Isolasi schema/logic lama | anti-corruption adapter | Legacy bocor ke domain |
| **Connection Pooling** | Pool per datasource | Hikari per DB role | DB overload |
| **Failover** | Primary/secondary topology | pgJDBC multi-host, infra endpoint | Wrong target during failover |
| **Cloud/On-Prem** | Network, DNS, TLS, secrets | config by environment | Timeout/security issue |
| **Migration Strategy** | Migration per DB/schema | ordered migration | Schema drift |
| **Observability** | Metric per datasource | tags: datasource, role | Bottleneck kabur |
| **Testing** | Multi-container topology | Testcontainers/separate DB | Routing bug tidak tertangkap |

---

## 4. Multi-Database Topology Decision Table

| Topology | Cocok Untuk | Pattern |
|---|---|---|
| **Primary only** | App kecil/awal | Single `SqlSessionFactory` |
| **Primary + replica** | Read-heavy workload | Write factory + read factory |
| **Database per tenant** | Strong tenant isolation | Tenant datasource resolver |
| **Schema per tenant** | Banyak tenant, shared DB engine | `search_path` / schema-qualified SQL |
| **Shared table + tenant_id** | SaaS umum | Mandatory tenant predicate + optional RLS |
| **Legacy + new DB** | Migration/modernization | Anti-corruption repository |
| **Reporting replica** | Heavy read/report | Separate read-only datasource |
| **Cloud managed DB** | AWS/Azure/RDS/Flexible Server | Endpoint/secret/TLS driven config |
| **On-prem HA cluster** | Enterprise datacenter | Virtual IP/proxy/multi-host JDBC |
| **Hybrid on-prem/cloud** | Gradual migration/DR | Async integration, outbox, CDC |

---

## 5. Multiple `SqlSessionFactory` Pattern

### 5.1 DataSource role enum

```java id="c4w56a"
public enum DatabaseRole {
    PRIMARY,
    REPLICA,
    LEGACY
}
```

### 5.2 Factory holder

```java id="9d55qv"
public final class SqlSessionFactories {

    private final SqlSessionFactory primary;
    private final SqlSessionFactory replica;
    private final SqlSessionFactory legacy;

    public SqlSessionFactories(
        SqlSessionFactory primary,
        SqlSessionFactory replica,
        SqlSessionFactory legacy
    ) {
        this.primary = primary;
        this.replica = replica;
        this.legacy = legacy;
    }

    public SqlSessionFactory primary() {
        return primary;
    }

    public SqlSessionFactory replica() {
        return replica;
    }

    public SqlSessionFactory legacy() {
        return legacy;
    }

    public SqlSessionFactory byRole(DatabaseRole role) {
        return switch (role) {
            case PRIMARY -> primary;
            case REPLICA -> replica;
            case LEGACY -> legacy;
        };
    }
}
```

### 5.3 Build factory programmatically

```java id="50arxe"
public final class MyBatisFactoryBuilder {

    public SqlSessionFactory build(
        String environmentId,
        DataSource dataSource,
        List<Class<?>> mapperClasses
    ) {
        TransactionFactory transactionFactory =
            new JdbcTransactionFactory();

        Environment environment = new Environment(
            environmentId,
            transactionFactory,
            dataSource
        );

        Configuration configuration = new Configuration(environment);
        configuration.setMapUnderscoreToCamelCase(true);

        for (Class<?> mapperClass : mapperClasses) {
            configuration.addMapper(mapperClass);
        }

        return new SqlSessionFactoryBuilder().build(configuration);
    }
}
```

MyBatis `SqlSessionFactory` membuat `SqlSession`; `SqlSession` adalah interface utama untuk menjalankan command, mengambil mapper, dan mengelola transaksi. Karena `SqlSessionFactory` dibangun dari environment/datasource tertentu, factory per datasource membuat routing lebih eksplisit. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/java-api.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 6. HikariCP per Datasource

### 6.1 Primary pool

```java id="9tmtsi"
public DataSource primaryDataSource() {
    HikariConfig config = new HikariConfig();

    config.setPoolName("app-primary-pool");
    config.setJdbcUrl(requiredEnv("DB_PRIMARY_URL"));
    config.setUsername(requiredEnv("DB_PRIMARY_USERNAME"));
    config.setPassword(requiredEnv("DB_PRIMARY_PASSWORD"));

    config.setMaximumPoolSize(intEnv("DB_PRIMARY_POOL_MAX", 20));
    config.setConnectionTimeout(longEnv("DB_PRIMARY_CONNECTION_TIMEOUT_MS", 3000));
    config.setMaxLifetime(longEnv("DB_PRIMARY_MAX_LIFETIME_MS", 1_740_000));

    return new HikariDataSource(config);
}
```

### 6.2 Replica pool

```java id="ytf38p"
public DataSource replicaDataSource() {
    HikariConfig config = new HikariConfig();

    config.setPoolName("app-replica-pool");
    config.setJdbcUrl(requiredEnv("DB_REPLICA_URL"));
    config.setUsername(requiredEnv("DB_REPLICA_USERNAME"));
    config.setPassword(requiredEnv("DB_REPLICA_PASSWORD"));

    config.setReadOnly(true);
    config.setMaximumPoolSize(intEnv("DB_REPLICA_POOL_MAX", 30));
    config.setConnectionTimeout(longEnv("DB_REPLICA_CONNECTION_TIMEOUT_MS", 3000));
    config.setMaxLifetime(longEnv("DB_REPLICA_MAX_LIFETIME_MS", 1_740_000));

    return new HikariDataSource(config);
}
```

HikariCP `maximumPoolSize` membatasi jumlah koneksi maksimum termasuk idle dan in-use, sementara `connectionTimeout` adalah waktu maksimum menunggu koneksi dari pool sebelum gagal; ketika pool penuh dan tidak ada koneksi idle, caller akan block sampai timeout. ([GitHub](https://github.com/brettwooldridge/hikaricp?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 7. Read/Write Splitting Pattern

### 7.1 Unit of Work split

```java id="v90h1z"
public final class DatabaseUnitOfWorkFactory {

    private final SqlSessionFactory primaryFactory;
    private final SqlSessionFactory replicaFactory;

    public DatabaseUnitOfWorkFactory(
        SqlSessionFactory primaryFactory,
        SqlSessionFactory replicaFactory
    ) {
        this.primaryFactory = primaryFactory;
        this.replicaFactory = replicaFactory;
    }

    public <T> T write(MyBatisWork<T> work) {
        return execute(primaryFactory, false, work);
    }

    public <T> T readStrong(MyBatisWork<T> work) {
        return execute(primaryFactory, true, work);
    }

    public <T> T readEventuallyConsistent(MyBatisWork<T> work) {
        return execute(replicaFactory, true, work);
    }

    private <T> T execute(
        SqlSessionFactory factory,
        boolean readOnly,
        MyBatisWork<T> work
    ) {
        try (SqlSession session = factory.openSession(false)) {
            try {
                Connection connection = session.getConnection();
                connection.setReadOnly(readOnly);

                T result = work.execute(new MyBatisSessionContext(session));

                session.commit();
                return result;
            } catch (RuntimeException | Error e) {
                session.rollback();
                throw e;
            }
        } catch (SQLException e) {
            throw new PersistenceInfrastructureException(e);
        }
    }
}
```

### 7.2 Service usage

```java id="uz6dy8"
public CaseResponse approve(ApproveCaseCommand command) {
    return unitOfWork.write(ctx -> {
        CaseRepository repository = repositoryFactory.caseRepository(ctx);

        Case aggregate = repository.findById(command.caseId());
        aggregate.approve(command.reason());
        repository.save(aggregate);

        return CaseResponse.from(aggregate);
    });
}
```

```java id="6c5ye6"
public List<CaseQueueItemDto> searchQueue(CaseQueueQuery query) {
    return unitOfWork.readEventuallyConsistent(ctx -> {
        CaseQueryRepository repository =
            repositoryFactory.caseQueryRepository(ctx);

        return repository.findQueueItems(query);
    });
}
```

**Rule:** command/write selalu ke primary. Read yang butuh data terbaru juga ke primary.

---

## 8. Strong Read vs Replica Read

| Read Type | Datasource | Contoh |
|---|---|---|
| **Read after write** | Primary | Setelah submit order, tampilkan detail terbaru |
| **Authorization-sensitive read** | Primary atau tenant-safe replica | Permission check, lock/check state |
| **Workflow decision read** | Primary | Cek status sebelum transition |
| **List/search eventually consistent** | Replica boleh | Case queue non-critical |
| **Dashboard aggregate** | Replica/reporting DB | Summary, chart |
| **Export/report besar** | Replica/reporting DB | Audit export |
| **Admin forensic read** | Primary/replica tergantung SLA | Butuh definisi freshness |

PostgreSQL hot standby memungkinkan standby server menerima koneksi dan menjalankan query read-only saat recovery/standby, tetapi sifat read replica/standby berarti aplikasi harus sadar bahwa read bisa tidak se-fresh primary tergantung replikasi dan lag. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/hot-standby.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 9. Read-After-Write Consistency

### Problem

```text id="fznjzz"
POST /orders
  -> write order ke primary
  -> response mengandung orderId

GET /orders/{id}
  -> routed ke replica
  -> replica belum catch up
  -> user melihat 404 atau status lama
```

### Policy

```text id="s3gdte"
Setelah write:
  - immediate read harus primary
  - atau response dari command harus berisi state yang diperlukan
  - atau gunakan consistency token / lag-aware routing
```

### Simple implementation

```java id="r8wf9m"
public OrderResponse createOrder(CreateOrderCommand command) {
    return unitOfWork.write(ctx -> {
        OrderRepository repository = repositoryFactory.orderRepository(ctx);

        Order order = Order.create(command);
        repository.save(order);

        return OrderResponse.from(order);
    });
}
```

Jangan create lalu immediately query replica untuk response.

---

## 10. pgJDBC Multi-Host URL dan Target Server Type

pgJDBC mendukung multi-host connection URL untuk failover sederhana, misalnya `jdbc:postgresql://host1:port1,host2:port2/database`. Driver akan mencoba host secara berurutan sampai koneksi berhasil. pgJDBC juga mendukung `targetServerType` seperti `primary`, `secondary`, `preferSecondary`, dan `preferPrimary`, serta `loadBalanceHosts=true` untuk memilih host suitable secara random. ([jdbc.postgresql.org](https://jdbc.postgresql.org/documentation/use/))

### Primary URL

```text id="cfpy9t"
jdbc:postgresql://pg1:5432,pg2:5432/appdb?targetServerType=primary
```

### Replica URL

```text id="4pgo7z"
jdbc:postgresql://pg1:5432,pg2:5432,pg3:5432/appdb?targetServerType=preferSecondary&loadBalanceHosts=true
```

### pgJDBC read-only mode

pgJDBC has `readOnly` and `readOnlyMode`; with `readOnlyMode=transaction`, when the connection is read-only and autocommit is false, the driver sends `BEGIN READ ONLY`. The default `readOnlyMode` is `transaction`. ([jdbc.postgresql.org](https://jdbc.postgresql.org/documentation/use/))

**Practical caution:** driver-level target routing is useful, tetapi jangan jadikan itu satu-satunya business rule. Service tetap harus memilih apakah operasi read boleh eventual atau harus primary.

---

## 11. Routing Policy Object

```java id="bdfzp2"
public enum ConsistencyLevel {
    STRONG,
    EVENTUAL
}
```

```java id="sk4hld"
public record DatabaseReadPolicy(
    ConsistencyLevel consistencyLevel,
    boolean allowReplicaFallbackToPrimary
) {
    public static DatabaseReadPolicy strong() {
        return new DatabaseReadPolicy(ConsistencyLevel.STRONG, false);
    }

    public static DatabaseReadPolicy eventual() {
        return new DatabaseReadPolicy(ConsistencyLevel.EVENTUAL, true);
    }
}
```

```java id="fki9zx"
public <T> T read(DatabaseReadPolicy policy, MyBatisWork<T> work) {
    if (policy.consistencyLevel() == ConsistencyLevel.STRONG) {
        return readStrong(work);
    }

    return readEventuallyConsistent(work);
}
```

### Usage

```java id="bwdhrv"
public CaseDetailDto getCaseDetail(UUID tenantId, UUID caseId, boolean justMutated) {
    DatabaseReadPolicy policy = justMutated
        ? DatabaseReadPolicy.strong()
        : DatabaseReadPolicy.eventual();

    return unitOfWork.read(policy, ctx ->
        repositoryFactory.caseQueryRepository(ctx)
            .findDetail(tenantId, caseId)
    );
}
```

---

## 12. Multi-Tenant Datasource Patterns

| Pattern | Cara Kerja | Kelebihan | Risiko |
|---|---|---|---|
| Shared table + `tenant_id` | Semua tenant di tabel sama | Sederhana, scalable | Tenant predicate wajib |
| Schema per tenant | Satu DB, banyak schema | Isolation sedang | Migration banyak schema |
| Database per tenant | Satu DB per tenant | Isolation kuat | Pool/factory explosion |
| Cluster per tenant | Dedicated infra | Isolation maksimum | Biaya/operasi tinggi |
| Hybrid | Big tenant dedicated, small tenant shared | Fleksibel | Routing kompleks |

---

## 13. Shared Table + Tenant ID

### Mapper signature

```java id="9g8r16"
CaseRecord findById(
    @Param("tenantId") UUID tenantId,
    @Param("id") UUID id
);
```

### XML

```xml id="t70ggn"
<select id="findById" resultMap="CaseRecordResultMap">
  SELECT
    id,
    tenant_id,
    case_number,
    status,
    version
  FROM cases
  WHERE tenant_id = #{tenantId}
    AND id = #{id}
</select>
```

**Rule:** untuk shared-table tenancy, `tenantId` harus menjadi parameter eksplisit di mapper method. Jangan rely pada global variable tidak terlihat.

---

## 14. Schema per Tenant

### Option A - Set `search_path` per transaction

pgJDBC supports the `currentSchema` connection property, and PostgreSQL connection options can also set session parameters. For tenant-per-schema routing, however, changing schema per transaction must be controlled carefully so a pooled connection does not leak schema context to the next request. ([jdbc.postgresql.org](https://jdbc.postgresql.org/documentation/use/))

```xml id="yh4vj1"
<update id="setSearchPath">
  SELECT set_config('search_path', #{schemaName}, true)
</update>
```

`true` membuat setting local untuk transaksi saat ini.

### Unit of Work

```java id="j5jvl4"
public <T> T tenantSchemaTransactional(
    String schemaName,
    MyBatisWork<T> work
) {
    return unitOfWork.write(ctx -> {
        RuntimeMapper runtimeMapper = ctx.mapper(RuntimeMapper.class);

        runtimeMapper.setSearchPath(schemaName);

        return work.execute(ctx);
    });
}
```

### Security warning

```java id="uw1yqw"
public final class TenantSchemaName {

    private static final Pattern SAFE_SCHEMA =
        Pattern.compile("[a-z][a-z0-9_]{0,62}");

    public static String validate(String schemaName) {
        if (schemaName == null || !SAFE_SCHEMA.matcher(schemaName).matches()) {
            throw new InvalidTenantSchemaException(schemaName);
        }

        return schemaName;
    }
}
```

**Rule:** schema name adalah SQL identifier. Jangan ambil raw request lalu set sebagai `search_path`.

---

## 15. Database per Tenant

### Tenant datasource registry

```java id="6gbuu7"
public final class TenantSqlSessionFactoryRegistry {

    private final ConcurrentMap<UUID, SqlSessionFactory> factories =
        new ConcurrentHashMap<>();

    private final TenantDatabaseConfigRepository configRepository;
    private final MyBatisFactoryBuilder factoryBuilder;

    public TenantSqlSessionFactoryRegistry(
        TenantDatabaseConfigRepository configRepository,
        MyBatisFactoryBuilder factoryBuilder
    ) {
        this.configRepository = configRepository;
        this.factoryBuilder = factoryBuilder;
    }

    public SqlSessionFactory getFactory(UUID tenantId) {
        return factories.computeIfAbsent(tenantId, this::createFactory);
    }

    private SqlSessionFactory createFactory(UUID tenantId) {
        TenantDatabaseConfig config = configRepository.findByTenantId(tenantId);

        DataSource dataSource = TenantDataSourceFactory.create(config);

        return factoryBuilder.build(
            "tenant-" + tenantId,
            dataSource,
            List.of(CaseMapper.class, OrderMapper.class, AuditMapper.class)
        );
    }
}
```

### Caution

```text id="hz7ior"
Database per tenant can create:
- many connection pools
- many idle connections
- many migrations
- many credentials
- memory pressure
- slow startup
```

### Mitigation

| Problem | Mitigation |
|---|---|
| Banyak tenant kecil | Shared DB/table model |
| Banyak pool idle | Lazy pool creation + eviction |
| Credential rotation banyak | Central secret manager |
| Migration banyak DB | Migration orchestrator |
| Observability sulit | tenant/datasource tags |
| Onboarding tenant baru | automated provisioning |
| Cross-tenant report | separate warehouse/reporting pipeline |

---

## 16. Tenant Routing Unit of Work

```java id="w1rp9g"
public final class TenantDatabaseUnitOfWorkFactory {

    private final TenantSqlSessionFactoryRegistry registry;

    public TenantDatabaseUnitOfWorkFactory(
        TenantSqlSessionFactoryRegistry registry
    ) {
        this.registry = registry;
    }

    public <T> T transactional(UUID tenantId, MyBatisWork<T> work) {
        SqlSessionFactory factory = registry.getFactory(tenantId);

        try (SqlSession session = factory.openSession(false)) {
            try {
                T result = work.execute(new MyBatisSessionContext(session));
                session.commit();
                return result;
            } catch (RuntimeException | Error e) {
                session.rollback();
                throw e;
            }
        }
    }
}
```

**Rule:** tenant ID harus berasal dari authenticated context, bukan dari path/body yang dipercaya mentah.

---

## 17. Cross-Datasource Transaction Boundary

### Local transaction only

MyBatis/JDBC transaction di satu `SqlSession` mengontrol satu connection/datasource. Jika service membuka dua datasource berbeda, commit/rollback tidak otomatis atomic bersama.

```text id="9el2i5"
DB A commit success
DB B commit fails
=> inconsistent state
```

### Bad pattern

```java id="m8r16z"
public void createOrderAndLegacyRecord(Command command) {
    primaryUnitOfWork.write(primaryCtx -> {
        legacyUnitOfWork.write(legacyCtx -> {
            primaryMapper.insertOrder(...);
            legacyMapper.insertLegacyRecord(...);
            return null;
        });
        return null;
    });
}
```

Ini terlihat nested, tetapi bukan atomic distributed transaction.

---

## 18. Cross-DB Strategy: Avoid 2PC by Design

| Problem | Better Pattern |
|---|---|
| Write primary DB + publish event | Transactional outbox |
| Write primary DB + update legacy | Outbox + async legacy sync |
| Write two bounded contexts | Saga/process manager |
| Need read from two DBs | Query composition, no joint transaction |
| Reporting | Async replication/ETL |
| Need exactly-once cross-system | Idempotency + outbox/inbox |
| Hard requirement atomic cross-DB | Evaluate XA/JTA carefully; operational complexity high |

**Practical rule:** untuk microservice/enterprise Java modern, hindari distributed transaction kecuali benar-benar diwajibkan. Gunakan **outbox, inbox, idempotency, compensating action**.

---

## 19. Outbox for Cross-Datasource Sync

### Primary transaction

```java id="5b9h8y"
public void createOrder(CreateOrderCommand command) {
    primaryUnitOfWork.write(ctx -> {
        OrderMapper orderMapper = ctx.mapper(OrderMapper.class);
        OutboxMapper outboxMapper = ctx.mapper(OutboxMapper.class);

        Order order = Order.create(command);

        orderMapper.insert(order);
        outboxMapper.insert(OutboxEvent.legacyOrderCreated(order));

        return null;
    });
}
```

### Legacy sync worker

```java id="jzme0v"
public void syncLegacyOrder(OutboxEvent event) {
    legacyUnitOfWork.write(ctx -> {
        LegacyOrderMapper mapper = ctx.mapper(LegacyOrderMapper.class);

        mapper.upsertLegacyOrder(event.toLegacyOrder());

        return null;
    });

    primaryUnitOfWork.write(ctx -> {
        OutboxMapper mapper = ctx.mapper(OutboxMapper.class);
        mapper.markPublished(event.id());
        return null;
    });
}
```

### Idempotent legacy upsert

```xml id="v2vxlf"
<insert id="upsertLegacyOrder">
  INSERT INTO legacy_order_sync (
    order_id,
    payload,
    synced_at
  )
  VALUES (
    #{orderId},
    #{payload, jdbcType=OTHER,
      typeHandler=com.company.app.persistence.JsonNodeJsonbTypeHandler},
    now()
  )
  ON CONFLICT (order_id)
  DO UPDATE SET
    payload = EXCLUDED.payload,
    synced_at = now()
</insert>
```

---

## 20. Read Replica Lag Awareness

### Lag metric query on primary

```sql id="75hj61"
SELECT
    application_name,
    client_addr,
    state,
    sync_state,
    write_lag,
    flush_lag,
    replay_lag
FROM pg_stat_replication;
```

### On replica

```sql id="4sbrgg"
SELECT
    now() - pg_last_xact_replay_timestamp() AS replica_lag;
```

### Routing policy

| Lag | Routing |
|---:|---|
| `< 1s` | Replica OK for eventual reads |
| `1-5s` | Replica OK for dashboards/search |
| `> 5s` | Fallback primary for freshness-sensitive reads |
| unknown | Conservative: primary for critical reads |
| after write | Primary |

PostgreSQL standby/read-replica is read-only while in hot standby, and managed replicas such as Azure Database for PostgreSQL read replicas use asynchronous physical replication, so lag-aware behavior is an application/operations concern. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/hot-standby.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 21. Read Replica Failure Strategy

| Failure | Recommended Behavior |
|---|---|
| Replica unreachable | Fallback primary for non-heavy reads |
| Replica lag high | Route freshness-sensitive reads to primary |
| Primary failover | Reconnect via infrastructure/driver endpoint |
| Replica returns read-only violation | Bug: write routed to replica |
| Replica query canceled due recovery conflict | Retry primary or later depending use case |
| Reporting load overloads replica | Separate reporting DB/warehouse |

### Service fallback example

```java id="y4o690"
public <T> T eventualReadWithFallback(MyBatisWork<T> work) {
    try {
        return readEventuallyConsistent(work);
    } catch (PersistenceInfrastructureException e) {
        log.warn("Replica read failed, falling back to primary", e);
        return readStrong(work);
    }
}
```

**Caution:** fallback primary untuk semua heavy report bisa overload primary. Gunakan policy per endpoint.

---

## 22. Cloud Managed PostgreSQL Concerns

### Azure read replicas

Azure Database for PostgreSQL Flexible Server read replicas are read-only replicas updated asynchronously using PostgreSQL physical replication, and Azure documents up to five replicas from a primary flexible server. ([Microsoft Learn](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/azure/postgresql/read-replica/concepts-read-replicas?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Common concerns

| Concern | AWS/Azure/On-Prem Pattern |
|---|---|
| Endpoint changes during failover | Use managed endpoint / DNS / pgJDBC multi-host |
| TLS/cert management | `sslmode=verify-full` where possible |
| Secret rotation | External secret manager + rolling deploy |
| Private networking | VPC/VNet/private endpoint |
| Connection limits | Pool sizing per instance |
| Read replica lag | Lag metric + routing policy |
| Maintenance window | Retry/backoff + graceful shutdown |
| Backup/restore | RPO/RTO tested |
| Parameter groups/config | Standardized per env |
| Observability | DB metrics + app datasource tags |

---

## 23. On-Prem Deployment Concerns

| Area | Concern | Pattern |
|---|---|---|
| HA | VIP/proxy/failover manager | Stable endpoint |
| DNS | Slow propagation | Low TTL / driver multi-host |
| Network | Firewall, NAT, MTU | Health checks and timeouts |
| TLS | Internal CA | Cert rollout process |
| Secrets | Vault/HSM/manual rotation | Runtime injection |
| Patching | Planned DB restart | Graceful app retry |
| Replication | Streaming replication | Lag monitoring |
| Backup | PITR restore test | Disaster recovery drill |
| Observability | DBA tools + app metrics | Shared runbook |

---

### Environment variables

```text id="2s81kh"
DB_PRIMARY_URL
DB_PRIMARY_USERNAME
```

```java id="f50wza"
public record DatabaseEndpointConfig(
    String jdbcUrl,
    String username,
    String password,
    int maximumPoolSize,
    long connectionTimeoutMs,
    boolean readOnly
) {}
```

### Loader

```java id="2ltp4v"
public final class DatabaseEndpointConfigLoader {

    public DatabaseEndpointConfig load(String prefix, boolean readOnly) {
        return new DatabaseEndpointConfig(
            requiredEnv(prefix + "_URL"),
            requiredEnv(prefix + "_USERNAME"),
            requiredEnv(prefix + "_PASSWORD"),
            intEnv(prefix + "_POOL_MAX", readOnly ? 30 : 20),

            readOnly
        );

}
```

## 25. Migration Strategy for Multi-DB

| Topology | Migration Strategy |
|---|---|
| Primary + replica | Migrate primary; replica follows replication |
| Schema per tenant | Run migration per schema, track status |
| DB per tenant | Run migration per tenant DB |
| Legacy DB | Separate migration ownership |
| Reporting DB | Separate schema pipeline |
| Blue/green DB | Expand-contract + compatibility |
| Cloud + on-prem | Ordered rollout + data sync validation |

### Migration status table

```sql id="fscp60"
CREATE TABLE tenant_schema_migration_status (
    tenant_id uuid PRIMARY KEY,
    schema_name text NOT NULL,
    target_version text NOT NULL,
    current_version text NOT NULL,
    status text NOT NULL,
    started_at timestamptz,
    completed_at timestamptz,
    error_message text
);
```

### Practical rule

```text id="aqu7iy"
Do not deploy application code that assumes all tenant DBs are migrated
unless migration completion is verified.
```

---

## 26. Mapper Organization for Multiple DBs

```text id="3q7rt4"
persistence/
  primary/
    mapper/
      OrderMapper.java
      CaseMapper.java
  replica/
    mapper/
      CaseQueryMapper.java
      DashboardMapper.java
  legacy/
    mapper/
      LegacyOrderMapper.java
      LegacyCustomerMapper.java
```

### Avoid same mapper for primary and replica if intent differs

Bad:

```java id="y9xz94"
CaseMapper mapper = ctx.mapper(CaseMapper.class); // unclear primary/replica?
```

Better:

```java id="3lb66u"
CaseCommandMapper commandMapper = ctx.mapper(CaseCommandMapper.class);
CaseReadModelMapper readMapper = ctx.mapper(CaseReadModelMapper.class);
```

**Rule:** mapper name should reveal intent: command/write, query/read, legacy, admin, backfill.

---

## 27. Read-Only Guard

### App-level

```java id="gg295f"
public <T> T readOnly(SqlSessionFactory factory, MyBatisWork<T> work) {
    try (SqlSession session = factory.openSession(false)) {
        try {
            Connection connection = session.getConnection();
            connection.setReadOnly(true);

            T result = work.execute(new MyBatisSessionContext(session));

            session.commit();
            return result;
        } catch (RuntimeException | Error e) {
            session.rollback();
            throw e;
        }
    } catch (SQLException e) {
        throw new PersistenceInfrastructureException(e);
    }
}
```

### DB-level role

```sql id="134mmr"
GRANT SELECT ON ALL TABLES IN SCHEMA public TO app_readonly;
REVOKE INSERT, UPDATE, DELETE ON ALL TABLES IN SCHEMA public FROM app_readonly;
```

### Endpoint-level

```java id="b58cd5"
public List<DashboardDto> dashboard() {
    return unitOfWork.readEventuallyConsistent(ctx -> {
        DashboardMapper mapper = ctx.mapper(DashboardMapper.class);
        return mapper.loadDashboard();
    });
}
```

**Defense-in-depth:** read-only connection + read-only DB user + mapper separation.

---

## 28. Transaction Isolation Across Topologies

PostgreSQL default isolation level is Read Committed; at this level, a `SELECT` sees a snapshot as of the start of that query, and two `SELECT`s in the same transaction can see different data if another transaction commits between them. Stronger isolation levels have different behavior and can require retry on serialization failures. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/transaction-iso.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Rules

| Use Case | Datasource | Isolation |
|---|---|---|
| Simple command | Primary | Read Committed + optimistic locking |
| Workflow transition | Primary | Read Committed + affected row check |
| Multi-row invariant | Primary | Serializable or explicit lock + retry |
| Reporting read | Replica | Read only, maybe Repeatable Read |
| Export | Replica/reporting | Read only + cursor/fetchSize |
| Cross-DB operation | Avoid single transaction assumption | Saga/outbox |

---

## 29. Observability per Datasource

### Required metric tags

```text id="lf56ng"
datasource=primary|replica|legacy|tenant
db.role=read_write|read_only
tenant.routing=shared|schema|database
operation=OrderMapper.insert
```

### Metrics

| Metric | Why |
|---|---|
| connection pool active/idle/pending per datasource | Identify pool pressure |
| connection acquire duration per datasource | Detect pool starvation |
| mapper duration per datasource | Find slow DB role |
| read replica fallback count | Replica instability |
| replica lag | Freshness risk |
| read-after-write primary count | Traffic pattern |
| cross-db sync backlog | Integration delay |
| migration status per tenant | Deployment safety |
| DB error by datasource | Root cause isolation |

### Log example

```java id="2e79lz"
log.info(
    "Database operation completed datasource={} role={} mapper={} durationMs={}",
    "replica",
    "read_only",
    "CaseQueryMapper.searchQueue",
    durationMs
);
```

## 30. Testing Strategy

### 30.1 Primary vs replica routing unit test

```java id="rlycin"
@Test
void commandShouldUsePrimary() {
    CaseApplicationService service = new CaseApplicationService(
        fakeUnitOfWork,
        repositoryFactory
    );

    service.approve(command);

    assertThat(fakeUnitOfWork.lastUsedRole())
        .isEqualTo(DatabaseRole.PRIMARY);
}
```

### 30.2 Integration test with two PostgreSQL containers

```java id="c2oo3c"
@Testcontainers
class MultiDatabaseRoutingTest {

    @Container
    static PostgreSQLContainer<?> primary =
        new PostgreSQLContainer<>("postgres:16");

    @Container
    static PostgreSQLContainer<?> replica =
        new PostgreSQLContainer<>("postgres:16");

    @Test
    void writeShouldGoToPrimaryAndReadReplicaShouldNotSeeItWithoutReplication() {
        SqlSessionFactory primaryFactory = buildFactory(primary);
        SqlSessionFactory replicaFactory = buildFactory(replica);

        DatabaseUnitOfWorkFactory unitOfWork =
            new DatabaseUnitOfWorkFactory(primaryFactory, replicaFactory);

        UUID id = UUID.randomUUID();

        unitOfWork.write(ctx -> {
            ctx.mapper(CaseMapper.class).insert(CaseRecord.fixture(id));
            return null;
        });

        CaseRecord fromPrimary = unitOfWork.readStrong(ctx ->
            ctx.mapper(CaseMapper.class).findById(id)
        );

        CaseRecord fromReplica = unitOfWork.readEventuallyConsistent(ctx ->
            ctx.mapper(CaseMapper.class).findById(id)
        );

        assertThat(fromPrimary).isNotNull();
        assertThat(fromReplica).isNull();
    }
}
```

### 30.3 Tenant routing test

```java id="v0pizk"
@Test
void tenantShouldUseItsOwnDatabase() {
    UUID tenantA = UUID.randomUUID();
    UUID tenantB = UUID.randomUUID();

    tenantUnitOfWork.transactional(tenantA, ctx -> {
        ctx.mapper(CaseMapper.class).insert(CaseRecord.fixture("CASE-A"));
        return null;
    });

    tenantUnitOfWork.transactional(tenantB, ctx -> {
        List<CaseRecord> cases = ctx.mapper(CaseMapper.class).findAll();
        assertThat(cases).isEmpty();
        return null;
    });
}
```

---

## 31. Failover Testing

| Scenario | Test |
|---|---|
| Replica down | Read fallback to primary |
| Primary down | App returns 503 or reconnects after failover |
| Wrong route write to replica | Read-only violation classified |
| Replica lag high | Policy routes critical read to primary |
| Tenant config missing | 404/500 with safe error, no default tenant |
| Secret rotation | New connections use new credential |
| Pool exhaustion on replica | Primary not affected |
| Legacy DB down | Main command either unaffected or outbox retry |

**Rule:** failover behavior must be explicit. “Driver will handle it” is not a sufficient application-level design.

---

## 32. Common Anti-Pattern

| Anti-Pattern | Impact |
|---|---|
| One global `SqlSessionFactory` for all DBs | Routing hidden/impossible |
| Write routed by method name convention only | Bug-prone |
| Read-after-write goes to replica | Stale data / false 404 |
| Transaction spans two DBs but treated as atomic | Half-commit |
| Tenant ID from request trusted directly | Tenant spoofing |
| Schema name from request in SQL | SQL injection / search_path leak |
| Pool per tenant with no eviction | Connection explosion |
| Replica fallback primary for huge reports | Primary overload |
| Runtime app uses migration DB role | Blast radius huge |
| Same mapper for primary write and replica read | Intent unclear |
| No datasource tag in metrics | Cannot diagnose bottleneck |
| Migration assumes all tenant DBs updated | Partial outage |
| Secret shared across all tenants/services | Rotation/audit hard |
| On-prem DNS failover not tested | Long outage during failover |

---

## 33. Production Checklist

| Checklist | Status Ideal |
|---|---|
| Separate `SqlSessionFactory` per datasource role | Ya |
| Write operations always use primary | Ya |
| Strong reads after write use primary | Ya |
| Replica reads are explicitly eventual | Ya |
| Read-only datasource uses read-only DB credential | Ya |
| pgJDBC multi-host/failover URL reviewed | Ya |
| Replica lag monitored | Ya |
| Fallback policy per endpoint defined | Ya |
| Tenant routing source is authenticated context | Ya |
| Tenant schema names are validated/whitelisted | Ya |
| Database-per-tenant pool count controlled | Ya |
| Cross-datasource transaction avoided or modeled as saga/outbox | Ya |
| Migration pipeline handles all DB/schema targets | Ya |
| Mapper packages separated by DB role/intent | Ya |
| Metrics/logs include datasource role | Ya |
| Failover and secret rotation tested | Ya |
| On-prem/cloud network timeouts configured | Ya |
| TLS/SSL policy configured per environment | Ya |

---

## 34. Decision Table

| Problem | Recommended Pattern |
|---|---|
| Need read scaling | Primary + replica factories |
| Need fresh read after command | Primary read |
| Dashboard/reporting heavy read | Replica/reporting datasource |
| Tenant isolation moderate | Shared table + tenant_id + RLS optional |
| Tenant isolation stronger | Schema per tenant |
| Tenant isolation strongest | DB per tenant |
| Many small tenants | Avoid DB per tenant pool explosion |
| Legacy database write | Outbox + async sync |
| Cross-DB consistency | Saga/outbox/idempotency |
| DB failover | Managed endpoint or pgJDBC multi-host |
| Read replica routing | Explicit read policy + targetServerType |
| Schema per tenant | Transaction-local `search_path` + validation |
| On-prem HA | VIP/proxy/multi-host + tested failover |
| Cloud secrets | External secret/K8s Secret + rotation |
| Migration per tenant | Migration orchestrator/status table |

---

## 35. Roadmap Belajar Singkat

| Urutan | Materi | Target |
|---:|---|---|
| 1 | Multiple `SqlSessionFactory` | Routing datasource eksplisit |
| 2 | Primary vs replica policy | Strong vs eventual read jelas |
| 3 | pgJDBC multi-host/failover | Paham target primary/secondary |
| 4 | Read replica lag | Bisa desain fallback |
| 5 | Multi-tenant patterns | Shared table vs schema vs database |
| 6 | Cross-datasource transaction | Hindari asumsi atomic palsu |
| 7 | Outbox/saga for DB integration | Sync aman antar DB |
| 8 | Pool sizing per datasource | Tidak overload DB |
| 9 | Migration per topology | Schema konsisten |
| 10 | Observability/failover test | Production topology terbukti |

---

## Response:
7/13/2026, 3:07:07 AM

# Cheatsheet - Java MyBatis Deployment & Runtime Operations: Docker/Kubernetes Config, Health Checks, Graceful Shutdown, Connection Pool Lifecycle, Migration Runner, dan Production Runbook

## 1. Introduction

Untuk aplikasi **Jersey/JAX-RS + MyBatis + PostgreSQL**, runtime operation bukan hanya “aplikasi bisa start”. Yang harus dijaga:

```text id="b4q1pi"
container image
  -> runtime config
  -> datasource/pool lifecycle
  -> database migration
  -> health/readiness/liveness
  -> graceful shutdown
  -> Kubernetes rollout
  -> observability
  -> failure runbook
```

Di Kubernetes, health probe punya peran berbeda: **startup probe** memberi waktu aplikasi start sebelum liveness/readiness aktif; **readiness probe** menentukan apakah Pod siap menerima traffic; **liveness probe** menentukan apakah container harus direstart. Kubernetes mendokumentasikan bahwa jika startup probe dikonfigurasi, liveness dan readiness probe tidak mulai sampai startup probe sukses. ([Kubernetes](https://kubernetes.io/docs/concepts/workloads/pods/probes/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 2. Mental Model Runtime

```mermaid id="19sk65"
flowchart TD
    A[Container Starts] --> B[Load Config / Env / Secrets]
    B --> C[Initialize DataSource / HikariCP]
    C --> D[Build SqlSessionFactory]
    D --> E{Migration Strategy}

    E -->|Init Job / CI/CD| F[Run Flyway/Liquibase before app ready]
    E -->|App-managed| G[Run migration at startup carefully]

    F --> H[Start HTTP Server]
    G --> H

    H --> I[Startup Probe]
    I --> J[Readiness Probe]
    J --> K[Receive Traffic]

    K --> L[Handle Requests]
    L --> M[MyBatis Unit of Work]
    M --> N[(PostgreSQL)]

    O[Shutdown Signal SIGTERM] --> P[Mark Not Ready]
    P --> Q[Stop Accepting New Requests]
    Q --> R[Drain In-flight Requests]
    R --> S[Close HikariDataSource]
    S --> T[Exit]
```

**Core invariant:**

```text id="3ww31r"
Aplikasi boleh hidup hanya jika:
- konfigurasi valid
- DB connection bisa dibuat sesuai readiness policy
- migration/schema compatible
- SqlSessionFactory siap
- health endpoint benar membedakan live vs ready
- shutdown menutup traffic sebelum pool ditutup
```

---

## 3. Decomposition Map

| Area | Fokus Praktis | Pattern Utama | Risiko Jika Salah |
|---|---|---|---|
| **Container Image** | Build repeatable dan kecil | multi-stage build, non-root user | image besar, insecure |
| **Runtime Config** | Env/secrets/profile | fail-fast config validation | config salah baru ketahuan runtime |
| **DataSource Lifecycle** | HikariCP init/close | singleton app scope | connection leak |
| **SqlSessionFactory Lifecycle** | Build sekali saat startup | singleton immutable | factory dibuat per request |
| **Migration Runner** | Schema update aman | CI/CD job/init job, not every pod | migration race |
| **Startup Probe** | Waktu boot/migration | tolerant startup check | pod dibunuh saat masih start |
| **Readiness Probe** | Siap menerima traffic | DB/schema/pool ready | traffic masuk saat belum siap |
| **Liveness Probe** | Restart saat stuck | shallow self check | restart loop karena DB down |
| **Graceful Shutdown** | Drain request | readiness false, stop server, close pool | request putus/partial write |
| **Connection Pool Runtime** | Pool sizing dan timeout | maxPool, connectionTimeout, maxLifetime | pool exhaustion |
| **Kubernetes Rollout** | Safe rolling deploy | maxUnavailable, maxSurge, PDB | downtime saat deploy |
| **Resource Limits** | CPU/memory benar | request/limit, JVM container awareness | OOMKilled/CPU throttling |
| **Secrets** | DB password aman | K8s Secret/external secret | secret leak |
| **Observability** | Logs/metrics/traces | datasource/pool/mapper metric | blind operation |
| **Runbook** | Incident response | DB down, migration fail, pool exhausted | panic debugging |

---

## 4. Container Image Pattern

### 4.1 Multi-stage Dockerfile

```dockerfile id="i8yn3p"
# Build stage
FROM maven:3.9-eclipse-temurin-21 AS build
WORKDIR /workspace

COPY pom.xml .
COPY src ./src

RUN mvn -B -DskipTests package

# Runtime stage
FROM eclipse-temurin:21-jre
WORKDIR /app

RUN useradd --create-home --uid 10001 appuser

COPY --from=build /workspace/target/app.jar /app/app.jar

USER appuser

EXPOSE 8080

ENV JAVA_OPTS=""

ENTRYPOINT ["sh", "-c", "java $JAVA_OPTS -jar /app/app.jar"]
```

### 4.2 Healthcheck di Dockerfile

Dockerfile `HEALTHCHECK` memberi Docker cara mengecek apakah container masih bekerja; instruksi ini bisa menjalankan command di dalam container atau men-disable inherited healthcheck. ([Docker Documentation](https://docs.docker.com/reference/dockerfile/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

```dockerfile id="d79s3q"
HEALTHCHECK --interval=30s --timeout=3s --start-period=30s --retries=3 \
  CMD wget -qO- http://127.0.0.1:8080/health/live || exit 1
```

**Catatan Kubernetes:** di Kubernetes, probe biasanya didefinisikan di manifest Deployment, bukan mengandalkan Dockerfile `HEALTHCHECK`.

---

## 5. Runtime Config

### 5.1 Required env

```text id="nmfji1"
APP_ENV
HTTP_PORT

DB_URL
DB_USERNAME
DB_PASSWORD
DB_POOL_MAX_SIZE
DB_POOL_CONNECTION_TIMEOUT_MS
DB_POOL_MAX_LIFETIME_MS

MIGRATION_ENABLED
MIGRATION_MODE

OTEL_SERVICE_NAME
OTEL_EXPORTER_OTLP_ENDPOINT
```

### 5.2 Config loader fail-fast

```java id="879d8d"
public final class RuntimeConfig {

    private final String dbUrl;
    private final String dbUsername;
    private final String dbPassword;
    private final int dbPoolMaxSize;
    private final long dbConnectionTimeoutMs;

    private RuntimeConfig(
        String dbUrl,
        String dbUsername,
        String dbPassword,
        int dbPoolMaxSize,
        long dbConnectionTimeoutMs
    ) {
        this.dbUrl = dbUrl;
        this.dbUsername = dbUsername;
        this.dbPassword = dbPassword;
        this.dbPoolMaxSize = dbPoolMaxSize;
        this.dbConnectionTimeoutMs = dbConnectionTimeoutMs;
    }

    public static RuntimeConfig load() {
        return new RuntimeConfig(
            requiredEnv("DB_URL"),
            requiredEnv("DB_USERNAME"),
            requiredEnv("DB_PASSWORD"),
            intEnv("DB_POOL_MAX_SIZE", 20),
            longEnv("DB_POOL_CONNECTION_TIMEOUT_MS", 3000)
        );
    }

    private static String requiredEnv(String name) {
        String value = System.getenv(name);

        if (value == null || value.isBlank()) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("Missing required env: " + name);
        }

        return value;
    }

    private static int intEnv(String name, int defaultValue) {
        String value = System.getenv(name);
        return value == null ? defaultValue : Integer.parseInt(value);
    }

    private static long longEnv(String name, long defaultValue) {
        String value = System.getenv(name);
        return value == null ? defaultValue : Long.parseLong(value);
    }

    public String dbUrl() {
        return dbUrl;
    }

    public String dbUsername() {
        return dbUsername;
    }

    public String dbPassword() {
        return dbPassword;
    }

    public int dbPoolMaxSize() {
        return dbPoolMaxSize;
    }

    public long dbConnectionTimeoutMs() {
        return dbConnectionTimeoutMs;
    }
}
```

**Rule:** missing DB credential harus membuat app gagal start, bukan diam-diam memakai default lokal.

---

## 6. DataSource Lifecycle

### 6.1 HikariCP singleton

```java id="843qub"
public final class DataSourceProvider implements AutoCloseable {

    private final HikariDataSource dataSource;

    public DataSourceProvider(RuntimeConfig config) {
        HikariConfig hikariConfig = new HikariConfig();

        hikariConfig.setJdbcUrl(config.dbUrl());
        hikariConfig.setUsername(config.dbUsername());
        hikariConfig.setPassword(config.dbPassword());
        hikariConfig.setMaximumPoolSize(config.dbPoolMaxSize());
        hikariConfig.setConnectionTimeout(config.dbConnectionTimeoutMs());
        hikariConfig.setPoolName("app-postgres-pool");

        hikariConfig.addDataSourceProperty("tcpKeepAlive", "true");

        this.dataSource = new HikariDataSource(hikariConfig);
    }

    public DataSource dataSource() {
        return dataSource;
    }

    @Override
    public void close() {
        dataSource.close();
    }
}
```

HikariCP mendokumentasikan `maximumPoolSize` sebagai jumlah maksimum koneksi termasuk idle dan in-use; ketika pool penuh dan tidak ada idle connection, pemanggil akan menunggu sampai `connectionTimeout`. HikariCP juga menekankan konfigurasi TCP keepalive untuk menghindari kondisi koneksi mati yang tidak cepat terdeteksi di beberapa environment jaringan. ([GitHub](https://github.com/brettwooldridge/hikaricp?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 7. SqlSessionFactory Lifecycle

### 7.1 Build once at startup

```java id="oybimz"
public final class MyBatisProvider {

    private final SqlSessionFactory sqlSessionFactory;

    public MyBatisProvider(DataSource dataSource) {
        TransactionFactory transactionFactory =
            new JdbcTransactionFactory();

        Environment environment = new Environment(
            "production",
            transactionFactory,
            dataSource
        );

        Configuration configuration = new Configuration(environment);
        configuration.setMapUnderscoreToCamelCase(true);

        configuration.addMapper(CaseMapper.class);
        configuration.addMapper(CaseQueryMapper.class);
        configuration.addMapper(AuditMapper.class);
        configuration.addMapper(OutboxMapper.class);

        this.sqlSessionFactory =
            new SqlSessionFactoryBuilder().build(configuration);
    }

    public SqlSessionFactory sqlSessionFactory() {
        return sqlSessionFactory;
    }
}
```

### 7.2 Scope rule

| Object | Scope |
|---|---|
| `HikariDataSource` | Application singleton |
| `SqlSessionFactory` | Application singleton |
| `SqlSession` | Request/use-case/method scope |
| Mapper proxy | Same as `SqlSession` |
| Repository with session context | Unit-of-work scope |
| Application service | Singleton/stateless |
| Jersey resource | Depends DI, preferably stateless |

**Anti-pattern:** membuat `SqlSessionFactory` atau `HikariDataSource` per request.

---

## 8. Migration Runner Strategy

Flyway command-line adalah standalone distribution untuk menjalankan migration tanpa perlu build tool; `flyway migrate` memigrasikan schema ke versi terbaru dan membuat schema history table jika belum ada. ([Redgate Documentation](https://documentation.red-gate.com/fd/command-line-277579359.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### 8.1 Pilihan deployment migration

| Pattern | Cocok Untuk | Risiko |
|---|---|---|
| CI/CD migration step | Enterprise controlled release | Butuh pipeline discipline |
| Kubernetes Job before rollout | Cloud-native deployment | Job ordering harus jelas |
| Init container | Per-pod migration | Bisa race jika banyak pod |
| App startup migration | Simple service kecil | Race antar replica, startup lambat |
| Manual DBA migration | Regulated/on-prem | Human error, drift |

### 8.2 Recommended

```text id="zees78"
Production:
  Prefer migration as separate CI/CD step or Kubernetes Job.

Avoid:
  every application pod running migration concurrently.
```

---

## 9. Kubernetes Migration Job

```yaml id="nj8nhq"
apiVersion: batch/v1
kind: Job
metadata:
  name: case-service-db-migrate
spec:
  backoffLimit: 1
  template:
    spec:
      restartPolicy: Never
      containers:
        - name: flyway
          image: registry.example.com/case-service-migrations:1.0.0
          env:
            - name: FLYWAY_URL
              valueFrom:
                secretKeyRef:
                  name: case-service-db
                  key: DB_URL
            - name: FLYWAY_USER
              valueFrom:
                secretKeyRef:
                  name: case-service-db
                  key: DB_MIGRATION_USERNAME
            - name: FLYWAY_PASSWORD
              valueFrom:
                secretKeyRef:
                  name: case-service-db
                  key: DB_MIGRATION_PASSWORD
          command:
            - flyway
          args:
            - migrate
```

### Rule

```text id="4tihcp"
Migration credential != runtime credential.

Migration user:
  can DDL

Runtime app user:
  cannot DDL
```

Flyway juga menyediakan command `validate` untuk mengecek perbedaan antara migration yang tersedia dan yang sudah diterapkan pada target. ([Redgate Documentation](https://documentation.red-gate.com/flyway/reference/commands?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 10. App Startup Sequence

```java id="h52hwk"
public final class ApplicationMain {

    public static void main(String[] args) {
        RuntimeConfig config = RuntimeConfig.load();

        DataSourceProvider dataSourceProvider =
            new DataSourceProvider(config);

        MyBatisProvider myBatisProvider =
            new MyBatisProvider(dataSourceProvider.dataSource());

        ApplicationRuntime runtime = new ApplicationRuntime(
            dataSourceProvider,
            myBatisProvider
        );

        Runtime.getRuntime().addShutdownHook(new Thread(runtime::shutdown));

        runtime.startHttpServer();
    }
}
```

```java id="o9lrpe"
public final class ApplicationRuntime {

    private final AtomicBoolean ready = new AtomicBoolean(false);
    private final DataSourceProvider dataSourceProvider;
    private final MyBatisProvider myBatisProvider;
    private HttpServer server;

    public void startHttpServer() {
        validateDatabaseReadiness();

        server = startJerseyServer();

        ready.set(true);
    }

    public boolean isReady() {
        return ready.get();
    }

    public void shutdown() {
        ready.set(false);

        stopAcceptingHttpTraffic();
        waitForInFlightRequests();

        dataSourceProvider.close();
    }

    private void validateDatabaseReadiness() {
        try (SqlSession session =
                 myBatisProvider.sqlSessionFactory().openSession(true)) {

            RuntimeMapper mapper = session.getMapper(RuntimeMapper.class);
            mapper.selectOne();

        }
    }
}
```

---

## 11. Health Endpoint Design

### 11.1 Endpoint split

```text id="3m09h1"
/health/live
  shallow check: process alive, event loop/server alive

/health/ready
  readiness check: app initialized, pool usable, schema compatible

/health/startup
  optional startup check if server exposes it
```

### 11.2 JAX-RS resource

```java id="qvybei"
@Path("/health")
public class HealthResource {

    private final HealthService healthService;

    @Inject
    public HealthResource(HealthService healthService) {
        this.healthService = healthService;
    }

    @GET
    @Path("/live")
    public Response live() {
        return Response.ok(Map.of("status", "UP")).build();
    }

    @GET
    @Path("/ready")
    public Response ready() {
        HealthStatus status = healthService.readiness();

        if (status.up()) {
            return Response.ok(status).build();
        }

        return Response.status(Response.Status.SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE)
            .entity(status)
            .build();
    }
}
```

### 11.3 Readiness service

```java id="dyk755"
public final class HealthService {

    private final SqlSessionFactory sqlSessionFactory;
    private final AtomicBoolean ready;

    public HealthStatus readiness() {
        if (!ready.get()) {
            return HealthStatus.down("APPLICATION_NOT_READY");
        }

        try (SqlSession session = sqlSessionFactory.openSession(true)) {
            RuntimeMapper mapper = session.getMapper(RuntimeMapper.class);

            Integer result = mapper.selectOne();

            if (result == 1) {
                return HealthStatus.up();
            }

            return HealthStatus.down("DB_UNEXPECTED_RESULT");
        } catch (RuntimeException e) {
            return HealthStatus.down("DB_NOT_READY");
        }
    }
}
```

### 11.4 Runtime mapper

```java id="f3zdjf"
public interface RuntimeMapper {
    Integer selectOne();

    String currentSchemaVersion();
}
```

```xml id="83npy1"
<select id="selectOne" resultType="int">
  SELECT 1
</select>
```

---

## 12. Kubernetes Probes

Kubernetes dokumentasi membedakan **liveness**, **readiness**, dan **startup probes** serta menyediakan konfigurasi probe HTTP/TCP/exec/gRPC. ([Kubernetes](https://kubernetes.io/docs/tasks/configure-pod-container/configure-liveness-readiness-startup-probes/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

```yaml id="6y1by5"
apiVersion: apps/v1
kind: Deployment
metadata:
  name: case-service
spec:
  replicas: 3
  selector:
    matchLabels:
      app: case-service
  template:
    metadata:
      labels:
        app: case-service
    spec:
      terminationGracePeriodSeconds: 45
      containers:
        - name: case-service
          image: registry.example.com/case-service:1.0.0
          ports:
            - containerPort: 8080

          startupProbe:
            httpGet:
              path: /health/live
              port: 8080
            failureThreshold: 30
            periodSeconds: 2

          readinessProbe:
            httpGet:
              path: /health/ready
              port: 8080
            initialDelaySeconds: 5
            periodSeconds: 5
            timeoutSeconds: 2
            failureThreshold: 2

          livenessProbe:
            httpGet:
              path: /health/live
              port: 8080
            initialDelaySeconds: 10
            periodSeconds: 10
            timeoutSeconds: 2
            failureThreshold: 3
```

### Probe rule

| Probe | Jangan |
|---|---|
| Startup | Jangan terlalu pendek untuk cold start/migration |
| Readiness | Jangan return UP sebelum app siap menerima traffic |
| Liveness | Jangan bergantung pada DB secara ketat |
| All probes | Jangan melakukan query mahal |

**Why liveness should be shallow:** kalau DB down sementara dan liveness ikut down, Kubernetes bisa me-restart semua pod padahal masalahnya external dependency. Readiness boleh down agar traffic berhenti sementara.

---

## 13. Graceful Shutdown

Kubernetes Pod lifecycle memberi waktu terminasi melalui `terminationGracePeriodSeconds`; saat Pod dihapus, kubelet menjalankan hook seperti `preStop` jika ada, lalu mengirim signal ke container dan menunggu grace period sebelum kill paksa. ([Kubernetes](https://kubernetes.io/docs/concepts/workloads/pods/pod-lifecycle/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### 13.1 Shutdown sequence ideal

```text id="occn5c"
1. Receive SIGTERM
2. Mark readiness = false
3. Wait short drain delay
4. Stop accepting new HTTP requests
5. Let in-flight requests finish
6. Stop background workers
7. Close SqlSession/HikariDataSource
8. Exit before terminationGracePeriodSeconds
```

### 13.2 Java shutdown hook

```java id="nucmjd"
Runtime.getRuntime().addShutdownHook(new Thread(() -> {
    log.info("Shutdown started");

    applicationRuntime.markNotReady();

    applicationRuntime.stopHttpServerGracefully(Duration.ofSeconds(30));

    applicationRuntime.stopBackgroundWorkers(Duration.ofSeconds(20));

    applicationRuntime.closeDataSources();

    log.info("Shutdown completed");
}));
```

### 13.3 Kubernetes preStop

```yaml id="pnvb7t"
lifecycle:
  preStop:
    exec:
      command:
        - sh
        - -c
        - "wget -qO- http://127.0.0.1:8080/internal/prestop || true; sleep 10"
```

```java id="n2t91w"
@POST
@Path("/internal/prestop")
public Response prestop() {
    runtime.markNotReady();
    return Response.ok().build();
}
```

**Catatan:** `preStop` membantu memberi waktu endpoint readiness berubah sebelum container menerima SIGTERM/traffic benar-benar berhenti. Tetapi jangan bergantung hanya pada sleep; aplikasi tetap harus handle SIGTERM dengan benar.

---

## 14. HTTP Server Graceful Stop

Contoh konseptual tergantung server yang dipakai: Grizzly, Jetty, Undertow, Tomcat embedded, atau app server.

```java id="gql2ui"
public final class HttpServerLifecycle {

    private final Server server;
    private final InFlightRequestTracker requestTracker;

    public void stopGracefully(Duration timeout) {
        server.stopAcceptingRequests();

        boolean drained = requestTracker.awaitZero(timeout);

        if (!drained) {
            log.warn("Graceful shutdown timeout; in-flight requests remain");
        }

        server.shutdown();
    }
}
```

### In-flight tracker filter

```java id="9203aw"
@Provider
public class InFlightRequestFilter
    implements ContainerRequestFilter, ContainerResponseFilter {

    private final InFlightRequestTracker tracker;

    @Override
    public void filter(ContainerRequestContext requestContext) {
        tracker.increment();
    }

    @Override
    public void filter(
        ContainerRequestContext requestContext,
        ContainerResponseContext responseContext
    ) {
        tracker.decrement();
    }
}
```

```java id="qpr0og"
public final class InFlightRequestTracker {

    private final AtomicInteger inFlight = new AtomicInteger();

    public void increment() {
        inFlight.incrementAndGet();
    }

    public void decrement() {
        inFlight.decrementAndGet();
    }

    public boolean awaitZero(Duration timeout) {
        long deadline = System.nanoTime() + timeout.toNanos();

        while (System.nanoTime() < deadline) {
            if (inFlight.get() == 0) {
                return true;
            }

            LockSupport.parkNanos(TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS.toNanos(100));
        }

        return inFlight.get() == 0;
    }

    public int current() {
        return inFlight.get();
    }
}
```

---

## 15. Background Worker Lifecycle

Jika aplikasi punya outbox publisher, scheduler, queue processor, atau backfill worker, shutdown harus menghentikan worker sebelum pool ditutup.

```java id="r4timg"
public final class OutboxWorker implements AutoCloseable {

    private final AtomicBoolean running = new AtomicBoolean(false);
    private final ExecutorService executor = Executors.newSingleThreadExecutor();

    public void start() {
        running.set(true);
        executor.submit(this::runLoop);
    }

    private void runLoop() {
        while (running.get()) {
            try {
                processOneBatch();
            } catch (Exception e) {
                log.warn("Outbox worker batch failed", e);
            }
        }
    }

    @Override
    public void close() {
        running.set(false);
        executor.shutdown();

        try {
            if (!executor.awaitTermination(20, TimeUnit.SECONDS)) {
                executor.shutdownNow();
            }
        } catch (InterruptedException e) {
            Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
            executor.shutdownNow();
        }
    }
}
```

**Rule:** worker stop dulu, lalu datasource close. Kalau datasource close dulu, worker bisa gagal dengan error noise.

---

## 16. Connection Pool Runtime Tuning

| Setting | Meaning | Initial Guideline |
|---|---|---|
| `maximumPoolSize` | max connection per app instance | mulai kecil, ukur |
| `minimumIdle` | idle connection minimum | sering sama dengan max untuk fixed pool, atau lebih kecil untuk bursty |
| `connectionTimeout` | waktu tunggu connection | lebih pendek dari HTTP timeout |
| `maxLifetime` | umur max connection | lebih pendek dari DB/LB idle timeout |
| `idleTimeout` | idle eviction | hanya jika minIdle < max |
| `keepaliveTime` | keepalive idle connection | berguna di network/LB agresif |
| JDBC `tcpKeepAlive` | OS/JDBC keepalive | recommended di env tertentu |
| pool name | observability | wajib distinct |

### Example

```java id="m0p8ah"
hikariConfig.setMaximumPoolSize(20);
hikariConfig.setMinimumIdle(20);
hikariConfig.setConnectionTimeout(3000);
hikariConfig.setMaxLifetime(1_740_000); // 29 minutes
hikariConfig.setKeepaliveTime(300_000);
hikariConfig.addDataSourceProperty("tcpKeepAlive", "true");
```

**Rule:** pool size bukan obat query lambat. Jika pool habis, cek query duration, transaction duration, lock wait, dan connection leak sebelum menaikkan pool.

## 17. Kubernetes Resource Requests/Limits & JVM

### Deployment snippet

```yaml id="h20ffm"
resources:
  requests:
    cpu: "500m"
    memory: "768Mi"
  limits:
    cpu: "2"
    memory: "1536Mi"
env:
  - name: JAVA_OPTS
    value: >-
      -XX:MaxRAMPercentage=70
      -XX:+ExitOnOutOfMemoryError
      -Duser.timezone=UTC
```

### Practical notes

| Area | Recommendation |
|---|---|
| JVM heap | Jangan gunakan seluruh container memory |
| Direct/metaspace/thread stack | Sisakan non-heap memory |
| OOM | `ExitOnOutOfMemoryError` agar container restart |
| Timezone | Prefer UTC untuk runtime |
| CPU throttling | Jangan limit terlalu ketat tanpa test |
| GC | Monitor pause time dan allocation |
| Thread count | Sesuaikan HTTP threads + DB pool |

## 18. Kubernetes Rolling Update

```yaml id="62h6xk"
strategy:
  type: RollingUpdate
  rollingUpdate:
    maxUnavailable: 0
    maxSurge: 1
minReadySeconds: 10
```

### PodDisruptionBudget

```yaml id="8m6s49"
apiVersion: policy/v1
kind: PodDisruptionBudget
metadata:
  name: case-service-pdb
spec:
  minAvailable: 2
  selector:
    matchLabels:
      app: case-service
```

### Rollout rule

```text id="6ymks6"
Migration expand first.
Deploy compatible app.
Backfill/switch.
Contract later.
```

**Readiness is critical:** rolling update hanya aman jika readiness probe benar-benar mencerminkan kesiapan instance.

## 20. Health Check Semantics for DB

| Condition | Live | Ready | Reason |
|---|---:|---:|---|
| Process running, DB OK | UP | UP | Normal |
| Process running, DB temporarily down | UP | DOWN | Jangan restart loop; stop traffic |
| App starting, factory not ready | UP | DOWN | Belum siap traffic |
| Migration not completed | UP | DOWN | Schema belum ready |
| Deadlock/retry spike | UP | Maybe UP | Depends severity |
| Pool exhausted | UP | DOWN or degraded | Stop traffic if cannot acquire |
| Main thread stuck | DOWN | DOWN | Restart may help |
| Fatal config error | app exits | DOWN | Fail fast |

**Rule:** liveness bukan dependency check berat. Readiness boleh mengecek dependency minimal yang menentukan traffic safety.

---

## 21. Readiness Check: Schema Version

Jika Flyway dipakai, readiness bisa mengecek schema history secara ringan.

```xml id="yahdy1"
<select id="latestFlywayVersion" resultType="string">
  SELECT version
  FROM flyway_schema_history
  WHERE success = true
  ORDER BY installed_rank DESC
  LIMIT 1
</select>
```

```java id="zv8kfn"
public HealthStatus readiness() {
    if (!runtime.isReadyFlag()) {
        return HealthStatus.down("NOT_READY");
    }

    try (SqlSession session = sqlSessionFactory.openSession(true)) {
        RuntimeMapper mapper = session.getMapper(RuntimeMapper.class);

        mapper.selectOne();

        String version = mapper.latestFlywayVersion();

        if (!schemaVersionPolicy.isCompatible(version)) {
            return HealthStatus.down("SCHEMA_VERSION_INCOMPATIBLE");
        }

        return HealthStatus.up();
    }
}
```

**Caution:** readiness check jangan terlalu mahal. Jangan menjalankan migration atau query berat di readiness.

---

## 22. Startup Failure Modes

| Failure | Symptom | Response |
|---|---|---|
| Missing env | app exits immediately | Fix config/secret |
| DB credential wrong | readiness down / startup fail | Rotate/fix secret |
| DB unreachable | readiness down | Check network/DNS/security group |
| Migration pending | readiness down if strict | Run migration job |
| Mapper XML invalid | startup fail or first query fail | Integration test should catch |
| TypeHandler missing | runtime mapper fail | Startup mapper smoke test |
| Pool cannot initialize | readiness down | DB/network/credential |
| Port conflict | container crash | Fix deployment |
| OOM during startup | OOMKilled | JVM/memory/image tuning |

---

## 23. Runtime Failure Modes

| Failure | Signal | Likely Cause |
|---|---|---|
| `Connection is not available` | Hikari timeout | Pool exhausted/query slow/leak |
| Readiness flapping | Probe too strict or DB unstable | DB/network/pool |
| Liveness restart loop | Liveness checks dependency | Wrong probe semantics |
| Long shutdown | In-flight/worker not stopping | Missing graceful logic |
| Partial writes during deploy | Shutdown interrupted transaction | Grace period too short |
| Migration race | multiple pods run migrate | Move migration out of app startup |
| Query timeout spike | DB load/index regression | EXPLAIN/pg_stat_statements |
| OOMKilled | container memory exceeded | heap/result set/export |
| CPU throttling latency | high p99 | CPU limit too low |
| Secret rotation failure | auth errors | pool old/new credentials mismatch |

---

## 24. Observability Required

| Metric/Log | Why |
|---|---|
| app startup duration | cold start regression |
| readiness status reason | deployment troubleshooting |
| liveness failures | restart diagnosis |
| Hikari active/idle/pending | pool saturation |
| connection acquire time | pool wait |
| connection timeout count | user-facing DB pressure |
| MyBatis mapper duration | slow SQL |
| transaction duration | lock duration |
| in-flight requests | graceful shutdown |
| shutdown duration | grace period sizing |
| migration job status | rollout safety |
| schema version | compatibility |
| worker backlog | outbox/job health |
| pod restart count | runtime stability |
| OOMKilled event | memory sizing |

---

## 25. Deployment Manifest Example

```yaml id="9nxu81"
apiVersion: apps/v1
kind: Deployment
metadata:
  name: case-service
spec:
  replicas: 3
  strategy:
    type: RollingUpdate
    rollingUpdate:
      maxUnavailable: 0
      maxSurge: 1
  minReadySeconds: 10
  selector:
    matchLabels:
      app: case-service
  template:
    metadata:
      labels:
        app: case-service
    spec:
      terminationGracePeriodSeconds: 45
      containers:
        - name: case-service
          image: registry.example.com/case-service:1.0.0
          imagePullPolicy: IfNotPresent
          ports:
            - name: http
              containerPort: 8080

          envFrom:
            - configMapRef:
                name: case-service-config
            - secretRef:
                name: case-service-db

          env:
            - name: JAVA_OPTS
              value: >-
                -XX:MaxRAMPercentage=70
                -XX:+ExitOnOutOfMemoryError
                -Duser.timezone=UTC

          lifecycle:
            preStop:
              exec:
                command:
                  - sh
                  - -c
                  - "wget -qO- http://127.0.0.1:8080/internal/prestop || true; sleep 10"

          startupProbe:
            httpGet:
              path: /health/live
              port: http
            failureThreshold: 30
            periodSeconds: 2

          readinessProbe:
            httpGet:
              path: /health/ready
              port: http
            periodSeconds: 5
            timeoutSeconds: 2
            failureThreshold: 2

          livenessProbe:
            httpGet:
              path: /health/live
              port: http
            periodSeconds: 10
            timeoutSeconds: 2
            failureThreshold: 3

          resources:
            requests:
              cpu: "500m"
              memory: "768Mi"
            limits:
              cpu: "2"
              memory: "1536Mi"
```

## 26. Production Runbook - Pod Not Ready

```text id="cwbwgt"
1. kubectl describe pod
2. Check readiness probe failure reason
3. Check app logs around startup/readiness
4. Verify env/config/secret mounted
5. Verify DB DNS/connectivity
6. Verify DB credential
7. Verify migration job status
8. Check schema version compatibility
9. Check Hikari connection timeout
10. Roll back app if schema/app incompatible
```

### Common commands

```bash id="68wgfh"
kubectl get pods -l app=case-service
kubectl describe pod <pod>
kubectl logs <pod> -c case-service --tail=200
kubectl get events --sort-by=.lastTimestamp
kubectl get job case-service-db-migrate
kubectl logs job/case-service-db-migrate
```

---

## 27. Production Runbook - Pool Exhaustion

```text id="myfe18"
1. Check Hikari active/idle/pending.
2. Check connection acquire time.
3. Check mapper duration p95/p99.
4. Check PostgreSQL pg_stat_activity.
5. Look for long transaction / idle in transaction.
6. Check slow queries / lock waits.
7. Check recent deploy/query/index changes.
8. Reduce traffic or scale carefully.
9. Avoid blindly increasing pool size.
10. Fix query/transaction/root cause.
```

### PostgreSQL diagnostics

```sql id="6wws23"
SELECT
    pid,
    usename,
    application_name,
    state,
    wait_event_type,
    wait_event,
    now() - query_start AS query_age,
    now() - xact_start AS transaction_age,
    query
FROM pg_stat_activity
WHERE datname = current_database()
ORDER BY query_age DESC;
```

---

## 28. Production Runbook - Migration Failed

```text id="thw4im"
1. Stop application rollout.
2. Check Flyway/Liquibase logs.
3. Identify failed migration version.
4. Check whether migration partially applied.
5. Check locks/blocking sessions.
6. Decide forward-fix vs rollback/restore.
7. Do not manually edit schema history unless approved.
8. Run validate after fix.
9. Re-run migration job.
10. Resume rollout only after schema compatibility confirmed.
```

Flyway `validate` checks differences between available migrations and migrations already applied to the target; this is useful after a failed or repaired deployment to verify consistency. ([Redgate Documentation](https://documentation.red-gate.com/flyway/reference/commands?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 29. Production Runbook - Graceful Shutdown Issue

```text id="8sc9sv"
Symptoms:
  499/502/503 during deploy
  partial user requests
  outbox worker duplicate processing
  connection closed errors during shutdown

Checks:
  readiness turns DOWN before SIGTERM?
  preStop called?
  terminationGracePeriodSeconds long enough?
  in-flight request metric drains?
  worker stops before datasource close?
  HTTP server stops accepting new requests?
```

### Fixes

| Issue | Fix |
|---|---|
| readiness still UP during termination | mark not ready on preStop/SIGTERM |
| requests killed mid-transaction | longer grace period + drain |
| worker still running | stop worker before closing datasource |
| load balancer still sends traffic | add preStop delay/readiness propagation |
| liveness restarts during shutdown | probe config review |

---

## 30. Production Runbook - DB Down

```text id="8l77cu"
1. Confirm DB endpoint/DNS/network.
2. Check managed DB status or on-prem cluster.
3. Check app readiness: should be DOWN.
4. Check liveness: should remain UP unless app stuck.
5. Check Hikari connection errors.
6. Stop retry storms if needed.
7. Scale down workers that hammer DB.
8. Recover DB/failover.
9. Verify app reconnects.
10. Watch backlog and retry metrics.
```

**Rule:** DB outage should not cause all pods to liveness-restart forever. Readiness should protect traffic; retry/backoff should protect DB recovery.

---

## 31. Local Docker Compose for Onboarding

```yaml id="iicdcs"
services:
  postgres:
    image: postgres:16
    environment:
      POSTGRES_DB: case_db
      POSTGRES_USER: app
      POSTGRES_PASSWORD: app
    ports:
      - "5432:5432"

  migrate:
    image: flyway/flyway:latest
    depends_on:
      - postgres
    command:
      - -url=jdbc:postgresql://postgres:5432/case_db
      - -user=app
      - -password=app
      - -locations=filesystem:/flyway/sql
      - migrate
    volumes:
      - ./src/main/resources/db/migration:/flyway/sql

  app:
    build: .
    depends_on:
      - postgres
      - migrate
    environment:
      DB_URL: jdbc:postgresql://postgres:5432/case_db
      DB_USERNAME: app
      DB_PASSWORD: app
      DB_POOL_MAX_SIZE: "5"
    ports:
      - "8080:8080"
```

**Note:** `depends_on` di Compose membantu urutan start container, tetapi readiness database/migration tetap perlu dicek aplikasi atau script. Jangan samakan local compose dengan production orchestration.

## 32. Security Runtime Checklist

| Checklist | Status Ideal |
|---|---|
| Container runs as non-root | Ya |
| DB runtime credential bukan migration owner | Ya |
| DB password dari Secret/external secret | Ya |
| Secret tidak dilog | Ya |
| TLS DB configured for non-trusted network | Ya |
| SQL DEBUG off in production | Ya |
| Health endpoint tidak membocorkan sensitive detail | Ya |
| Internal prestop endpoint tidak public | Ya |
| Migration image/runner restricted | Ya |
| App cannot perform DDL in production | Ya |
| Kubernetes RBAC least privilege | Ya |
| Image scanning/signing sesuai policy | Ya |

---

## 33. Anti-Pattern

| Anti-Pattern | Dampak |
|---|---|
| App pod menjalankan migration bersamaan | migration race |
| Liveness probe cek DB | restart storm saat DB outage |
| Readiness selalu UP setelah HTTP server start | traffic masuk sebelum DB/schema siap |
| Close datasource sebelum request drain | in-flight request gagal |
| Worker tidak distop saat shutdown | error/duplicate/noisy logs |
| Pool size terlalu besar per replica | DB overload |
| Missing connection timeout | request menggantung |
| Secret hardcoded di image | credential leak |
| Runtime DB user punya DDL | blast radius besar |
| `SqlSessionFactory` dibuat per request | overhead/resource leak |
| `SqlSession` singleton | thread-safety bug |
| Migration contract sebelum old pods mati | rolling deploy gagal |
| No schema version check | app/schema mismatch |
| No datasource metrics | pool issue blind |
| No runbook | incident lambat ditangani |

---

## 34. Production Checklist

| Checklist | Status Ideal |
|---|---|
| Docker image multi-stage dan non-root | Ya |
| Config fail-fast | Ya |
| HikariDataSource singleton dan ditutup saat shutdown | Ya |
| SqlSessionFactory singleton | Ya |
| SqlSession per request/use case | Ya |
| Migration dijalankan sebagai job/pipeline, bukan semua pod | Ya |
| Startup/readiness/liveness probe dipisah | Ya |
| Readiness mengecek app ready + DB/schema minimal | Ya |
| Liveness shallow dan tidak dependency-heavy | Ya |
| SIGTERM ditangani | Ya |
| Readiness false sebelum drain | Ya |
| In-flight request drain | Ya |
| Background worker stop sebelum datasource close | Ya |
| Pool metrics aktif | Ya |
| Schema version terlihat | Ya |
| Rollout strategy aman | Ya |
| Secret runtime dan migration dipisah | Ya |
| Runbook tersedia untuk DB down, pool exhausted, migration fail | Ya |

---

## 35. Decision Table

| Problem | Recommended Pattern |
|---|---|
| App cold start lama | startupProbe |
| App belum siap DB/schema | readiness DOWN |
| DB down sementara | readiness DOWN, liveness tetap UP |
| App deadlock/stuck | liveness DOWN |
| Migration production | CI/CD step atau Kubernetes Job |
| Banyak pod start bersamaan | jangan app-managed migration |
| Graceful deploy | preStop + readiness false + drain |
| Pool exhausted | diagnose query/lock/leak before raising pool |
| Worker shutdown | stop worker before closing datasource |
| Secret rotation | external secret + rolling deploy + pool recycle |
| Schema compatibility | expand-contract + readiness/schema check |
| Long export | async worker, not request thread |
| Kubernetes rollout | maxUnavailable=0 + correct readiness |
| On-prem DB failover | tested driver/proxy endpoint + retry/backoff |

---

| Urutan | Materi | Target |
|---:|---|---|
| 1 | Docker image Java service | Build image aman dan repeatable |
| 2 | Runtime config/secrets | Config fail-fast dan aman |
| 3 | Hikari/DataSource lifecycle | Pool singleton dan close benar |
| 4 | SqlSessionFactory lifecycle | MyBatis init benar |
| 5 | Migration runner | Schema deploy aman |
| 6 | Kubernetes probes | Startup/readiness/liveness tepat |
| 7 | Graceful shutdown | Deploy tanpa memutus request |
| 8 | Pool/resource tuning | Stabil di traffic production |
| 9 | Observability/runbook | Incident bisa didiagnosis |
| 10 | Rollout/failover testing | Runtime behavior terbukti |
