7/13/2026, 3:12:31 AM

# Cheatsheet - Java MyBatis Production Troubleshooting Playbook: SQL Error, Pool Exhaustion, Deadlock, Slow Query, Migration Drift, Data Inconsistency, dan Incident Response

## 1. Introduction

Troubleshooting production untuk **Jersey/JAX-RS + MyBatis + PostgreSQL** harus dimulai dari prinsip:

```text id="9q6k7p"
Jangan langsung menebak root cause.

Selalu korelasikan:
HTTP request
  -> correlationId / traceId
  -> application service
  -> MyBatis mapper statement
  -> SqlSession / transaction
  -> HikariCP pool
  -> PostgreSQL session/query/lock/plan
  -> migration/schema version
```

MyBatis menempatkan `SqlSession` sebagai interface utama untuk menjalankan command, mengambil mapper, dan mengelola transaksi; `SqlSession` dibuat dari `SqlSessionFactory`, sehingga troubleshooting harus selalu memperhatikan session lifecycle dan transaction boundary. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/java-api.html))

---

## 2. Mental Model Incident

```mermaid id="s5i5fe"
flowchart TD
    A[User/API Symptom] --> B{Symptom Type}

    B -->|5xx / SQL Error| C[Check SQLSTATE + Mapper]
    B -->|Slow API| D[Trace + Mapper Duration + Pool]
    B -->|Timeout| E[Pool Wait / Lock Wait / Query Timeout]
    B -->|Data Wrong| F[Transaction / Replica / Migration / Mapper]
    B -->|Deployment Failure| G[Migration / Config / Readiness]

    C --> H[Classify Error]
    D --> I[pg_stat_activity / pg_stat_statements / EXPLAIN]
    E --> J[Hikari Metrics + pg_locks]
    F --> K[Audit / Outbox / Idempotency / Schema Version]
    G --> L[Flyway/Liquibase + App Version Matrix]

    H --> M[Mitigate]
    I --> M
    J --> M
    K --> M
    L --> M

    M --> N[Fix Root Cause]
    N --> O[Add Regression Test / Runbook Update]
```

**Core invariant:**

```text id="gcdksa"
Incident response sequence:
1. Stabilize impact.
2. Identify blast radius.
3. Classify failure mode.
4. Mitigate safely.
5. Fix root cause.
6. Add regression protection.
```

---

## 3. Decomposition Map

| Area | Gejala | Bukti yang Dicari | Tool/Query |
|---|---|---|---|
| **SQL Error** | 500, PersistenceException | SQLSTATE, mapper id, params shape | app logs, SQLSTATE classifier |
| **Pool Exhaustion** | connection timeout, high p95 | active=max, pending threads, acquire time | Hikari metrics |
| **Slow Query** | endpoint lambat | mapper duration, plan, rows, buffers | `pg_stat_statements`, `EXPLAIN` |
| **Lock Wait** | query menggantung | `wait_event_type='Lock'` | `pg_stat_activity`, `pg_locks` |
| **Deadlock** | transaction aborted | SQLSTATE `40P01` | logs, retry metric, lock ordering |
| **Serialization Failure** | retryable failure | SQLSTATE `40001` | retry metric, isolation level |
| **Migration Drift** | app gagal start/query column missing | schema history mismatch | Flyway validate/info |
| **Data Inconsistency** | state salah/partial write | transaction logs, audit, outbox | audit table, DB queries |
| **Replica Staleness** | read after write missing | read routed to replica | datasource logs, replica lag |
| **Mapper Binding Error** | parameter/resultMap fail | mapper XML mismatch | startup/test logs |
| **Connection Leak** | pool slowly exhausted | long usage time, leak log | Hikari leak detection |
| **Large Result/OOM** | memory spike/OOMKilled | huge result list/export | heap logs, row counts |
| **Tenant Leak** | cross-tenant data visible | missing `tenant_id` predicate | mapper review/test |

---

## 4. First 10 Minutes Incident Triage

```text id="pd14av"
0-2 min:
  Identify symptom, start time, affected endpoints, affected tenants.

2-5 min:
  Check recent deploy/migration/config change.
  Check error rate, p95/p99 latency, DB pool metrics.

5-10 min:
  Classify: SQL error, pool exhaustion, slow query, lock/deadlock,
  migration drift, data issue, or external DB outage.
```

### Quick triage table

| Signal | Kemungkinan Root Cause | First Action |
|---|---|---|
| 5xx spike + SQLSTATE `42P01`/`42703` | table/column missing | check migration/app version |
| Hikari acquire timeout | pool exhausted | check active/pending + slow queries |
| high DB CPU | expensive query/load spike | check `pg_stat_statements` |
| many lock waits | blocking transaction | inspect `pg_stat_activity`/`pg_locks` |
| deadlock errors | lock order conflict | retry + identify code paths |
| stale read after write | replica read | force primary read |
| duplicate key errors | idempotency/business key | classify `23505` |
| readiness down after deploy | schema/config/DB connectivity | check logs/migration |
| OOMKilled | huge result/export/heap | check recent endpoint/data volume |

---

## 5. SQL Error Classification

PostgreSQL exposes standardized SQLSTATE error codes; for transaction rollback class, `40001` is `serialization_failure` and `40P01` is `deadlock_detected`. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/errcodes-appendix.html))

| SQLSTATE | Meaning | Common Cause | HTTP/Action |
|---|---|---|---|
| `23505` | unique violation | duplicate business key/idempotency key | `409 Conflict` |
| `23503` | foreign key violation | invalid reference/order of write | `409` / bug |
| `23514` | check violation | invalid domain state | `400` / `409` |
| `23502` | not null violation | mapper/request missing field | bug or `400` |
| `40001` | serialization failure | concurrent serializable/repeatable read conflict | retry full transaction |
| `40P01` | deadlock detected | inconsistent lock ordering | retry + fix ordering |
| `55P03` | lock not available | `NOWAIT`/lock timeout style conflict | retry/busy response |
| `57014` | query canceled | statement timeout/manual cancel | tune query/timeout |
| `42P01` | undefined table | migration drift/wrong schema | deployment rollback/fix |
| `42703` | undefined column | app/schema mismatch | migration compatibility fix |
| `08006` | connection failure | DB/network/failover | retry/backoff/readiness down |

### Java classifier

```java id="wsg1zy"
public enum PersistenceErrorKind {
    UNIQUE_VIOLATION,
    FOREIGN_KEY_VIOLATION,
    CHECK_VIOLATION,
    NOT_NULL_VIOLATION,
    SERIALIZATION_FAILURE,
    DEADLOCK,
    LOCK_NOT_AVAILABLE,
    QUERY_CANCELED,
    UNDEFINED_TABLE,
    UNDEFINED_COLUMN,
    CONNECTION_FAILURE,
    UNKNOWN
}
```

```java id="5u2f4x"
public final class PostgresErrorClassifier {

    public static PersistenceErrorKind classify(Throwable throwable) {
        SQLException sqlException = findSqlException(throwable);

        if (sqlException == null) {
            return PersistenceErrorKind.UNKNOWN;
        }

        return switch (sqlException.getSQLState()) {
            case "23505" -> PersistenceErrorKind.UNIQUE_VIOLATION;
            case "23503" -> PersistenceErrorKind.FOREIGN_KEY_VIOLATION;
            case "23514" -> PersistenceErrorKind.CHECK_VIOLATION;
            case "23502" -> PersistenceErrorKind.NOT_NULL_VIOLATION;
            case "40001" -> PersistenceErrorKind.SERIALIZATION_FAILURE;
            case "40P01" -> PersistenceErrorKind.DEADLOCK;
            case "55P03" -> PersistenceErrorKind.LOCK_NOT_AVAILABLE;
            case "57014" -> PersistenceErrorKind.QUERY_CANCELED;
            case "42P01" -> PersistenceErrorKind.UNDEFINED_TABLE;
            case "42703" -> PersistenceErrorKind.UNDEFINED_COLUMN;
            case "08000", "08001", "08003", "08006" ->
                PersistenceErrorKind.CONNECTION_FAILURE;
            default -> PersistenceErrorKind.UNKNOWN;
        };
    }

    private static SQLException findSqlException(Throwable throwable) {
        Throwable current = throwable;

        while (current != null) {
            if (current instanceof SQLException sqlException) {
                return sqlException;
            }
            current = current.getCause();
        }

        return null;
    }
}
```

---

## 6. Playbook - SQL Error Spike

### Symptoms

```text id="ky7lzz"
- HTTP 500 spike
- PersistenceException / SQLException
- specific mapper failing
- error after deployment or migration
```

### Steps

```text id="hlghdx"
1. Ambil sample error log dengan correlationId.
2. Extract SQLSTATE.
3. Extract mapper statement id.
4. Check apakah error mulai setelah deploy/migration.
5. Classify: constraint, schema mismatch, transient, connection.
6. Mitigate:
   - constraint conflict: return correct 409/400
   - schema mismatch: rollback app or forward migration
   - transient: retry/backoff if safe
   - connection: readiness down / failover
7. Add regression test.
```

### Logging pattern

```java id="j5pzbz"
catch (RuntimeException e) {
    PersistenceErrorKind kind = PostgresErrorClassifier.classify(e);

    log.error(
        "Persistence failure operation={} mapper={} errorKind={} sqlState={} correlationId={}",
        operationName,
        mapperStatementId,
        kind,
        SqlExceptionExtractor.sqlState(e),
        correlationId,
        e
    );

    throw e;
}
```

---

## 7. Playbook - HikariCP Pool Exhaustion

HikariCP documents that `maximumPoolSize` is the maximum pool size including idle and in-use connections; if the pool reaches this size and no idle connection is available, `getConnection()` blocks up to `connectionTimeout` before timing out. ([GitHub](https://github.com/brettwooldridge/hikaricp))

### Symptoms

```text id="5n5obf"
- SQLTransientConnectionException
- "Connection is not available, request timed out"
- p95/p99 latency naik
- active connections = max
- pending threads > 0
```

### First questions

| Question | Why |
|---|---|
| Active connections full? | Pool saturated |
| Pending threads rising? | Request waiting for DB connection |
| Acquire time rising? | Pool bottleneck |
| Usage time rising? | Transaction/query too long |
| DB CPU high? | DB overloaded |
| DB CPU low but pool full? | lock wait, connection leak, idle transaction |
| Recent deploy? | query/transaction regression |
| Export/backfill running? | long-held connections |

### Required metrics

```text id="vh5uta"
hikari.connections.active
hikari.connections.idle
hikari.connections.pending
hikari.connections.timeout
hikari.connections.acquire
hikari.connections.usage
db.mybatis.statement.duration
db.transaction.duration
```

### Immediate mitigation

| Situation | Mitigation |
|---|---|
| One heavy endpoint causing saturation | throttle/disable endpoint |
| Backfill/export consuming pool | pause worker |
| DB lock wait holding connections | terminate blocker carefully |
| Connection leak suspected | enable leak detection temporarily |
| Traffic spike | scale app carefully; check DB max connections |
| DB overloaded | reduce concurrency, not increase pool blindly |

### Hikari leak detection

HikariCP `leakDetectionThreshold` logs a possible leak when a connection is out of the pool longer than the threshold; `0` disables it and the lowest accepted enabled value is 2000 ms. ([GitHub](https://github.com/brettwooldridge/hikaricp))

```java id="qv8kkq"
hikariConfig.setLeakDetectionThreshold(20_000);
```

**Caution:** aktifkan leak detection sebagai diagnostic sementara atau dengan threshold realistis. Jangan set terlalu rendah untuk request normal yang memang bisa lama.

---

## 8. SQL Diagnostic - Active Queries

PostgreSQL `pg_stat_activity` exposes one row per backend process and includes current activity such as state and current query. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/monitoring-stats.html))

```sql id="lo49ft"
SELECT
    pid,
    usename,
    application_name,
    client_addr,
    state,
    wait_event_type,
    wait_event,
    now() - query_start AS query_age,
    now() - xact_start AS transaction_age,
    query
FROM pg_stat_activity
WHERE datname = current_database()
ORDER BY query_age DESC NULLS LAST;
```

### Interpretation

| Signal | Meaning |
|---|---|
| `state='active'` + long query_age | slow query or waiting |
| `wait_event_type='Lock'` | blocked by lock |
| `state='idle in transaction'` | app left transaction open |
| long `transaction_age` | lock/vacuum risk |
| many same query | hot endpoint/N+1/retry storm |
| many app connections idle | pool too large or traffic low |

---

## 9. Playbook - Slow Query

### Symptoms

```text id="y5l4tc"
- endpoint latency high
- mapper timing high
- DB CPU or IO high
- pg_stat_statements shows high total/mean time
```

`pg_stat_statements` tracks planning and execution statistics for SQL statements executed by a PostgreSQL server, which makes it useful for finding expensive query patterns historically, not just queries currently running. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/pgstatstatements.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Top total time

```sql id="nd21cc"
SELECT
    queryid,
    calls,
    total_exec_time,
    mean_exec_time,
    max_exec_time,
    rows,
    shared_blks_hit,
    shared_blks_read,
    query
FROM pg_stat_statements
ORDER BY total_exec_time DESC
LIMIT 20;
```

### Top mean time

```sql id="ogfqw7"
SELECT
    queryid,
    calls,
    mean_exec_time,
    max_exec_time,
    rows,
    query
FROM pg_stat_statements
WHERE calls >= 10
ORDER BY mean_exec_time DESC
LIMIT 20;
```

### Investigation sequence

```text id="23ynk9"
1. Identify mapper statement id.
2. Get normalized query from pg_stat_statements.
3. Reproduce with representative parameters.
4. Run EXPLAIN (ANALYZE, BUFFERS) in safe environment.
5. Compare estimated vs actual rows.
6. Check scan type, join type, sort, rows removed by filter.
7. Check index support.
8. Check returned row count and projection width.
9. Apply minimal fix.
10. Re-test and add regression.
```

---

## 10. EXPLAIN Playbook

PostgreSQL `EXPLAIN` shows the plan selected by the planner; `EXPLAIN ANALYZE` actually runs the query, and PostgreSQL warns that side effects of data-changing statements still happen unless wrapped and rolled back. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/using-explain.html))

### Safe pattern for SELECT

```sql id="q6vmax"
EXPLAIN (ANALYZE, BUFFERS)
SELECT
    id,
    case_number,
    status,
    due_at
FROM cases
WHERE tenant_id = '00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000001'
  AND status = 'OPEN'
ORDER BY due_at ASC, id ASC
LIMIT 50;
```

### Safe pattern for UPDATE diagnosis

```sql id="rh53nf"
BEGIN;

EXPLAIN (ANALYZE, BUFFERS)
UPDATE cases
SET status = 'ESCALATED'
WHERE tenant_id = '00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000001'
  AND status = 'OPEN'
  AND due_at < now();

ROLLBACK;
```

### What to inspect

| Plan Signal | Meaning |
|---|---|
| Actual rows much higher than estimate | stale stats / poor predicate selectivity |
| Seq Scan on large table | missing index or low selectivity |
| Rows Removed by Filter high | reading too many rows |
| Sort large | missing order-supporting index / memory |
| Nested Loop with high loops | join amplification |
| Shared blocks read high | disk IO pressure |
| Planning time high | query too complex / many partitions |
| Execution time high | runtime bottleneck |
| No network cost included | app latency may still differ |

PostgreSQL notes that `EXPLAIN ANALYZE` execution time does not include normal result delivery to the client, so application-observed latency can still be higher due to network and object mapping. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/using-explain.html))

---

## 11. Playbook - Lock Wait

PostgreSQL `pg_locks` shows locks held or awaited; `granted=false` indicates a process is waiting for a lock, and `pg_locks.pid` can be joined to `pg_stat_activity.pid` to inspect sessions. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/view-pg-locks.html))

### Find waiting sessions

```sql id="c2ylv6"
SELECT
    a.pid,
    a.usename,
    a.application_name,
    a.state,
    a.wait_event_type,
    a.wait_event,
    now() - a.query_start AS waiting_for,
    a.query
FROM pg_stat_activity a
WHERE a.wait_event_type = 'Lock'
ORDER BY waiting_for DESC;
```

### Blocked vs blocking sessions

```sql id="zg7s3c"
SELECT
    blocked.pid AS blocked_pid,
    blocked.query AS blocked_query,
    blocking.pid AS blocking_pid,
    blocking.query AS blocking_query,
    now() - blocked.query_start AS blocked_duration,
    now() - blocking.query_start AS blocking_query_age
FROM pg_stat_activity blocked
JOIN pg_locks blocked_locks
  ON blocked_locks.pid = blocked.pid
JOIN pg_locks blocking_locks
  ON blocking_locks.locktype = blocked_locks.locktype
 AND blocking_locks.database IS NOT DISTINCT FROM blocked_locks.database
 AND blocking_locks.relation IS NOT DISTINCT FROM blocked_locks.relation
 AND blocking_locks.page IS NOT DISTINCT FROM blocked_locks.page
 AND blocking_locks.tuple IS NOT DISTINCT FROM blocked_locks.tuple
 AND blocking_locks.virtualxid IS NOT DISTINCT FROM blocked_locks.virtualxid
 AND blocking_locks.transactionid IS NOT DISTINCT FROM blocked_locks.transactionid
 AND blocking_locks.classid IS NOT DISTINCT FROM blocked_locks.classid
 AND blocking_locks.objid IS NOT DISTINCT FROM blocked_locks.objid
 AND blocking_locks.objsubid IS NOT DISTINCT FROM blocked_locks.objsubid
 AND blocking_locks.pid <> blocked_locks.pid
JOIN pg_stat_activity blocking
  ON blocking.pid = blocking_locks.pid
WHERE NOT blocked_locks.granted
  AND blocking_locks.granted
ORDER BY blocked_duration DESC;
```

### Mitigation

| Situation | Action |
|---|---|
| blocker is idle in transaction | terminate session carefully |
| blocker is long migration | pause rollout, assess lock |
| blocker is application query | identify endpoint/mapper |
| repeated lock waits | add timeout + reduce transaction scope |
| lock from batch job | reduce chunk size/throttle |
| lock from manual DBA session | coordinate immediately |

### PostgreSQL logging

`deadlock_timeout` also determines how long to wait before logging lock waits when `log_lock_waits` is enabled, so lowering it temporarily can help diagnose locking delays. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/runtime-config-locks.html))

---

## 12. Playbook - Deadlock

### Symptoms

```text id="z6nsmn"
SQLSTATE 40P01
Deadlock detected
One transaction is aborted by PostgreSQL
Retry may succeed
```

### Immediate action

```text id="tdnhae"
1. Confirm SQLSTATE = 40P01.
2. Verify retry policy exists for safe operations.
3. Identify two or more code paths acquiring locks in different order.
4. Check recent deploy/backfill/batch job.
5. Add deterministic lock ordering.
6. Add concurrency regression test.
```

### Common cause

```text id="g9yl9i"
Transaction A:
  lock order: order -> customer

Transaction B:
  lock order: customer -> order

Result:
  circular wait -> deadlock
```

### Fix: deterministic ordering

```xml id="6z0zka"
<select id="findCasesForUpdate" resultMap="CaseRecordResultMap">
  SELECT
    id,
    status,
    version
  FROM cases
  WHERE id IN
  <foreach collection="ids" item="id" open="(" separator="," close=")">
    #{id}
  </foreach>
  ORDER BY id ASC
  FOR UPDATE
</select>
```

### Retry wrapper

```java id="et2czs"
public <T> T retryDeadlockOrSerialization(
    String operation,
    Supplier<T> transaction
) {
    int maxAttempts = 3;

    for (int attempt = 1; attempt <= maxAttempts; attempt++) {
        try {
            return transaction.get();
        } catch (RuntimeException e) {
            PersistenceErrorKind kind = PostgresErrorClassifier.classify(e);

            boolean retryable =
                kind == PersistenceErrorKind.DEADLOCK
                    || kind == PersistenceErrorKind.SERIALIZATION_FAILURE;

            if (!retryable || attempt == maxAttempts) {
                throw e;
            }

            log.warn(
                "Retrying transaction operation={} attempt={} reason={}",
                operation,
                attempt,
                kind
            );

            sleepBackoff(attempt);
        }
    }

    throw new IllegalStateException("unreachable");
}
```

**Rule:** retry must repeat the **entire transaction**, not only the failed mapper call.

---

## 13. Playbook - Serialization Failure

PostgreSQL’s Repeatable Read section states applications using that level must be prepared to retry transactions due to serialization failures; PostgreSQL’s table also shows Serializable prevents serialization anomalies but may require retry. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/transaction-iso.html))

### Symptoms

```text id="xy8fca"
SQLSTATE 40001
Transaction aborted
Often appears under SERIALIZABLE / REPEATABLE READ / concurrent writes
```

### Correct response

| Step | Action |
|---|---|
| 1 | classify as retryable |
| 2 | rollback current transaction |
| 3 | backoff |
| 4 | re-run full unit-of-work |
| 5 | cap attempts |
| 6 | expose final failure as 503/409 depending operation |

### Not safe to retry blindly

| Operation | Retry Safe? |
|---|---:|
| DB-only transaction | Usually yes |
| DB + outbox in same transaction | Yes |
| DB + external HTTP call inside transaction | No |
| DB + email sent inside transaction | No |
| DB + Kafka publish before commit | No |
| idempotent external call | Maybe |

---

## 14. Playbook - Migration Drift / App-Schema Mismatch

### Symptoms

```text id="2idp2u"
- undefined table / column
- mapper fails after deploy
- app readiness down
- Flyway validate fails
- one pod version works, another fails
```

Flyway provides commands for database migration lifecycle such as `migrate`, `validate`, `repair`, `info`, and others; `validate` is specifically useful to compare applied migrations with resolved migrations and detect drift or accidental changes. ([Redgate Documentation](https://documentation.red-gate.com/flyway/reference/commands?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Diagnosis

```sql id="b5u0g8"
SELECT
    installed_rank,
    version,
    description,
    type,
    script,
    checksum,
    installed_by,
    installed_on,
    success
FROM flyway_schema_history
ORDER BY installed_rank DESC
LIMIT 20;
```

### Playbook

```text id="ryjid3"
1. Stop rollout.
2. Identify failing SQLSTATE: 42P01, 42703, duplicate object, etc.
3. Check app version vs migration version.
4. Check Flyway schema history.
5. Run validate in pipeline/job context.
6. If migration failed halfway:
   - inspect actual DB state
   - decide forward-fix vs restore
   - do not manually edit schema history casually
7. Roll forward with compatible migration or rollback app.
8. Add migration compatibility test.
```

### `repair` caution

Flyway `repair` modifies schema history metadata to recover from validation errors or failed migrations, so it should be used only after the actual database state is understood and the fix strategy is approved. ([Redgate Documentation](https://documentation.red-gate.com/fd/repair-277578892.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 15. Playbook - Data Inconsistency

### Symptoms

```text id="rm84fq"
- order exists but outbox missing
- status changed without audit
- duplicate business command
- UI shows impossible state
- integration target has different state
```

### Investigation sequence

```text id="wvv8gr"
1. Identify aggregate ID and tenant ID.
2. Pull audit trail for aggregate.
3. Pull outbox/inbox/idempotency rows.
4. Check transaction boundary in service.
5. Check whether external call happened inside transaction.
6. Check mapper affected-row handling.
7. Check migration/backfill around incident time.
8. Check replica vs primary read.
9. Check retry logs.
10. Reconstruct timeline.
```

### Useful queries

```sql id="ad4squ"
SELECT *
FROM audit_events
WHERE tenant_id = :tenant_id
  AND aggregate_type = 'ORDER'
  AND aggregate_id = :order_id
ORDER BY created_at ASC;
```

```sql id="fxf9dg"
SELECT *
FROM outbox_events
WHERE aggregate_type = 'ORDER'
  AND aggregate_id = :order_id
ORDER BY created_at ASC;
```

```sql id="ff2wan"
SELECT *
FROM idempotency_keys
WHERE tenant_id = :tenant_id
  AND key = :idempotency_key;
```

### Likely root causes

| Evidence | Likely Cause |
|---|---|
| business row committed, outbox missing | outbox not in same transaction |
| duplicate row with same client request | missing idempotency |
| affected row = 0 ignored | optimistic lock conflict ignored |
| status impossible by domain | direct DB/manual update or missing constraint |
| audit missing | audit outside transaction or skipped path |
| read model stale | async projection lag |
| primary correct, UI wrong | replica/cache/read model issue |

---

## 16. Playbook - MyBatis Mapper Binding / ResultMap Error

### Symptoms

```text id="7faz6q"
- BindingException
- parameter not found
- result mapping failure
- TypeHandler not found
- column does not map to property
- null in primitive field
```

### Common causes

| Error | Cause | Fix |
|---|---|---|
| `Parameter 'x' not found` | missing `@Param` or wrong name | add `@Param`, fix XML |
| result property null | alias mismatch | use column alias |
| primitive gets null | DB nullable but Java primitive | use wrapper type |
| TypeHandler missing | custom JSONB/enum not registered | register handler |
| duplicate statement id | namespace/id collision | fix mapper namespace |
| invalid bound statement | mapper XML not loaded | config/package scan fix |
| constructor mapping fails | record constructor mismatch | update resultMap |

### Safe mapper logging

```text id="at4wgv"
Log:
  mapper statement id
  SQLSTATE
  parameter shape, not full sensitive values

Avoid:
  full JSONB payload
  auth token
  PII-heavy request body
```

---

## 17. Playbook - Connection Leak

### Symptoms

```text id="r1v9d6"
- pool gradually exhausts
- active connections never return
- traffic normal but pending rises
- leak detection logs stack trace
```

### Common causes

```text id="h6sm7c"
- SqlSession not closed
- Cursor not closed
- Result stream escapes session scope
- manual Connection not closed
- long-running export holds session
- worker stuck inside transaction
```

### Correct MyBatis pattern

```java id="omelbf"
try (SqlSession session = sqlSessionFactory.openSession(false)) {
    try {
        OrderMapper mapper = session.getMapper(OrderMapper.class);

        mapper.insert(order);

        session.commit();
    } catch (RuntimeException | Error e) {
        session.rollback();
        throw e;
    }
}
```

### Cursor pattern

```java id="9ncldy"
try (SqlSession session = sqlSessionFactory.openSession(false);
     Cursor<AuditLog> cursor =
         session.getMapper(AuditMapper.class).streamLogs(criteria)) {

    for (AuditLog log : cursor) {
        sink.write(log);
    }

    session.commit();
}
```

**Rule:** cursor, session, and transaction must live and close together.

---

## 18. Playbook - Large Result / OOM

### Symptoms

```text id="b0yk0l"
- OOMKilled
- GC pressure
- endpoint export kills pod
- heap grows during query
- response serialization very slow
```

### Root causes

| Cause | Fix |
|---|---|
| `select *` large table | projection |
| export loads `List<T>` | cursor/fetchSize/async export |
| unbounded limit | max limit |
| JSONB payload over-fetch | select only needed fields |
| nested resultMap huge graph | DTO/read model |
| count + full search | optional count |
| request returns millions rows | async job/file export |

### Emergency mitigation

```text id="n4nmo1"
- disable or rate-limit endpoint
- reduce max limit
- route export to worker
- scale memory only as temporary mitigation
- add streaming or async job
```

---

## 19. Playbook - Replica Stale Read / Read-After-Write Bug

### Symptoms

```text id="75v0uy"
- POST success then GET returns 404
- status appears old after update
- user refresh later sees correct data
- only happens in production topology
```

### Diagnosis

```text id="3y1xgg"
1. Check datasource used by GET.
2. Check whether request after write was routed to replica.
3. Check replica lag.
4. Check if response could have been built from command state.
5. Force primary for read-after-write path.
```

### Fix pattern

```java id="5h63bq"
public OrderResponse createOrder(CreateOrderCommand command) {
    return unitOfWork.write(ctx -> {
        Order order = Order.create(command);

        repositoryFactory.orderRepository(ctx).save(order);

        return OrderResponse.from(order);
    });
}
```

**Rule:** jangan create/update di primary lalu membangun response dari replica.

---

## 20. Playbook - Tenant Data Leak

### Symptoms

```text id="05hq0t"
- user sees another tenant data
- admin query reused by normal endpoint
- one mapper lacks tenant predicate
```

### Immediate response

```text id="j0fu0t"
1. Treat as security incident.
2. Identify endpoints and tenants affected.
3. Disable endpoint if needed.
4. Check mapper SQL for tenant_id predicate.
5. Check authorization layer.
6. Check audit logs for access.
7. Patch with tenant predicate and test.
8. Add static review guard for tenant-owned tables.
```

### Query check

```bash id="wb3kui"
grep -R "FROM cases" src/main/resources/mapper
grep -R "tenant_id" src/main/resources/mapper
```

### Regression test

```java id="7w0wr7"
@Test
void shouldNotReturnCaseFromAnotherTenant() {
    UUID tenantA = UUID.randomUUID();
    UUID tenantB = UUID.randomUUID();

    UUID caseInTenantB = fixture.insertCase(tenantB);

    CaseRecord result = mapper.findById(tenantA, caseInTenantB);

    assertThat(result).isNull();
}
```

---

## 21. Playbook - Outbox Backlog

### Symptoms

```text id="ow1esr"
- integration events delayed
- outbox pending grows
- worker processing errors
- duplicate publishes
```

### Queries

```sql id="m07px3"
SELECT
    status,
    count(*) AS total,
    min(created_at) AS oldest_created_at,
    max(created_at) AS newest_created_at
FROM outbox_events
GROUP BY status
ORDER BY status;
```

```sql id="u761fh"
SELECT
    id,
    event_type,
    aggregate_type,
    aggregate_id,
    status,
    attempt_count,
    available_at,
    last_error,
    created_at
FROM outbox_events
WHERE status IN ('PENDING', 'FAILED')
ORDER BY available_at ASC, created_at ASC
LIMIT 100;
```

### Root causes

| Evidence | Likely Cause |
|---|---|
| many `FAILED` same error | downstream contract/auth issue |
| pending grows but workers idle | scheduler/worker disabled |
| processing stuck | lock/worker crash |
| duplicates downstream | missing idempotent consumer |
| claim query slow | missing partial index |
| worker pool too high | downstream overload |

### Fix strategy

```text id="dl9sar"
- fix downstream error
- replay failed safely
- ensure idempotency key/event id
- tune worker batch size
- verify claim query index
- avoid manual status update without audit
```

---

## 22. Standard PostgreSQL Diagnostic Query Pack

### 22.1 Long active queries

```sql id="9fvbo3"
SELECT
    pid,
    usename,
    application_name,
    state,
    wait_event_type,
    wait_event,
    now() - query_start AS query_age,
    query
FROM pg_stat_activity
WHERE state = 'active'
ORDER BY query_age DESC;
```

### 22.2 Idle in transaction

```sql id="ul4yaw"
SELECT
    pid,
    usename,
    application_name,
    now() - xact_start AS transaction_age,
    now() - state_change AS idle_age,
    query
FROM pg_stat_activity
WHERE state LIKE 'idle in transaction%'
ORDER BY transaction_age DESC;
```

### 22.3 Waiting locks

```sql id="6m40d8"
SELECT
    a.pid,
    a.usename,
    a.application_name,
    l.locktype,
    l.mode,
    l.granted,
    now() - l.waitstart AS wait_age,
    a.query
FROM pg_locks l
JOIN pg_stat_activity a
  ON a.pid = l.pid
WHERE l.granted = false
ORDER BY wait_age DESC;
```

### 22.4 Query workload

```sql id="m99m4s"
SELECT
    calls,
    total_exec_time,
    mean_exec_time,
    max_exec_time,
    rows,
    query
FROM pg_stat_statements
ORDER BY total_exec_time DESC
LIMIT 20;
```

---

## 23. Terminating a Backend Safely

### Identify before terminate

```sql id="gz66ni"
SELECT
    pid,
    usename,
    application_name,
    state,
    now() - xact_start AS transaction_age,
    now() - query_start AS query_age,
    query
FROM pg_stat_activity
WHERE pid = :pid;
```

### Terminate

```sql id="fjpe5x"
SELECT pg_terminate_backend(:pid);
```

### Use only when

| Condition | OK? |
|---|---:|
| blocker is idle in transaction and causing outage | Usually yes after confirmation |
| manual session forgot transaction | yes |
| migration holding lock | coordinate first |
| critical business transaction active | risky |
| unknown session | investigate first |
| prepared transaction | special handling |

**Rule:** terminate backend is mitigation, not root cause fix. Always capture evidence first.

---

## 24. Application-Level Incident Logging Template

```java id="rps7xr"
public record PersistenceIncidentContext(
    String operation,
    String mapperStatement,
    String datasource,
    String correlationId,
    UUID tenantId,
    UUID actorId,
    PersistenceErrorKind errorKind,
    String sqlState
) {}
```

```java id="f4z4ib"
public void logPersistenceIncident(
    PersistenceIncidentContext context,
    Throwable throwable
) {
    log.error(
        "Persistence incident operation={} mapper={} datasource={} tenantId={} actorId={} errorKind={} sqlState={} correlationId={}",
        context.operation(),
        context.mapperStatement(),
        context.datasource(),
        context.tenantId(),
        context.actorId(),
        context.errorKind(),
        context.sqlState(),
        context.correlationId(),
        throwable
    );
}
```

### Do not log

```text id="fxs8so"
- DB password
- Authorization header
- refresh/access token
- full JSONB payload
- full PII/customer profile
- payment data
- legal/enforcement sensitive note
```

---

## 25. War Room Checklist

```text id="i1mab5"
Incident Commander:
  owns timeline and decisions

App Owner:
  checks service logs, traces, mapper ids

DB Owner:
  checks pg_stat_activity, locks, pg_stat_statements

Platform Owner:
  checks pods, rollout, config, network, secrets

Comms Owner:
  updates stakeholders
```

### Timeline template

```text id="2qpq8e"
T+00: Alert triggered
T+03: Impact confirmed: endpoints/tenants/users
T+07: Recent deploy/migration checked
T+10: Failure class identified
T+15: Mitigation applied
T+30: Root cause candidate
T+60: Permanent fix or rollback complete
T+N: Postmortem items created
```

---

## 26. Rollback vs Forward-Fix Decision

| Condition | Prefer Rollback | Prefer Forward-Fix |
|---|---:|---:|
| app-only bug | Ya | Maybe |
| destructive DB migration already applied | Hard | Often |
| expand migration additive | App rollback safe | Maybe |
| mapper references missing column | rollback app or run migration | depends |
| data corruption ongoing | stop/rollback first | then fix |
| security leak | disable/rollback quickly | then patch |
| failed migration halfway | maybe stop | careful forward-fix |
| bad index migration | maybe drop concurrently | forward DBA fix |
| external dependency auth expired | no app rollback | fix secret/config |

---

## 27. Regression Protection After Incident

| Incident | Add This Test/Guard |
|---|---|
| SQL syntax/schema mismatch | mapper integration test |
| missing tenant predicate | tenant isolation test + mapper review |
| duplicate command | idempotency test |
| deadlock | concurrency lock-order test |
| serialization retry failed | retry wrapper test |
| pool exhaustion due cursor | cursor close test |
| slow query | query regression/golden dataset/EXPLAIN smoke |
| migration drift | Flyway validate in CI |
| data partial commit | transaction rollback test |
| outbox missing | outbox same-transaction test |
| stale replica read | routing policy unit test |
| error exposed SQL detail | API error mapping test |

---

## 28. Incident Severity Matrix

| Severity | Example | Response |
|---|---|---|
| SEV1 | data leak, data corruption, full outage | war room, immediate mitigation |
| SEV2 | major endpoint unavailable, many users affected | rollback/fix quickly |
| SEV3 | degraded performance, limited tenants | normal incident process |
| SEV4 | isolated error, no broad impact | ticket + fix |
| Security Incident | cross-tenant/PII/secrets | separate security response |

---

## 29. Common Anti-Patterns

| Anti-Pattern | Why Dangerous |
|---|---|
| Increasing pool size blindly | can overload DB further |
| Retrying every SQLException | can duplicate side effects |
| Logging full SQL params in prod | PII/secrets exposure |
| Killing DB sessions without evidence | can worsen data integrity |
| Running `repair` without DB state review | hides migration problem |
| Ignoring affected row count | lost update/conflict missed |
| Treating replica read as strong read | stale data bugs |
| Putting DB check in liveness | restart storm during DB outage |
| Manual DB update without audit | postmortem impossible |
| Fixing data without preserving evidence | root cause lost |
| Using production as first repro | risky |
| Not adding regression tests | incident repeats |

---

| Checklist | Status Ideal |
|---|---|
| Every error log has correlationId | Ya |
| Mapper statement id visible in logs/metrics | Ya |
| SQLSTATE classifier implemented | Ya |
| Hikari pool metrics exported | Ya |
| Transaction duration measured | Ya |

| `pg_stat_activity` access/runbook available | Ya |
| `pg_stat_statements` enabled where allowed | Ya |
| EXPLAIN workflow documented | Ya |
| Migration history visible | Ya |
| Flyway validate in CI/CD | Ya |
| Retry policy limited to safe SQLSTATE | Ya |
| Deadlock/serialization retry metric exists | Ya |
| Tenant isolation tests exist | Ya |
| Outbox backlog dashboard exists | Ya |
| Read replica lag monitored if replica used | Ya |
| Runbook for pool/lock/migration/data issue exists | Ya |
| Incident postmortem creates regression test | Ya |

## 31. Decision Table

| Symptom | First Diagnostic | Likely Fix |
|---|---|---|
| `Connection timeout` | Hikari metrics + `pg_stat_activity` | fix slow query/leak/lock |
| `40P01` | lock ordering + retry logs | deterministic lock order |
| `40001` | isolation/retry policy | full transaction retry |
| `42P01`/`42703` | migration/app version | rollback/forward migration |
| slow endpoint | trace + mapper duration | EXPLAIN/index/projection |
| DB CPU high | `pg_stat_statements` | tune hot query |
| DB CPU low, app waiting | locks/pool/network | fix blocker/leak |
| stale read | datasource routing | force primary |
| wrong data state | audit/outbox/idempotency | fix transaction boundary |
| tenant leak | mapper predicate | patch + security incident |
| outbox backlog | outbox status/error | fix worker/downstream |
| readiness down | config/DB/schema | fix dependency/migration |

Lanjut ke final production checklist dan architecture review: bagian ini merangkum seluruh seri MyBatis menjadi checklist praktis untuk menilai codebase nyata saat onboarding atau sebelum production release.

# Cheatsheet - Java MyBatis Final Production Checklist & Architecture Review

## 1. Introduction

Ini adalah **penutup seri MyBatis** untuk aplikasi **Jersey/JAX-RS + MyBatis + PostgreSQL**. Tujuannya bukan menambah pola baru, tetapi membuat **checklist review praktis** yang bisa dipakai saat onboarding ke codebase nyata, PR review, pre-production review, atau post-incident hardening.

MyBatis memposisikan `SqlSession` sebagai interface utama untuk menjalankan command, mengambil mapper, dan mengelola transaksi; Mapper XML adalah pusat mapped statement dan result mapping. Karena itu, production-readiness MyBatis sangat bergantung pada kualitas **session lifecycle, mapper XML, transaction boundary, SQL design, schema compatibility, testing, observability, dan runtime operations**. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/java-api.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 2. Final Reference Architecture

```mermaid id="b6t4aa"
flowchart TD
    A[JAX-RS Resource] --> B[Request Validation]
    B --> C[Application Service]
    C --> D[Authorization / Tenant Context]
    C --> E[Unit of Work / Transaction Boundary]

    E --> F[Repository Interface]
    F --> G[MyBatis Repository Implementation]
    G --> H[MyBatis Mapper Interface]
    H --> I[Mapper XML]

    I --> J[(PostgreSQL)]

    C --> K[Domain Model / Domain Service]
    C --> L[Outbox / Audit Repository]

    J --> M[Constraints / Indexes / Locks]
    J --> N[Migration History]
    J --> O[pg_stat_activity / pg_stat_statements]

    P[Observability] --> C
    P --> G
    P --> J

    Q[Docker / Kubernetes Runtime] --> C
    Q --> R[HikariCP Pool]
    R --> J
```

**Production invariant:**

```text id="e57czn"
Resource tidak membuka SqlSession.
Application service mengontrol use case dan transaction boundary.
Repository menyembunyikan persistence detail.
Mapper hanya menjalankan SQL contract.
Mapper XML harus aman, tested, observable, dan schema-compatible.
```

---

## 3. Final Readiness Scorecard

Gunakan skor sederhana:

| Score | Meaning |
|---:|---|
| 0 | Tidak ada / belum jelas |
| 1 | Ada tapi manual, tidak konsisten, atau belum dites |
| 2 | Ada, konsisten, dan sudah dites |
| 3 | Production-grade: tested, observable, documented, dan punya runbook |

| Area | Target Minimum |
|---|---:|
| Layering & boundary | 2 |
| SqlSession lifecycle | 3 |
| Mapper XML quality | 3 |
| Transaction boundary | 3 |
| Concurrency/idempotency | 2-3 |
| Dynamic SQL security | 3 |
| PostgreSQL schema/index awareness | 2-3 |
| Migration compatibility | 3 |
| Testing coverage | 3 |
| Observability | 3 |
| Runtime/Kubernetes readiness | 2-3 |
| Troubleshooting runbook | 2-3 |

**Interpretasi cepat:**

```text id="ja32ie"
Total score < 20:
  belum siap production review serius.

20-30:
  cukup untuk staging, tapi banyak risk.

30-40:
  production-capable dengan beberapa gap.

40+:
  mature baseline, siap masuk operational hardening.
```

---

## 4. Architecture Boundary Checklist

| Checklist | Expected |
|---|---:|
| JAX-RS resource hanya mengurus HTTP, request/response, status code | Ya |
| Resource tidak membuka `SqlSession` | Ya |
| Resource tidak memanggil mapper langsung untuk command penting | Ya |
| Application service mengontrol transaction boundary | Ya |
| Domain layer bebas dari MyBatis/JDBC/Jersey | Ya |
| Repository interface domain-oriented untuk command side | Ya |
| Query/read side boleh memakai DTO projection | Ya |
| Mapper interface tetap SQL-oriented | Ya |
| Mapper XML tidak berisi business orchestration kompleks | Ya |
| Outbox/audit ditulis dalam transaksi yang sama dengan command utama | Ya |
| External call tidak dilakukan di dalam DB transaction | Ya |

### Red flags

```text id="xtcixg"
@Path resource membuka sqlSessionFactory.openSession()
Mapper dipakai langsung dari banyak resource
Domain object import org.apache.ibatis.*
Repository menyimpan SqlSession sebagai singleton field
Satu DTO dipakai sebagai request, domain, DB row, dan response sekaligus
```

---

## 5. SqlSession & Transaction Lifecycle Checklist

| Checklist | Expected |
|---|---:|
| `SqlSessionFactory` dibuat sekali saat startup | Ya |
| `SqlSession` dibuat per use case/request/job batch | Ya |
| `SqlSession` selalu ditutup dengan `try-with-resources` | Ya |
| Commit hanya setelah semua operasi use case sukses | Ya |
| Rollback pada exception | Ya |
| Mapper proxy tidak keluar dari scope `SqlSession` | Ya |
| Cursor tidak dikembalikan keluar dari session scope | Ya |
| Batch executor selalu di-flush/commit dengan jelas | Ya |
| Transaction timeout/statement timeout tersedia untuk operasi mahal | Ya |
| Transaction duration dimonitor | Ya |

### Standard unit of work

```java id="a8z45p"
public <T> T transactional(MyBatisWork<T> work) {
    try (SqlSession session = sqlSessionFactory.openSession(false)) {
        try {
            T result = work.execute(new MyBatisSessionContext(session));
            session.commit();
            return result;
        } catch (RuntimeException | Error e) {
            session.rollback();
            throw e;
        }
    }
}
```

**Review question:**  
“Kalau operation ini gagal di tengah, apakah semua write ikut rollback?”

---

## 6. Mapper XML Quality Gate

| Checklist | Expected |
|---|---:|
| Namespace mapper XML sesuai mapper interface | Ya |
| Statement ID jelas dan intent-revealing | Ya |
| Parameter memakai `@Param` jika lebih dari satu argumen | Ya |
| `resultMap` eksplisit untuk object penting | Ya |
| DTO projection memakai alias kolom yang jelas | Ya |
| Tidak memakai `SELECT *` untuk production endpoint | Ya |
| Semua query tenant-owned punya `tenant_id` predicate | Ya |
| Update/delete punya tenant predicate atau admin guard | Ya |
| Update state memakai status/version guard jika perlu | Ya |
| Affected row count dicek untuk optimistic locking | Ya |
| Dynamic SQL branch punya test | Ya |
| Mapper method tidak terlalu generic seperti `execute(String sql)` | Ya |

MyBatis Mapper XML memang dirancang agar SQL tetap eksplisit dan dekat dengan mapped statement, jadi review mapper harus memperlakukan XML sebagai **production SQL contract**, bukan detail kecil yang bisa dibiarkan tanpa test. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/sqlmap-xml.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 7. Dynamic SQL Security Gate

| Checklist | Expected |
|---|---:|
| Semua user value memakai `#{}` | Ya |
| `${}` hanya dipakai untuk identifier yang sudah allow-listed | Ya |
| Sort field berasal dari enum whitelist | Ya |
| Sort direction berasal dari enum whitelist | Ya |
| Filter API eksplisit, bukan arbitrary `field/operator/value` liar | Ya |
| Limit dibatasi | Ya |
| Date range mahal dibatasi | Ya |
| `IN` list punya max size | Ya |
| Keyword search punya max length dan escaping | Ya |
| Tidak ada raw SQL dari request | Ya |
| Semua `${}` terdokumentasi di PR review | Ya |

OWASP menempatkan prepared statement/parameterized query sebagai defense utama terhadap SQL injection, dan allow-list validation sebagai pendekatan untuk bagian SQL yang tidak bisa diparameterisasi seperti nama kolom atau arah sorting. ([OWASP Cheat Sheet Series](https://cheatsheetseries.owasp.org/cheatsheets/SQL_Injection_Prevention_Cheat_Sheet.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Mandatory review rule

```text id="7k4s1r"
Setiap kemunculan ${} di mapper XML harus dijawab:

1. Ini untuk apa?
2. Apakah bisa diganti #{}
3. Kalau tidak bisa, enum/allow-list-nya di mana?
4. Apakah ada test injection/regression?
```

---

## 8. Transaction Boundary Review

| Scenario | Required Design |
|---|---|
| Single aggregate command | One transaction |
| Multi-table write | One transaction |
| Business row + audit | Same transaction |
| Business row + outbox | Same transaction |
| DB write + external API | Outbox/saga, bukan external call dalam transaction |
| Cross-datasource write | Saga/outbox/idempotency |
| Retryable DB error | Retry full transaction only |
| Batch import | Chunk boundary explicit |
| Long export | Read-only cursor/async job, not huge transaction |
| Stored procedure call | Java service tetap jelas sebagai transaction owner kecuali maintenance top-level |

### Review questions

```text id="qp4mia"
Apakah transaction boundary ada di application service?
Apakah repository diam-diam commit sendiri?
Apakah ada external call di dalam transaction?
Apakah retry mengulang full use case?
Apakah partial commit mungkin terjadi?
```

---

## 9. Concurrency & Idempotency Review

| Checklist | Expected |
|---|---:|
| State transition memakai optimistic locking atau row lock | Ya |
| Affected row `0` diterjemahkan menjadi conflict | Ya |
| Deadlock/serialization failure punya retry policy terbatas | Ya |
| Lock ordering deterministic untuk multi-row update | Ya |
| `FOR UPDATE` dipakai hanya saat memang perlu | Ya |
| Queue worker memakai claim pattern yang aman | Ya |
| Idempotency key tersedia untuk POST/command retryable | Ya |
| Idempotency key scoped by tenant/client | Ya |
| Same key + different payload ditolak | Ya |
| Duplicate side effect dicegah dengan outbox/inbox/idempotency | Ya |

PostgreSQL menjelaskan isolation level dan locking behavior secara eksplisit; Serializable/Repeatable Read dapat memerlukan retry pada konflik tertentu, sementara explicit locking tersedia untuk application-controlled locking. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/transaction-iso.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 10. PostgreSQL Schema & Index Review

| Checklist | Expected |
|---|---:|
| Primary key jelas | Ya |
| Business key punya unique constraint | Ya |
| Tenant-owned unique key memasukkan `tenant_id` | Ya |
| FK/check/not-null constraint menegakkan invariant penting | Ya |
| Query utama punya index berdasarkan access pattern | Ya |
| Index tidak dibuat asal untuk semua kolom | Ya |
| Partial index dipakai untuk status/predicate stabil seperti outbox pending | Bila perlu |
| Query pagination punya deterministic ordering | Ya |
| Keyset pagination dipakai untuk high-volume scrolling | Bila perlu |
| JSONB search punya index jika query sering | Bila perlu |
| `EXPLAIN` baseline tersedia untuk query kritis | Ya |
| `ANALYZE`/statistics dipahami saat query plan buruk | Ya |

PostgreSQL `EXPLAIN` digunakan untuk melihat query plan yang dipilih planner; `EXPLAIN ANALYZE` benar-benar menjalankan query, sehingga data-changing statement harus dianalisis hati-hati, misalnya di dalam transaksi lalu rollback. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/using-explain.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 11. Migration & Schema Evolution Gate

| Checklist | Expected |
|---|---:|
| Migration tool jelas: Flyway atau Liquibase | Ya |
| Runtime DB user berbeda dari migration DB user | Ya |
| Migration dijalankan oleh pipeline/job, bukan semua pod sembarang | Ya |
| Additive migration dilakukan sebelum app butuh schema baru | Ya |
| Rename/drop/type-change memakai expand-contract | Ya |
| Backfill chunked dan resumable | Ya |
| `CREATE INDEX CONCURRENTLY` dipisahkan jika perlu | Ya |
| Constraint besar memakai `NOT VALID` lalu validate jika perlu | Ya |
| Mapper compatibility test berjalan setelah migration | Ya |
| Contract/drop dilakukan setelah rollback window aman | Ya |
| Migration drift dicek di CI/CD | Ya |

### Expand-contract reminder

```text id="phb98z"
Expand:
  add new nullable column/table/index/constraint-compatible

Deploy:
  app reads/writes old + new safely

Backfill:
  chunked, idempotent, observable

Switch:
  read path moves to new schema

Contract:
  drop old schema only after all versions safe
```

---

## 12. Security & Data Protection Review

| Checklist | Expected |
|---|---:|
| SQL injection prevention memakai `#{}` | Ya |
| Dynamic identifier memakai whitelist | Ya |
| Runtime DB role least privilege | Ya |
| Migration role terpisah | Ya |
| Tenant predicate mandatory | Ya |
| Cross-tenant admin query explicit + audited | Ya |
| Sensitive endpoint punya audit trail | Ya |
| Audit payload tidak menyimpan secret/PII mentah | Ya |
| SQL DEBUG production off by default | Ya |
| Logs mask token/password/PII | Ya |
| Error response tidak mengekspos raw SQL/constraint detail | Ya |
| Secrets tidak ada di Git/image/log | Ya |
| DB TLS/SSL policy jelas untuk environment non-trusted | Ya |

### High-risk smells

```text id="n24kuf"
ORDER BY ${request.sort}
WHERE id = #{id} pada tenant-owned table tanpa tenant_id
Runtime DB user bisa ALTER/DROP table
Log berisi request body lengkap dengan token/PII
Admin mapper dipakai endpoint biasa
```

---

## 13. TypeHandler & PostgreSQL-Specific Mapping Review

| Area | Checklist |
|---|---|
| UUID | Java `UUID` mapping tested |
| JSONB | Custom TypeHandler round-trip tested |
| Enum | DB enum/text enum mapping consistent |
| Array | TypeHandler jelas jika dipakai |
| Timestamp | `Instant`/timezone handling tested |
| Numeric money/amount | `BigDecimal`, scale/rounding policy jelas |
| Nullable DB field | Java wrapper/nullable model, bukan primitive |
| Value object | Converter/TypeHandler tested |
| Large JSON payload | Tidak over-fetch di list endpoint |

### Review question

```text id="4h00ld"
Kalau data ini disimpan lalu dibaca ulang dari PostgreSQL nyata,
apakah nilainya identik secara domain?
```

---

## 14. Performance Review Gate

| Checklist | Expected |
|---|---:|
| Endpoint list memakai projection DTO | Ya |
| Tidak ada N+1 query di path utama | Ya |
| Query high-volume punya pagination aman | Ya |
| Export besar memakai cursor/fetchSize atau async job | Ya |
| Batch write memakai chunking | Ya |
| `ExecutorType.BATCH` dipakai hanya untuk write batch | Ya |
| `fetchSize`/cursor tidak menahan connection terlalu lama tanpa kontrol | Ya |
| `count(*)` tidak otomatis untuk semua search | Ya |
| Query kritis punya EXPLAIN evidence | Ya |
| Pool size ditentukan berdasarkan workload dan DB capacity | Ya |
| Slow query metric tersedia | Ya |
| Query timeout/statement timeout tersedia | Ya |

HikariCP mengatur batas concurrency DB melalui pool; `maximumPoolSize` membatasi koneksi maksimum dan `connectionTimeout` menentukan berapa lama caller menunggu connection sebelum gagal. Karena itu, menaikkan pool tanpa memperbaiki query/lock/transaction bisa memperparah tekanan ke database. ([GitHub](https://github.com/brettwooldridge/hikaricp?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 15. Testing Coverage Gate

| Test Type | Required For |
|---|---|
| Domain unit test | State transition, invariant |
| Service unit test | Orchestration, authz, retry decision |
| Mapper integration test | SQL/resultMap/TypeHandler |
| Migration test | Empty DB migrate + mapper compatibility |
| Constraint negative test | Unique/FK/check/not-null |
| Transaction rollback test | Multi-write atomicity |
| Concurrency test | Optimistic lock, idempotency, queue claim |
| API integration test | HTTP contract, error mapping |
| Security test | Injection, tenant isolation |
| Performance smoke | Query critical path |
| Deployment smoke | Health/readiness/schema version |

### Minimum production baseline

```text id="k4zaem"
Every important mapper:
  at least one integration test against PostgreSQL.

Every important command:
  unit test + transaction/integration test.

Every schema migration:
  migration test.

Every tenant-owned query:
  cross-tenant negative test.
```

---

## 16. Observability Review Gate

| Checklist | Expected |
|---|---:|
| Every request has correlation ID | Ya |
| Mapper statement ID appears in logs/metrics | Ya |
| SQLSTATE classification exists | Ya |
| Hikari active/idle/pending/acquire metrics exported | Ya |
| Transaction duration measured | Ya |
| Slow mapper metric exists | Ya |
| Retry/deadlock/serialization metric exists | Ya |
| Outbox backlog metric exists if outbox used | Ya |
| Audit command has traceable event | Ya |
| `pg_stat_activity` runbook exists | Ya |
| `pg_stat_statements` enabled where allowed | Ya |
| Dashboard separates API, DB, pool, business workflow | Ya |

PostgreSQL exposes `pg_stat_activity` for current backend activity and `pg_stat_statements` for aggregated planning/execution statistics, which makes them core tools for diagnosing runtime query behavior. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/monitoring-stats.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 17. Runtime & Kubernetes Readiness Gate

| Checklist | Expected |
|---|---:|
| Docker image multi-stage | Ya |
| Container runs non-root | Ya |
| Config fail-fast | Ya |
| Secrets from runtime secret manager/Kubernetes Secret | Ya |
| HikariDataSource singleton and closed on shutdown | Ya |
| SqlSessionFactory singleton | Ya |
| Startup/readiness/liveness probes separated | Ya |
| Readiness checks app + DB/schema minimally | Ya |
| Liveness is shallow, not heavy DB check | Ya |
| Graceful shutdown marks not-ready first | Ya |
| Background workers stop before datasource close | Ya |
| Migration runs as job/pipeline | Ya |
| Rolling update strategy safe | Ya |
| Resource requests/limits tested | Ya |

Kubernetes distinguishes startup, readiness, and liveness probes; startup probes can delay liveness/readiness until startup succeeds, readiness controls traffic routing, and liveness can restart unhealthy containers. ([Kubernetes](https://kubernetes.io/docs/concepts/workloads/pods/probes/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 18. Multi-DB / Replica / Tenant Routing Gate

| Checklist | Expected |
|---|---:|
| Separate SqlSessionFactory per datasource role | Ya |
| Command/write always goes primary | Ya |
| Read-after-write goes primary | Ya |
| Replica read explicitly eventual | Ya |
| Replica lag monitored if replica used | Ya |
| Read-only DB credential for replica/reporting | Ya |
| Tenant ID from authenticated context | Ya |
| Schema/database tenant routing validated | Ya |
| Cross-datasource transaction not assumed atomic | Ya |
| Outbox/saga used for cross-DB sync | Ya |
| Metrics tagged by datasource role | Ya |

### Red flag

```text id="7qs0hb"
Service menulis ke DB A dan DB B dalam nested unitOfWork,
lalu menganggapnya atomic.
```

---

## 19. Stored Procedure / PL/pgSQL Boundary Gate

| Checklist | Expected |
|---|---:|
| Function/procedure signature treated as API contract | Ya |
| MyBatis callable statement has explicit `mode` and `jdbcType` | Ya |
| OUT params read and checked | Ya |
| Procedure does not hide application transaction boundary | Ya |
| Java service still handles authorization | Ya |
| PL/pgSQL exceptions use classifiable SQLSTATE | Ya |
| `SECURITY DEFINER` has fixed `search_path` | Ya |
| EXECUTE grants least-privilege | Ya |
| Routine migration/versioning tested | Ya |
| Routine integration test exists | Ya |

## 20. PR Review Template for Mapper Change

Gunakan template ini untuk setiap PR yang mengubah mapper XML, mapper interface, migration, atau repository.

```text id="rpz1jq"
Mapper / SQL Review

1. What mapper statements changed?
2. Is this command, query, batch, procedure, or migration-related?
3. Does every tenant-owned query include tenant_id?
4. Are all user values bound with #{}
5. Is every ${} backed by enum/allow-list?
6. Is resultMap/projection tested?
7. Does update/delete check affected row when needed?
8. Does this change need a new/updated index?
9. Does this change affect transaction boundary?
10. Does this change affect migration compatibility?
11. Are tests added/updated?
12. Are logs/metrics safe and useful?
```

## 21. Architecture Review Questions for Onboarding

Saat masuk codebase baru, tanyakan ini:

```text id="wh1uxu"
1. Di mana SqlSessionFactory dibuat?

3. Di mana transaction boundary utama?

10. Bagaimana outbox/audit ditulis?
11. Bagaimana SQLSTATE diklasifikasi?
12. Bagaimana slow query didiagnosis?
13. Apakah pg_stat_statements aktif?
14. Bagaimana pool metrics dipantau?
15. Bagaimana graceful shutdown dilakukan?
16. Bagaimana rollback saat schema berubah?
17. Bagaimana read replica digunakan?
18. Apakah ada mapper integration test?
```

## 22. Go / No-Go Release Checklist

### Go

```text id="fjlksq"
Release boleh lanjut jika:
- migration sudah tested
- mapper integration test pass
- no known SQL injection/dynamic SQL gap
- transaction boundary reviewed
- critical query performance checked
- readiness/liveness sane
- rollback/forward-fix plan clear
- observability dashboard ready
```

### No-Go

```text id="x67h5o"
Tahan release jika:
- migration destructive tanpa expand-contract
- mapper baru belum dites against PostgreSQL
- tenant predicate hilang
- raw ${requestParam} muncul
- app requires schema that is not yet deployed
- write path has partial commit risk
- outbox/audit consistency unclear
- production cannot diagnose pool/query failure
```

---

## 23. Severity-Based Hardening Priority

| Severity | Fix First |
|---|---|
| P0 | SQL injection risk, tenant data leak, destructive migration risk |
| P1 | Partial commit, missing rollback, missing idempotency on critical command |
| P2 | Missing mapper integration test, poor error mapping |
| P3 | Slow query without EXPLAIN baseline, missing pool metric |
| P4 | Naming/style cleanup, minor layering refinement |

**Rule:** jangan habiskan waktu merapikan style mapper jika masih ada raw `${}` dari request atau missing tenant predicate.

---

## 24. Final Anti-Pattern Catalog

| Anti-Pattern | Why Dangerous |
|---|---|
| `Resource -> SqlSession -> Mapper` | boundary dan transaction kacau |
| Singleton `SqlSession` | thread-safety/resource bug |
| Mapper accepts raw request DTO | HTTP/persistence coupling |
| `SELECT *` everywhere | over-fetch + data exposure |
| Raw `${}` from request | SQL injection |
| Missing tenant predicate | cross-tenant leak |
| No affected row check | lost update |
| External call inside transaction | lock lama + duplicate side effect |
| Outbox outside business transaction | inconsistent integration |
| App pod runs migration concurrently | migration race |
| Drop/rename column directly | rolling deploy broken |
| No PostgreSQL integration test | SQL bugs runtime-only |
| Liveness checks DB | restart storm |
| Pool size raised blindly | DB overload |
| No SQLSTATE classifier | retry/error mapping wrong |
| No pg diagnostics access | blind incident response |

---

## 25. Final Production Definition of Done

Sebuah MyBatis-based service bisa disebut production-ready jika minimal memenuhi ini:

```text id="3w2r4y"
Architecture:
  clear resource-service-repository-mapper boundary

Persistence:
  SqlSession lifecycle correct
  transaction boundary explicit
  mapper XML reviewed and tested

Security:
  no unsafe dynamic SQL
  tenant filtering enforced
  least-privilege DB roles
  sensitive data masked

Correctness:
  constraints exist
  idempotency exists for retryable commands
  concurrency conflicts handled

Performance:
  critical queries have index/EXPLAIN evidence
  batch/streaming used for large data
  pool metrics monitored

Migration:
  expand-contract for breaking changes
  migration compatibility tested

Operations:
  health probes sane
  graceful shutdown works
  runbooks exist
  observability available
```

## Response:
7/15/2026, 1:21:34 PM

Saya akan rangkum sebagai cheatsheet praktis: elemen `mybatis-config.xml`, opsi penting, contoh XML, padanan Java programmatic setup, plus catatan mana yang sebaiknya dipakai di production.

# Cheatsheet - MyBatis XML Config vs Programmatic Java Setup

MyBatis punya dua area konfigurasi yang sering tertukar:

| Area | File/Tempat | Isi |
|---|---|---|
| **Global configuration** | `mybatis-config.xml` atau `org.apache.ibatis.session.Configuration` | settings global, datasource, transaction manager, type aliases, type handlers, plugins, mappers |
| **Mapper XML** | `XxxMapper.xml` | SQL statement: `select`, `insert`, `update`, `delete`, `resultMap`, `cache`, dynamic SQL |

MyBatis mendokumentasikan struktur global config sebagai `properties`, `settings`, `typeAliases`, `typeHandlers`, `objectFactory`, `plugins`, `environments`, `databaseIdProvider`, dan `mappers`. `SqlSessionFactoryBuilder` bisa membuat `SqlSessionFactory` dari XML config atau langsung dari object `Configuration`. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/configuration.html))

---

# 1. Minimal XML Config vs Programmatic Setup

## 1.1 XML style

```xml id="d8yp50"
<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8" ?>
<!DOCTYPE configuration
  PUBLIC "-//mybatis.org//DTD Config 3.0//EN"
  "https://mybatis.org/dtd/mybatis-3-config.dtd">

<configuration>

  <settings>
    <setting name="mapUnderscoreToCamelCase" value="true"/>
    <setting name="logImpl" value="SLF4J"/>
    <setting name="localCacheScope" value="STATEMENT"/>
    <setting name="defaultExecutorType" value="SIMPLE"/>
    <setting name="defaultStatementTimeout" value="10"/>
    <setting name="defaultFetchSize" value="500"/>
    <setting name="jdbcTypeForNull" value="OTHER"/>
  </settings>

  <typeAliases>
    <package name="com.company.app.domain"/>
    <package name="com.company.app.dto"/>
  </typeAliases>

  <typeHandlers>
    <typeHandler handler="com.company.app.mybatis.JsonNodeJsonbTypeHandler"/>
    <typeHandler handler="com.company.app.mybatis.PostgresEnumTypeHandler"/>
  </typeHandlers>

  <environments default="production">
    <environment id="production">
      <transactionManager type="JDBC">
        <property name="skipSetAutoCommitOnClose" value="true"/>
      </transactionManager>

      <dataSource type="POOLED">
        <property name="driver" value="${db.driver}"/>
        <property name="url" value="${db.url}"/>
        <property name="username" value="${db.username}"/>
        <property name="password" value="${db.password}"/>
        <property name="poolMaximumActiveConnections" value="20"/>
      </dataSource>
    </environment>
  </environments>

  <mappers>
    <mapper resource="mapper/CaseMapper.xml"/>
    <mapper resource="mapper/CaseQueryMapper.xml"/>
    <mapper resource="mapper/AuditMapper.xml"/>
  </mappers>

</configuration>
```

## 1.2 Programmatic style - recommended untuk Jersey/HK2/Weld non-Spring

```java id="2osovj"
public final class MyBatisFactory {

    public SqlSessionFactory build(DataSource dataSource) {
        TransactionFactory transactionFactory =
            new JdbcTransactionFactory();

        Environment environment = new Environment(
            "production",
            transactionFactory,
            dataSource
        );

        Configuration configuration = new Configuration(environment);

        // settings
        configuration.setMapUnderscoreToCamelCase(true);
        configuration.setLogImpl(Slf4jImpl.class);
        configuration.setLocalCacheScope(LocalCacheScope.STATEMENT);
        configuration.setDefaultExecutorType(ExecutorType.SIMPLE);
        configuration.setDefaultStatementTimeout(10);
        configuration.setDefaultFetchSize(500);
        configuration.setJdbcTypeForNull(JdbcType.OTHER);
        configuration.setAutoMappingBehavior(AutoMappingBehavior.PARTIAL);
        configuration.setAutoMappingUnknownColumnBehavior(
            AutoMappingUnknownColumnBehavior.WARNING
        );
        configuration.setUseGeneratedKeys(false);
        configuration.setUseColumnLabel(true);
        configuration.setLazyLoadingEnabled(false);
        configuration.setAggressiveLazyLoading(false);
        configuration.setCallSettersOnNulls(false);
        configuration.setReturnInstanceForEmptyRow(false);
        configuration.setUseActualParamName(true);

        // type aliases
        configuration.getTypeAliasRegistry()
            .registerAliases("com.company.app.domain");
        configuration.getTypeAliasRegistry()
            .registerAliases("com.company.app.dto");

        // type handlers
        configuration.getTypeHandlerRegistry()
            .register(JsonNode.class, JdbcType.OTHER, JsonNodeJsonbTypeHandler.class);
        configuration.getTypeHandlerRegistry()
            .register(PostgresEnumTypeHandler.class);

        // interceptors/plugins
        configuration.addInterceptor(new SqlTimingInterceptor());

        // mappers
        configuration.addMapper(CaseMapper.class);
        configuration.addMapper(CaseQueryMapper.class);
        configuration.addMapper(AuditMapper.class);

        return new SqlSessionFactoryBuilder().build(configuration);
    }
}
```

Catatan penting: MyBatis menyarankan `SqlSessionFactory` hidup selama aplikasi berjalan, sedangkan `SqlSession` tidak thread-safe dan harus scope request/method, bukan singleton. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/getting-started.html))

---

# 2. Top-Level `mybatis-config.xml` Elements

| XML Element | Fungsi | Programmatic Equivalent | Catatan Production |
|---|---|---|---|
| `<properties>` | Placeholder `${...}` untuk config | `SqlSessionFactoryBuilder.build(reader, props)` atau config loader sendiri | Untuk non-Spring/Jersey, biasanya lebih aman load env/secret sendiri |
| `<settings>` | Global behavior MyBatis | `configuration.setXxx(...)` | Paling penting untuk runtime behavior |
| `<typeAliases>` | Alias class agar XML tidak verbose | `configuration.getTypeAliasRegistry().registerAlias(...)` / `registerAliases(package)` | Jangan terlalu banyak alias kalau bikin XML ambigu |
| `<typeHandlers>` | Java ↔ JDBC mapping | `configuration.getTypeHandlerRegistry().register(...)` | Wajib untuk JSONB, enum custom, array, value object |
| `<objectFactory>` | Cara MyBatis membuat result object | `configuration.setObjectFactory(...)` | Jarang perlu; gunakan hanya kalau constructor/instantiation khusus |
| `<plugins>` | Interceptor MyBatis | `configuration.addInterceptor(...)` | Cocok untuk timing/logging; jangan ubah core behavior sembarangan |
| `<environments>` | DB environment, transaction manager, datasource | `new Environment(id, transactionFactory, dataSource)` | Satu `SqlSessionFactory` hanya memilih satu environment |
| `<databaseIdProvider>` | Vendor-specific statement | `configuration.setDatabaseId(...)` atau custom provider saat build | Jarang perlu kalau hanya PostgreSQL |
| `<mappers>` | Register mapper XML/interface | `configuration.addMapper(...)` | Mapper XML peer bisa auto-loaded jika classpath/name cocok |

MyBatis menyatakan satu `SqlSessionFactory` hanya memilih satu environment; jika aplikasi connect ke dua database, buat dua `SqlSessionFactory`, satu per database. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/configuration.html))

---

# 3. `<properties>`

## XML

```xml id="j19skd"
<properties resource="mybatis/application-db.properties">
  <property name="db.driver" value="org.postgresql.Driver"/>
</properties>
```

```xml id="y8ingg"
<dataSource type="POOLED">
  <property name="driver" value="${db.driver}"/>
  <property name="url" value="${db.url}"/>
  <property name="username" value="${db.username}"/>
  <property name="password" value="${db.password}"/>
</dataSource>
```

MyBatis property precedence adalah: property body dibaca dulu, lalu property dari `resource`/`url` override, lalu properties yang dikirim ke `SqlSessionFactoryBuilder.build(..., props)` punya prioritas tertinggi. MyBatis juga mendukung default value placeholder sejak 3.4.2, tetapi fitur itu disabled by default dan perlu property parser khusus. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/configuration.html))

## Programmatic

```java id="mbbi7j"
Properties props = new Properties();
props.setProperty("db.url", System.getenv("DB_URL"));
props.setProperty("db.username", System.getenv("DB_USERNAME"));
props.setProperty("db.password", System.getenv("DB_PASSWORD"));

try (InputStream in = Resources.getResourceAsStream("mybatis-config.xml")) {
    SqlSessionFactory factory =
        new SqlSessionFactoryBuilder().build(in, "production", props);
}
```

## Programmatic tanpa XML

```java id="3xsfqp"
RuntimeConfig runtimeConfig = RuntimeConfig.loadFromEnv();

DataSource dataSource = createHikariDataSource(runtimeConfig);

Environment environment = new Environment(
    "production",
    new JdbcTransactionFactory(),
    dataSource
);

Configuration configuration = new Configuration(environment);
```

**Practical recommendation:** untuk production Kubernetes/cloud, jangan taruh password di `mybatis-config.xml`. Ambil dari secret/env/external config lalu buat `DataSource` programmatically.

---

# 4. `<settings>` Complete Practical Cheat Sheet

## 4.1 Core runtime settings

| XML Setting | Programmatic Setter | Default | Practical Recommendation |
|---|---|---:|---|
| `cacheEnabled` | `setCacheEnabled(boolean)` | `true` | Biasanya tetap `true`, tapi hati-hati dengan 2nd-level cache |
| `lazyLoadingEnabled` | `setLazyLoadingEnabled(boolean)` | `false` | Untuk microservice/API, biasanya `false` |
| `aggressiveLazyLoading` | `setAggressiveLazyLoading(boolean)` | `false` | Biasanya `false` |
| `useColumnLabel` | `setUseColumnLabel(boolean)` | `true` | Tetap `true`; gunakan alias SQL jelas |
| `useGeneratedKeys` | `setUseGeneratedKeys(boolean)` | `false` | Untuk PostgreSQL UUID app-generated biasanya `false`; serial identity bisa per statement |
| `defaultExecutorType` | `setDefaultExecutorType(ExecutorType.SIMPLE)` | `SIMPLE` | `SIMPLE` default; `BATCH` jangan global |
| `defaultStatementTimeout` | `setDefaultStatementTimeout(Integer)` | unset | Set 5-30 detik sesuai SLA |
| `defaultFetchSize` | `setDefaultFetchSize(Integer)` | unset | Berguna untuk query besar/cursor; validasi pgJDBC behavior |
| `defaultResultSetType` | `setDefaultResultSetType(ResultSetType.FORWARD_ONLY)` | unset | Biasanya `FORWARD_ONLY` untuk streaming |
| `mapUnderscoreToCamelCase` | `setMapUnderscoreToCamelCase(boolean)` | `false` | Sering `true` untuk PostgreSQL `snake_case` → Java `camelCase` |
| `localCacheScope` | `setLocalCacheScope(LocalCacheScope.STATEMENT)` | `SESSION` | Untuk service request transactional, sering lebih aman `STATEMENT` |
| `jdbcTypeForNull` | `setJdbcTypeForNull(JdbcType.OTHER)` | `OTHER` | Untuk PostgreSQL sering tetap `OTHER`; eksplisitkan `jdbcType` pada field penting |

MyBatis mendokumentasikan `defaultExecutorType` dengan `SIMPLE`, `REUSE`, dan `BATCH`; `defaultStatementTimeout`, `defaultFetchSize`, `defaultResultSetType`, `mapUnderscoreToCamelCase`, `localCacheScope`, dan `jdbcTypeForNull` adalah global settings yang memengaruhi runtime behavior query/mapper. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/configuration.html))

## 4.2 Auto-mapping settings

| XML Setting | Programmatic Setter | Default | Recommendation |
|---|---|---:|---|
| `autoMappingBehavior` | `setAutoMappingBehavior(AutoMappingBehavior.PARTIAL)` | `PARTIAL` | `PARTIAL` aman; hindari `FULL` untuk join kompleks |
| `autoMappingUnknownColumnBehavior` | `setAutoMappingUnknownColumnBehavior(...)` | `NONE` | `WARNING` bagus untuk staging/dev; `FAILING` bisa untuk strict integration test |
| `callSettersOnNulls` | `setCallSettersOnNulls(boolean)` | `false` | `false`; gunakan wrapper type untuk nullable |
| `returnInstanceForEmptyRow` | `setReturnInstanceForEmptyRow(boolean)` | `false` | Biasanya `false` |

MyBatis menjelaskan `PARTIAL` sebagai default auto-mapping dan memberi peringatan bahwa `FULL` pada join/nested result dapat menghasilkan mapping yang tidak diinginkan. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/sqlmap-xml.html))

## 4.3 Logging/proxy/provider settings

| XML Setting | Programmatic Setter | Default | Recommendation |
|---|---|---:|---|
| `logImpl` | `setLogImpl(Slf4jImpl.class)` | autodetect | Gunakan `SLF4J` di production |
| `logPrefix` | `setLogPrefix(String)` | unset | Opsional |
| `proxyFactory` | `setProxyFactory(...)` | `JAVASSIST` | Jarang disentuh |
| `vfsImpl` | `setVfsImpl(Class<? extends VFS>)` | unset | Hanya jika environment classpath butuh custom VFS |
| `useActualParamName` | `setUseActualParamName(boolean)` | `true` | Tetap `true`, tapi tetap gunakan `@Param` untuk mapper multi-arg |
| `configurationFactory` | `setConfigurationFactory(Class<?>)` | unset | Rare; lazy deserialization use case |
| `shrinkWhitespacesInSql` | `setShrinkWhitespacesInSql(boolean)` | `false` | Hati-hati, bisa memengaruhi literal string SQL |
| `defaultSqlProviderType` | `setDefaultSqlProviderType(Class<?>)` | unset | Untuk annotation provider style |
| `nullableOnForEach` | `setNullableOnForEach(Boolean)` | `false` | Biasanya `false`; handle list kosong di service |
| `argNameBasedConstructorAutoMapping` | `setArgNameBasedConstructorAutoMapping(boolean)` | `false` | Bisa berguna untuk record/constructor mapping jika compiled dengan parameter names |

MyBatis mendukung beberapa logging implementation seperti `SLF4J`, `LOG4J2`, `JDK_LOGGING`, `COMMONS_LOGGING`, `STDOUT_LOGGING`, dan `NO_LOGGING`; `LOG4J` lama sudah deprecated sejak 3.5.9. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/configuration.html))

---

# 5. Recommended Production Settings untuk PostgreSQL/Jersey

```xml id="lh3oqy"
<settings>
  <setting name="mapUnderscoreToCamelCase" value="true"/>
  <setting name="autoMappingBehavior" value="PARTIAL"/>
  <setting name="autoMappingUnknownColumnBehavior" value="WARNING"/>

  <setting name="lazyLoadingEnabled" value="false"/>
  <setting name="aggressiveLazyLoading" value="false"/>

  <setting name="defaultExecutorType" value="SIMPLE"/>
  <setting name="defaultStatementTimeout" value="10"/>
  <setting name="defaultFetchSize" value="500"/>
  <setting name="defaultResultSetType" value="FORWARD_ONLY"/>

  <setting name="localCacheScope" value="STATEMENT"/>
  <setting name="jdbcTypeForNull" value="OTHER"/>

  <setting name="useColumnLabel" value="true"/>
  <setting name="useGeneratedKeys" value="false"/>
  <setting name="useActualParamName" value="true"/>

  <setting name="logImpl" value="SLF4J"/>
</settings>
```

Programmatic equivalent:

```java id="q4chsp"
configuration.setMapUnderscoreToCamelCase(true);
configuration.setAutoMappingBehavior(AutoMappingBehavior.PARTIAL);
configuration.setAutoMappingUnknownColumnBehavior(
    AutoMappingUnknownColumnBehavior.WARNING
);

configuration.setLazyLoadingEnabled(false);
configuration.setAggressiveLazyLoading(false);

configuration.setDefaultExecutorType(ExecutorType.SIMPLE);
configuration.setDefaultStatementTimeout(10);
configuration.setDefaultFetchSize(500);
configuration.setDefaultResultSetType(ResultSetType.FORWARD_ONLY);

configuration.setLocalCacheScope(LocalCacheScope.STATEMENT);
configuration.setJdbcTypeForNull(JdbcType.OTHER);

configuration.setUseColumnLabel(true);
configuration.setUseGeneratedKeys(false);
configuration.setUseActualParamName(true);

configuration.setLogImpl(Slf4jImpl.class);
```

---

# 6. `<typeAliases>`

## XML

```xml id="xgyp8d"
<typeAliases>
  <typeAlias alias="CaseRecord"
             type="com.company.app.persistence.record.CaseRecord"/>
  <package name="com.company.app.dto"/>
</typeAliases>
```

MyBatis type alias hanya nama pendek untuk Java type dan terutama berguna agar XML tidak perlu menulis fully-qualified class name. Jika package scan dipakai, class tanpa `@Alias` akan didaftarkan dengan uncapitalized simple class name. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/configuration.html))

## Programmatic

```java id="y3be8h"
configuration.getTypeAliasRegistry()
    .registerAlias("CaseRecord", CaseRecord.class);

configuration.getTypeAliasRegistry()
    .registerAliases("com.company.app.dto");
```

## Practical recommendation

| Use Case | Recommendation |
|---|---|
| DTO/projection banyak | Package alias boleh |
| Domain object penting | Alias eksplisit lebih readable |
| Banyak class dengan nama sama | Hindari package alias luas |
| Shared library | Pakai FQCN atau alias eksplisit |

---

# 7. `<typeHandlers>`

## XML

```xml id="5frq3v"
<typeHandlers>
  <typeHandler
      javaType="com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.JsonNode"
      jdbcType="OTHER"
      handler="com.company.app.mybatis.JsonNodeJsonbTypeHandler"/>

  <typeHandler handler="com.company.app.mybatis.PostgresEnumTypeHandler"/>

  <package name="com.company.app.mybatis.typehandler"/>
</typeHandlers>
```

MyBatis memakai `TypeHandler` saat set parameter ke `PreparedStatement` dan saat mengambil value dari `ResultSet`; MyBatis juga mendukung Java Time API/JSR-310 by default sejak 3.4.5. Untuk custom type, handler bisa diregister dengan XML atau autodiscovery package, tetapi JDBC type autodiscovery perlu annotation. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/configuration.html))

## Programmatic

```java id="bavh5f"
TypeHandlerRegistry registry = configuration.getTypeHandlerRegistry();

registry.register(
    JsonNode.class,
    JdbcType.OTHER,
    JsonNodeJsonbTypeHandler.class
);

registry.register(PostgresEnumTypeHandler.class);

registry.register("com.company.app.mybatis.typehandler");
```

## Mapper usage

```xml id="ezk9zr"
<result property="payload"
        column="payload"
        jdbcType="OTHER"
        typeHandler="com.company.app.mybatis.JsonNodeJsonbTypeHandler"/>
```

```xml id="kz81o5"
#{payload, jdbcType=OTHER,
  typeHandler=com.company.app.mybatis.JsonNodeJsonbTypeHandler}
```

## PostgreSQL-specific reminder

| PostgreSQL Type | Java Type | Typical Handling |
|---|---|---|
| `uuid` | `UUID` | built-in/driver support |
| `jsonb` | `JsonNode`, POJO, `Map` | custom TypeHandler |
| `text enum` | Java enum | `EnumTypeHandler` or custom |
| native enum | Java enum | custom handler often needed |
| `timestamptz` | `Instant`, `OffsetDateTime` | test round-trip carefully |
| array | `List<T>` / array | custom handler often needed |
| `numeric` | `BigDecimal` | built-in |

---
