# 8. `<objectFactory>`

## XML

```xml id="wgfaf1"
<objectFactory type="com.company.app.mybatis.CustomObjectFactory">
  <property name="someProperty" value="100"/>
</objectFactory>
```

MyBatis `ObjectFactory` dipakai setiap kali MyBatis membuat result object; default-nya membuat object via default constructor atau constructor yang diparameterisasi jika ada constructor mapping. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/configuration.html))

## Programmatic

```java id="pktq02"
CustomObjectFactory objectFactory = new CustomObjectFactory();

Properties properties = new Properties();
properties.setProperty("someProperty", "100");
objectFactory.setProperties(properties);

configuration.setObjectFactory(objectFactory);
```

## Practical recommendation

Jarang perlu. Untuk Java record/immutable object, lebih sering cukup pakai constructor mapping/resultMap daripada custom `ObjectFactory`.

---

# 9. `<plugins>` / Interceptors

## XML

```xml id="jbdw4a"
<plugins>
  <plugin interceptor="com.company.app.mybatis.SqlTimingInterceptor">
    <property name="slowQueryMs" value="500"/>
  </plugin>
</plugins>
```

MyBatis plugin bisa intercept `Executor`, `ParameterHandler`, `ResultSetHandler`, dan `StatementHandler`. Dokumentasi MyBatis memperingatkan bahwa ini low-level extension point, jadi plugin harus digunakan hati-hati karena bisa mematahkan core behavior jika mengubah flow secara salah. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/configuration.html))

## Programmatic

```java id="7jd7tc"
SqlTimingInterceptor interceptor = new SqlTimingInterceptor();

Properties props = new Properties();
props.setProperty("slowQueryMs", "500");
interceptor.setProperties(props);

configuration.addInterceptor(interceptor);
```

## Example interceptor skeleton

```java id="w2idry"
@Intercepts({
    @Signature(
        type = Executor.class,
        method = "query",
        args = {
            MappedStatement.class,
            Object.class,
            RowBounds.class,
            ResultHandler.class
        }
    ),
    @Signature(
        type = Executor.class,
        method = "update",
        args = {
            MappedStatement.class,
            Object.class
        }
    )
})
public final class SqlTimingInterceptor implements Interceptor {

    @Override
    public Object intercept(Invocation invocation) throws Throwable {
        long start = System.nanoTime();

        try {
            return invocation.proceed();
        } finally {
            long durationMs =
                TimeUnit.NANOSECONDS.toMillis(System.nanoTime() - start);

            Object[] args = invocation.getArgs();
            MappedStatement ms = (MappedStatement) args[0];

            log.debug(
                "mybatis statement={} durationMs={}",
                ms.getId(),
                durationMs
            );
        }
    }
}
```

---

# 10. `<environments>`, Transaction Manager, DataSource

## 10.1 XML built-in transaction manager

```xml id="ocq00q"
<transactionManager type="JDBC">
  <property name="skipSetAutoCommitOnClose" value="true"/>
</transactionManager>
```

```xml id="5cd92b"
<transactionManager type="MANAGED">
  <property name="closeConnection" value="false"/>
</transactionManager>
```

MyBatis built-in transaction manager hanya ada `JDBC` dan `MANAGED`. `JDBC` memakai commit/rollback JDBC langsung; `MANAGED` tidak melakukan commit/rollback dan membiarkan container mengelola transaction lifecycle. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/configuration.html))

## Programmatic

```java id="yr8u5s"
TransactionFactory transactionFactory = new JdbcTransactionFactory();

Environment environment = new Environment(
    "production",
    transactionFactory,
    dataSource
);

Configuration configuration = new Configuration(environment);
```

Jika butuh property:

```java id="b1187l"
JdbcTransactionFactory transactionFactory = new JdbcTransactionFactory();

Properties txProps = new Properties();
txProps.setProperty("skipSetAutoCommitOnClose", "true");
transactionFactory.setProperties(txProps);
```

## 10.2 XML built-in datasource

```xml id="5f449q"
<dataSource type="UNPOOLED">
  <property name="driver" value="org.postgresql.Driver"/>
  <property name="url" value="${db.url}"/>
  <property name="username" value="${db.username}"/>
  <property name="password" value="${db.password}"/>
</dataSource>
```

```xml id="mspl60"
<dataSource type="POOLED">
  <property name="driver" value="org.postgresql.Driver"/>
  <property name="url" value="${db.url}"/>
  <property name="username" value="${db.username}"/>
  <property name="password" value="${db.password}"/>
  <property name="poolMaximumActiveConnections" value="20"/>
  <property name="poolMaximumIdleConnections" value="10"/>
  <property name="poolPingEnabled" value="true"/>
  <property name="poolPingQuery" value="SELECT 1"/>
</dataSource>
```

```xml id="pv35ov"
<dataSource type="JNDI">
  <property name="data_source" value="java:comp/env/jdbc/AppDataSource"/>
</dataSource>
```

MyBatis built-in datasource types adalah `UNPOOLED`, `POOLED`, dan `JNDI`. `UNPOOLED` membuka/menutup connection setiap request; `POOLED` memakai pool MyBatis built-in; `JNDI` mengambil datasource dari container. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/configuration.html))

## Programmatic dengan HikariCP - recommended

```java id="eegmsl"
HikariConfig hikari = new HikariConfig();
hikari.setJdbcUrl(System.getenv("DB_URL"));
hikari.setUsername(System.getenv("DB_USERNAME"));
hikari.setPassword(System.getenv("DB_PASSWORD"));
hikari.setMaximumPoolSize(20);
hikari.setConnectionTimeout(3000);
hikari.setPoolName("case-service-postgres");

DataSource dataSource = new HikariDataSource(hikari);

Environment environment = new Environment(
    "production",
    new JdbcTransactionFactory(),
    dataSource
);

Configuration configuration = new Configuration(environment);
```

**Practical recommendation:** untuk production Java service, lebih umum memakai HikariCP sebagai external `DataSource`, lalu inject ke MyBatis programmatically. Built-in `POOLED` cukup untuk simple app/test, tetapi Hikari lebih umum untuk production pool observability/tuning.

---

# 11. `<databaseIdProvider>`

## XML

```xml id="tp83yd"
<databaseIdProvider type="DB_VENDOR">
  <property name="PostgreSQL" value="postgresql"/>
  <property name="Oracle" value="oracle"/>
  <property name="SQL Server" value="sqlserver"/>
</databaseIdProvider>
```

MyBatis bisa memilih statement berdasarkan `databaseId`; provider `DB_VENDOR` memakai `DatabaseMetaData#getDatabaseProductName()` lalu bisa dimapping menjadi nama pendek. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/configuration.html))

## Mapper XML

```xml id="9zfbe5"
<select id="findNow" resultType="java.time.Instant" databaseId="postgresql">
  SELECT now()
</select>

<select id="findNow" resultType="java.time.Instant" databaseId="oracle">
  SELECT systimestamp FROM dual
</select>
```

## Programmatic

```java id="w4wyn5"
configuration.setDatabaseId("postgresql");
```

Atau custom provider saat build config:

```java id="dcf9t3"
VendorDatabaseIdProvider provider = new VendorDatabaseIdProvider();

Properties props = new Properties();
props.setProperty("PostgreSQL", "postgresql");
props.setProperty("Oracle", "oracle");

provider.setProperties(props);

String databaseId = provider.getDatabaseId(dataSource);
configuration.setDatabaseId(databaseId);
```

**Recommendation:** kalau stack hanya PostgreSQL, biasanya tidak perlu `databaseIdProvider`.

---

# 12. `<mappers>`

## XML options

```xml id="mot4gn"
<mappers>
  <mapper resource="mapper/CaseMapper.xml"/>
  <mapper url="file:///opt/app/mapper/CaseMapper.xml"/>
  <mapper class="com.company.app.persistence.mapper.CaseMapper"/>
  <package name="com.company.app.persistence.mapper"/>
</mappers>
```

## Programmatic

```java id="b5b5fy"
configuration.addMapper(CaseMapper.class);
configuration.addMapper(CaseQueryMapper.class);
configuration.addMapper(AuditMapper.class);
```

Package scan programmatic tergantung utilitas yang dipakai. Core MyBatis paling aman eksplisit `addMapper(...)`. Jika mapper interface dan XML peer berada pada classpath dengan nama/package yang sama, MyBatis dapat memuat peer XML ketika mapper class ditambahkan. Dokumentasi getting started menyebut MyBatis akan mencari dan load peer XML jika ada berdasarkan classpath dan nama mapper class. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/getting-started.html))

## Recommended layout

```text id="zi25jc"
src/main/java/com/company/app/persistence/mapper/CaseMapper.java
src/main/resources/com/company/app/persistence/mapper/CaseMapper.xml
```

Dengan layout peer seperti ini:

```java id="t5oudu"
configuration.addMapper(CaseMapper.class);
```

---

# 13. Mapper XML Statement Options

## 13.1 `<select>`

```xml id="bfkgar"
<select
    id="searchCases"
    parameterType="com.company.app.query.CaseSearchCriteria"
    resultType="com.company.app.dto.CaseQueueItemDto"
    flushCache="false"
    useCache="false"
    timeout="5"
    fetchSize="500"
    statementType="PREPARED"
    resultSetType="FORWARD_ONLY">
  SELECT
    id,
    case_number AS caseNumber,
    status,
    due_at AS dueAt
  FROM cases
  WHERE tenant_id = #{tenantId}
  ORDER BY due_at ASC, id ASC
  LIMIT #{limit}
</select>
```

| Attribute | Fungsi | Default/Note |
|---|---|---|
| `id` | Statement ID dalam namespace | wajib |
| `parameterType` | Java parameter type/alias | optional; sering tidak perlu |
| `resultType` | Java result type/alias | pakai ini **atau** `resultMap` |
| `resultMap` | Reference resultMap | pakai ini **atau** `resultType` |
| `flushCache` | Flush local/2nd level cache | `false` untuk select |
| `useCache` | Pakai 2nd-level cache | `true` untuk select |
| `timeout` | Statement timeout detik | unset/driver dependent |
| `fetchSize` | JDBC fetch size hint | per-query override |
| `statementType` | `STATEMENT`, `PREPARED`, `CALLABLE` | default `PREPARED` |
| `resultSetType` | `FORWARD_ONLY`, scrollable, etc | optional |
| `databaseId` | Vendor-specific SQL | jika pakai databaseIdProvider |
| `resultOrdered` | Optimasi nested result grouped | default `false` |
| `resultSets` | Multiple result sets | stored procedure |
| `affectData` | DML yang return data | since 3.5.12 |

MyBatis mendokumentasikan `select` attributes seperti `id`, `parameterType`, `resultType`, `resultMap`, `flushCache`, `useCache`, `timeout`, `fetchSize`, `statementType`, dan `resultSetType`; `#{}` akan dibuat sebagai `PreparedStatement` parameter. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/sqlmap-xml.html))

## 13.2 `<insert>`, `<update>`, `<delete>`

```xml id="z3z3l3"
<insert
    id="insertCase"
    parameterType="com.company.app.persistence.record.CaseRecord"
    flushCache="true"
    statementType="PREPARED"
    timeout="5">
  INSERT INTO cases (
    id,
    tenant_id,
    case_number,
    status,
    created_at
  )
  VALUES (
    #{id},
    #{tenantId},
    #{caseNumber},
    #{status},
    #{createdAt}
  )
</insert>
```

```xml id="y4c369"
<update
    id="transitionStatus"
    flushCache="true"
    statementType="PREPARED"
    timeout="5">
  UPDATE cases
  SET
    status = #{toStatus},
    version = version + 1,
    updated_at = now()
  WHERE tenant_id = #{tenantId}
    AND id = #{id}
    AND status = #{fromStatus}
    AND version = #{expectedVersion}
</update>
```

| Attribute | Element | Fungsi |
|---|---|---|
| `id` | all | statement ID |
| `parameterType` | all | parameter class/alias |
| `flushCache` | all DML | default `true` |
| `timeout` | all | max wait seconds |
| `statementType` | all | `PREPARED` default |
| `useGeneratedKeys` | insert/update | JDBC `getGeneratedKeys` |
| `keyProperty` | insert/update | property target generated key |
| `keyColumn` | insert/update | DB generated key column; penting di beberapa DB termasuk PostgreSQL |
| `databaseId` | all | vendor-specific statement |

MyBatis mendokumentasikan DML attributes dan menyebut `keyColumn` diperlukan di database tertentu seperti PostgreSQL ketika generated key column bukan kolom pertama. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/sqlmap-xml.html))

## 13.3 PostgreSQL generated key example

```xml id="hwcz7q"
<insert id="insertOrder"
        useGeneratedKeys="true"
        keyProperty="id"
        keyColumn="id">
  INSERT INTO orders (
    customer_id,
    status,
    created_at
  )
  VALUES (
    #{customerId},
    #{status},
    now()
  )
</insert>
```

Namun untuk enterprise app, sering lebih clean pakai UUID dari application:

```java id="pnf1mp"
UUID id = UUID.randomUUID();
```

```xml id="y3x48v"
<insert id="insertOrder">
  INSERT INTO orders (
    id,
    customer_id,
    status,
    created_at
  )
  VALUES (
    #{id},
    #{customerId},
    #{status},
    now()
  )
</insert>
```

---

# 14. `resultMap`

## XML

```xml id="ngx7q8"
<resultMap id="CaseRecordResultMap"
           type="com.company.app.persistence.record.CaseRecord"
           autoMapping="false">
  <id property="id" column="id"/>
  <result property="tenantId" column="tenant_id"/>
  <result property="caseNumber" column="case_number"/>
  <result property="status" column="status"/>
  <result property="version" column="version"/>
  <result property="createdAt" column="created_at"/>
</resultMap>
```

| Attribute/Element | Fungsi |
|---|---|
| `id` | unique resultMap ID |
| `type` | target Java type |
| `autoMapping` | override global automapping |
| `<id>` | identity column/property |
| `<result>` | normal column/property |
| `<constructor>` | constructor injection/record-like mapping |
| `<association>` | has-one nested mapping |
| `<collection>` | has-many nested mapping |
| `<discriminator>` | conditional/polymorphic mapping |

MyBatis menyebut `resultMap` sebagai salah satu elemen paling penting dan powerful karena menghilangkan banyak kode JDBC mapping manual; dokumentasinya juga menyarankan resultMap dibangun incremental dan dites. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/sqlmap-xml.html))

## Programmatic equivalent?

Tidak ada padanan programmatic yang praktis untuk semua `resultMap` kompleks. Bisa membuat `ResultMap` object via internal builder API, tetapi untuk production biasanya **tetap gunakan Mapper XML** untuk `resultMap`.

**Recommendation:**

```text id="aj79vs"
Programmatic setup:
  global config, datasource, transaction, type handlers, plugins, mappers

Mapper XML:
  SQL, resultMap, dynamic SQL, stored procedure mapping
```

---

# 15. Mapper Cache

## XML

```xml id="b5fkxu"
<cache
    eviction="LRU"
    flushInterval="60000"
    size="512"
    readOnly="false"/>
```

```xml id="n2fb9u"
<cache-ref namespace="com.company.app.persistence.mapper.ReferenceDataMapper"/>
```

By default, MyBatis hanya memakai local session cache; second-level cache aktif jika `<cache/>` ditambahkan di mapper XML namespace. Statement default cache behavior adalah `select` memakai `flushCache="false"` dan `useCache="true"`, sedangkan `insert/update/delete` memakai `flushCache="true"`. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/sqlmap-xml.html))

## Programmatic

Second-level cache biasanya dikonfigurasi di mapper XML via `<cache>`. Untuk production microservice, saya biasanya rekomendasikan:

```text id="bbdqgb"
- jangan aktifkan 2nd-level cache untuk data mutable/high-write
- gunakan cache eksternal eksplisit jika memang dibutuhkan
- untuk reference data kecil dan jarang berubah, boleh dipertimbangkan
```

---

# 16. Dynamic SQL XML Options

Walaupun ini bukan `mybatis-config.xml`, ini “command/options” yang sering dipakai di mapper XML.

| Element | Fungsi | Example |
|---|---|---|
| `<if>` | conditional SQL | optional filter |
| `<choose>` / `<when>` / `<otherwise>` | switch/case SQL | lookup by ID vs external ref |
| `<where>` | auto-prefix `WHERE`, remove leading `AND/OR` | search query |
| `<trim>` | custom prefix/suffix override | advanced where/set |
| `<set>` | dynamic update SET clause | patch update |
| `<foreach>` | iterate list/array/map | `IN (...)`, batch values |
| `<bind>` | bind OGNL expression | keyword pattern |
| `<sql>` | reusable SQL fragment | column list |
| `<include>` | include fragment | common projection |

## Example

```xml id="oqexr3"
<select id="searchCases" resultType="CaseQueueItemDto">
  SELECT
    <include refid="CaseQueueColumns"/>
  FROM cases

  <where>
    tenant_id = #{tenantId}

    <if test="status != null">
      AND status = #{status}
    </if>

    <if test="priority != null">
      AND priority = #{priority}
    </if>

    <if test="ids != null and ids.size() > 0">
      AND id IN
      <foreach collection="ids"
               item="id"
               open="("
               separator=","
               close=")">
        #{id}
      </foreach>
    </if>
  </where>

  ORDER BY created_at DESC, id DESC
  LIMIT #{limit}
</select>
```

---

# 17. Full Programmatic Setup Template untuk Jersey/JAX-RS + PostgreSQL

```java id="dq7eg6"
public final class PersistenceModule {

    public HikariDataSource dataSource(DatabaseConfig db) {
        HikariConfig config = new HikariConfig();

        config.setPoolName("case-service-postgres");
        config.setJdbcUrl(db.jdbcUrl());
        config.setUsername(db.username());
        config.setPassword(db.password());

        config.setMaximumPoolSize(db.maximumPoolSize());
        config.setConnectionTimeout(db.connectionTimeoutMs());
        config.setMaxLifetime(db.maxLifetimeMs());
        config.setKeepaliveTime(db.keepaliveMs());

        config.addDataSourceProperty("tcpKeepAlive", "true");

        return new HikariDataSource(config);
    }

    public SqlSessionFactory sqlSessionFactory(DataSource dataSource) {
        JdbcTransactionFactory transactionFactory =
            new JdbcTransactionFactory();

        Properties txProps = new Properties();
        txProps.setProperty("skipSetAutoCommitOnClose", "true");
        transactionFactory.setProperties(txProps);

        Environment environment = new Environment(
            "production",
            transactionFactory,
            dataSource
        );

        Configuration c = new Configuration(environment);

        // Runtime behavior
        c.setMapUnderscoreToCamelCase(true);
        c.setAutoMappingBehavior(AutoMappingBehavior.PARTIAL);
        c.setAutoMappingUnknownColumnBehavior(
            AutoMappingUnknownColumnBehavior.WARNING
        );
        c.setLazyLoadingEnabled(false);
        c.setAggressiveLazyLoading(false);
        c.setDefaultExecutorType(ExecutorType.SIMPLE);
        c.setDefaultStatementTimeout(10);
        c.setDefaultFetchSize(500);
        c.setDefaultResultSetType(ResultSetType.FORWARD_ONLY);
        c.setLocalCacheScope(LocalCacheScope.STATEMENT);
        c.setJdbcTypeForNull(JdbcType.OTHER);
        c.setUseColumnLabel(true);
        c.setUseGeneratedKeys(false);
        c.setUseActualParamName(true);
        c.setLogImpl(Slf4jImpl.class);

        // Aliases
        c.getTypeAliasRegistry()
            .registerAliases("com.company.app.persistence.record");
        c.getTypeAliasRegistry()
            .registerAliases("com.company.app.dto");

        // Type handlers
        c.getTypeHandlerRegistry()
            .register(JsonNode.class, JdbcType.OTHER, JsonNodeJsonbTypeHandler.class);
        c.getTypeHandlerRegistry()
            .register(PostgresEnumTypeHandler.class);

        // Plugins
        c.addInterceptor(new SqlTimingInterceptor());

        // Mappers
        c.addMapper(CaseMapper.class);
        c.addMapper(CaseQueryMapper.class);
        c.addMapper(OrderMapper.class);
        c.addMapper(AuditMapper.class);
        c.addMapper(OutboxMapper.class);

        return new SqlSessionFactoryBuilder().build(c);
    }
}
```

---

# 18. XML Config vs Programmatic - Kapan Pakai?

| Situation | Prefer XML Config | Prefer Programmatic |
|---|---:|---:|
| Simple standalone app | Ya | Bisa |
| Enterprise Jersey/HK2/Weld | Bisa | Ya |
| Runtime secret/env config | Kurang ideal | Ya |
| HikariCP external pool | Bisa via custom factory | Ya |
| Multiple datasource/factory | XML bisa, tapi verbose | Ya |
| Mapper SQL/resultMap | Ya | Tidak disarankan |
| Dynamic SQL | Ya | MyBatis Dynamic SQL library jika perlu |
| TypeHandler registration | Bisa | Ya |
| Interceptor registration | Bisa | Ya |
| Ops-friendly config | Tergantung | Ya jika config object jelas |

**Practical recommendation untuk stack kamu:**

```text id="insr3x"
Use programmatic setup for:
  DataSource/Hikari
  Environment
  SqlSessionFactory
  global settings
  type handlers
  interceptors
  mapper registration

Use Mapper XML for:
  SQL statement
  resultMap
  dynamic SQL
  stored procedure call
  cache declaration if really needed
```

---

# 19. Production Quick Checklist

| Checklist | Target |
|---|---:|
| `SqlSessionFactory` singleton | Ya |
| `SqlSession` per request/use case | Ya |
| `mapUnderscoreToCamelCase=true` jika pakai snake_case | Ya |
| `logImpl=SLF4J` | Ya |
| `defaultExecutorType=SIMPLE` global | Ya |
| `BATCH` hanya di session khusus batch | Ya |
| `defaultStatementTimeout` ada | Ya |
| `localCacheScope=STATEMENT` dipertimbangkan untuk service | Ya |
| Custom TypeHandler untuk JSONB/enum dites | Ya |
| Mapper XML peer loaded via `addMapper` | Ya |
| No raw `${}` kecuali allow-listed identifier | Ya |
| Production datasource pakai Hikari/external pool | Ya |
| Runtime DB password tidak di XML | Ya |
| Mapper integration test against PostgreSQL | Ya |

---

## Rekomendasi Materi Selanjutnya

Lanjutkan ke **“Cheatsheet MyBatis Mapper XML Options: select/insert/update/delete/resultMap/sql/include/cache/dynamic SQL/statementType/fetchSize/timeout/useGeneratedKeys dengan contoh PostgreSQL production-style”** supaya bagian konfigurasi global ini disambungkan langsung ke opsi-opsi mapper XML yang paling sering dipakai sehari-hari.

---

**Sources:**

- [MyBatis 3 | Configuration – mybatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/configuration.html)

## Response:
7/15/2026, 1:25:45 PM

Saya akan buat versi enterprise-grade yang fokus ke mapper XML harian: atribut statement, resultMap, reusable fragment, dynamic SQL aman, CTE untuk query/write, callable procedure PL/pgSQL, dan checklist review production.

# Cheatsheet - MyBatis Mapper XML Options: Enterprise Production-Grade untuk PostgreSQL

Fokus cheatsheet ini adalah **Mapper XML**, bukan `mybatis-config.xml`. Jadi yang dibahas adalah elemen seperti:

```text id="77h9ol"
<mapper>
  <cache>
  <cache-ref>
  <resultMap>
  <sql>
  <include>
  <select>
  <insert>
  <update>
  <delete>
  dynamic SQL:
    <if>
    <choose>
    <where>
    <trim>
    <set>
    <foreach>
    <bind>
  callable:
    statementType="CALLABLE"
    mode=IN/OUT/INOUT
```

MyBatis Mapper XML memang dirancang sebagai tempat utama mapped statement seperti `select`, `insert`, `update`, `delete`, result mapping, cache, dan reusable SQL fragments. MyBatis juga mendukung SQL biasa maupun stored procedure melalui XML descriptor/annotations, sehingga Mapper XML perlu diperlakukan sebagai **production SQL contract**, bukan sekadar file konfigurasi pendukung. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/sqlmap-xml.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 1. Struktur Mapper XML

```xml id="fa73m2"
<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8" ?>
<!DOCTYPE mapper
  PUBLIC "-//mybatis.org//DTD Mapper 3.0//EN"
  "https://mybatis.org/dtd/mybatis-3-mapper.dtd">

<mapper namespace="com.company.app.persistence.mapper.CaseMapper">

  <cache/>

  <resultMap id="CaseRecordResultMap"
             type="com.company.app.persistence.record.CaseRecord">
    ...
  </resultMap>

  <sql id="CaseBaseColumns">
    ...
  </sql>

  <select id="findById" resultMap="CaseRecordResultMap">
    ...
  </select>

  <insert id="insertCase">
    ...
  </insert>

  <update id="transitionStatus">
    ...
  </update>

  <delete id="softDeleteCase">
    ...
  </delete>

</mapper>
```

## Urutan elemen yang disarankan

MyBatis mendokumentasikan first-class elements Mapper XML dalam urutan seperti `cache`, `cache-ref`, `resultMap`, `sql`, lalu mapped statements seperti `insert`, `update`, `delete`, dan `select`. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/sqlmap-xml.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

```text id="68jg3n"
Recommended order:
1. cache / cache-ref
2. resultMap
3. sql fragments
4. select / insert / update / delete statements
```

---

# 2. Production Naming Convention

## 2.1 Mapper namespace

```xml id="jub8wf"
<mapper namespace="com.company.app.persistence.mapper.CaseMapper">
```

Harus match dengan interface:

```java id="5oxmr6"
package com.company.app.persistence.mapper;

public interface CaseMapper {
    CaseRecord findById(UUID tenantId, UUID id);
}
```

## 2.2 Statement naming

| Bad | Better | Reason |
|---|---|---|
| `getData` | `findById` | jelas |
| `update` | `transitionStatus` | intent domain |
| `search` | `searchQueueItems` | query purpose jelas |
| `delete` | `softDeleteById` | destructive intent jelas |
| `callProc` | `approveCaseProcedure` | callable contract jelas |
| `insert` | `insertOutboxEvent` | table/aggregate jelas |

## 2.3 Mapper grouping

```text id="6x3kim"
CaseMapper.xml
  command/write mapper

CaseQueryMapper.xml
  read projection/search/report mapper

CaseProcedureMapper.xml
  stored procedure mapper

CaseBackfillMapper.xml
  migration/backfill operational mapper

AdminCaseMapper.xml
  explicit admin/cross-tenant mapper
```

---

# 3. `<select>` Options

## 3.1 Attribute lengkap yang sering dipakai

```xml id="7f4pxd"
<select
    id="searchCases"
    parameterType="com.company.app.query.CaseSearchCriteria"
    resultType="com.company.app.dto.CaseQueueItemDto"
    resultMap="CaseQueueItemResultMap"
    flushCache="false"
    useCache="false"
    timeout="5"
    fetchSize="500"
    statementType="PREPARED"
    resultSetType="FORWARD_ONLY"
    databaseId="postgresql"
    resultOrdered="false"
    resultSets="cases,total"
    affectData="false">
  ...
</select>
```

| Attribute | Value Umum | Fungsi | Production Note |
|---|---|---|---|
| `id` | `findById` | statement id | wajib unik dalam namespace |
| `parameterType` | FQCN/alias | parameter class | optional; MyBatis sering bisa infer |
| `resultType` | FQCN/alias | target result simple/DTO | pakai **atau** `resultMap` |
| `resultMap` | resultMap id | explicit mapping | preferred untuk mapping kompleks |
| `flushCache` | `false` | flush cache saat statement dipanggil | default select `false` |
| `useCache` | `false/true` | second-level cache | hindari untuk mutable data |
| `timeout` | `5`, `10` | statement timeout detik | set untuk endpoint production |
| `fetchSize` | `100`, `500`, `1000` | JDBC fetch-size hint | penting untuk streaming/large result |
| `statementType` | `PREPARED` | statement mode | default `PREPARED`; procedure pakai `CALLABLE` |
| `resultSetType` | `FORWARD_ONLY` | cursor direction/scrolling | production biasanya `FORWARD_ONLY` |
| `databaseId` | `postgresql` | vendor-specific statement | hanya jika multi-DB vendor |
| `resultOrdered` | `true/false` | nested result optimization | jarang, untuk nested result ordered |
| `resultSets` | names | multiple result sets | mainly procedure/callable |
| `affectData` | `true/false` | untuk statement yang return data tapi memodifikasi data | relevant untuk PostgreSQL `RETURNING` via `<select>` |

MyBatis mendokumentasikan attribute mapped statement seperti `id`, `parameterType`, `resultType`, `resultMap`, `flushCache`, `useCache`, `timeout`, `fetchSize`, `statementType`, dan `resultSetType`; PostgreSQL mendukung `RETURNING` pada DML sehingga MyBatis `affectData` relevan saat DML dipetakan sebagai statement yang mengembalikan data. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/sqlmap-xml.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 3.2 Simple select by tenant + id

```xml id="0gwf91"
<select id="findById" resultMap="CaseRecordResultMap" timeout="3">
  SELECT
    <include refid="CaseBaseColumns"/>
  FROM cases
  WHERE tenant_id = #{tenantId}
    AND id = #{id}
</select>
```

Interface:

```java id="km69jo"
CaseRecord findById(
    @Param("tenantId") UUID tenantId,
    @Param("id") UUID id
);
```

Production rules:

```text id="9tnoyt"
Tenant-owned table:
  always include tenant_id predicate.

Single-row lookup:
  always use indexed key.

Do not:
  SELECT *.
```

---

## 3.3 Search select with timeout, fetchSize, projection DTO

```xml id="7n2f7r"
<select id="searchQueueItems"
        resultType="com.company.app.dto.CaseQueueItemDto"
        timeout="5"
        fetchSize="500"
        resultSetType="FORWARD_ONLY"
        useCache="false">
  SELECT
    c.id,
    c.case_number AS caseNumber,
    c.status,
    c.priority,
    c.assigned_team AS assignedTeam,
    c.due_at AS dueAt
  FROM cases c
  <where>
    c.tenant_id = #{criteria.tenantId}

    <if test="criteria.status != null">
      AND c.status = #{criteria.status.name()}
    </if>

    <if test="criteria.priority != null">
      AND c.priority = #{criteria.priority.name()}
    </if>

    <if test="criteria.assignedTeam != null">
      AND c.assigned_team = #{criteria.assignedTeam}
    </if>
  </where>
  ORDER BY c.due_at ASC, c.id ASC
  LIMIT #{criteria.limit}
</select>
```

Interface:

```java id="pryku4"
List<CaseQueueItemDto> searchQueueItems(
    @Param("criteria") CaseSearchCriteria criteria
);
```

Enterprise notes:

| Concern | Pattern |
|---|---|
| data exposure | projection DTO, no `SELECT *` |
| tenant safety | mandatory `tenant_id` |
| slow query | timeout + index review |
| large result | bounded `limit`, `fetchSize` |
| pagination stability | deterministic `ORDER BY due_at, id` |

---

# 4. `<insert>` Options

## 4.1 Attribute umum

```xml id="vn3967"
<insert
    id="insertCase"
    parameterType="com.company.app.persistence.record.CaseRecord"
    flushCache="true"
    timeout="5"
    statementType="PREPARED"
    useGeneratedKeys="false"
    keyProperty="id"
    keyColumn="id"
    databaseId="postgresql">
  ...
</insert>
```

| Attribute | Fungsi | Production Note |
|---|---|---|
| `id` | statement id | wajib |
| `parameterType` | parameter object | optional tapi dokumentatif |
| `flushCache` | flush cache | default DML `true` |
| `timeout` | max execution detik | recommended |
| `statementType` | `PREPARED`/`CALLABLE` | `PREPARED` normal |
| `useGeneratedKeys` | JDBC generated key | bisa untuk identity/serial |
| `keyProperty` | Java property target key | wajib jika generated key dipakai |
| `keyColumn` | DB column generated key | penting untuk PostgreSQL pada kasus tertentu |
| `databaseId` | vendor-specific | jarang jika hanya PostgreSQL |

MyBatis mendokumentasikan `useGeneratedKeys`, `keyProperty`, dan `keyColumn` untuk `insert`/`update`; `keyColumn` berguna pada database tertentu termasuk PostgreSQL ketika generated key column bukan kolom pertama. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/sqlmap-xml.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 4.2 Insert dengan UUID dari application

```xml id="uhazfz"
<insert id="insertCase"
        parameterType="com.company.app.persistence.record.CaseRecord"
        timeout="5">
  INSERT INTO cases (
    id,
    tenant_id,
    case_number,
    status,
    priority,
    version,
    created_at,
    updated_at
  )
  VALUES (
    #{id},
    #{tenantId},
    #{caseNumber},
    #{status},
    #{priority},
    #{version},
    #{createdAt},
    #{updatedAt}
  )
</insert>
```

Java:

```java id="svdz09"
UUID id = UUID.randomUUID();

CaseRecord record = new CaseRecord(
    id,
    tenantId,
    caseNumber,
    "DRAFT",
    "MEDIUM",
    0L,
    clock.instant(),
    clock.instant()
);
```

Recommended for enterprise:

```text id="9usaw7"
Prefer app-generated UUID/ULID when:
- ID needed before insert
- outbox/audit must reference aggregate ID
- distributed command needs stable ID
```

---

## 4.3 Insert dengan PostgreSQL identity/serial generated key

```xml id="3s74qn"
<insert id="insertAuditLog"
        useGeneratedKeys="true"
        keyProperty="id"
        keyColumn="id"
        timeout="5">
  INSERT INTO audit_logs (
    tenant_id,
    actor_id,
    action,
    aggregate_type,
    aggregate_id,
    created_at
  )
  VALUES (
    #{tenantId},
    #{actorId},
    #{action},
    #{aggregateType},
    #{aggregateId},
    #{createdAt}
  )
</insert>
```

Parameter object harus punya setter:

```java id="jqphn4"
public class AuditLogInsert {
    private Long id;
    private UUID tenantId;
    private UUID actorId;
    private String action;

    public Long getId() {
        return id;
    }

    public void setId(Long id) {
        this.id = id;
    }
}
```

---

## 4.4 Insert dengan `RETURNING` sebagai `<select affectData="true">`

PostgreSQL `INSERT`, `UPDATE`, `DELETE`, dan `MERGE` mendukung `RETURNING` untuk mengembalikan data dari row yang dimodifikasi, menghindari query tambahan untuk mengambil nilai yang dihasilkan database. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/dml-returning.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

```xml id="7kzt3q"
<select id="insertCaseReturning"
        parameterType="com.company.app.persistence.record.CaseInsertCommand"
        resultMap="CaseRecordResultMap"
        affectData="true"
        flushCache="true"
        timeout="5">
  INSERT INTO cases (
    id,
    tenant_id,
    case_number,
    status,
    priority,
    version,
    created_at,
    updated_at
  )
  VALUES (
    #{id},
    #{tenantId},
    #{caseNumber},
    'DRAFT',
    #{priority},
    0,
    now(),
    now()
  )
  RETURNING
    id,
    tenant_id,
    case_number,
    status,
    priority,
    version,
    created_at,
    updated_at
</select>
```

Interface:

```java id="camdwp"
CaseRecord insertCaseReturning(CaseInsertCommand command);
```

Production notes:

```text id="p7wzun"
Use RETURNING when:
- DB default/generated field must be returned immediately
- one round-trip is better than insert + select
- inserted row contract must be exact

Avoid overusing when:
- normal affected-row count is enough
- return payload is large
```

---

# 5. `<update>` Options

## 5.1 Optimistic locking update

```xml id="pmew33"
<update id="transitionStatus"
        timeout="5"
        flushCache="true">
  UPDATE cases
  SET
    status = #{toStatus},
    version = version + 1,
    updated_at = now()
  WHERE tenant_id = #{tenantId}
    AND id = #{id}
    AND status = #{fromStatus}
    AND version = #{expectedVersion}
</update>
```

Interface:

```java id="07idcm"
int transitionStatus(
    @Param("tenantId") UUID tenantId,
    @Param("id") UUID id,
    @Param("fromStatus") String fromStatus,
    @Param("toStatus") String toStatus,
    @Param("expectedVersion") long expectedVersion
);
```

Service:

```java id="o7mpys"
int updated = mapper.transitionStatus(
    tenantId,
    caseId,
    "SUBMITTED",
    "APPROVED",
    expectedVersion
);

if (updated != 1) {
    throw new ConcurrentCaseModificationException(caseId);
}
```

Enterprise rule:

```text id="eckm6i"
Every state transition update should usually include:
- tenant_id
- id
- expected current state
- expected version
```

---

## 5.2 Patch update with `<set>`

```xml id="ur0sf1"
<update id="patchCase"
        parameterType="com.company.app.command.CasePatch"
        timeout="5">
  UPDATE cases
  <set>
    <if test="title != null">
      title = #{title},
    </if>

    <if test="priority != null">
      priority = #{priority.name()},
    </if>

    <if test="assignedTeam != null">
      assigned_team = #{assignedTeam},
    </if>

    version = version + 1,
    updated_at = now()
  </set>
  WHERE tenant_id = #{tenantId}
    AND id = #{caseId}
    AND version = #{expectedVersion}
</update>
```

Dynamic SQL `<set>` menghindari trailing comma pada dynamic update. MyBatis mendokumentasikan dynamic SQL tags seperti `if`, `choose`, `where`, `trim`, `set`, dan `foreach`. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/dynamic-sql.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Production guard di service:

```java id="kmelz0"
if (patch.isEmpty()) {
    throw new EmptyPatchException();
}
```

---

## 5.3 Update with `RETURNING`

```xml id="2lqngh"
<select id="approveCaseReturning"
        parameterType="com.company.app.command.ApproveCaseCommand"
        resultMap="CaseRecordResultMap"
        affectData="true"
        flushCache="true"
        timeout="5">
  UPDATE cases
  SET
    status = 'APPROVED',
    approval_reason = #{reason},
    approved_by = #{actorId},
    approved_at = now(),
    version = version + 1,
    updated_at = now()
  WHERE tenant_id = #{tenantId}
    AND id = #{caseId}
    AND status = 'SUBMITTED'
    AND version = #{expectedVersion}
  RETURNING
    id,
    tenant_id,
    case_number,
    status,
    priority,
    approval_reason,
    version,
    created_at,
    updated_at
</select>
```

Interface:

```java id="e5828f"
CaseRecord approveCaseReturning(ApproveCaseCommand command);
```

Service:

```java id="lqy3i1"
CaseRecord updated = mapper.approveCaseReturning(command);

if (updated == null) {
    throw new ConcurrentCaseModificationException(command.caseId());
}
```

PostgreSQL `UPDATE ... RETURNING` returns values based on rows actually updated; this is useful when Java needs the new version/state without doing a second select. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-update.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 6. `<delete>` Options

## 6.1 Prefer soft delete untuk enterprise domain data

```xml id="pq6ni8"
<update id="softDeleteCase"
        timeout="5">
  UPDATE cases
  SET
    deleted_at = now(),
    deleted_by = #{actorId},
    version = version + 1,
    updated_at = now()
  WHERE tenant_id = #{tenantId}
    AND id = #{id}
    AND deleted_at IS NULL
    AND version = #{expectedVersion}
</update>
```

Interface:

```java id="73l0u7"
int softDeleteCase(
    @Param("tenantId") UUID tenantId,
    @Param("id") UUID id,
    @Param("actorId") UUID actorId,
    @Param("expectedVersion") long expectedVersion
);
```

## 6.2 Hard delete with guard

```xml id="pz9z9s"
<delete id="deleteExpiredIdempotencyKeys"
        timeout="10">
  DELETE FROM idempotency_keys
  WHERE created_at < #{before}
    AND status IN ('COMPLETED', 'FAILED')
</delete>
```

Interface:

```java id="l9ddlr"
int deleteExpiredIdempotencyKeys(@Param("before") Instant before);
```

## 6.3 Delete with `RETURNING`

```xml id="hva64g"
<select id="deleteExpiredOutboxReturning"
        resultType="java.util.UUID"
        affectData="true"
        flushCache="true"
        timeout="10">
  DELETE FROM outbox_events
  WHERE status = 'PUBLISHED'
    AND published_at < #{before}
  RETURNING id
</select>
```

PostgreSQL `DELETE ... RETURNING` returns values from rows actually deleted, which is useful for audit/cleanup reporting. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-delete.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 7. Inline Parameter Options: `#{...}`

## 7.1 Common parameter attributes

```xml id="62xpld"
#{property,
  javaType=java.lang.String,
  jdbcType=VARCHAR,
  typeHandler=com.company.app.mybatis.SomeTypeHandler,
  numericScale=2,
  mode=IN,
  resultMap=SomeResultMap,
  jdbcTypeName=some_pg_type}
```

| Attribute | Use Case |
|---|---|
| `javaType` | disambiguate Java type |
| `jdbcType` | required/important for nullable values |
| `typeHandler` | custom mapping JSONB/enum/value object |
| `numericScale` | decimal scale |
| `mode` | stored procedure `IN`, `OUT`, `INOUT` |
| `resultMap` | cursor/result set mapping in callable |
| `jdbcTypeName` | DB-specific type name |

## 7.2 PostgreSQL JSONB parameter

```xml id="fv82pd"
#{payload,
  jdbcType=OTHER,
  typeHandler=com.company.app.mybatis.JsonNodeJsonbTypeHandler}
```

## 7.3 Null-safe parameter

```xml id="50sdka"
#{approvalReason, jdbcType=VARCHAR}
```

Production rule:

```text id="4782c8"
For nullable fields:
  specify jdbcType explicitly when ambiguity exists.

For PostgreSQL-specific type:
  specify typeHandler explicitly if not globally registered.
```

---

# 8. `${...}` String Substitution

## 8.1 Bad

```xml id="nkwcwg"
ORDER BY ${sort}
```

## 8.2 Good with whitelist enum

Java:

```java id="ddlg30"
public enum CaseSortField {
    DUE_AT("due_at"),
    CREATED_AT("created_at"),
    PRIORITY("priority");

    private final String column;

    CaseSortField(String column) {
        this.column = column;
    }

    public String column() {
        return column;
    }
}
```

XML:

```xml id="sfxu9s"
ORDER BY ${criteria.sortField.column} ${criteria.direction}, id ASC
```

MyBatis warns that `${}` performs string substitution and user input passed directly into it can lead to SQL injection; for user values use `#{}` so MyBatis creates prepared statement parameters. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/sqlmap-xml.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Production rule:

```text id="gj7ndr"
Allowed ${}:
  only enum/allow-listed SQL identifier
  example: sort column, sort direction after validation

Forbidden ${}:
  raw request value
  raw table name
  raw where clause
  raw operator
```

---

# 9. `<resultMap>` Enterprise Patterns

## 9.1 Basic resultMap

```xml id="6y1xex"
<resultMap id="CaseRecordResultMap"
           type="com.company.app.persistence.record.CaseRecord"
           autoMapping="false">
  <id property="id" column="id"/>
  <result property="tenantId" column="tenant_id"/>
  <result property="caseNumber" column="case_number"/>
  <result property="status" column="status"/>
  <result property="priority" column="priority"/>
  <result property="version" column="version"/>
  <result property="createdAt" column="created_at"/>
  <result property="updatedAt" column="updated_at"/>
</resultMap>
```

`resultMap` adalah elemen MyBatis yang paling powerful untuk memetakan result set ke object; dokumentasi MyBatis juga menyarankan membangun resultMap secara incremental dan melakukan unit/integration test karena mapping kompleks bisa sulit dilacak. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/sqlmap-xml.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 9.2 Constructor mapping untuk immutable/record-style object

```xml id="1sq5sh"
<resultMap id="CaseRecordConstructorMap"
           type="com.company.app.persistence.record.CaseRecord">
  <constructor>
    <idArg column="id" javaType="java.util.UUID"/>
    <arg column="tenant_id" javaType="java.util.UUID"/>
    <arg column="case_number" javaType="string"/>
    <arg column="status" javaType="string"/>
    <arg column="priority" javaType="string"/>
    <arg column="version" javaType="long"/>
    <arg column="created_at" javaType="java.time.Instant"/>
    <arg column="updated_at" javaType="java.time.Instant"/>
  </constructor>
</resultMap>
```

Use when:

```text id="awtpyb"
- Java record
- immutable persistence object
- no default constructor
```

---

## 9.3 JSONB field with TypeHandler

```xml id="ugytdh"
<resultMap id="OutboxEventResultMap"
           type="com.company.app.persistence.record.OutboxEventRecord">
  <id property="id" column="id"/>
  <result property="tenantId" column="tenant_id"/>
  <result property="eventType" column="event_type"/>
  <result property="payload"
          column="payload"
          jdbcType="OTHER"
          typeHandler="com.company.app.mybatis.JsonNodeJsonbTypeHandler"/>
  <result property="status" column="status"/>
  <result property="createdAt" column="created_at"/>
</resultMap>
```

---

## 9.4 Association - use carefully

```xml id="f09y2z"
<resultMap id="CaseDetailResultMap"
           type="com.company.app.dto.CaseDetailDto">
  <id property="id" column="case_id"/>
  <result property="caseNumber" column="case_number"/>
  <result property="status" column="case_status"/>

  <association property="customer"
               javaType="com.company.app.dto.CustomerSummaryDto">
    <id property="id" column="customer_id"/>
    <result property="name" column="customer_name"/>
  </association>
</resultMap>
```

Production warning:

```text id="f8y22h"
For API list/search:
  prefer flat DTO projection.

For detail page:
  association can be okay.

Avoid:
  large nested object graph with many joins unless tested and benchmarked.
```

---

## 9.5 Collection - avoid for high-volume list

```xml id="zh5pyh"
<resultMap id="OrderDetailResultMap"
           type="com.company.app.dto.OrderDetailDto">
  <id property="id" column="order_id"/>
  <result property="orderNumber" column="order_number"/>

  <collection property="items"
              ofType="com.company.app.dto.OrderItemDto">
    <id property="id" column="item_id"/>
    <result property="sku" column="item_sku"/>
    <result property="quantity" column="item_quantity"/>
  </collection>
</resultMap>
```

Use only when:

```text id="5sjzhm"
- bounded child rows
- query is for detail endpoint
- no huge cardinality explosion
- integration test verifies de-duplication
```

---

# 10. `<sql>` and `<include>`

## 10.1 Base columns

```xml id="xjdfiq"
<sql id="CaseBaseColumns">
  id,
  tenant_id,
  case_number,
  status,
  priority,
  version,
  created_at,
  updated_at
</sql>
```

Usage:

```xml id="b6o4c3"
<select id="findById" resultMap="CaseRecordResultMap">
  SELECT
    <include refid="CaseBaseColumns"/>
  FROM cases
  WHERE tenant_id = #{tenantId}
    AND id = #{id}
</select>
```

## 10.2 Alias-aware column fragment

```xml id="7212ft"
<sql id="CaseColumnsWithAlias">
  ${alias}.id,
  ${alias}.tenant_id,
  ${alias}.case_number,
  ${alias}.status,
  ${alias}.priority,
  ${alias}.version,
  ${alias}.created_at,
  ${alias}.updated_at
</sql>
```

Usage:

```xml id="31nvfr"
<select id="findById" resultMap="CaseRecordResultMap">
  SELECT
    <include refid="CaseColumnsWithAlias">
      <property name="alias" value="c"/>
    </include>
  FROM cases c
  WHERE c.tenant_id = #{tenantId}
    AND c.id = #{id}
</select>
```

Production warning:

```text id="708r8u"
<property> inside <include> becomes string substitution.

Use only for internal static fragments like alias names.
Never pass request/user value into include property.
```

---

# 11. Dynamic SQL Tags

MyBatis dynamic SQL XML supports conditional and structural tags like `if`, `choose`, `where`, `trim`, `set`, `foreach`, and `bind`; these are intended to avoid manual string concatenation. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/dynamic-sql.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## 11.1 `<if>`

```xml id="hksb23"
<if test="criteria.status != null">
  AND c.status = #{criteria.status.name()}
</if>
```

## 11.2 `<choose>`

```xml id="iw4a6s"
<choose>
  <when test="lookup.mode.name() == 'ID'">
    AND c.id = #{lookup.id}
  </when>
  <when test="lookup.mode.name() == 'CASE_NUMBER'">
    AND c.case_number = #{lookup.caseNumber}
  </when>
  <otherwise>
    AND 1 = 0
  </otherwise>
</choose>
```

## 11.3 `<where>`

```xml id="gwp3ox"
<where>
  c.tenant_id = #{criteria.tenantId}

  <if test="criteria.status != null">
    AND c.status = #{criteria.status.name()}
  </if>
</where>
```

## 11.4 `<trim>`

```xml id="fzi4zx"
<trim prefix="WHERE" prefixOverrides="AND |OR ">
  AND c.tenant_id = #{tenantId}

  <if test="status != null">
    AND c.status = #{status}
  </if>
</trim>
```

## 11.5 `<set>`

```xml id="2z5q14"
<set>
  <if test="title != null">
    title = #{title},
  </if>
  <if test="priority != null">
    priority = #{priority.name()},
  </if>
  updated_at = now(),
</set>
```

## 11.6 `<foreach>`

```xml id="uy4yt1"
AND c.id IN
<foreach collection="ids"
         item="id"
         open="("
         separator=","
         close=")">
  #{id}
</foreach>
```

Service guard:

```java id="h79n2y"
if (ids == null || ids.isEmpty()) {
    return List.of();
}

if (ids.size() > 500) {
    throw new TooManyIdsException(ids.size());
}
```

## 11.7 `<bind>`

```xml id="47vy27"
<bind name="keywordPattern"
      value="'%' + criteria.keywordEscaped + '%'"/>

AND (
  c.case_number ILIKE #{keywordPattern} ESCAPE '\'
  OR c.title ILIKE #{keywordPattern} ESCAPE '\'
)
```

Production notes:

| Tag | Main Risk | Production Guard |
|---|---|---|
| `<if>` | missing predicate | test all branches |
| `<choose>` | unexpected mode | enum + otherwise `AND 1=0` |
| `<where>` | conditional tenant predicate | tenant predicate must be unconditional |
| `<set>` | empty patch | guard in service |
| `<foreach>` | empty/huge list | validate size |
| `<bind>` | wildcard injection/cost | escape `%`, `_`, `\`; bound length |

---

# 12. Cache Options

## 12.1 `<cache>`

```xml id="t0m244"
<cache
    eviction="LRU"
    flushInterval="60000"
    size="512"
    readOnly="false"/>
```

| Attribute | Values | Meaning |
|---|---|---|
| `eviction` | `LRU`, `FIFO`, `SOFT`, `WEAK` | eviction strategy |
| `flushInterval` | millis | periodic flush |
| `size` | int | max object refs |
| `readOnly` | `true/false` | return same instance vs serialized copy |
| `blocking` | `true/false` | block cache miss concurrency |

## 12.2 `<cache-ref>`

```xml id="7sp1ia"
<cache-ref namespace="com.company.app.persistence.mapper.ReferenceDataMapper"/>
```

Production recommendation:

```text id="mo83mr"
Use MyBatis second-level cache only for:
- small reference data
- rarely changed data
- no tenant/security sensitivity issue
- explicit invalidation strategy

Avoid for:
- mutable transactional data
- tenant-specific sensitive data
- data that must be immediately consistent
```

MyBatis Mapper XML supports `cache` for namespace cache and `cache-ref` to reference another namespace cache. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/sqlmap-xml.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 13. PostgreSQL CTE Patterns in MyBatis

PostgreSQL `WITH` defines Common Table Expressions; each auxiliary statement in a `WITH` clause can be `SELECT`, `INSERT`, `UPDATE`, `DELETE`, or `MERGE`, and the primary statement can also be one of those statement types. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/queries-with.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 13.1 CTE for SELECT - readable complex query

```xml id="kd9dxh"
<select id="findEscalationCandidates"
        resultType="com.company.app.dto.EscalationCandidateDto"
        timeout="10"
        fetchSize="500">
  WITH eligible_cases AS (
    SELECT
      c.id,
      c.case_number,
      c.status,
      c.priority,
      c.due_at,
      c.assigned_team
    FROM cases c
    WHERE c.tenant_id = #{tenantId}
      AND c.status IN ('OPEN', 'IN_REVIEW')
      AND c.due_at < now()
      AND c.deleted_at IS NULL
  ),
  team_capacity AS (
    SELECT
      t.team_code,
      t.max_escalation_per_batch
    FROM teams t
    WHERE t.tenant_id = #{tenantId}
  )
  SELECT
    e.id,
    e.case_number AS caseNumber,
    e.status,
    e.priority,
    e.due_at AS dueAt,
    e.assigned_team AS assignedTeam
  FROM eligible_cases e
  JOIN team_capacity tc
    ON tc.team_code = e.assigned_team
  ORDER BY e.due_at ASC, e.id ASC
  LIMIT #{limit}
</select>
```

Use CTE when:

```text id="860thm"
- query has logical stages
- same subquery reused
- readability is more important than one huge nested SQL
- EXPLAIN has been checked for critical query
```

---

## 13.2 CTE for INSERT - insert outbox from inserted aggregate

```xml id="w3n4er"
<insert id="insertCaseAndOutbox"
        timeout="5">
  WITH inserted_case AS (
    INSERT INTO cases (
      id,
      tenant_id,
      case_number,
      status,
      priority,
      version,
      created_at,
      updated_at
    )
    VALUES (
      #{caseRecord.id},
      #{caseRecord.tenantId},
      #{caseRecord.caseNumber},
      'DRAFT',
      #{caseRecord.priority},
      0,
      now(),
      now()
    )
    RETURNING
      id,
      tenant_id,
      case_number,
      status
  )
  INSERT INTO outbox_events (
    id,
    tenant_id,
    aggregate_type,
    aggregate_id,
    event_type,
    payload,
    status,
    created_at
  )
  SELECT
    #{outboxId},
    ic.tenant_id,
    'CASE',
    ic.id::text,
    'CASE_CREATED',
    jsonb_build_object(
      'caseId', ic.id,
      'caseNumber', ic.case_number,
      'status', ic.status
    ),
    'PENDING',
    now()
  FROM inserted_case ic
</insert>
```

Production note:

```text id="zedzmp"
This keeps aggregate insert and outbox insert in one SQL statement,
but still keep it inside Java transaction boundary.
```

---

## 13.3 CTE for INSERT with returned row

```xml id="io0fqo"
<select id="insertCaseAndReturn"
        resultMap="CaseRecordResultMap"
        affectData="true"
        flushCache="true"
        timeout="5">
  WITH inserted_case AS (
    INSERT INTO cases (
      id,
      tenant_id,
      case_number,
      status,
      priority,
      version,
      created_at,
      updated_at
    )
    VALUES (
      #{id},
      #{tenantId},
      #{caseNumber},
      'DRAFT',
      #{priority},
      0,
      now(),
      now()
    )
    RETURNING
      id,
      tenant_id,
      case_number,
      status,
      priority,
      version,
      created_at,
      updated_at
  )
  SELECT
    id,
    tenant_id,
    case_number,
    status,
    priority,
    version,
    created_at,
    updated_at
  FROM inserted_case
</select>
```

---

## 13.4 CTE for UPDATE - claim queue jobs safely

```xml id="bmygv5"
<select id="claimOutboxEvents"
        resultMap="OutboxEventResultMap"
        affectData="true"
        flushCache="true"
        timeout="5"
        fetchSize="100">
  WITH candidate AS (
    SELECT id
    FROM outbox_events
    WHERE status = 'PENDING'
      AND available_at <= now()
    ORDER BY available_at ASC, id ASC
    LIMIT #{limit}
    FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED
  ),
  claimed AS (
    UPDATE outbox_events o
    SET
      status = 'PROCESSING',
      locked_by = #{workerId},
      locked_at = now(),
      attempt_count = attempt_count + 1
    FROM candidate c
    WHERE o.id = c.id
    RETURNING
      o.id,
      o.tenant_id,
      o.aggregate_type,
      o.aggregate_id,
      o.event_type,
      o.payload,
      o.status,
      o.attempt_count,
      o.created_at
  )
  SELECT
    id,
    tenant_id,
    aggregate_type,
    aggregate_id,
    event_type,
    payload,
    status,
    attempt_count,
    created_at
  FROM claimed
</select>
```

Use this for:

```text id="dzsejm"
- worker queue
- outbox processing
- batch claiming
- avoiding duplicate processing across workers
```

---

## 13.5 CTE for DELETE - archive then delete

```xml id="pgegh9"
<select id="archiveAndDeleteOldAuditLogs"
        resultType="java.util.UUID"
        affectData="true"
        flushCache="true"
        timeout="30">
  WITH deleted AS (
    DELETE FROM audit_logs
    WHERE created_at < #{before}
      AND tenant_id = #{tenantId}
    RETURNING
      id,
      tenant_id,
      actor_id,
      action,
      aggregate_type,
      aggregate_id,
      created_at
  ),
  archived AS (
    INSERT INTO audit_log_archive (
      id,
      tenant_id,
      actor_id,
      action,
      aggregate_type,
      aggregate_id,
      original_created_at,
      archived_at
    )
    SELECT
      id,
      tenant_id,
      actor_id,
      action,
      aggregate_type,
      aggregate_id,
      created_at,
      now()
    FROM deleted
    RETURNING id
  )
  SELECT id
  FROM archived
</select>
```

Production warning:

```text id="kib443"
For large data:
  do not delete/archive millions of rows in one transaction.
  Use chunking with LIMIT via CTE candidate IDs.
```

---

## 13.6 Chunked CTE delete

```xml id="b1kv27"
<select id="deleteOldOutboxChunk"
        resultType="java.util.UUID"
        affectData="true"
        flushCache="true"
        timeout="10">
  WITH candidate AS (
    SELECT id
    FROM outbox_events
    WHERE status = 'PUBLISHED'
      AND published_at < #{before}
    ORDER BY id
    LIMIT #{limit}
  ),
  deleted AS (
    DELETE FROM outbox_events o
    USING candidate c
    WHERE o.id = c.id
    RETURNING o.id
  )
  SELECT id
  FROM deleted
</select>
```

---

# 14. Stored Procedure / PL/pgSQL Callable

PostgreSQL `CREATE PROCEDURE` defines a procedure, and `CALL` invokes a procedure. PostgreSQL `CALL` returns a result row if the procedure has output parameters. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-createprocedure.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 14.1 PL/pgSQL procedure with OUT params

```sql id="f0e4cx"
CREATE OR REPLACE PROCEDURE approve_case_proc(
    IN p_tenant_id uuid,
    IN p_case_id uuid,
    IN p_actor_id uuid,
    IN p_expected_version bigint,
    OUT p_updated integer,
    OUT p_new_version bigint
)
LANGUAGE plpgsql
AS $$
BEGIN
    UPDATE cases
    SET
      status = 'APPROVED',
      approved_by = p_actor_id,
      approved_at = now(),
      version = version + 1,
      updated_at = now()
    WHERE tenant_id = p_tenant_id
      AND id = p_case_id
      AND status = 'SUBMITTED'
      AND version = p_expected_version;

    GET DIAGNOSTICS p_updated = ROW_COUNT;

    IF p_updated = 1 THEN
        SELECT version
        INTO p_new_version
        FROM cases
        WHERE tenant_id = p_tenant_id
          AND id = p_case_id;
    ELSE
        p_new_version := NULL;
    END IF;
END;
$$;
```

---

## 14.2 Parameter object

```java id="0tf1ma"
public class ApproveCaseProcedureParams {

    private UUID tenantId;
    private UUID caseId;
    private UUID actorId;
    private long expectedVersion;

    private Integer updated;
    private Long newVersion;

    public UUID getTenantId() {
        return tenantId;
    }

    public void setTenantId(UUID tenantId) {
        this.tenantId = tenantId;
    }

    public UUID getCaseId() {
        return caseId;
    }

    public void setCaseId(UUID caseId) {
        this.caseId = caseId;
    }

    public UUID getActorId() {
        return actorId;
    }

    public void setActorId(UUID actorId) {
        this.actorId = actorId;
    }

    public long getExpectedVersion() {
        return expectedVersion;
    }

    public void setExpectedVersion(long expectedVersion) {
        this.expectedVersion = expectedVersion;
    }

    public Integer getUpdated() {
        return updated;
    }

    public void setUpdated(Integer updated) {
        this.updated = updated;
    }

    public Long getNewVersion() {
        return newVersion;
    }

    public void setNewVersion(Long newVersion) {
        this.newVersion = newVersion;
    }
}
```

---

## 14.3 Mapper XML `statementType="CALLABLE"`

```xml id="2jwsyp"
<update id="approveCaseProcedure"
        parameterType="com.company.app.persistence.proc.ApproveCaseProcedureParams"
        statementType="CALLABLE"
        timeout="5">
  { CALL approve_case_proc(
      #{tenantId, mode=IN, jdbcType=OTHER},
      #{caseId, mode=IN, jdbcType=OTHER},
      #{actorId, mode=IN, jdbcType=OTHER},
      #{expectedVersion, mode=IN, jdbcType=BIGINT},
      #{updated, mode=OUT, jdbcType=INTEGER},
      #{newVersion, mode=OUT, jdbcType=BIGINT}
    )
  }
</update>
```

Interface:

```java id="n27m8l"
void approveCaseProcedure(ApproveCaseProcedureParams params);
```

Service:

```java id="d8et0t"
ApproveCaseProcedureParams params = new ApproveCaseProcedureParams();
params.setTenantId(tenantId);
params.setCaseId(caseId);
params.setActorId(actorId);
params.setExpectedVersion(expectedVersion);

mapper.approveCaseProcedure(params);

if (!Integer.valueOf(1).equals(params.getUpdated())) {
    throw new ConcurrentCaseModificationException(caseId);
}

long newVersion = params.getNewVersion();
```

MyBatis supports `statementType="CALLABLE"` and inline parameters with `mode=IN`, `OUT`, or `INOUT`; OUT/INOUT values are written back into parameter object/map properties after execution. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/sqlmap-xml.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 14.4 Callable with `Map`

```xml id="tcfv3s"
<update id="approveCaseProcedureMap"
        parameterType="map"
        statementType="CALLABLE"
        timeout="5">
  { CALL approve_case_proc(
      #{tenantId, mode=IN, jdbcType=OTHER},
      #{caseId, mode=IN, jdbcType=OTHER},
      #{actorId, mode=IN, jdbcType=OTHER},
      #{expectedVersion, mode=IN, jdbcType=BIGINT},
      #{updated, mode=OUT, jdbcType=INTEGER},
      #{newVersion, mode=OUT, jdbcType=BIGINT}
    )
  }
</update>
```

Java:

```java id="fo1zur"
Map<String, Object> params = new HashMap<>();
params.put("tenantId", tenantId);
params.put("caseId", caseId);
params.put("actorId", actorId);
params.put("expectedVersion", expectedVersion);

mapper.approveCaseProcedureMap(params);

Integer updated = (Integer) params.get("updated");
Long newVersion = (Long) params.get("newVersion");
```

Recommendation:

```text id="h770yl"
Use parameter object for enterprise procedure contracts.
Use Map only for quick/internal experiments or generic tooling.
```

---

## 14.5 PostgreSQL function returning table - often better than refcursor

```sql id="is3x9y"
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION find_cases_for_escalation(
    p_tenant_id uuid,
    p_limit integer
)
RETURNS TABLE (
    id uuid,
    case_number text,
    status text,
    priority text,
    due_at timestamptz
)
LANGUAGE sql
STABLE
AS $$
    SELECT
      c.id,
      c.case_number,
      c.status,
      c.priority,
      c.due_at
    FROM cases c
    WHERE c.tenant_id = p_tenant_id
      AND c.status IN ('OPEN', 'IN_REVIEW')
      AND c.due_at < now()
    ORDER BY c.due_at ASC, c.id ASC
    LIMIT p_limit;
$$;
```

Mapper XML:

```xml id="m48wpi"
<select id="findCasesForEscalationFunction"
        resultType="com.company.app.dto.EscalationCandidateDto"
        timeout="10"
        fetchSize="500">
  SELECT
    id,
    case_number AS caseNumber,
    status,
    priority,
    due_at AS dueAt
  FROM find_cases_for_escalation(
    #{tenantId},
    #{limit}
  )
</select>
```

Production recommendation:

```text id="7uah32"
Prefer RETURNS TABLE for list/result contracts.

Use refcursor only when:
- legacy DB contract requires it
- transaction/session lifecycle is controlled
- integration test verifies driver behavior
```

---

# 15. `statementType`

| Value | Use Case | Production Note |
|---|---|---|
| `PREPARED` | normal parameterized SQL | default and recommended |
| `STATEMENT` | raw SQL without prepared params | avoid for user input |
| `CALLABLE` | stored procedure/function call | explicit procedure boundary |

Examples:

```xml id="qqi8vh"
<select id="findById" statementType="PREPARED" resultMap="CaseRecordResultMap">
  SELECT ...
</select>
```

```xml id="yl3vv9"
<update id="approveCaseProcedure" statementType="CALLABLE">
  { CALL approve_case_proc(...) }
</update>
```

Production rule:

```text id="fdxes8"
Default:
  PREPARED

Avoid:
  STATEMENT unless internal fixed SQL and strong reason

Use:
  CALLABLE only for stored procedure/function call contract
```

---

# 16. `timeout`

## 16.1 Per-statement timeout

```xml id="8pwp7m"
<select id="searchCases"
        resultType="CaseQueueItemDto"
        timeout="5">
  SELECT ...
</select>
```

```xml id="knjmes"
<update id="transitionStatus"
        timeout="3">
  UPDATE ...
</update>
```

## 16.2 Recommended timeout by operation

| Operation | Timeout Start Point |
|---|---:|
| single row lookup | 1-3 sec |
| command update | 3-5 sec |
| search/list | 5-10 sec |
| report query | 10-30 sec |
| backfill batch | 10-60 sec |
| maintenance operation | explicit job timeout |

Production rule:

```text id="3ldj3o"
Statement timeout should be shorter than HTTP request timeout.

Do not allow DB query to outlive user/request timeout by a lot.
```

---

# 17. `fetchSize`

## 17.1 Mapper-level fetchSize

```xml id="v816e8"
<select id="streamAuditLogs"
        resultMap="AuditLogResultMap"
        fetchSize="500"
        resultSetType="FORWARD_ONLY"
        timeout="30">
  SELECT
    id,
    tenant_id,
    action,
    aggregate_type,
    aggregate_id,
    created_at
  FROM audit_logs
  WHERE tenant_id = #{tenantId}
    AND created_at >= #{from}
    AND created_at < #{to}
  ORDER BY created_at ASC, id ASC
</select>
```

## 17.2 Cursor mapper

```java id="rnvu3v"
Cursor<AuditLogRecord> streamAuditLogs(AuditLogExportCriteria criteria);
```

Service:

```java id="9qqzte"
try (SqlSession session = sqlSessionFactory.openSession(false);
     Cursor<AuditLogRecord> cursor =
         session.getMapper(AuditMapper.class).streamAuditLogs(criteria)) {

    for (AuditLogRecord record : cursor) {
        sink.write(record);
    }

    session.commit();
}
```

Production warning:

```text id="la06k1"
Cursor/fetchSize holds DB connection while streaming.
Use only for controlled export/job.
Never return Cursor outside SqlSession scope.
```

---

# 18. `useGeneratedKeys`, `selectKey`, and PostgreSQL `RETURNING`

## 18.1 `useGeneratedKeys`

```xml id="tcip9f"
<insert id="insertUser"
        useGeneratedKeys="true"
        keyProperty="id"
        keyColumn="id">
  INSERT INTO users (
    tenant_id,
    email,
    display_name
  )
  VALUES (
    #{tenantId},
    #{email},
    #{displayName}
  )
</insert>
```

## 18.2 `<selectKey>` before insert

```xml id="44syrh"
<insert id="insertCaseWithSelectKey">
  <selectKey keyProperty="id"
             resultType="java.util.UUID"
             order="BEFORE">
    SELECT gen_random_uuid()
  </selectKey>

  INSERT INTO cases (
    id,
    tenant_id,
    case_number,
    status
  )
  VALUES (
    #{id},
    #{tenantId},
    #{caseNumber},
    'DRAFT'
  )
</insert>
```

## 18.3 Prefer app-generated UUID for distributed command

```java id="e0ycjd"
UUID id = UUID.randomUUID();
```

## 18.4 Prefer `RETURNING` when full DB-generated row is needed

```xml id="pot507"
<select id="insertOutboxReturning"
        resultMap="OutboxEventResultMap"
        affectData="true"
        flushCache="true">
  INSERT INTO outbox_events (
    id,
    tenant_id,
    event_type,
    payload,
    status,
    created_at
  )
  VALUES (
    #{id},
    #{tenantId},
    #{eventType},
    #{payload, jdbcType=OTHER,
      typeHandler=com.company.app.mybatis.JsonNodeJsonbTypeHandler},
    'PENDING',
    now()
  )
  RETURNING
    id,
    tenant_id,
    event_type,
    payload,
    status,
    created_at
</select>
```

---

# 19. Enterprise Mapper XML Template

```xml id="wzibsf"
<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8" ?>
<!DOCTYPE mapper
  PUBLIC "-//mybatis.org//DTD Mapper 3.0//EN"
  "https://mybatis.org/dtd/mybatis-3-mapper.dtd">

<mapper namespace="com.company.app.persistence.mapper.CaseMapper">

  <!-- Result Maps -->
  <resultMap id="CaseRecordResultMap"
             type="com.company.app.persistence.record.CaseRecord"
             autoMapping="false">
    <id property="id" column="id"/>
    <result property="tenantId" column="tenant_id"/>
    <result property="caseNumber" column="case_number"/>
    <result property="status" column="status"/>
    <result property="priority" column="priority"/>
    <result property="version" column="version"/>
    <result property="createdAt" column="created_at"/>
    <result property="updatedAt" column="updated_at"/>
  </resultMap>

  <!-- SQL Fragments -->
  <sql id="CaseBaseColumns">
    id,
    tenant_id,
    case_number,
    status,
    priority,
    version,
    created_at,
    updated_at
  </sql>

  <!-- Select -->
  <select id="findById"
          resultMap="CaseRecordResultMap"
          timeout="3">
    SELECT
      <include refid="CaseBaseColumns"/>
    FROM cases
    WHERE tenant_id = #{tenantId}
      AND id = #{id}
  </select>

  <!-- Insert -->
  <insert id="insertCase"
          timeout="5">
    INSERT INTO cases (
      id,
      tenant_id,
      case_number,
      status,
      priority,
      version,
      created_at,
      updated_at
    )
    VALUES (
      #{id},
      #{tenantId},
      #{caseNumber},
      #{status},
      #{priority},
      #{version},
      #{createdAt},
      #{updatedAt}
    )
  </insert>

  <!-- Update with optimistic locking -->
  <update id="transitionStatus"
          timeout="5">
    UPDATE cases
    SET
      status = #{toStatus},
      version = version + 1,
      updated_at = now()
    WHERE tenant_id = #{tenantId}
      AND id = #{id}
      AND status = #{fromStatus}
      AND version = #{expectedVersion}
  </update>

  <!-- DML returning -->
  <select id="approveReturning"
          resultMap="CaseRecordResultMap"
          affectData="true"
          flushCache="true"
          timeout="5">
    UPDATE cases
    SET
      status = 'APPROVED',
      approval_reason = #{reason},
      approved_by = #{actorId},
      approved_at = now(),
      version = version + 1,
      updated_at = now()
    WHERE tenant_id = #{tenantId}
      AND id = #{caseId}
      AND status = 'SUBMITTED'
      AND version = #{expectedVersion}
    RETURNING
      <include refid="CaseBaseColumns"/>
  </select>

  <!-- Callable procedure -->
  <update id="approveCaseProcedure"
          parameterType="com.company.app.persistence.proc.ApproveCaseProcedureParams"
          statementType="CALLABLE"
          timeout="5">
    { CALL approve_case_proc(
        #{tenantId, mode=IN, jdbcType=OTHER},
        #{caseId, mode=IN, jdbcType=OTHER},
        #{actorId, mode=IN, jdbcType=OTHER},
        #{expectedVersion, mode=IN, jdbcType=BIGINT},
        #{updated, mode=OUT, jdbcType=INTEGER},
        #{newVersion, mode=OUT, jdbcType=BIGINT}
      )
    }
  </update>

</mapper>
```

---

# 20. Mapper XML Production Review Checklist

| Area | Checklist | Required |
|---|---|---:|
| Namespace | XML namespace matches mapper interface | Ya |
| Statement ID | clear, intent-revealing | Ya |
| Params | multi-arg mapper uses `@Param` | Ya |
| Tenant | tenant-owned query has `tenant_id` | Ya |
| Security | user values use `#{}` | Ya |
| Dynamic SQL | every `${}` is allow-listed | Ya |
| Result | no `SELECT *` in production endpoint | Ya |
| Mapping | resultMap/DTO aliases tested | Ya |
| Update | state/version guard where needed | Ya |
| Affected row | service checks `int updated` | Ya |
| Timeout | important statements have timeout | Ya |
| Fetch | large queries use fetchSize/cursor carefully | Bila perlu |
| Cache | second-level cache avoided for mutable data | Ya |
| CTE | complex CTE has EXPLAIN evidence | Ya |
| Procedure | callable params explicit `mode`/`jdbcType` | Ya |
| RETURNING | DML returning uses `<select affectData="true">` | Ya |
| Test | mapper integration test with PostgreSQL | Ya |

---

# 21. Common Enterprise Anti-Patterns

| Anti-Pattern | Risk |
|---|---|
| `SELECT *` | over-fetching, data exposure, mapping surprise |
| `WHERE id = #{id}` on tenant table | cross-tenant leak |
| `${request.sort}` | SQL injection |
| no timeout | stuck request/DB pressure |
| huge `IN` list | slow query / SQL size explosion |
| patch update without empty patch guard | invalid SQL or unwanted version bump |
| state update without version/status guard | lost update |
| stored procedure OUT params ignored | invalid business state accepted |
| CTE modifies huge dataset in one transaction | lock/rollback disaster |
| delete without chunking | long lock/table bloat |
| collection resultMap for large list endpoint | memory/cardinality explosion |
| using MyBatis cache for mutable tenant data | stale/security issue |
| mapper XML not integration-tested | runtime-only SQL bugs |

---

# 22. Decision Table

| Need | Recommended Mapper Pattern |
|---|---|
| Single row lookup | `<select resultMap timeout>` |
| Search/list | `<select resultType DTO timeout fetchSize limit>` |
| Complex readable query | CTE in `<select>` |
| Insert simple row | `<insert>` |
| Insert and return generated/default row | `<select affectData="true"> INSERT ... RETURNING` |
| Update state with optimistic locking | `<update>` returning affected row count |
| Update and return new row | `<select affectData="true"> UPDATE ... RETURNING` |
| Soft delete | `<update>` set `deleted_at` |
| Hard delete cleanup | chunked `<delete>` or `<select DELETE ... RETURNING>` |
| Claim worker jobs | CTE `SELECT ... FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED` + `UPDATE ... RETURNING` |
| Patch update | `<update><set>...</set>` |
| Dynamic search | `<where>`, `<if>`, `<choose>` with criteria object |
| Batch `IN` query | `<foreach>` with service-side size guard |
| Call PL/pgSQL procedure | `statementType="CALLABLE"` |
| Query from DB function | `SELECT * FROM function(...)` |
| Return OUT params | parameter object with `mode=OUT` |
| Reuse column list | `<sql>` + `<include>` |
| Reference data cache | optional `<cache>` with strict review |

---

# 23. Roadmap Belajar Singkat

| Urutan | Materi | Target |
|---:|---|---|
| 1 | `<select>/<insert>/<update>/<delete>` attributes | Paham statement behavior |
| 2 | `resultMap` | Mapping aman dan eksplisit |
| 3 | `#{}` vs `${}` | SQL injection prevention |
| 4 | Dynamic SQL tags | Query fleksibel tapi aman |
| 5 | `timeout`, `fetchSize`, `resultSetType` | Runtime stability |
| 6 | PostgreSQL `RETURNING` | DML + returned row |
| 7 | CTE for select/write | Query/write flow lebih ekspresif |
| 8 | Callable procedure | Integrasi PL/pgSQL |
| 9 | Cache and reusable SQL | Reuse tanpa stale/security issue |
| 10 | Integration testing | Mapper terbukti against PostgreSQL nyata |
