7/15/2026, 1:28:44 PM

# Cheatsheet - MyBatis Annotation-Based & DSL-Based Mapper untuk PostgreSQL Enterprise

Materi ini adalah pasangan dari Mapper XML cheatsheet sebelumnya, tetapi fokus ke:

```text id="hefzvd"
1. Annotation-based mapper
   @Select, @Insert, @Update, @Delete
   @Results, @Result, @ResultMap
   @Options
   @SelectKey
   @SelectProvider, @InsertProvider, @UpdateProvider, @DeleteProvider
   @CacheNamespace, @Flush, @Lang

2. DSL-based mapper
   MyBatis Dynamic SQL Library
   SelectStatementProvider
   InsertStatementProvider
   UpdateStatementProvider
   DeleteStatementProvider
   RenderingStrategies.MYBATIS3
   MyBatis3Utils
```

MyBatis mendukung mapping SQL melalui XML descriptor **atau annotation**, sedangkan MyBatis Dynamic SQL adalah library terpisah yang menghasilkan SQL `SELECT`, `INSERT`, `UPDATE`, dan `DELETE` beserta parameter object yang bisa dipakai langsung oleh mapper MyBatis. ([GitHub](https://github.com/mybatis/mybatis-3?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 1. Kapan Pakai XML vs Annotation vs DSL?

| Style | Cocok Untuk | Hindari Untuk |
|---|---|---|
| **XML Mapper** | SQL kompleks, CTE panjang, PostgreSQL-specific SQL, `resultMap` kompleks, procedure call kompleks | Mapper kecil/simple jika tim ingin Java-only |
| **Annotation Literal** | Query pendek, lookup sederhana, update sederhana, CTE singkat, `RETURNING` sederhana | SQL panjang, banyak dynamic branch |
| **Annotation + `<script>`** | Dynamic SQL ringan tanpa XML file | Dynamic SQL besar/spaghetti |
| **Provider Annotation** | SQL dibangun oleh Java provider method | Jika provider hanya jadi string concatenation liar |
| **MyBatis Dynamic SQL DSL** | Query composable, type-safe-ish, criteria object, CRUD repetitive, search dengan banyak optional predicate | PostgreSQL CTE/procedure/SQL dialect khusus yang tidak nyaman diekspresikan di DSL |
| **Hybrid** | DSL untuk CRUD/search umum; annotation/XML untuk CTE/procedure/advanced PostgreSQL | Jika boundary tidak jelas |

**Rekomendasi enterprise untuk stack kamu:**

```text id="3vnwpc"
Use XML:
  complex PostgreSQL SQL, CTE write, PL/pgSQL callable, complex resultMap

Use Annotation:
  simple CRUD, simple lookup, small RETURNING query, explicit operational mapper

Use MyBatis Dynamic SQL:
  safe composable search, criteria-based query, CRUD boilerplate, dynamic predicate
```

---

# 2. Annotation-Based Mapper - Available Options

MyBatis annotation package menyediakan annotation untuk SQL statement, provider method, result mapping, cache namespace, flush, parameter naming, nested mapping, result map reuse, dan options seperti timeout/fetchSize/generated key. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/apidocs/org/apache/ibatis/annotations/package-summary.html))

## 2.1 Main annotation list

| Annotation | XML Equivalent | Fungsi |
|---|---|---|
| `@Select` | `<select>` | SQL SELECT literal |
| `@Insert` | `<insert>` | SQL INSERT literal |
| `@Update` | `<update>` | SQL UPDATE literal |
| `@Delete` | `<delete>` | SQL DELETE literal |
| `@SelectProvider` | provider SQL | SELECT dari Java provider |
| `@InsertProvider` | provider SQL | INSERT dari Java provider / DSL |
| `@UpdateProvider` | provider SQL | UPDATE dari Java provider / DSL |
| `@DeleteProvider` | provider SQL | DELETE dari Java provider / DSL |
| `@Options` | statement attributes | timeout, fetchSize, useGeneratedKeys, statementType, cache |
| `@Results` | `<resultMap>` inline | grouping result mapping |
| `@Result` | `<result>` / `<id>` | column-property mapping |
| `@ResultMap` | `resultMap="..."` | reuse XML/named result map |
| `@ConstructorArgs` | `<constructor>` | constructor mapping |
| `@Arg` | `<arg>` / `<idArg>` | constructor arg mapping |
| `@SelectKey` | `<selectKey>` | generated key query |
| `@Param` | parameter name | named parameter |
| `@MapKey` | map key result | return `Map<K,V>` |
| `@CacheNamespace` | `<cache>` | mapper namespace cache |
| `@CacheNamespaceRef` | `<cache-ref>` | reference cache namespace |
| `@Flush` | `SqlSession.flushStatements()` | flush batch statements |
| `@Lang` | `lang` | custom language driver |

---

# 3. `@Options` Cheat Sheet

`@Options` adalah padanan utama untuk attribute XML seperti `timeout`, `fetchSize`, `statementType`, `resultSetType`, `useCache`, `flushCache`, `useGeneratedKeys`, `keyProperty`, `keyColumn`, `resultSets`, dan `databaseId`. Default penting: `statementType=PREPARED`, `fetchSize=-1`, `timeout=-1`, `useGeneratedKeys=false`, dan `useCache=true`. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/apidocs/org/apache/ibatis/annotations/Options.html))

```java id="2as2ns"
@Options(
    useCache = false,
    flushCache = Options.FlushCachePolicy.FALSE,
    timeout = 5,
    fetchSize = 500,
    statementType = StatementType.PREPARED,
    resultSetType = ResultSetType.FORWARD_ONLY
)
@Select("""
    SELECT
      id,
      tenant_id,
      case_number,
      status,
      priority,
      version,
      created_at,
      updated_at
    FROM cases
    WHERE tenant_id = #{tenantId}
      AND id = #{id}
    """)
CaseRecord findById(
    @Param("tenantId") UUID tenantId,
    @Param("id") UUID id
);
```

## `@Options` production mapping

| XML Attribute | Annotation Equivalent |
|---|---|
| `timeout="5"` | `@Options(timeout = 5)` |
| `fetchSize="500"` | `@Options(fetchSize = 500)` |
| `statementType="PREPARED"` | `@Options(statementType = StatementType.PREPARED)` |
| `statementType="CALLABLE"` | `@Options(statementType = StatementType.CALLABLE)` |
| `resultSetType="FORWARD_ONLY"` | `@Options(resultSetType = ResultSetType.FORWARD_ONLY)` |
| `useGeneratedKeys="true"` | `@Options(useGeneratedKeys = true)` |
| `keyProperty="id"` | `@Options(keyProperty = "id")` |
| `keyColumn="id"` | `@Options(keyColumn = "id")` |
| `useCache="false"` | `@Options(useCache = false)` |
| `flushCache="true"` | `@Options(flushCache = Options.FlushCachePolicy.TRUE)` |
| `databaseId="postgresql"` | `@Options(databaseId = "postgresql")` |

---

# 4. Annotation `@Select`

## 4.1 Simple lookup

```java id="91wu1y"
public interface CaseAnnotationMapper {

    @Options(
        useCache = false,
        timeout = 3,
        fetchSize = 1,
        statementType = StatementType.PREPARED
    )
    @Select("""
        SELECT
          id,
          tenant_id,
          case_number,
          status,
          priority,
          version,
          created_at,
          updated_at
        FROM cases
        WHERE tenant_id = #{tenantId}
          AND id = #{id}
        """)
    @Results(id = "CaseRecordResult", value = {
        @Result(property = "id", column = "id", id = true),
        @Result(property = "tenantId", column = "tenant_id"),
        @Result(property = "caseNumber", column = "case_number"),
        @Result(property = "status", column = "status"),
        @Result(property = "priority", column = "priority"),
        @Result(property = "version", column = "version"),
        @Result(property = "createdAt", column = "created_at"),
        @Result(property = "updatedAt", column = "updated_at")
    })
    CaseRecord findById(
        @Param("tenantId") UUID tenantId,
        @Param("id") UUID id
    );
}
```

`@Select` menerima array/string SQL dan sejak MyBatis 3.5.12 memiliki `affectData` untuk SELECT yang memodifikasi data, misalnya PostgreSQL `RETURNING`. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/apidocs/org/apache/ibatis/annotations/Select.html))

---

## 4.2 Reuse result mapping dengan `@ResultMap`

```java id="glvlyf"
@Select("""
    SELECT
      id,
      tenant_id,
      case_number,
      status,
      priority,
      version,
      created_at,
      updated_at
    FROM cases
    WHERE tenant_id = #{tenantId}
      AND id = #{id}
    """)
@ResultMap("com.company.app.persistence.mapper.CaseXmlMapper.CaseRecordResultMap")
CaseRecord findByIdUsingXmlResultMap(
    @Param("tenantId") UUID tenantId,
    @Param("id") UUID id
);
```

`@ResultMap` bisa menunjuk ke resultMap yang didefinisikan di XML, dan MyBatis menyatakan annotation ini akan override `@Results` atau `@ConstructorArgs` jika digunakan bersama pada annotated select. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/java-api.html))

**Practical recommendation:** kalau mapping kompleks, tetap gunakan XML `resultMap`, lalu annotation query cukup reuse `@ResultMap`.

---

# 5. Annotation Dynamic SQL dengan `<script>`

`@Select` dapat berisi dynamic SQL memakai `<script>` dan tag MyBatis seperti `<if>`, seperti contoh di dokumentasi API annotation. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/apidocs/org/apache/ibatis/annotations/Select.html))

```java id="t7o7ts"
@Options(timeout = 5, fetchSize = 500, useCache = false)
@Select("""
    <script>
    SELECT
      id,
      tenant_id,
      case_number,
      status,
      priority,
      due_at
    FROM cases
    <where>
      tenant_id = #{criteria.tenantId}

      <if test="criteria.status != null">
        AND status = #{criteria.status.name()}
      </if>

      <if test="criteria.priority != null">
        AND priority = #{criteria.priority.name()}
      </if>

      <if test="criteria.assignedTeam != null">
        AND assigned_team = #{criteria.assignedTeam}
      </if>
    </where>
    ORDER BY due_at ASC, id ASC
    LIMIT #{criteria.limit}
    </script>
    """)
@Results(id = "CaseQueueItemResult", value = {
    @Result(property = "id", column = "id", id = true),
    @Result(property = "tenantId", column = "tenant_id"),
    @Result(property = "caseNumber", column = "case_number"),
    @Result(property = "status", column = "status"),
    @Result(property = "priority", column = "priority"),
    @Result(property = "dueAt", column = "due_at")
})
List<CaseQueueItemDto> searchQueueItems(
    @Param("criteria") CaseSearchCriteria criteria
);
```

**Rule:** annotation `<script>` boleh untuk dynamic ringan. Jika sudah panjang, pindahkan ke XML atau MyBatis Dynamic SQL.

---

# 6. Annotation Dynamic Sorting - Safe Whitelist

Jangan lakukan ini:

```java id="ptl45n"
@Select("""
    SELECT *
    FROM cases
    ORDER BY ${sort}
    """)
List<CaseRecord> unsafeSearch(@Param("sort") String sort);
```

Gunakan enum whitelist:

```java id="douuag"
public enum CaseSortField {
    DUE_AT("due_at"),
    CREATED_AT("created_at"),
    PRIORITY("priority");

    private final String column;

    CaseSortField(String column) {
        this.column = column;
    }

    public String column() {
        return column;
    }
}
```

```java id="o08hhq"
public record CaseSearchCriteria(
    UUID tenantId,
    CaseStatus status,
    CaseSortField sortField,
    SortDirection direction,
    int limit
) {}
```

```java id="ed8s3g"
@Select("""
    <script>
    SELECT
      id,
      tenant_id,
      case_number,
      status,
      priority,
      due_at
    FROM cases
    WHERE tenant_id = #{criteria.tenantId}

    <if test="criteria.status != null">
      AND status = #{criteria.status.name()}
    </if>

    ORDER BY ${criteria.sortField.column} ${criteria.direction}, id ASC
    LIMIT #{criteria.limit}
    </script>
    """)
List<CaseQueueItemDto> searchSafeSort(
    @Param("criteria") CaseSearchCriteria criteria
);
```

`#{}` adalah value binding, sedangkan `${}` adalah string substitution. MyBatis memperingatkan bahwa `${}` dengan input user yang tidak divalidasi dapat menyebabkan SQL injection. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/sqlmap-xml.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 7. Annotation `@Insert`

## 7.1 Insert dengan UUID app-generated

```java id="xpu6ff"
@Options(timeout = 5, flushCache = Options.FlushCachePolicy.TRUE)
@Insert("""
    INSERT INTO cases (
      id,
      tenant_id,
      case_number,
      status,
      priority,
      version,
      created_at,
      updated_at
    )
    VALUES (
      #{id},
      #{tenantId},
      #{caseNumber},
      #{status},
      #{priority},
      #{version},
      #{createdAt},
      #{updatedAt}
    )
    """)
int insertCase(CaseRecord record);
```

## 7.2 Insert dengan generated key

```java id="m5jhg7"
@Options(
    useGeneratedKeys = true,
    keyProperty = "id",
    keyColumn = "id",
    timeout = 5,
    flushCache = Options.FlushCachePolicy.TRUE
)
@Insert("""
    INSERT INTO audit_logs (
      tenant_id,
      actor_id,
      action,
      aggregate_type,
      aggregate_id,
      created_at
    )
    VALUES (
      #{tenantId},
      #{actorId},
      #{action},
      #{aggregateType},
      #{aggregateId},
      #{createdAt}
    )
    """)
int insertAuditLog(AuditLogInsert insert);
```

`@Options` menyediakan `useGeneratedKeys`, `keyProperty`, dan `keyColumn` untuk generated key. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/apidocs/org/apache/ibatis/annotations/Options.html))

---

# 8. PostgreSQL `RETURNING` dengan Annotation

PostgreSQL mendukung `RETURNING` pada `INSERT`, `UPDATE`, `DELETE`, dan `MERGE`; ini menghindari query tambahan untuk mengambil data dari row yang dimodifikasi. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/dml-returning.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## 8.1 Insert returning

```java id="gjeig9"
@Select(
    value = """
        INSERT INTO cases (
          id,
          tenant_id,
          case_number,
          status,
          priority,
          version,
          created_at,
          updated_at
        )
        VALUES (
          #{id},
          #{tenantId},
          #{caseNumber},
          'DRAFT',
          #{priority},
          0,
          now(),
          now()
        )
        RETURNING
          id,
          tenant_id,
          case_number,
          status,
          priority,
          version,
          created_at,
          updated_at
        """,
    affectData = true
)
@Options(timeout = 5, flushCache = Options.FlushCachePolicy.TRUE)
@ResultMap("CaseRecordResult")
CaseRecord insertCaseReturning(CaseInsertCommand command);
```

## 8.2 Update returning

```java id="vj7hje"
@Select(
    value = """
        UPDATE cases
        SET
          status = 'APPROVED',
          approval_reason = #{reason},
          approved_by = #{actorId},
          approved_at = now(),
          version = version + 1,
          updated_at = now()
        WHERE tenant_id = #{tenantId}
          AND id = #{caseId}
          AND status = 'SUBMITTED'
          AND version = #{expectedVersion}
        RETURNING
          id,
          tenant_id,
          case_number,
          status,
          priority,
          version,
          created_at,
          updated_at
        """,
    affectData = true
)
@Options(timeout = 5, flushCache = Options.FlushCachePolicy.TRUE)
@ResultMap("CaseRecordResult")
CaseRecord approveReturning(ApproveCaseCommand command);
```

**Production note:** method return `null` berarti tidak ada row updated; treat as optimistic locking conflict.

---

# 9. Annotation `@Update` dan `@Delete`

## 9.1 State transition update

```java id="w59ic0"
@Options(timeout = 5, flushCache = Options.FlushCachePolicy.TRUE)
@Update("""
    UPDATE cases
    SET
      status = #{toStatus},
      version = version + 1,
      updated_at = now()
    WHERE tenant_id = #{tenantId}
      AND id = #{id}
      AND status = #{fromStatus}
      AND version = #{expectedVersion}
    """)
int transitionStatus(
    @Param("tenantId") UUID tenantId,
    @Param("id") UUID id,
    @Param("fromStatus") String fromStatus,
    @Param("toStatus") String toStatus,
    @Param("expectedVersion") long expectedVersion
);
```

## 9.2 Soft delete

```java id="yw8p11"
@Options(timeout = 5, flushCache = Options.FlushCachePolicy.TRUE)
@Update("""
    UPDATE cases
    SET
      deleted_at = now(),
      deleted_by = #{actorId},
      version = version + 1,
      updated_at = now()
    WHERE tenant_id = #{tenantId}
      AND id = #{id}
      AND deleted_at IS NULL
      AND version = #{expectedVersion}
    """)
int softDelete(
    @Param("tenantId") UUID tenantId,
    @Param("id") UUID id,
    @Param("actorId") UUID actorId,
    @Param("expectedVersion") long expectedVersion
);
```

## 9.3 Hard cleanup delete

```java id="ibxgzr"
@Options(timeout = 10, flushCache = Options.FlushCachePolicy.TRUE)
@Delete("""
    DELETE FROM idempotency_keys
    WHERE tenant_id = #{tenantId}
      AND status IN ('COMPLETED', 'FAILED')
      AND created_at < #{before}
    """)
int deleteExpiredIdempotencyKeys(
    @Param("tenantId") UUID tenantId,
    @Param("before") Instant before
);
```

---

# 10. Annotation CTE untuk PostgreSQL

PostgreSQL `WITH` CTE dapat berisi auxiliary statement `SELECT`, `INSERT`, `UPDATE`, `DELETE`, atau `MERGE`, dan primary statement juga bisa salah satu dari statement tersebut. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/queries-with.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## 10.1 CTE SELECT

```java id="muj586"
@Options(timeout = 10, fetchSize = 500, useCache = false)
@Select("""
    WITH eligible_cases AS (
      SELECT
        c.id,
        c.tenant_id,
        c.case_number,
        c.status,
        c.priority,
        c.due_at
      FROM cases c
      WHERE c.tenant_id = #{tenantId}
        AND c.status IN ('OPEN', 'IN_REVIEW')
        AND c.due_at < now()
        AND c.deleted_at IS NULL
    )
    SELECT
      id,
      tenant_id,
      case_number,
      status,
      priority,
      due_at
    FROM eligible_cases
    ORDER BY due_at ASC, id ASC
    LIMIT #{limit}
    """)
@Results(id = "EscalationCandidateResult", value = {
    @Result(property = "id", column = "id", id = true),
    @Result(property = "tenantId", column = "tenant_id"),
    @Result(property = "caseNumber", column = "case_number"),
    @Result(property = "status", column = "status"),
    @Result(property = "priority", column = "priority"),
    @Result(property = "dueAt", column = "due_at")
})
List<EscalationCandidateDto> findEscalationCandidates(
    @Param("tenantId") UUID tenantId,
    @Param("limit") int limit
);
```

## 10.2 CTE INSERT + outbox

```java id="nbhr6a"
@Options(timeout = 5, flushCache = Options.FlushCachePolicy.TRUE)
@Insert("""
    WITH inserted_case AS (
      INSERT INTO cases (
        id,
        tenant_id,
        case_number,
        status,
        priority,
        version,
        created_at,
        updated_at
      )
      VALUES (
        #{caseRecord.id},
        #{caseRecord.tenantId},
        #{caseRecord.caseNumber},
        'DRAFT',
        #{caseRecord.priority},
        0,
        now(),
        now()
      )
      RETURNING
        id,
        tenant_id,
        case_number,
        status
    )
    INSERT INTO outbox_events (
      id,
      tenant_id,
      aggregate_type,
      aggregate_id,
      event_type,
      payload,
      status,
      created_at
    )
    SELECT
      #{outboxId},
      ic.tenant_id,
      'CASE',
      ic.id::text,
      'CASE_CREATED',
      jsonb_build_object(
        'caseId', ic.id,
        'caseNumber', ic.case_number,
        'status', ic.status
      ),
      'PENDING',
      now()
    FROM inserted_case ic
    """)
int insertCaseAndOutbox(
    @Param("caseRecord") CaseRecord caseRecord,
    @Param("outboxId") UUID outboxId
);
```

## 10.3 CTE UPDATE claim queue

```java id="kxdqgb"
@Select(
    value = """
        WITH candidate AS (
          SELECT id
          FROM outbox_events
          WHERE status = 'PENDING'
            AND available_at <= now()
          ORDER BY available_at ASC, id ASC
          LIMIT #{limit}
          FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED
        ),
        claimed AS (
          UPDATE outbox_events o
          SET
            status = 'PROCESSING',
            locked_by = #{workerId},
            locked_at = now(),
            attempt_count = attempt_count + 1
          FROM candidate c
          WHERE o.id = c.id
          RETURNING
            o.id,
            o.tenant_id,
            o.aggregate_type,
            o.aggregate_id,
            o.event_type,
            o.payload,
            o.status,
            o.attempt_count,
            o.created_at
        )
        SELECT
          id,
          tenant_id,
          aggregate_type,
          aggregate_id,
          event_type,
          payload,
          status,
          attempt_count,
          created_at
        FROM claimed
        """,
    affectData = true
)
@Options(timeout = 5, fetchSize = 100, flushCache = Options.FlushCachePolicy.TRUE)
@ResultMap("OutboxEventResult")
List<OutboxEventRecord> claimOutboxEvents(
    @Param("workerId") String workerId,
    @Param("limit") int limit
);
```

## 10.4 CTE DELETE chunk

```java id="kkcexh"
@Select(
    value = """
        WITH candidate AS (
          SELECT id
          FROM outbox_events
          WHERE status = 'PUBLISHED'
            AND published_at < #{before}
          ORDER BY id
          LIMIT #{limit}
        ),
        deleted AS (
          DELETE FROM outbox_events o
          USING candidate c
          WHERE o.id = c.id
          RETURNING o.id
        )
        SELECT id
        FROM deleted
        """,
    affectData = true
)
@Options(timeout = 10, flushCache = Options.FlushCachePolicy.TRUE)
List<UUID> deleteOldOutboxChunk(
    @Param("before") Instant before,
    @Param("limit") int limit
);
```

# 11. Stored Procedure / PL/pgSQL dengan Annotation

PostgreSQL `CALL` menjalankan procedure, dan jika procedure punya output parameter maka `CALL` mengembalikan satu result row berisi nilai output parameter tersebut. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-call.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## 11.1 Procedure

```sql id="44x3mf"
CREATE OR REPLACE PROCEDURE approve_case_proc(
    IN p_tenant_id uuid,
    IN p_case_id uuid,
    IN p_actor_id uuid,
    IN p_expected_version bigint,
    OUT p_updated integer,
    OUT p_new_version bigint
)
LANGUAGE plpgsql
AS $$
BEGIN
    UPDATE cases
    SET
      status = 'APPROVED',
      approved_by = p_actor_id,
      approved_at = now(),
      version = version + 1,
      updated_at = now()
    WHERE tenant_id = p_tenant_id
      AND id = p_case_id
      AND status = 'SUBMITTED'
      AND version = p_expected_version;

    GET DIAGNOSTICS p_updated = ROW_COUNT;

    IF p_updated = 1 THEN
      SELECT version
      INTO p_new_version
      FROM cases
      WHERE tenant_id = p_tenant_id
        AND id = p_case_id;
    ELSE
      p_new_version := NULL;
    END IF;
END;
$$;
```

## 11.2 Annotation callable mapper

```java id="unccpl"
@Options(
    statementType = StatementType.CALLABLE,
    timeout = 5,
    flushCache = Options.FlushCachePolicy.TRUE
)
@Update("""
    { CALL approve_case_proc(
        #{tenantId, mode=IN, jdbcType=OTHER},
        #{caseId, mode=IN, jdbcType=OTHER},
        #{actorId, mode=IN, jdbcType=OTHER},
        #{expectedVersion, mode=IN, jdbcType=BIGINT},
        #{updated, mode=OUT, jdbcType=INTEGER},
        #{newVersion, mode=OUT, jdbcType=BIGINT}
      )
    }
    """)
void approveCaseProcedure(ApproveCaseProcedureParams params);
```

`@Options.statementType` dapat diset ke `CALLABLE`; inline parameter MyBatis mendukung `mode=IN`, `OUT`, dan `INOUT` sebagaimana pada Mapper XML. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/apidocs/org/apache/ibatis/annotations/Options.html))

---

# 12. `@SelectKey`, `@Flush`, `@MapKey`

## 12.1 `@SelectKey`

```java id="lnqdeu"
@Insert("""
    INSERT INTO cases (
      id,
      tenant_id,
      case_number,
      status
    )
    VALUES (
      #{id},
      #{tenantId},
      #{caseNumber},
      'DRAFT'
    )
    """)
@SelectKey(
    statement = "SELECT gen_random_uuid()",
    keyProperty = "id",
    before = true,
    resultType = UUID.class,
    statementType = StatementType.PREPARED
)
int insertCaseWithSelectKey(CaseInsertCommand command);
```

`@SelectKey` menyediakan SQL statement untuk mengambil key, `keyProperty`, apakah dijalankan `before`/after insert, `resultType`, dan `statementType`; default `statementType` adalah `PREPARED`. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/java-api.html))

## 12.2 `@Flush`

```java id="3gkmsy"
@Flush
List<BatchResult> flush();
```

`@Flush` dapat digunakan di mapper interface untuk memanggil `SqlSession#flushStatements()`. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/java-api.html))

## 12.3 `@MapKey`

```java id="ml97mr"
@MapKey("id")
@Select("""
    SELECT
      id,
      tenant_id,
      case_number,
      status
    FROM cases
    WHERE tenant_id = #{tenantId}
    """)
Map<UUID, CaseRecord> findCasesAsMap(@Param("tenantId") UUID tenantId);
```

# 13. Annotation Cache

```java id="swanc8"
@CacheNamespace(
    implementation = PerpetualCache.class,
    eviction = LruCache.class,
    size = 512,
    readWrite = true,
    blocking = false
)
public interface ReferenceDataMapper {

    @Options(useCache = true, flushCache = Options.FlushCachePolicy.FALSE)
    @Select("""
        SELECT code, label
        FROM reference_status
        ORDER BY code
        """)
    List<ReferenceStatusDto> findStatuses();
}
```

**Production recommendation:** cache namespace hanya untuk reference data kecil dan jarang berubah. Hindari cache untuk mutable tenant/business data.

---

# 14. Provider Annotation

Provider annotation memindahkan SQL ke Java class. `@SelectProvider` menerima `type`/`value`, `method`, `databaseId`, dan `affectData`; sejak MyBatis 3.5.1 method bisa diomit jika provider menerapkan resolver atau memiliki fallback `provideSql`. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/apidocs/org/apache/ibatis/annotations/SelectProvider.html))

## 14.1 Simple provider

```java id="lq7wug"
public interface CaseProviderMapper {

    @Options(timeout = 5, useCache = false)
    @SelectProvider(type = CaseSqlProvider.class, method = "searchCases")
    @ResultMap("CaseRecordResult")
    List<CaseRecord> searchCases(@Param("criteria") CaseSearchCriteria criteria);
}
```

```java id="7a0z3l"
public final class CaseSqlProvider {

    public static String searchCases() {
        return """
            <script>
            SELECT
              id,
              tenant_id,
              case_number,
              status,
              priority,
              version,
              created_at,
              updated_at
            FROM cases
            <where>
              tenant_id = #{criteria.tenantId}

              <if test="criteria.status != null">
                AND status = #{criteria.status.name()}
              </if>

              <if test="criteria.priority != null">
                AND priority = #{criteria.priority.name()}
              </if>
            </where>
            ORDER BY created_at DESC, id DESC
            LIMIT #{criteria.limit}
            </script>
            """;
    }
}
```

## 14.2 Provider caveat

```text id="tuuhv7"
Provider is not automatically safe.

Bad provider:
  string concatenates raw user input

Good provider:
  returns SQL with #{...} bindings
  uses whitelist for identifiers
  tested with mapper integration test
```

---

# 15. MyBatis Dynamic SQL DSL - Core Concept

MyBatis Dynamic SQL adalah typesafe SQL templating library yang menghasilkan SQL statement penuh beserta parameter object, dan statement object itu bisa langsung dipakai sebagai parameter mapper MyBatis. Library ini menghasilkan `COUNT`, `DELETE`, `INSERT`, `SELECT`, dan `UPDATE`, termasuk SELECT dengan flexible column list, WHERE, GROUP BY, JOIN, UNION, ORDER BY, dan lain-lain. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-dynamic-sql/docs/introduction.html))

## 15.1 Dependency

```xml id="f5fsew"
<dependency>
  <groupId>org.mybatis.dynamic-sql</groupId>
  <artifactId>mybatis-dynamic-sql</artifactId>
  <version>${mybatis.dynamic.sql.version}</version>
</dependency>
```

Versi MyBatis Dynamic SQL 2.0.0 memindahkan minimum Java ke Java 17; jika masih memakai Java 8, dokumentasi menyebut gunakan rilis 1.x line. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-dynamic-sql/docs/CHANGELOG.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 16. DSL Table Model

```java id="9ym8eh"
public final class CaseDynamicSqlSupport {

    public static final CaseTable cases = new CaseTable();

    public static final SqlColumn<UUID> id = cases.id;
    public static final SqlColumn<UUID> tenantId = cases.tenantId;
    public static final SqlColumn<String> caseNumber = cases.caseNumber;
    public static final SqlColumn<String> status = cases.status;
    public static final SqlColumn<String> priority = cases.priority;
    public static final SqlColumn<Long> version = cases.version;
    public static final SqlColumn<Instant> createdAt = cases.createdAt;
    public static final SqlColumn<Instant> updatedAt = cases.updatedAt;
    public static final SqlColumn<Instant> dueAt = cases.dueAt;

    public static final class CaseTable extends SqlTable {
        public final SqlColumn<UUID> id = column("id");
        public final SqlColumn<UUID> tenantId = column("tenant_id");
        public final SqlColumn<String> caseNumber = column("case_number");
        public final SqlColumn<String> status = column("status");
        public final SqlColumn<String> priority = column("priority");
        public final SqlColumn<Long> version = column("version");
        public final SqlColumn<Instant> createdAt = column("created_at");
        public final SqlColumn<Instant> updatedAt = column("updated_at");
        public final SqlColumn<Instant> dueAt = column("due_at");

        public CaseTable() {
            super("cases");
        }
    }

    private CaseDynamicSqlSupport() {}
}
```

Dynamic SQL library merepresentasikan table/view dengan `SqlTable` dan kolom dengan `SqlColumn`. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-dynamic-sql/docs/databaseObjects.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 17. DSL Mapper Interface

MyBatis Dynamic SQL specialized support menyediakan common mapper untuk count, delete, insert, update, dan select, serta bisa mengurangi boilerplate CRUD mapper. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-dynamic-sql/docs/mybatis3.html))

```java id="3o78sj"
public interface CaseDynamicMapper {

    @SelectProvider(type = SqlProviderAdapter.class, method = "select")
    @Results(id = "CaseRecordResult", value = {
        @Result(property = "id", column = "id", id = true),
        @Result(property = "tenantId", column = "tenant_id"),
        @Result(property = "caseNumber", column = "case_number"),
        @Result(property = "status", column = "status"),
        @Result(property = "priority", column = "priority"),
        @Result(property = "version", column = "version"),
        @Result(property = "createdAt", column = "created_at"),
        @Result(property = "updatedAt", column = "updated_at")
    })
    List<CaseRecord> selectMany(SelectStatementProvider selectStatement);

    @SelectProvider(type = SqlProviderAdapter.class, method = "select")
    @ResultMap("CaseRecordResult")
    Optional<CaseRecord> selectOne(SelectStatementProvider selectStatement);

    @InsertProvider(type = SqlProviderAdapter.class, method = "insert")
    int insert(InsertStatementProvider<CaseRecord> insertStatement);

    @UpdateProvider(type = SqlProviderAdapter.class, method = "update")
    int update(UpdateStatementProvider updateStatement);

    @DeleteProvider(type = SqlProviderAdapter.class, method = "delete")
    int delete(DeleteStatementProvider deleteStatement);
}
```

---

# 18. DSL SELECT

```java id="d51c8s"
public List<CaseRecord> searchCases(CaseSearchCriteria criteria) {
    SelectStatementProvider statement =
        select(
            id,
            tenantId,
            caseNumber,
            status,
            priority,
            version,
            createdAt,
            updatedAt
        )
        .from(cases)
        .where(tenantId, isEqualTo(criteria.tenantId()))
        .and(status, isEqualToWhenPresent(
            criteria.status() == null ? null : criteria.status().name()
        ))
        .and(priority, isEqualToWhenPresent(
            criteria.priority() == null ? null : criteria.priority().name()
        ))
        .orderBy(dueAt, id)
        .limit(criteria.limit())
        .build()
        .render(RenderingStrategies.MYBATIS3);

    return mapper.selectMany(statement);
}
```

Dynamic SQL select mendukung limit/offset untuk paging, tetapi renderer menulis klausa limit/offset apa adanya, jadi user harus memastikan database target mendukung syntax tersebut; PostgreSQL mendukung `LIMIT/OFFSET`. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-dynamic-sql/docs/select.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 19. DSL INSERT

```java id="ga5nvu"
public int insertCase(CaseRecord record) {
    InsertStatementProvider<CaseRecord> statement =
        insert(record)
            .into(cases)
            .map(id).toProperty("id")
            .map(tenantId).toProperty("tenantId")
            .map(caseNumber).toProperty("caseNumber")
            .map(status).toProperty("status")
            .map(priority).toProperty("priority")
            .map(version).toProperty("version")
            .map(createdAt).toProperty("createdAt")
            .map(updatedAt).toProperty("updatedAt")
            .build()
            .render(RenderingStrategies.MYBATIS3);

    return mapper.insert(statement);
}
```

Dynamic SQL insert mendukung single-row insert, multi-row insert, JDBC batch insert, general insert, dan insert-select. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-dynamic-sql/docs/insert.html))

## 19.1 Insert selective

```java id="e9j74h"
InsertStatementProvider<CaseRecord> statement =
    insert(record)
        .into(cases)
        .map(id).toProperty("id")
        .map(tenantId).toProperty("tenantId")
        .map(caseNumber).toProperty("caseNumber")
        .map(status).toProperty("status")
        .map(priority).toPropertyWhenPresent("priority", record::priority)
        .map(createdAt).toProperty("createdAt")
        .map(updatedAt).toProperty("updatedAt")
        .build()
        .render(RenderingStrategies.MYBATIS3);
```

Dynamic SQL insert mapping menyediakan `toNull`, `toConstant`, `toStringConstant`, `toValue`, dan `toValueWhenPresent`; value mapping akan dibind sebagai prepared statement parameter. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-dynamic-sql/docs/insert.html))

---

# 20. DSL UPDATE

```java id="yp4zc3"
public int transitionStatus(
    UUID tenantIdValue,
    UUID caseId,
    String fromStatus,
    String toStatus,
    long expectedVersion
) {
    UpdateStatementProvider statement =
        update(cases)
            .set(status).equalTo(toStatus)
            .set(version).equalToConstant("version + 1")
            .set(updatedAt).equalToConstant("now()")
            .where(tenantId, isEqualTo(tenantIdValue))
            .and(id, isEqualTo(caseId))
            .and(status, isEqualTo(fromStatus))
            .and(version, isEqualTo(expectedVersion))
            .build()
            .render(RenderingStrategies.MYBATIS3);

    return mapper.update(statement);
}
```

Dynamic SQL update mendukung `set(column).equalTo(...)`, `equalToWhenPresent(...)`, `equalToNull()`, constants, subquery, dan assignment ke kolom lain; dokumentasi juga memperingatkan update tanpa WHERE akan meng-update semua row. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-dynamic-sql/docs/update.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## 20.1 Patch update selective

```java id="jfxuqb"
public int patchCase(CasePatch patch) {
    if (patch.isEmpty()) {
        throw new EmptyPatchException();
    }

    UpdateStatementProvider statement =
        update(cases)
            .set(priority).equalToWhenPresent(
                patch.priority() == null ? null : patch.priority().name()
            )
            .set(updatedAt).equalToConstant("now()")
            .set(version).equalToConstant("version + 1")
            .where(tenantId, isEqualTo(patch.tenantId()))
            .and(id, isEqualTo(patch.caseId()))
            .and(version, isEqualTo(patch.expectedVersion()))
            .build()
            .render(RenderingStrategies.MYBATIS3);

    return mapper.update(statement);
}
```

---

# 21. DSL DELETE

```java id="tipu9x"
public int deleteExpiredIdempotencyKeys(
    UUID tenantIdValue,
    Instant before
) {
    DeleteStatementProvider statement =
        deleteFrom(idempotencyKeys)
            .where(idempotencyTenantId, isEqualTo(tenantIdValue))
            .and(idempotencyCreatedAt, isLessThan(before))
            .and(idempotencyStatus, isIn("COMPLETED", "FAILED"))
            .build()
            .render(RenderingStrategies.MYBATIS3);

    return idempotencyMapper.delete(statement);
}
```

Dynamic SQL delete statements terdiri dari table dan optional where clause; dokumentasi menunjukkan delete tanpa WHERE akan menghapus semua row, jadi production code harus punya guard untuk operasi delete. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-dynamic-sql/docs/delete.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 22. DSL `IN` Condition Safety

Dynamic SQL v1.5.2+ mengubah rendering `isIn` agar empty list menghasilkan `InvalidSqlException`, bukan condition hilang diam-diam; ini menghindari risiko operasi delete/update yang tanpa sengaja menjadi terlalu luas. `isInWhenPresent` dapat dipakai jika memang ingin condition di-drop saat list kosong/null. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-dynamic-sql/docs/conditions.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

```java id="l4qf9y"
public List<CaseRecord> findByIds(UUID tenantIdValue, List<UUID> ids) {
    if (ids == null || ids.isEmpty()) {
        return List.of();
    }

    if (ids.size() > 500) {
        throw new TooManyIdsException(ids.size());
    }

    SelectStatementProvider statement =
        select(id, tenantId, caseNumber, status, priority, version)
            .from(cases)
            .where(tenantId, isEqualTo(tenantIdValue))
            .and(id, isIn(ids))
            .orderBy(id)
            .build()
            .render(RenderingStrategies.MYBATIS3);

    return mapper.selectMany(statement);
}
```

---

# 23. DSL Dynamic Criteria Pattern

```java id="46ez6p"
public List<CaseQueueItemDto> searchQueue(CaseSearchCriteria criteria) {
    SelectStatementProvider statement =
        select(id, tenantId, caseNumber, status, priority, dueAt)
            .from(cases)
            .where(tenantId, isEqualTo(criteria.tenantId()))
            .and(status, isEqualToWhenPresent(criteria.statusName()))
            .and(priority, isEqualToWhenPresent(criteria.priorityName()))
            .and(dueAt, isLessThanWhenPresent(criteria.dueBefore()))
            .orderBy(criteria.sortField().toSortSpecification())
            .limit(criteria.limit())
            .build()
            .render(RenderingStrategies.MYBATIS3);

    return caseQueryMapper.selectQueueItems(statement);
}
```

## Safe sort adapter

```java id="3jfj3d"
public enum CaseSortField {
    DUE_AT(CaseDynamicSqlSupport.dueAt),
    CREATED_AT(CaseDynamicSqlSupport.createdAt),
    PRIORITY(CaseDynamicSqlSupport.priority);

    private final SqlColumn<?> column;

    CaseSortField(SqlColumn<?> column) {
        this.column = column;
    }

    public SortSpecification toSortSpecification() {
        return column;
    }
}
```

**Note:** DSL mengurangi kebutuhan `${}` untuk dynamic sort karena sort bisa dibangun dari `SqlColumn` internal, bukan raw string request.

---

# 24. DSL vs PostgreSQL `RETURNING`

MyBatis Dynamic SQL docs fokus pada generated `SELECT`, `INSERT`, `UPDATE`, dan `DELETE` provider statements; untuk PostgreSQL `RETURNING`, terutama `UPDATE/DELETE/INSERT ... RETURNING` yang ingin mengembalikan row, pattern paling eksplisit dan mudah direview biasanya masih annotation literal atau XML dengan `@Select(affectData=true)` / `<select affectData="true">`. PostgreSQL sendiri mendukung `RETURNING` untuk DML dan MyBatis `@Select` punya `affectData` untuk kasus seperti PostgreSQL `RETURNING`. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-dynamic-sql/docs/introduction.html))

Recommended pattern:

```java id="2ph7zf"
@Select(
    value = """
        UPDATE cases
        SET
          status = 'APPROVED',
          version = version + 1,
          updated_at = now()
        WHERE tenant_id = #{tenantId}
          AND id = #{caseId}
          AND version = #{expectedVersion}
        RETURNING
          id,
          tenant_id,
          case_number,
          status,
          priority,
          version,
          created_at,
          updated_at
        """,
    affectData = true
)
@Options(timeout = 5, flushCache = Options.FlushCachePolicy.TRUE)
@ResultMap("CaseRecordResult")
CaseRecord approveReturning(ApproveCaseCommand command);
```

---

# 25. DSL vs PostgreSQL CTE

Untuk CTE PostgreSQL, terutama CTE write seperti `WITH candidate AS (...) UPDATE ... RETURNING`, annotation literal/XML sering lebih cocok. PostgreSQL CTE bisa mencakup SELECT/INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE/MERGE, sedangkan MyBatis Dynamic SQL documentation menekankan generated statement utama `SELECT/INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE` dan tidak menjadikan CTE PostgreSQL sebagai contoh first-class pattern. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/queries-with.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

**Recommended hybrid:**

| Use Case | Recommended |
|---|---|
| Simple dynamic search | DSL |
| Optional predicate | DSL |
| Safe sort by enum column | DSL |
| CTE SELECT panjang | Annotation/XML |
| CTE UPDATE claim queue | Annotation/XML |
| CTE INSERT + outbox | Annotation/XML |
| DML `RETURNING` row | Annotation/XML |
| PL/pgSQL `CALL` | Annotation/XML callable |
| Stored function `RETURNS TABLE` | Annotation/XML select |

---

# 26. DSL with Provider + Options

Provider mapper tetap bisa diberi `@Options`.

```java id="n95ef8"
@Options(timeout = 5, fetchSize = 500, useCache = false)
@SelectProvider(type = SqlProviderAdapter.class, method = "select")
@ResultMap("CaseRecordResult")
List<CaseRecord> selectMany(SelectStatementProvider selectStatement);
```

For update:

```java id="e8b7mf"
@Options(timeout = 5, flushCache = Options.FlushCachePolicy.TRUE)
@UpdateProvider(type = SqlProviderAdapter.class, method = "update")
int update(UpdateStatementProvider updateStatement);
```

For insert generated key:

```java id="g0htbn"
@Options(
    useGeneratedKeys = true,
    keyProperty = "row.id",
    keyColumn = "id",
    timeout = 5,
    flushCache = Options.FlushCachePolicy.TRUE
)
@InsertProvider(type = SqlProviderAdapter.class, method = "insert")
int insertGenerated(InsertStatementProvider<AuditLogInsert> insertStatement);
```

Dynamic SQL docs show annotated mapper methods use provider annotations such as `@InsertProvider(type=SqlProviderAdapter.class, method="insert")`, `@DeleteProvider(..., method="delete")`, `@UpdateProvider(..., method="update")`, and `@SelectProvider(..., method="select")`. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-dynamic-sql/docs/mybatis3.html))

---

# 27. Annotation vs DSL Side-by-Side

## XML equivalent

```xml id="5i09fi"
<select id="findById" resultMap="CaseRecordResultMap" timeout="3">
  SELECT ...
  FROM cases
  WHERE tenant_id = #{tenantId}
    AND id = #{id}
</select>
```

## Annotation equivalent

```java id="9gjjbo"
@Options(timeout = 3, useCache = false)
@Select("""
    SELECT ...
    FROM cases
    WHERE tenant_id = #{tenantId}
      AND id = #{id}
    """)
@ResultMap("CaseRecordResult")
CaseRecord findById(@Param("tenantId") UUID tenantId, @Param("id") UUID id);
```

## DSL equivalent

```java id="ttm0uk"
default Optional<CaseRecord> findById(UUID tenantIdValue, UUID idValue) {
    SelectStatementProvider statement =
        select(id, tenantId, caseNumber, status, priority, version, createdAt, updatedAt)
            .from(cases)
            .where(tenantId, isEqualTo(tenantIdValue))
            .and(id, isEqualTo(idValue))
            .build()
            .render(RenderingStrategies.MYBATIS3);

    return selectOne(statement);
}
```

---

# 28. Enterprise Recommended Hybrid Mapper Layout

```text id="hjrjxf"
persistence/mapper/
  CaseXmlMapper.java + CaseXmlMapper.xml
    - complex resultMap
    - CTE write
    - PL/pgSQL callable
    - PostgreSQL-specific queries

  CaseAnnotationMapper.java
    - simple lookup
    - simple update
    - small RETURNING query

  CaseDynamicMapper.java
    - MyBatis Dynamic SQL provider methods
    - composable criteria search
    - CRUD boilerplate

  CaseDynamicSqlSupport.java
    - table/column DSL model
```

**Boundary rule:**

```text id="ctn0cc"
Do not mix everything in one God Mapper.

Separate by intent:
- command
- query
- dynamic DSL
- procedure
- backfill/admin
```

---

# 29. Production Review Checklist - Annotation & DSL

| Area | Checklist | Required |
|---|---|---:|
| Annotation SQL | query short/readable | Ya |
| Annotation dynamic SQL | not too large | Ya |
| `@Options.timeout` | set for important statements | Ya |
| `@Options.fetchSize` | set for large query | Bila perlu |
| `@Options.statementType` | `CALLABLE` only for procedure | Ya |
| `@Options.useGeneratedKeys` | correct keyProperty/keyColumn | Bila perlu |
| `@Select(affectData=true)` | used for PostgreSQL `RETURNING` DML | Ya |
| `@Results` | named and reused where possible | Ya |
| `@ResultMap` | used for complex XML resultMap reuse | Ya |
| `@Param` | used for multi-parameter methods | Ya |
| Provider | no raw user string concat | Ya |
| DSL | criteria object validated before building SQL | Ya |
| DSL `isIn` | empty list handled intentionally | Ya |
| DSL update/delete | WHERE guard always present | Ya |
| PostgreSQL CTE | prefer annotation/XML, tested | Ya |
| Procedure | callable param mode/jdbcType explicit | Ya |
| Tenant | tenant predicate mandatory | Ya |
| Test | mapper integration against PostgreSQL | Ya |

---

# 30. Anti-Pattern

| Anti-Pattern | Why Dangerous |
|---|---|
| Long 200-line SQL inside `@Select` | hard to review |
| Raw string concatenation in provider | SQL injection |
| Raw request field converted to `sortColumn(String)` | injection/invalid SQL risk |
| DSL delete without where | deletes all rows |
| DSL update without tenant/version guard | data corruption |
| Annotation method without `@Param` for multi-args | parameter binding bug |
| `@Results` duplicated everywhere | mapping drift |
| Procedure `OUT` params ignored | invalid state accepted |
| `@Select(affectData=true)` forgotten for DML returning | transaction/cache semantics risk |
| Using DSL for PostgreSQL-specific CTE just to avoid XML | less readable |
| Cache annotation on mutable tenant data | stale/security risk |
| `@Options(timeout=-1)` default for critical query | stuck request risk |

---

# 31. Decision Table

| Need | Best Style |
|---|---|
| Simple lookup | Annotation or DSL |
| Simple insert/update/delete | Annotation or DSL |
| Criteria-based search | DSL |
| Dynamic optional filters | DSL |
| Safe dynamic sort | DSL enum/column mapping |
| Short CTE query | Annotation |
| Long CTE write | XML |
| `INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE RETURNING` | Annotation/XML with `affectData=true` |
| PL/pgSQL procedure call | Annotation/XML `CALLABLE` |
| Complex nested resultMap | XML resultMap + `@ResultMap` if needed |
| Generated key | `@Options(useGeneratedKeys=true)` or XML |
| Batch flush | `@Flush` |
| Reference data cache | `@CacheNamespace` or XML `<cache>` with strict review |
| PostgreSQL-specific SQL | Annotation/XML |
| Repetitive CRUD | Dynamic SQL DSL |

---

# 32. Roadmap Belajar Singkat

| Urutan | Materi | Target |
|---:|---|---|
| 1 | Basic annotations | `@Select`, `@Insert`, `@Update`, `@Delete` |
| 2 | `@Options` | timeout, fetchSize, generated keys, statement type |
| 3 | `@Results` / `@ResultMap` | mapping aman |
| 4 | Annotation `<script>` | dynamic ringan |
| 5 | Provider annotations | SQL provider pattern |
| 6 | MyBatis Dynamic SQL table model | `SqlTable`, `SqlColumn` |
| 7 | DSL SELECT/INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE | CRUD/search composable |
| 8 | DSL safety | empty IN, where guard, criteria validation |
| 9 | PostgreSQL-specific escape hatch | CTE/RETURNING/procedure via annotation/XML |
| 10 | Testing | annotation/DSL mapper integration test |
