## Response:
7/15/2026, 1:31:48 PM

Lanjut ke deep dive MyBatis Dynamic SQL Library: fokusnya membuat query builder yang aman, reusable, dan tetap mudah direview untuk enterprise PostgreSQL, tanpa berubah menjadi string-concatenation framework.

# Cheatsheet - MyBatis Dynamic SQL Library Deep Dive: Enterprise Production-Grade untuk PostgreSQL

MyBatis Dynamic SQL adalah library terpisah dari MyBatis core untuk membangun SQL secara programmatic memakai Java DSL. Library ini menghasilkan statement `SELECT`, `INSERT`, `UPDATE`, `DELETE`, `COUNT`, beserta parameter object yang bisa langsung diberikan ke mapper MyBatis. Dokumentasi resminya menyebut library ini sebagai **typesafe SQL templating library** yang mendukung MyBatis3 dan Spring JDBC template. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-dynamic-sql/docs/introduction.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 1. Kapan Dynamic SQL Library Layak Dipakai?

Gunakan MyBatis Dynamic SQL untuk:

```text id="yk9xni"
- criteria-based search
- optional predicates
- dynamic sorting yang aman
- CRUD repetitive
- selective insert/update
- count/delete/update completer
- reusable predicate
- mapper boilerplate reduction
```

Jangan paksa Dynamic SQL untuk semua SQL. Dokumentasi `SELECT` sendiri menyatakan library ini meniru syntax SELECT yang umum, tetapi memang tidak berusaha mencakup semua kemungkinan SQL. Untuk PostgreSQL-specific SQL seperti CTE write, `RETURNING`, `CALL` procedure, `FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED`, window/report query kompleks, biasanya XML atau annotation literal lebih mudah direview. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-dynamic-sql/docs/select.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 2. Mental Model

```mermaid id="4sj7tw"
flowchart TD
    A[HTTP Request DTO] --> B[Validated Criteria Object]
    B --> C[Query Repository]
    C --> D[MyBatis Dynamic SQL Builder]
    D --> E[StatementProvider]
    E --> F[Mapper Provider Method]
    F --> G[SqlProviderAdapter]
    G --> H[(PostgreSQL)]

    I[DynamicSqlSupport Table Model] --> D
    J[Reusable Predicates] --> D
    K[Safe Sort Enum] --> D
    L[TypeHandler / JDBCType] --> I
```

Core invariant:

```text id="gwwpzd"
Raw request tidak boleh langsung membangun SQL.

Flow aman:
  Request DTO
    -> validation / normalization / whitelist
    -> Criteria object
    -> DSL builder
    -> StatementProvider
    -> Mapper provider
```

---

# 3. Setup Dependency

```xml id="gvvb3a"
<dependency>
  <groupId>org.mybatis.dynamic-sql</groupId>
  <artifactId>mybatis-dynamic-sql</artifactId>
  <version>${mybatis.dynamic.sql.version}</version>
</dependency>
```

Workflow resmi MyBatis Dynamic SQL terdiri dari tiga langkah utama: membuat object table/column, membuat mapper untuk MyBatis3, lalu menulis dan memakai SQL. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-dynamic-sql/docs/quickStart.html))

---

# 4. Recommended Package Layout

```text id="92ldo4"
com.company.app.persistence.dynamic
  CaseDynamicSqlSupport.java
  OutboxDynamicSqlSupport.java
  AuditDynamicSqlSupport.java

com.company.app.persistence.mapper.dynamic
  CaseDynamicMapper.java
  OutboxDynamicMapper.java
  AuditDynamicMapper.java

com.company.app.persistence.repository
  MyBatisCaseQueryRepository.java
  MyBatisCaseCommandRepository.java

com.company.app.application.query
  CaseSearchCriteria.java
  CaseSortField.java
  SortDirection.java
  CaseSearchCriteriaFactory.java
```

Practical rule:

```text id="g85y8c"
DynamicSqlSupport:
  table/column model only

DynamicMapper:
  provider methods only

Repository:
  builds DSL from validated criteria

Resource:
  never builds DSL directly
```

---

# 5. Table Model / DynamicSqlSupport

Dokumentasi menyebut MyBatis Dynamic SQL memakai Java object untuk merepresentasikan table/view; `SqlTable` menyimpan nama table dan kumpulan `SqlColumn`, sedangkan `AliasableSqlTable` direkomendasikan untuk fleksibilitas alias dan runtime table name. Column object memuat Java type, nama column, JDBC type, dan optional type handler. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-dynamic-sql/docs/databaseObjects.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## 5.1 Case table

```java id="n6bp5f"
package com.company.app.persistence.dynamic;

import static java.sql.JDBCType.BIGINT;
import static java.sql.JDBCType.TIMESTAMP_WITH_TIMEZONE;
import static java.sql.JDBCType.VARCHAR;
import static java.sql.JDBCType.OTHER;

import java.sql.JDBCType;
import java.time.Instant;
import java.util.UUID;

import org.mybatis.dynamic.sql.AliasableSqlTable;
import org.mybatis.dynamic.sql.SqlColumn;

public final class CaseDynamicSqlSupport {

    public static final CaseTable cases = new CaseTable();

    public static final SqlColumn<UUID> id = cases.id;
    public static final SqlColumn<UUID> tenantId = cases.tenantId;
    public static final SqlColumn<String> caseNumber = cases.caseNumber;
    public static final SqlColumn<String> status = cases.status;
    public static final SqlColumn<String> priority = cases.priority;
    public static final SqlColumn<String> assignedTeam = cases.assignedTeam;
    public static final SqlColumn<Long> version = cases.version;
    public static final SqlColumn<Instant> dueAt = cases.dueAt;
    public static final SqlColumn<Instant> createdAt = cases.createdAt;
    public static final SqlColumn<Instant> updatedAt = cases.updatedAt;
    public static final SqlColumn<Instant> deletedAt = cases.deletedAt;

    public static final class CaseTable extends AliasableSqlTable<CaseTable> {

        public final SqlColumn<UUID> id = column("id", OTHER);
        public final SqlColumn<UUID> tenantId = column("tenant_id", OTHER);
        public final SqlColumn<String> caseNumber = column("case_number", VARCHAR);
        public final SqlColumn<String> status = column("status", VARCHAR);
        public final SqlColumn<String> priority = column("priority", VARCHAR);
        public final SqlColumn<String> assignedTeam = column("assigned_team", VARCHAR);
        public final SqlColumn<Long> version = column("version", BIGINT);
        public final SqlColumn<Instant> dueAt = column("due_at", TIMESTAMP_WITH_TIMEZONE);
        public final SqlColumn<Instant> createdAt = column("created_at", TIMESTAMP_WITH_TIMEZONE);
        public final SqlColumn<Instant> updatedAt = column("updated_at", TIMESTAMP_WITH_TIMEZONE);
        public final SqlColumn<Instant> deletedAt = column("deleted_at", TIMESTAMP_WITH_TIMEZONE);

        public CaseTable() {
            super("cases", CaseTable::new);
        }
    }

    private CaseDynamicSqlSupport() {}
}
```

## 5.2 JSONB column dengan TypeHandler

```java id="0wtqhz"
public final class OutboxDynamicSqlSupport {

    public static final OutboxTable outboxEvents = new OutboxTable();

    public static final SqlColumn<UUID> id = outboxEvents.id;
    public static final SqlColumn<UUID> tenantId = outboxEvents.tenantId;
    public static final SqlColumn<String> eventType = outboxEvents.eventType;
    public static final SqlColumn<JsonNode> payload = outboxEvents.payload;
    public static final SqlColumn<String> status = outboxEvents.status;
    public static final SqlColumn<Integer> attemptCount = outboxEvents.attemptCount;
    public static final SqlColumn<Instant> availableAt = outboxEvents.availableAt;
    public static final SqlColumn<Instant> createdAt = outboxEvents.createdAt;

    public static final class OutboxTable extends AliasableSqlTable<OutboxTable> {

        public final SqlColumn<UUID> id = column("id", JDBCType.OTHER);
        public final SqlColumn<UUID> tenantId = column("tenant_id", JDBCType.OTHER);
        public final SqlColumn<String> eventType = column("event_type", JDBCType.VARCHAR);

        public final SqlColumn<JsonNode> payload =
            column("payload", JDBCType.OTHER,
                "com.company.app.mybatis.JsonNodeJsonbTypeHandler");

        public final SqlColumn<String> status = column("status", JDBCType.VARCHAR);
        public final SqlColumn<Integer> attemptCount = column("attempt_count", JDBCType.INTEGER);
        public final SqlColumn<Instant> availableAt = column("available_at", JDBCType.TIMESTAMP_WITH_TIMEZONE);
        public final SqlColumn<Instant> createdAt = column("created_at", JDBCType.TIMESTAMP_WITH_TIMEZONE);

        public OutboxTable() {
            super("outbox_events", OutboxTable::new);
        }
    }

    private OutboxDynamicSqlSupport() {}
}
```

---

# 6. Mapper Provider Interface

Dynamic SQL mapper biasanya menerima `SelectStatementProvider`, `InsertStatementProvider`, `UpdateStatementProvider`, dan `DeleteStatementProvider`, lalu memakai `SqlProviderAdapter`. Dokumentasi MyBatis Dynamic SQL menunjukkan provider annotations seperti `@SelectProvider(... method="select")`, `@InsertProvider(... method="insert")`, `@UpdateProvider(... method="update")`, dan `@DeleteProvider(... method="delete")`. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-dynamic-sql/docs/mybatis3.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

```java id="glhy1j"
package com.company.app.persistence.mapper.dynamic;

import java.util.List;
import java.util.Optional;

import org.apache.ibatis.annotations.DeleteProvider;
import org.apache.ibatis.annotations.InsertProvider;
import org.apache.ibatis.annotations.Options;
import org.apache.ibatis.annotations.Result;
import org.apache.ibatis.annotations.ResultMap;
import org.apache.ibatis.annotations.Results;
import org.apache.ibatis.annotations.SelectProvider;
import org.apache.ibatis.annotations.UpdateProvider;
import org.mybatis.dynamic.sql.delete.render.DeleteStatementProvider;
import org.mybatis.dynamic.sql.insert.render.InsertStatementProvider;
import org.mybatis.dynamic.sql.select.render.SelectStatementProvider;
import org.mybatis.dynamic.sql.update.render.UpdateStatementProvider;
import org.mybatis.dynamic.sql.util.SqlProviderAdapter;

public interface CaseDynamicMapper {

    @SelectProvider(type = SqlProviderAdapter.class, method = "select")
    @Results(id = "CaseRecordResult", value = {
        @Result(property = "id", column = "id", id = true),
        @Result(property = "tenantId", column = "tenant_id"),
        @Result(property = "caseNumber", column = "case_number"),
        @Result(property = "status", column = "status"),
        @Result(property = "priority", column = "priority"),
        @Result(property = "assignedTeam", column = "assigned_team"),
        @Result(property = "version", column = "version"),
        @Result(property = "dueAt", column = "due_at"),
        @Result(property = "createdAt", column = "created_at"),
        @Result(property = "updatedAt", column = "updated_at")
    })
    List<CaseRecord> selectMany(SelectStatementProvider selectStatement);

    @SelectProvider(type = SqlProviderAdapter.class, method = "select")
    @ResultMap("CaseRecordResult")
    Optional<CaseRecord> selectOne(SelectStatementProvider selectStatement);

    @InsertProvider(type = SqlProviderAdapter.class, method = "insert")
    @Options(timeout = 5)
    int insert(InsertStatementProvider<CaseRecord> insertStatement);

    @UpdateProvider(type = SqlProviderAdapter.class, method = "update")
    @Options(timeout = 5)
    int update(UpdateStatementProvider updateStatement);

    @DeleteProvider(type = SqlProviderAdapter.class, method = "delete")
    @Options(timeout = 10)
    int delete(DeleteStatementProvider deleteStatement);
}
```

Rendering strategy menentukan format placeholder parameter. Untuk MyBatis3, `RenderingStrategies.MYBATIS3` menghasilkan placeholder seperti `#{foo,jdbcType=INTEGER}`. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-dynamic-sql/docs/extending.html))

---

# 7. Criteria Object Pattern

## 7.1 Request DTO

```java id="0ft3y6"
public record CaseSearchRequest(
    String status,
    String priority,
    String assignedTeam,
    Instant dueBefore,
    Instant createdFrom,
    Instant createdTo,
    String sort,
    String direction,
    Integer limit
) {}
```

## 7.2 Safe Criteria

```java id="45vjnm"
public record CaseSearchCriteria(
    UUID tenantId,
    CaseStatus status,
    CasePriority priority,
    String assignedTeam,
    Instant dueBefore,
    Instant createdFrom,
    Instant createdTo,
    CaseSortField sortField,
    SortDirection direction,
    int limit
) {
    public String statusName() {
        return status == null ? null : status.name();
    }

    public String priorityName() {
        return priority == null ? null : priority.name();
    }
}
```

## 7.3 Criteria factory

```java id="pzv4lh"
public final class CaseSearchCriteriaFactory {

    private static final int DEFAULT_LIMIT = 50;
    private static final int MAX_LIMIT = 200;

    private CaseSearchCriteriaFactory() {}

    public static CaseSearchCriteria from(
        CaseSearchRequest request,
        UUID tenantId
    ) {
        return new CaseSearchCriteria(
            tenantId,
            parseEnum(request.status(), CaseStatus.class),
            parseEnum(request.priority(), CasePriority.class),
            normalizeTeam(request.assignedTeam()),
            request.dueBefore(),
            request.createdFrom(),
            request.createdTo(),
            CaseSortField.fromApiValue(request.sort()),
            SortDirection.fromApiValue(request.direction()),
            normalizeLimit(request.limit())
        );
    }

    private static int normalizeLimit(Integer limit) {
        if (limit == null) {
            return DEFAULT_LIMIT;
        }

        if (limit < 1 || limit > MAX_LIMIT) {
            throw new InvalidLimitException(limit);
        }

        return limit;
    }

    private static String normalizeTeam(String assignedTeam) {
        if (assignedTeam == null || assignedTeam.isBlank()) {
            return null;
        }

        String normalized = assignedTeam.trim();

        if (normalized.length() > 50) {
            throw new InvalidAssignedTeamException();
        }

        return normalized;
    }

    private static <E extends Enum<E>> E parseEnum(String value, Class<E> enumType) {
        if (value == null || value.isBlank()) {
            return null;
        }

        try {
            return Enum.valueOf(enumType, value.trim().toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT));
        } catch (IllegalArgumentException e) {
            throw new InvalidEnumValueException(enumType.getSimpleName(), value);
        }
    }
}
```

Production rule:

```text id="7gngd3"
Dynamic SQL builder hanya menerima Criteria yang sudah aman.

Jangan:
  repository.search(requestDto)
  mapper.select(providerFromRawRequest)
```

---

# 8. Safe Sorting dengan DSL

## 8.1 Sort enum

```java id="hdgklv"
import org.mybatis.dynamic.sql.SortSpecification;

import static com.company.app.persistence.dynamic.CaseDynamicSqlSupport.createdAt;
import static com.company.app.persistence.dynamic.CaseDynamicSqlSupport.dueAt;
import static com.company.app.persistence.dynamic.CaseDynamicSqlSupport.priority;
import static com.company.app.persistence.dynamic.CaseDynamicSqlSupport.updatedAt;

public enum CaseSortField {
    DUE_AT("dueAt", dueAt),
    CREATED_AT("createdAt", createdAt),
    UPDATED_AT("updatedAt", updatedAt),
    PRIORITY("priority", priority);

    private final String apiName;
    private final SortSpecification sortSpecification;

    CaseSortField(String apiName, SortSpecification sortSpecification) {
        this.apiName = apiName;
        this.sortSpecification = sortSpecification;
    }

    public SortSpecification ascendingSpec() {
        return sortSpecification;
    }

    public SortSpecification descendingSpec() {
        return sortSpecification.descending();
    }

    public static CaseSortField fromApiValue(String value) {
        if (value == null || value.isBlank()) {
            return DUE_AT;
        }

        for (CaseSortField field : values()) {
            if (field.apiName.equals(value)) {
                return field;
            }
        }

        throw new InvalidSortFieldException(value);
    }
}
```

## 8.2 Direction enum

```java id="x2iz6z"
public enum SortDirection {
    ASC,
    DESC;

    public static SortDirection fromApiValue(String value) {
        if (value == null || value.isBlank()) {
            return ASC;
        }

        return switch (value.toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT)) {
            case "asc" -> ASC;
            case "desc" -> DESC;
            default -> throw new InvalidSortDirectionException(value);
        };
    }
}
```

## 8.3 Sort helper

```java id="fvh71q"
public final class CaseSort {

    private CaseSort() {}

    public static SortSpecification primary(CaseSearchCriteria criteria) {
        return criteria.direction() == SortDirection.DESC
            ? criteria.sortField().descendingSpec()
            : criteria.sortField().ascendingSpec();
    }
}
```

**Benefit:** tidak perlu `${sortColumn}`. Sort dibangun dari `SqlColumn`/`SortSpecification` internal, bukan raw string user.

---

# 9. SELECT Deep Dive

## 9.1 Find by tenant + id

```java id="r8hj1w"
import static org.mybatis.dynamic.sql.SqlBuilder.*;
import static org.mybatis.dynamic.sql.render.RenderingStrategies.MYBATIS3;
import static com.company.app.persistence.dynamic.CaseDynamicSqlSupport.*;

public Optional<CaseRecord> findById(UUID tenantIdValue, UUID caseId) {
    SelectStatementProvider statement =
        select(
            id,
            tenantId,
            caseNumber,
            status,
            priority,
            assignedTeam,
            version,
            dueAt,
            createdAt,
            updatedAt
        )
        .from(cases)
        .where(tenantId, isEqualTo(tenantIdValue))
        .and(id, isEqualTo(caseId))
        .and(deletedAt, isNull())
        .build()
        .render(MYBATIS3);

    return mapper.selectOne(statement);
}
```

## 9.2 Search dengan optional predicates

Conditions bisa digabung dengan `and` dan `or`, serta menyediakan operator umum seperti `isEqualTo`, `isGreaterThan`, `isBetween`, `isIn`, dan varian case-insensitive. Nilai pada condition dirender sebagai prepared statement parameter sesuai runtime target. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-dynamic-sql/docs/conditions.html))

```java id="bkul4n"
public List<CaseRecord> search(CaseSearchCriteria criteria) {
    SelectStatementProvider statement =
        select(
            id,
            tenantId,
            caseNumber,
            status,
            priority,
            assignedTeam,
            version,
            dueAt,
            createdAt,
            updatedAt
        )
        .from(cases)
        .where(tenantId, isEqualTo(criteria.tenantId()))
        .and(deletedAt, isNull())
        .and(status, isEqualToWhenPresent(criteria.statusName()))
        .and(priority, isEqualToWhenPresent(criteria.priorityName()))
        .and(assignedTeam, isEqualToWhenPresent(criteria.assignedTeam()))
        .and(dueAt, isLessThanWhenPresent(criteria.dueBefore()))
        .and(createdAt, isGreaterThanOrEqualToWhenPresent(criteria.createdFrom()))
        .and(createdAt, isLessThanWhenPresent(criteria.createdTo()))
        .orderBy(CaseSort.primary(criteria), id)
        .limit(criteria.limit())
        .build()
        .render(MYBATIS3);

    return mapper.selectMany(statement);
}
```

Production notes:

```text id="84n3yl"
Always:
  tenant predicate
  bounded limit
  deterministic order
  deleted/archived default filter where applicable
```

---

# 10. Reusable Predicate Pattern

## 10.1 Utility class

```java id="an52mm"
import org.mybatis.dynamic.sql.SqlColumn;
import org.mybatis.dynamic.sql.where.condition.IsEqualTo;
import org.mybatis.dynamic.sql.where.condition.IsNull;

import java.util.UUID;

import static org.mybatis.dynamic.sql.SqlBuilder.*;

public final class CommonPredicates {

    private CommonPredicates() {}

    public static IsEqualTo<UUID> tenantIs(UUID tenantId) {
        if (tenantId == null) {
            throw new MissingTenantException();
        }

        return isEqualTo(tenantId);
    }

    public static IsNull<Instant> notDeleted() {
        return isNull();
    }
}
```

## 10.2 Usage

```java id="k6m22n"
SelectStatementProvider statement =
    select(id, tenantId, caseNumber, status)
        .from(cases)
        .where(tenantId, CommonPredicates.tenantIs(criteria.tenantId()))
        .and(deletedAt, CommonPredicates.notDeleted())
        .and(status, isEqualToWhenPresent(criteria.statusName()))
        .build()
        .render(MYBATIS3);
```

Practical rule:

```text id="8ri3kg"
Reusable predicate bagus untuk invariant:
- tenant scope
- soft delete
- status active
- visibility scope

Jangan abstraksikan semua hal sampai query susah dibaca.
```

---

# 11. Authorization-Aware Query

```java id="bm5s6a"
public enum VisibilityScope {
    OWNED,
    TEAM,
    TENANT
}
```

```java id="l8sv6c"
public record CaseVisibilityCriteria(
    UUID tenantId,
    UUID actorId,
    String actorTeam,
    VisibilityScope scope,
    CaseStatus status,
    int limit
) {
    public String statusName() {
        return status == null ? null : status.name();
    }
}
```

```java id="excs6r"
public List<CaseRecord> searchVisibleCases(CaseVisibilityCriteria criteria) {
    var base =
        select(id, tenantId, caseNumber, status, assignedTeam, dueAt)
            .from(cases)
            .where(tenantId, isEqualTo(criteria.tenantId()))
            .and(deletedAt, isNull())
            .and(status, isEqualToWhenPresent(criteria.statusName()));

    switch (criteria.scope()) {
        case OWNED -> base.and(assigneeId, isEqualTo(criteria.actorId()));
        case TEAM -> base.and(assignedTeam, isEqualTo(criteria.actorTeam()));
        case TENANT -> {
            // tenant predicate already applied
        }
    }

    SelectStatementProvider statement =
        base.orderBy(dueAt, id)
            .limit(criteria.limit())
            .build()
            .render(MYBATIS3);

    return mapper.selectMany(statement);
}
```

**Important:** permission scope dihitung oleh authorization layer, bukan dari query parameter user.

---

# 12. Keyword Search Pattern

## 12.1 Criteria normalization

```java id="0lhj6c"
public record CaseKeywordSearchCriteria(
    UUID tenantId,
    String keywordPattern,
    int limit
) {
    public static CaseKeywordSearchCriteria of(UUID tenantId, String keyword, int limit) {
        return new CaseKeywordSearchCriteria(
            tenantId,
            keyword == null || keyword.isBlank()
                ? null
                : "%" + escapeLike(keyword.trim()) + "%",
            limit
        );
    }

    private static String escapeLike(String value) {
        return value
            .replace("\\", "\\\\")
            .replace("%", "\\%")
            .replace("_", "\\_");
    }
}
```

## 12.2 DSL query

```java id="ce2g4p"
public List<CaseRecord> searchByKeyword(CaseKeywordSearchCriteria criteria) {
    SelectStatementProvider statement =
        select(id, tenantId, caseNumber, status, priority, createdAt)
            .from(cases)
            .where(tenantId, isEqualTo(criteria.tenantId()))
            .and(deletedAt, isNull())
            .and(caseNumber, isLikeWhenPresent(criteria.keywordPattern()))
            .orderBy(createdAt.descending(), id.descending())
            .limit(criteria.limit())
            .build()
            .render(MYBATIS3);

    return mapper.selectMany(statement);
}
```

Production note:

```text id="k454mh"
LIKE/ILIKE '%keyword%' can be expensive.
For serious search:
  use trigram/full-text/search engine and index-aware design.
```

---

# 13. `IN` Condition Safety

Dynamic SQL 1.5.2 mengubah behavior `isIn`/`isNotIn` agar empty list tetap dirender dan menyebabkan runtime exception; perubahan ini disengaja demi safety. Jika ingin condition dihapus saat list kosong/null, gunakan versi `WhenPresent`, tetapi untuk `UPDATE`/`DELETE`, itu bisa berbahaya bila WHERE menjadi terlalu luas. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-dynamic-sql/docs/CHANGELOG.html))

## 13.1 Safe find by ids

```java id="t58xpx"
public List<CaseRecord> findByIds(UUID tenantIdValue, List<UUID> idsValue) {
    if (idsValue == null || idsValue.isEmpty()) {
        return List.of();
    }

    if (idsValue.size() > 500) {
        throw new TooManyIdsException(idsValue.size());
    }

    SelectStatementProvider statement =
        select(id, tenantId, caseNumber, status, version)
            .from(cases)
            .where(tenantId, isEqualTo(tenantIdValue))
            .and(id, isIn(idsValue))
            .orderBy(id)
            .build()
            .render(MYBATIS3);

    return mapper.selectMany(statement);
}
```

## 13.2 Dangerous delete example

```java id="4hpldf"
DeleteStatementProvider statement =
    deleteFrom(cases)
        .where(tenantId, isEqualTo(tenantIdValue))
        .and(id, isInWhenPresent(idsValue))
        .build()
        .render(MYBATIS3);
```

Risk:

```text id="f3j4be"
Jika idsValue empty/null dan isInWhenPresent menghapus condition,
query bisa menjadi:
  DELETE FROM cases WHERE tenant_id = ?
```

Rule:

```text id="7agsag"
For SELECT:
  empty list -> return List.of()

For UPDATE/DELETE:
  empty list -> throw exception

Do not rely on WhenPresent for destructive operations unless explicitly intended.
```

---

# 14. Pagination

## 14.1 Offset pagination

```java id="gfsz9f"
public List<CaseRecord> searchPage(CaseSearchCriteria criteria, int offset) {
    SelectStatementProvider statement =
        select(id, tenantId, caseNumber, status, priority, createdAt)
            .from(cases)
            .where(tenantId, isEqualTo(criteria.tenantId()))
            .and(status, isEqualToWhenPresent(criteria.statusName()))
            .orderBy(createdAt.descending(), id.descending())
            .limit(criteria.limit())
            .offset(offset)
            .build()
            .render(MYBATIS3);

    return mapper.selectMany(statement);
}
```

Dynamic SQL select mendukung `limit` dan `offset`, tetapi library menulis klausa tersebut apa adanya sehingga developer harus memastikan database target mendukung syntax itu; PostgreSQL mendukungnya. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-dynamic-sql/docs/select.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## 14.2 Keyset pagination

```java id="j2lg05"
public record CaseCursor(
    Instant createdAt,
    UUID id
) {}
```

```java id="cld5ly"
public List<CaseRecord> searchAfter(UUID tenantIdValue, CaseCursor cursor, int limit) {
    var builder =
        select(id, tenantId, caseNumber, status, priority, createdAt)
            .from(cases)
            .where(tenantId, isEqualTo(tenantIdValue))
            .and(deletedAt, isNull());

    if (cursor != null) {
        builder.and(
            createdAt,
            isLessThan(cursor.createdAt()),
            or(createdAt, isEqualTo(cursor.createdAt()), and(id, isLessThan(cursor.id())))
        );
    }

    SelectStatementProvider statement =
        builder.orderBy(createdAt.descending(), id.descending())
            .limit(limit)
            .build()
            .render(MYBATIS3);

    return mapper.selectMany(statement);
}
```

Catatan: expression nested seperti di atas bisa jadi verbose di DSL. Untuk keyset kompleks, XML/annotation literal kadang lebih jelas.

---

# 15. COUNT dengan DSL

## 15.1 Count mapper

```java id="fi2anl"
public interface CaseCountMapper {

    @SelectProvider(type = SqlProviderAdapter.class, method = "select")
    long count(SelectStatementProvider selectStatement);
}
```

## 15.2 Count query

```java id="wyjzo1"
public long countOpenCases(UUID tenantIdValue) {
    SelectStatementProvider statement =
        count()
            .from(cases)
            .where(tenantId, isEqualTo(tenantIdValue))
            .and(status, isEqualTo("OPEN"))
            .and(deletedAt, isNull())
            .build()
            .render(MYBATIS3);

    return countMapper.count(statement);
}
```

## 15.3 Production recommendation

```text id="wmx8ry"
Do not always count for search endpoint.

Prefer:
  limit + 1 for hasMore
  optional count endpoint
  cached/materialized dashboard count
```

---

# 16. MyBatis3Utils / Completer Pattern

Specialized MyBatis3 support menyediakan common mapper dan utility untuk mengurangi boilerplate `count`, `delete`, `insert`, `update`, dan `select`. Dokumentasi menyebut common mapper seperti `CommonCountMapper`, `CommonDeleteMapper`, `CommonInsertMapper`, `CommonUpdateMapper`, dan `CommonSelectMapper`, serta utility `MyBatis3Utils` dengan completer seperti `SelectDSLCompleter`. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-dynamic-sql/docs/mybatis3.html))

## 16.1 Mapper with provider methods

```java id="nzyydj"
public interface CaseCompleterMapper {

    @SelectProvider(type = SqlProviderAdapter.class, method = "select")
    @ResultMap("CaseRecordResult")
    List<CaseRecord> selectMany(SelectStatementProvider selectStatement);

    @SelectProvider(type = SqlProviderAdapter.class, method = "select")
    @ResultMap("CaseRecordResult")
    Optional<CaseRecord> selectOne(SelectStatementProvider selectStatement);

    @SelectProvider(type = SqlProviderAdapter.class, method = "select")
    long count(SelectStatementProvider selectStatement);

    @UpdateProvider(type = SqlProviderAdapter.class, method = "update")
    int update(UpdateStatementProvider updateStatement);

    @DeleteProvider(type = SqlProviderAdapter.class, method = "delete")
    int delete(DeleteStatementProvider deleteStatement);
}
```

## 16.2 Default select method

```java id="40qfig"
public interface CaseCompleterMapper {

    BasicColumn[] selectList = BasicColumn.columnList(
        id,
        tenantId,
        caseNumber,
        status,
        priority,
        assignedTeam,
        version,
        dueAt,
        createdAt,
        updatedAt
    );

    @SelectProvider(type = SqlProviderAdapter.class, method = "select")
    @ResultMap("CaseRecordResult")
    List<CaseRecord> selectMany(SelectStatementProvider selectStatement);

    default List<CaseRecord> select(SelectDSLCompleter completer) {
        return MyBatis3Utils.selectList(
            this::selectMany,
            selectList,
            cases,
            completer
        );
    }
}
```

## 16.3 Usage

```java id="n59q0n"
List<CaseRecord> rows = mapper.select(c ->
    c.where(tenantId, isEqualTo(criteria.tenantId()))
     .and(status, isEqualTo("OPEN"))
     .orderBy(createdAt.descending(), id.descending())
     .limit(criteria.limit())
);
```

Production warning:

```text id="oe7h0l"
Completer pattern is powerful.
Do not expose arbitrary completer outside repository boundary.

Good:
  repository builds completer from safe criteria

Bad:
  resource/controller passes lambda to mapper directly
```

---

# 17. INSERT Deep Dive

## 17.1 Single insert

```java id="8ddlk2"
public int insertCase(CaseRecord record) {
    InsertStatementProvider<CaseRecord> statement =
        insert(record)
            .into(cases)
            .map(id).toProperty("id")
            .map(tenantId).toProperty("tenantId")
            .map(caseNumber).toProperty("caseNumber")
            .map(status).toProperty("status")
            .map(priority).toProperty("priority")
            .map(assignedTeam).toProperty("assignedTeam")
            .map(version).toProperty("version")
            .map(dueAt).toProperty("dueAt")
            .map(createdAt).toProperty("createdAt")
            .map(updatedAt).toProperty("updatedAt")
            .build()
            .render(MYBATIS3);

    return mapper.insert(statement);
}
```

## 17.2 Selective insert

Dynamic SQL insert supports mapping variants like `toNull`, `toConstant`, `toStringConstant`, `toProperty`, `toPropertyWhenPresent`, `toValue`, and `toValueWhenPresent`. Values mapped through normal value/property methods are bound as prepared statement parameters. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-dynamic-sql/docs/insert.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

```java id="akv7fu"
public int insertSelective(CaseRecord record) {
    InsertStatementProvider<CaseRecord> statement =
        insert(record)
            .into(cases)
            .map(id).toProperty("id")
            .map(tenantId).toProperty("tenantId")
            .map(caseNumber).toProperty("caseNumber")
            .map(status).toProperty("status")
            .map(priority).toPropertyWhenPresent("priority", record::priority)
            .map(assignedTeam).toPropertyWhenPresent("assignedTeam", record::assignedTeam)
            .map(version).toConstant("0")
            .map(createdAt).toConstant("now()")
            .map(updatedAt).toConstant("now()")
            .build()
            .render(MYBATIS3);

    return mapper.insert(statement);
}
```

## 17.3 Generated keys

For single-row insert generated values, mapper `@Options(useGeneratedKeys=true, keyProperty="row.<attribute>")` must point to `row.<attribute>` because the statement provider wraps the record as `row`. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-dynamic-sql/docs/insert.html))

```java id="hg1k06"
@InsertProvider(type = SqlProviderAdapter.class, method = "insert")
@Options(
    useGeneratedKeys = true,
    keyProperty = "row.id",
    keyColumn = "id",
    timeout = 5
)
int insertGenerated(InsertStatementProvider<AuditLogInsert> statement);
```

---

# 18. Multiple Row Insert vs JDBC Batch

Dynamic SQL supports multiple-row insert, but docs warn it can generate many prepared statement parameters and may hit JDBC/database limits; for many records, JDBC batch is usually more efficient. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-dynamic-sql/docs/insert.html))

## 18.1 Multiple-row insert for small batch

```java id="72zm43"
public int insertMultipleSmall(List<CaseRecord> records) {
    if (records.isEmpty()) {
        return 0;
    }

    if (records.size() > 100) {
        throw new TooManyRowsForMultiInsertException(records.size());
    }

    MultiRowInsertStatementProvider<CaseRecord> statement =
        insertMultiple(records)
            .into(cases)
            .map(id).toProperty("id")
            .map(tenantId).toProperty("tenantId")
            .map(caseNumber).toProperty("caseNumber")
            .map(status).toProperty("status")
            .map(priority).toProperty("priority")
            .map(version).toProperty("version")
            .map(createdAt).toProperty("createdAt")
            .map(updatedAt).toProperty("updatedAt")
            .build()
            .render(MYBATIS3);

    return mapper.insertMultiple(statement);
}
```

Mapper:

```java id="qoevux"
@InsertProvider(type = SqlProviderAdapter.class, method = "insertMultiple")
@Options(timeout = 10)
int insertMultiple(MultiRowInsertStatementProvider<CaseRecord> statement);
```

## 18.2 JDBC batch for large import

```java id="p9ginv"
public int batchInsertCases(List<CaseRecord> records) {
    int total = 0;

    try (SqlSession session =
             sqlSessionFactory.openSession(ExecutorType.BATCH, false)) {

        CaseDynamicMapper mapper = session.getMapper(CaseDynamicMapper.class);

        for (CaseRecord record : records) {
            InsertStatementProvider<CaseRecord> statement =
                insert(record)
                    .into(cases)
                    .map(id).toProperty("id")
                    .map(tenantId).toProperty("tenantId")
                    .map(caseNumber).toProperty("caseNumber")
                    .map(status).toProperty("status")
                    .map(priority).toProperty("priority")
                    .map(version).toProperty("version")
                    .map(createdAt).toProperty("createdAt")
                    .map(updatedAt).toProperty("updatedAt")
                    .build()
                    .render(MYBATIS3);

            mapper.insert(statement);
            total++;
        }

        session.flushStatements();
        session.commit();
    }

    return total;
}
```

Production rule:

```text id="aywuf3"
Small list:
  multi-row insert is OK

Large import:
  use JDBC batch + chunking

Need generated keys for many rows:
  test carefully; dynamic SQL docs mention limitations for generated values with multiple-row insert.
```

---

# 19. UPDATE Deep Dive

Dynamic SQL update consists of table, flexible `SET` clauses, and optional `WHERE`; docs warn that update without a WHERE clause updates every row. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-dynamic-sql/docs/update.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## 19.1 Optimistic locking update

```java id="392t2r"
public int transitionStatus(
    UUID tenantIdValue,
    UUID caseId,
    String fromStatus,
    String toStatus,
    long expectedVersion
) {
    UpdateStatementProvider statement =
        update(cases)
            .set(status).equalTo(toStatus)
            .set(version).equalToConstant("version + 1")
            .set(updatedAt).equalToConstant("now()")
            .where(tenantId, isEqualTo(tenantIdValue))
            .and(id, isEqualTo(caseId))
            .and(status, isEqualTo(fromStatus))
            .and(version, isEqualTo(expectedVersion))
            .build()
            .render(MYBATIS3);

    return mapper.update(statement);
}
```

Service:

```java id="9i5ott"
int updated = repository.transitionStatus(
    tenantId,
    caseId,
    "SUBMITTED",
    "APPROVED",
    expectedVersion
);

if (updated != 1) {
    throw new ConcurrentCaseModificationException(caseId);
}
```

## 19.2 Selective patch update

```java id="d8f5qw"
public int patchCase(CasePatch patch) {
    if (patch.isEmpty()) {
        throw new EmptyPatchException();
    }

    UpdateStatementProvider statement =
        update(cases)
            .set(priority).equalToWhenPresent(
                patch.priority() == null ? null : patch.priority().name()
            )
            .set(assignedTeam).equalToWhenPresent(patch.assignedTeam())
            .set(updatedAt).equalToConstant("now()")
            .set(version).equalToConstant("version + 1")
            .where(tenantId, isEqualTo(patch.tenantId()))
            .and(id, isEqualTo(patch.caseId()))
            .and(version, isEqualTo(patch.expectedVersion()))
            .build()
            .render(MYBATIS3);

    return mapper.update(statement);
}
```

Production checklist:

```text id="ogfwti"
For update:
  tenant predicate
  id/business key predicate
  version/status predicate for state transition
  affected-row checked
  empty patch rejected
```

---

# 20. DELETE Deep Dive

Dynamic SQL delete consists of table and optional where clause; docs explicitly note that delete without where will delete every row in the table. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-dynamic-sql/docs/delete.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## 20.1 Safe cleanup delete

```java id="v9glxc"
public int deleteExpiredIdempotencyKeys(UUID tenantIdValue, Instant before) {
    DeleteStatementProvider statement =
        deleteFrom(idempotencyKeys)
            .where(idempotencyTenantId, isEqualTo(tenantIdValue))
            .and(idempotencyStatus, isIn("COMPLETED", "FAILED"))
            .and(idempotencyCreatedAt, isLessThan(before))
            .build()
            .render(MYBATIS3);

    return idempotencyMapper.delete(statement);
}
```

## 20.2 Prevent accidental full delete

```java id="73tmxj"
public int deleteByIds(UUID tenantIdValue, List<UUID> idsValue) {
    if (tenantIdValue == null) {
        throw new MissingTenantException();
    }

    if (idsValue == null || idsValue.isEmpty()) {
        throw new EmptyDeleteTargetException();
    }

    DeleteStatementProvider statement =
        deleteFrom(cases)
            .where(tenantId, isEqualTo(tenantIdValue))
            .and(id, isIn(idsValue))
            .build()
            .render(MYBATIS3);

    return mapper.delete(statement);
}
```

Production rule:

```text id="9gkm0i"
DELETE/UPDATE DSL must never rely on optional WHERE accidentally.

Guard destructive commands in Java before building DSL.
```

---

# 21. PostgreSQL Escape Hatch: `RETURNING`, CTE, Procedure

Dynamic SQL is not always the best tool for PostgreSQL-specific patterns.

## 21.1 `RETURNING`

Use annotation/XML:

```java id="4k1l47"
@Select(
    value = """
        UPDATE cases
        SET
          status = 'APPROVED',
          version = version + 1,
          updated_at = now()
        WHERE tenant_id = #{tenantId}
          AND id = #{caseId}
          AND status = 'SUBMITTED'
          AND version = #{expectedVersion}
        RETURNING
          id,
          tenant_id,
          case_number,
          status,
          priority,
          assigned_team,
          version,
          due_at,
          created_at,
          updated_at
        """,
    affectData = true
)
@Options(timeout = 5, flushCache = Options.FlushCachePolicy.TRUE)
@ResultMap("CaseRecordResult")
CaseRecord approveReturning(ApproveCaseCommand command);
```

## 21.2 CTE queue claim

Use annotation/XML:

```java id="63akv0"
@Select(
    value = """
        WITH candidate AS (
          SELECT id
          FROM outbox_events
          WHERE status = 'PENDING'
            AND available_at <= now()
          ORDER BY available_at ASC, id ASC
          LIMIT #{limit}
          FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED
        ),
        claimed AS (
          UPDATE outbox_events o
          SET
            status = 'PROCESSING',
            locked_by = #{workerId},
            locked_at = now(),
            attempt_count = attempt_count + 1
          FROM candidate c
          WHERE o.id = c.id
          RETURNING
            o.id,
            o.tenant_id,
            o.event_type,
            o.payload,
            o.status,
            o.attempt_count,
            o.created_at
        )
        SELECT *
        FROM claimed
        """,
    affectData = true
)
@Options(timeout = 5, fetchSize = 100, flushCache = Options.FlushCachePolicy.TRUE)
@ResultMap("OutboxEventResult")
List<OutboxEventRecord> claimOutboxEvents(
    @Param("workerId") String workerId,
    @Param("limit") int limit
);
```

## 21.3 Procedure call

```java id="8ya2ig"
@Options(
    statementType = StatementType.CALLABLE,
    timeout = 5,
    flushCache = Options.FlushCachePolicy.TRUE
)
@Update("""
    { CALL approve_case_proc(
        #{tenantId, mode=IN, jdbcType=OTHER},
        #{caseId, mode=IN, jdbcType=OTHER},
        #{actorId, mode=IN, jdbcType=OTHER},
        #{expectedVersion, mode=IN, jdbcType=BIGINT},
        #{updated, mode=OUT, jdbcType=INTEGER},
        #{newVersion, mode=OUT, jdbcType=BIGINT}
      )
    }
    """)
void approveCaseProcedure(ApproveCaseProcedureParams params);
```

Decision rule:

```text id="xpqh0w"
DSL:
  common CRUD/search

Annotation/XML:
  PostgreSQL CTE
  DML RETURNING
  stored procedure/function call
  advanced locking
  window/report query
```

---

# 22. Database Functions / Extensions

Library ships with some common database function implementations, but docs state it intentionally does not implement a huge set; instead, it provides examples and extension points so teams can add project-specific functions. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-dynamic-sql/docs/functions.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## 22.1 Lowercase search example

```java id="5hh2j7"
SelectStatementProvider statement =
    select(id, tenantId, caseNumber, status)
        .from(cases)
        .where(tenantId, isEqualTo(tenantIdValue))
        .and(caseNumber, isLikeCaseInsensitive(keywordPattern))
        .build()
        .render(MYBATIS3);
```

## 22.2 Custom expression escape hatch

For PostgreSQL-specific expression, sometimes use constant/derived column carefully:

```java id="xm1o1p"
BasicColumn searchRank = DerivedColumn.of("ts_rank(search_vector, plainto_tsquery(#{parameters.p1}))");
```

Production warning:

```text id="a4qqxi"
Be careful with raw derived SQL.
If raw SQL includes user value:
  bind it as parameter, not string concatenate.

For complex expression:
  prefer XML/annotation where reviewers can see exact SQL.
```

---

# 23. Rendering and Logging

## 23.1 Rendered SQL

```java id="7to93g"
SelectStatementProvider statement =
    select(id, tenantId, caseNumber)
        .from(cases)
        .where(tenantId, isEqualTo(tenantIdValue))
        .and(status, isEqualTo("OPEN"))
        .build()
        .render(MYBATIS3);

log.debug("Rendered SQL: {}", statement.getSelectStatement());
```

Do not log full parameter values if they may contain PII.

## 23.2 Safe debug utility

```java id="9e4ce4"
public final class DynamicSqlDebug {

    private DynamicSqlDebug() {}

    public static void logSelect(
        Logger log,
        String operation,
        SelectStatementProvider statement
    ) {
        if (!log.isDebugEnabled()) {
            return;
        }

        log.debug(
            "Dynamic SQL operation={} sql={}",
            operation,
            statement.getSelectStatement()
        );
    }
}
```

---

# 24. Testing Strategy

## 24.1 Unit test criteria conversion

```java id="nk7nl3"
@Test
void shouldRejectInvalidSort() {
    CaseSearchRequest request = new CaseSearchRequest(
        "OPEN",
        null,
        null,
        null,
        null,
        null,
        "created_at desc; drop table cases; --",
        "desc",
        50
    );

    assertThatThrownBy(() ->
        CaseSearchCriteriaFactory.from(request, tenantId)
    ).isInstanceOf(InvalidSortFieldException.class);
}
```

## 24.2 Rendered SQL smoke test

```java id="5uc8se"
@Test
void searchShouldRenderTenantPredicate() {
    CaseSearchCriteria criteria = new CaseSearchCriteria(
        tenantId,
        CaseStatus.OPEN,
        null,
        null,
        null,
        null,
        null,
        CaseSortField.CREATED_AT,
        SortDirection.DESC,
        50
    );

    SelectStatementProvider statement =
        caseQueryBuilder.searchStatement(criteria);

    assertThat(statement.getSelectStatement())
        .contains("where tenant_id =")
        .contains("status =")
        .contains("order by created_at desc");
}
```

## 24.3 Integration test against PostgreSQL

```java id="flb79e"
@Test
void searchShouldNotReturnOtherTenantData() {
    UUID tenantA = UUID.randomUUID();
    UUID tenantB = UUID.randomUUID();

    fixture.insertCase(tenantA, "CASE-A", "OPEN");
    fixture.insertCase(tenantB, "CASE-B", "OPEN");

    CaseSearchCriteria criteria = new CaseSearchCriteria(
        tenantA,
        CaseStatus.OPEN,
        null,
        null,
        null,
        null,
        null,
        CaseSortField.CREATED_AT,
        SortDirection.DESC,
        50
    );

    List<CaseRecord> rows = repository.search(criteria);

    assertThat(rows)
        .extracting(CaseRecord::caseNumber)
        .containsExactly("CASE-A");
}
```

## 24.4 Destructive operation guard test

```java id="k1ptzf"
@Test
void deleteByIdsShouldRejectEmptyList() {
    assertThatThrownBy(() -> repository.deleteByIds(tenantId, List.of()))
        .isInstanceOf(EmptyDeleteTargetException.class);
}
```

---

# 25. Observability Pattern

## 25.1 Repository-level timing

```java id="di2ajl"
public List<CaseRecord> search(CaseSearchCriteria criteria) {
    long start = System.nanoTime();

    try {
        SelectStatementProvider statement = searchStatement(criteria);
        return mapper.selectMany(statement);
    } finally {
        long durationMs =
            TimeUnit.NANOSECONDS.toMillis(System.nanoTime() - start);

        log.info(
            "DynamicSQL operation={} tenantId={} status={} sort={} limit={} durationMs={}",
            "CaseRepository.search",
            criteria.tenantId(),
            criteria.status(),
            criteria.sortField(),
            criteria.limit(),
            durationMs
        );
    }
}
```

## 25.2 Metric dimensions

| Tag | Safe? | Note |
|---|---:|---|
| operation | Ya | `case.search` |
| mapper | Ya | `CaseDynamicMapper.selectMany` |
| sort field enum | Ya | bounded cardinality |
| has keyword | Ya | boolean |
| filter count | Ya | low cardinality |
| tenantId | hati-hati | avoid high-cardinality metric tag |
| keyword value | Tidak | PII/cardinality |
| raw parameters | Tidak | PII/security |

---

# 26. Enterprise Decision Table

| Need | DSL Pattern |
|---|---|
| Safe optional search | `isEqualToWhenPresent`, criteria object |
| Safe sort | enum → `SortSpecification` |
| Tenant filter | mandatory `.where(tenantId, isEqualTo(...))` |
| Find by IDs | validate list, then `isIn(ids)` |
| Empty `IN` select | return empty list |
| Empty `IN` delete/update | throw exception |
| Patch update | `equalToWhenPresent` + empty patch guard |
| Optimistic locking | `.and(version, isEqualTo(expectedVersion))` |
| Count | `count().from(...).where(...)` |
| Delete cleanup | explicit WHERE + max scope |
| Large insert | JDBC batch, not huge multi-row statement |
| Generated key | `@Options(keyProperty="row.id")` |
| PostgreSQL RETURNING | annotation/XML escape hatch |
| CTE queue claim | annotation/XML escape hatch |
| Procedure call | annotation/XML `CALLABLE` |
| Complex report | XML/annotation SQL |

---

# 27. Anti-Pattern

| Anti-Pattern | Risk |
|---|---|
| Resource builds DSL directly | persistence concern leaks to API |
| Raw request becomes sort column | injection/invalid SQL |
| `isInWhenPresent` on delete/update | condition dropped, too broad mutation |
| Update/delete without WHERE | full table modification |
| Dynamic table name from tenant/request | SQL injection/routing bug |
| Huge multi-row insert | too many parameters, poor performance |
| Logging rendered SQL + parameters with PII | data exposure |
| DSL builder method 300 lines long | unreadable query |
| Using DSL for every PostgreSQL-specific CTE | less readable than SQL |
| No integration test | false confidence |
| No tenant predicate helper/test | cross-tenant leak |
| Generated key property wrong | ID not populated |
| No affected row check | lost update missed |

---

# 28. Production Checklist

| Checklist | Status Ideal |
|---|---:|
| Table/column model uses explicit JDBCType | Ya |
| JSONB/enum custom TypeHandler registered | Ya |
| DSL hidden behind repository/query service | Ya |
| Criteria object validated before DSL | Ya |
| Tenant predicate mandatory | Ya |
| Sort uses enum/SqlColumn, not raw string | Ya |
| `limit` bounded | Ya |
| Empty list behavior explicit | Ya |
| Update/delete have WHERE guards | Ya |
| Affected row checked for command update | Ya |
| Batch insert uses chunking/JDBC batch | Ya |
| PostgreSQL escape hatch used for `RETURNING`/CTE/procedure | Ya |
| Rendered SQL smoke tests for critical query | Ya |
| PostgreSQL integration tests for mapper behavior | Ya |
| Observability logs safe criteria summary | Ya |

---

# 29. Roadmap Belajar Singkat

| Urutan | Materi | Target |
|---:|---|---|
| 1 | DynamicSqlSupport | Table/column model benar |
| 2 | Provider mapper | `SqlProviderAdapter` dan `StatementProvider` |
| 3 | Criteria object | raw request tidak masuk DSL |
| 4 | SELECT DSL | optional predicates/search |
| 5 | Safe sorting | enum + `SortSpecification` |
| 6 | IN/list safety | empty list behavior jelas |
| 7 | INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE DSL | selective write dan guard |
| 8 | MyBatis3Utils | completer pattern untuk reusable mapper |
| 9 | PostgreSQL escape hatch | CTE/RETURNING/procedure via XML/annotation |
| 10 | Testing/observability | render smoke + Testcontainers PostgreSQL |

---
